Chapter 751: The Goddess Has Given a Bit Too Much!

The fishman youngster remained calm at Cang Xu's proposal, even nodding slightly in agreement.

In truth, involving the fishman youngster in the sacrilegious ritual had always been part of their plan.

The slumber of the Charming Blue Goddess had granted the survivors countless opportunities and now represented their greatest hope for escape.

Cang Xu had already reaped significant benefits from blasphemous rituals. He used this to gradually break the deadlock. If they continued, the survivors could not only successfully leave this place but also acquire a wealth of valuable resources.

But Cang Xu was ultimately just one person.

At this moment, if someone could cooperate with him... Then undoubtedly, more hands make light work, and progress would be faster. Even a powerhouse like Jian Gui required the aid of figures like Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, and Qi Ling to execute his Memory Fabrication Project.

Yet, for the fishman youngster to participate in the blasphemous ritual, two major hurdles remained.

First: Performing the ritual demanded mastery of Memory Switching, Memory Concealment, and Memory Fabrication. These were tasks only a magician could perform, and only the blasphemer himself could execute them.

Another was the safety hazard.

By now, both issues have been resolved.

The fishman youngster embarked on his magician training, and his magician level had already advanced to iron level.

He not only mastered water spells but could even wield undead magic.

The fishman youngster was now fully capable of mastering and wielding memory-related spells as a magician.

With the first problem solved, the second was security.

The greatest risk of becoming a blasphemer lay in memory corruption, leading to distorted self-perception and subsequent destruction aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Or memory confusion causing the blasphemer to lose themselves, unable to perform even basic life functions like eating.

Should either scenario occur, a companion would need to intervene.

Zhong Yong was unsuitable.

He lacked the clearance to access the survivors' core secrets. Involving him would expose too much of their inner workings.

Cang Xu, however, was a viable option.

His loyalty was unquestionable. The experiences on Mystifying Monster Island had earned the trust of the fishman youngster.

Cang Xu's strength had previously been a concern.

He was still too weak. If the fishman youngster went rogue aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Cang Xu would struggle to contain the situation.

But now, that concern is nearly gone.

Cang Xu's personal strength has surged dramatically!

First, he underwent bloodline refinement, acquiring the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline. This comprehensive bloodline enhancement significantly boosted his combat prowess.

Second, he has mastered divine magic. The synergy between magic and divine spells made him an even more formidable opponent.

Beyond that, he still had the tower spirit and those alchemy puppets.

He even possessed the Deceptive Disguise spell.

With these assets, Cang Xu was confident he could stabilize the Deep Sea Monster Fish even if unexpected events arose.

“Then I'll give it a try.” The fishman youngster made his decision.

Cang Xu's face lit up with delight.

Before proposing this suggestion, he'd harbored some apprehension.

After all, their commander worshiped the Justice God. Would performing a blasphemous ritual conflict with the youngster's sense of righteousness?

The truth was, after his recent psychological transformation, the fishman youngster had grown more pragmatic, evolving into a superior leader.

Perhaps it was also because Charming Blue Goddess was a deity of the fishmen, and not of humans?

In any case, the fishman youngster's initiative delighted Cang Xu.

He promptly shared his experience with the youngster and suggested, “Commander, you could use my fabricated memories. They only require minor adjustments.”

"This approach is more reliable and increases our chances of success. On the other hand, we can conduct comparative experiments to gather more intelligence."

The fishman youngster listened attentively, then asked numerous questions.

When it came to practical implementation, there were indeed many issues to resolve.

Not long after.

The fishman youngster entered the compartment. The metal door closed, blocking Cang Xu's view.

This time, Cang Xu remained outside the door, ready for any unexpected developments.

While maintaining vigilance, Cang Xu silently assessed the situation.

“The base success rates for Memory Concealment and Memory Switching are exceptionally high.”

"When You Shen designed these spells, he did so deliberately. Extending the incantation time, increasing magic power consumption, all these sacrifices were made solely to maximize the base success rate.

His approach was remarkably wise and logical."

"Memory is too crucial; if the spell fails and memories become scrambled, the caster will suffer terribly."

Therefore, even if the fishman youngster was attempting Memory Concealment and Memory Switching for the first time, Cang Xu felt no concern.

"Charming Blue Goddess has fallen into slumber. I succeeded time and again before, there's no reason the commander shouldn't."

"What will the commander receive in this divine blessing?"

Cang Xu thought of himself.

In his first divine blessing, constrained by his own aptitude, he hadn't caught the Charming Blue Goddess' attention. Thus, he merely regained his magic power and mental energy.

“The commander's aptitude far surpasses mine.”

“Though the exact nature of his fishman bloodline remains unknown, his efficiency in mastering magic suggests it must be of a high level.”

“And being in his fishman form, he should receive greater favor and a more substantial divine blessing.”

Cang Xu pondered deeply.

“However, even with the forged memories, his level of faith remains only at the devout level.”

“This severely limits the extent of his divine blessing.”

“Combining this with intelligence I gathered from Zhou Zhang and members of the Charming Blue Church, the commander will likely receive a clerical position and doctrine.”

“For clerical positions, the most basic is monk, equivalent to a magician apprentice.”

“The commander might receive more, perhaps becoming a missionary directly?”

"But acquiring my set of missionary divine spells would be difficult for him. After all, this memory fabrication didn't involve necromancers. He himself only prayed using the fishman bloodline, without incorporating the Ghostface Fish bloodline."

The moment this thought crossed his mind, Cang Xu’s expression shifted dramatically!

A potent aura of divine power abruptly descended, exploding within the sealed chamber with unprecedented intensity!

Cang Xu stood on full alert.

Moments later, the metal hatch hissed softly as it slid open.

The fishman youngster emerged with a peculiar yet delighted expression: “Cang Xu, I know what my fishman bloodline is now.”

Cang Xu's face remained cold: “No rush. Verify the password first.”

This was an essential procedure.

The password was correct, restoring the fishman youngster and Cang Xu to their former level of trust.

Upon learning the answer from the youngster, Cang Xu couldn't help but lose himself for a moment.

He murmured, “It's actually the Surging Wave fishman bloodline!”

“That's one of the three god level bloodlines unique to the fishman race.”

True to his magician heritage, Cang Xu possessed this specialized knowledge.

The fishman was a lesser intelligent race, inferior to humans and elves, possessing only three god level bloodlines.

They were the Azure Shark bloodline, the Algae-Washing bloodline, and the Surging Wave bloodline.

The Azure Shark bloodline was suited for cultivating battle qi, while those with the Algae-Washing bloodline achieve astonishing progress when practicing magic. As for the Surging Wave bloodline, it was perfect for cultivating divine magic!

“I never imagined the fishman saint corpse buried on Two Eyes Island would contain this bloodline.”

“This is truly fantastic!”

"It could be said that it is the best choice available at this level. Far more suitable for us than the Azure Shark or Algae-Washing bloodlines."

Cang Xu sighed in admiration before inquiring, “With such a god level bloodline, and one most suited for divine cultivation...”

“Commander, your divine blessing this time must have been quite bountiful, right?”

“Yes.” The fishman youngster nodded, revealing everything without reservation.

“I've advanced.”

“I am now a silver level magician.”

Cang Xu nodded. “Outstanding. That's the highest tier of divine blessing I'm aware of.”

“I also received a clerical position, similar to yours before,” the fishman youngster continued. “A cleric.”

Cang Xu nodded again. "The cleric position, that's one level higher than missionary. Beyond spreading the faith, you can also gain divine favor by performing divine spells, healing others, and such."

“My Lord, your gains this time are immense!”

The fishman youngster nodded and added, “I also received a series of divine spells, 28 in total.”

Cang Xu nodded again. "Twenty-eight divine spells? That's incredible. I never imagined Charming Blue Goddess would favor you so greatly. To receive such a substantial investment on your very first prayer, isn't that fitting for a god level bloodline?"

“If next time, my lord, you perform the hybrid transformation of the Ghostface Fish bloodline, you might even obtain the special missionary divine spells I received before.”

The fishman youngster finally shook his head. “Cang Xu, the divine spells I've obtained already include the ones you mentioned.”

Cang Xu froze, about to nod again, but finally couldn't hold back.

“Th-that doesn't make sense!”

“Lord, even if you possess a god level bloodline, its concentration is only 11%, correct?”

The fishman youngster nodded.

Cang Xu continued, “Even if the Charming Blue Goddess favors your god level bloodline, 11% is still too weak. That concentration isn't even strong enough to support rapid cultivation of silver level spells.”

“Based on our previous estimates, 11% god level bloodline should only allow you to reach gold level at most.”

"I also made subtle inquiries about the specifications of divine blessings from Zhou Zhang and the other gold level church members."

“Theoretically, your divine blessing yield should be lower than theirs.”

“Because you're merely a future gold level, while they are all genuine, present-day gold level experts!”

“But the content of your divine blessing this time not only surpassed their best records—it far exceeded them.”

"Based on the intelligence I've gathered and calculated return rates, this divine blessing should either directly elevate you to a silver level magician or grant you the cleric position to wield standard divine magic."

“Yet now, you've gained both, and even my unique set of divine spells!”

At this point, Cang Xu stared at the fishman youngster.

The youngster smiled faintly: “Perhaps this is how a god level bloodline truly differs?”

“I shall perform a second blasphemous ritual and pray to see.”

Cang Xu nodded, eager yet mindful to reassure him first: “Commander, your first divine blessing yielded such extraordinary gains, it's already beyond what's expected.”

“A second consecutive blessing likely won't bring such immense rewards.”

The fishman youngster nodded, shot Cang Xu a reassuring glance, then turned and returned to his cabin.

Chapter 752: How Could the Second Time be Even More?

The metal hatch closed once more.

Cang Xu remained vigilant.

After all, this was only the second time the fishman youngster had performed such an act. Should memory confusion or similar issues arise, it wouldn't just be the Deep Sea Monster Fish that faced danger, more importantly, the youngster himself would be at risk.

“Though the Deep Sea Monster Fish is a gold level magic ship, compared to the commander, it's utterly insignificant!”

Cang Xu's greatest concern remained the fishman youngster.

Moments later.

The divine blessing manifested once more.

Cang Xu's eyes widened in astonishment. “What's happening?”

“The divine power fluctuations from this second prayer are even stronger than before.”

The metal hatch opened. The fishman youngster stepped out, his stride steady though his expression remained peculiar.

Cang Xu remained vigilant, exchanging the agreed-upon password with the youngster.

Satisfied the youngster was unharmed, the old necromancer sighed in relief. “Commander, this time...”

The fishman youngster cleared his throat. “This time... my clergy cultivation has advanced to silver level!”

Cang Xu was stunned.

As one of the three major transcendent branches, clergymen naturally possessed their own transcendent levels.

Like fighters and magicians, the lowest tier was bronze level, followed by iron level, silver level, and so on, ascending all the way to god level.

Cang Xu attempted to analyze: “This is actually understandable.”

“Though a clergyman's transcendent level isn't directly tied to their clerical position.”

“But generally speaking, in practical terms, higher clerical positions require higher transcendent levels.”

“For a cleric, pairing with a silver level transcendent level is perfectly normal.”

Since returning via the second teleportation, Cang Xu had drawn close to Zhou Zhang and other members of the Charming Blue Church, gathering much intelligence on their enemies from them.

Within the Charming Blue Church, monks were typically bronze level, while missionaries and clerics ranged between iron level and silver level. Priests and bishops, meanwhile, were generally gold level or saint level.

Monk, missionary, cleric, priest, bishop... these were job classifications in the church, also known as clerical positions.

The relationship between clergymen and these clerical positions was roughly analogous to that between magicians and titles such as alchemists, apothecaries, battle mages, and other such professions.

In contrast, fighters could serve as soldiers, knights, mercenaries, thieves, or assassins based on their battle skills and battle qi.

At this point, Cang Xu shifted his tone: “But, Commander, your second prayer yielded such remarkable results.”

“You must understand, despite my numerous prayers and attaining the position of missionary, I remain merely a bronze level clergyman.”

“Yet your single prayer propelled your clergy level straight from bronze to silver level.”

“This is truly...”

Most others might feel unfairness under such circumstances.

But Cang Xu was not like that.

He felt profound gratitude toward the fishman youngster and was willing to follow his lead. Learning of the youngster's immense gains filled him with delight and awe.

“Ahem,” the fishman youngster cleared his throat. “Actually, there's more than just these gains.”

“Could it be... you've also received more divine spells?” Cang Xu asked curiously.

“No, it's a divine statue,” the youngster replied without further delay.

Cang Xu then entered the ship's cabin and saw the divine statue the fishman youngster had mentioned.

It was an image of the Charming Blue Goddess, carved in the form of a mermaid.

Originally, it was quite ordinary.

Just one of the cabin's furnishings, deliberately placed, without a hint of supernatural elements.

A commonplace object.

But now, this statue radiated a silver level aura!

It had ascended.

Its transformation was even more extraordinary than that of the fishman youngster.

From an ordinary object, it had ascended into a silver level sacred artifact!

"I-Is this the power of the gods? The ability to transcend the mundane?!" Cang Xu was stunned.

He had knelt before this statue in prayer more than once.

He understood deeply that the statue was an ordinary item.

Yet now, it has become a silver level sacred artifact.

Sacred artifacts were transcendent items forged by divine power, naturally possessing corresponding transcendent levels.

Cang Xu's expression immediately turned grave: “Though I've only recently become a clergyman, I know this much.”

“Crafting sacred artifacts requires transcendent materials.”

“To forge something transcendent from ordinary materials... I've never heard of such a thing.”

The two exchanged glances, speechless for a moment.

Compared to the gods, they were still too small and weak, unable to comprehend or fathom many things.

After a moment of silence, the fishman youngster spoke first, his eyes gleaming faintly: “I know the purpose of the divine statue. It channels faith, accumulating and storing spirituality.”

“With a silver level sacred artifact statue, continuing my prayers will yield greater benefits.”

“But the statue isn't just a conduit for divine power or a reservoir for faith, it's also a monitoring device for the deity.”

Hearing his commander speak thus, Cang Xu felt his own worries and doubts ease somewhat.

This showed the fishman youngster lacked such profound devotion, retaining a strong sense of self.

Cang Xu remarked, “Something feels off about this.”

“Commander, the rewards from your second prayer surpassed the first.”

"The first time, your magician level advanced from iron to silver. The second time, your clergyman cultivation ascended from bronze to silver level."

"The first time, you gained the clerical position and 28 divine spells. The second time, you obtained a silver level sacred artifact, a divine statue!"

Cang Xu couldn't fathom it.

He couldn't help but speculate: “Could it be that this Surging Wave fishman bloodline holds significant importance to the Charming Blue Goddess?”

“Even if its concentration is only 11%?”

The fishman youngster pondered, “Perhaps the Charming Blue Goddess is in a dire state. She urgently needs a crucial element to break the stalemate. A god level bloodline represents hope for her.”

“Just as She accepted your prayers when you prayed as a necromancer.”

Cang Xu pondered relentlessly, yet no other explanation came to mind in the short term.

He tried to follow the fishman youngster's line of reasoning: “If that's the case, and it truly is because of the Surging Wave fishman bloodline, then Commander, your top priority moving forward must be to increase its bloodline concentration!”

The fishman youngster nodded.

He had already made this decision long ago.

All along, he had actively sought out bloodlines, steadily accumulating them.

Through his efforts, his previous Flame Dragon King bloodline had already reached 34%. The reason the Surging Wave bloodline showed no signs of activity was purely because he had no way to proceed.

Now that he understood it was the Surging Wave bloodline, he could identify downstream lifeforms from the bloodline spectrum and pinpoint targets.

“Given the current situation, we could have Zhou Zhang and her team select specific sea beast groups to teleport in for my absorption,” the fishman youngster suggested.

Cang Xu nodded. “That's a sound approach.”

“Given Zhou Zhang's group's strength and the beast tide from the White Hair Trench, they'll surely provide you with ample bloodline targets, my lord.”

Outside the Ocean Mother Nest, Zhou Zhang stood silently awaiting on the golden altar, unaware that she, a mighty saint level being, had been reduced to a mere tool.

The fishman youngster sighed. “The higher the bloodline concentration, the greater the favor I receive.”

“However, the Surging Wave fishman bloodline concentration must reach around 60% to ascend to the saint level.”

“If reaching a qualitative breakthrough is necessary to earn the gods' attention and greater regard, then careful selection is required.”

Cang Xu nodded in agreement.

Research on bloodlines revealed that the cultivation ceiling for different life forms is determined by varying bloodline concentrations.

In practical terms, this meant that among lower races like fishmen, high level transcendents were rare. Their transcendents were predominantly concentrated in the mid-to-low levels.

Specifically, regarding bloodline concentration, even with relatively low transcendent bloodline purity, fishmen could often cultivate to iron or silver level.

The youngster only had 11% Surging Wave bloodline, but the god level quality allowed him to cultivate to gold level. However, reaching the saint level demands a steeply increased bloodline concentration requirement.

Cang Xu stated, “Commander, you need to accumulate both bloodline and divine favor.”

“Either path will yield greater benefits for you.”

“I cannot compare to you in any way. From this day forward, I will dedicate myself entirely to supporting you!”

The fishman youngster's potential gains prompted Cang Xu to voluntarily step back, willingly ceding the primary role.

“We've prayed twice consecutively, yet haven't tested divine favor.”

“Since you now hold the clerical position of cleric and possess numerous divine spells, why not first aid others in conversion to earn divine favor for use in prayer?”

“Let's see what benefits we gain by praying with divine favor this time!”

The fishman youngster nodded, and the comparative experiment continued.

Earning divine favor proved surprisingly easy.

They effortlessly retrieved a Charming Blue believer from the Mother Nest.

The octopus saint beast remained utterly unaware under the Deceptive Disguise spell.

The fishman youngster cast a divine spell for the first time. His strongest impression was how effortlessly it was released and its remarkably high success rate.

The set of conversion spells was quite unique, but since the fishman youngster had already practiced numerous undead spells before, it posed no problem at all.

After casting the entire set, the dragonman youngster instantly earned 10 divine favor points.

This was because the subject he helped convert was merely a bronze level fishman.

Cang Xu was more concerned with another matter: “Commander, when you used the Spiritual Navigation spell, what did you see?”

“As per Zhou Zhang's guidance, this spell directly locates the deity, channeling the believer's faith straight to the divine entity itself.”

“Therefore, the image you saw was the divine entity's embodiment as perceived within your heart.”

The fishman youngster grasped Cang Xu's meaning: “I see.”

“So you're saying we can reverse-engineer the caster's own closeness to the deity, or rather, their level of faith, from this image?”

Cang Xu nodded. “Exactly. You could also call it their level of understanding.”

"I saw the Charming Blue Goddess statue first, then the gold level altar used for blood sacrifices. This signifies I'm drawing ever closer to the Charming Blue Goddess."

“What did you see?”

The fishman youngster wore a puzzled expression: “I only saw an ocean. Strangely, this ocean kept spinning, forming a massive whirlpool.”

Chapter 753: The Third Time Silenced Cang Xu

“A massive whirlpool?” Cang Xu froze.

He was a magician who had only recently become a clergyman.

He pondered for a moment, attempting to analyze: “Charming Blue Goddess likely holds the divine authority of a sea god or water god. Even if she doesn't, she must possess profound insight into the laws of water.”

“Therefore, the great whirlpool you witnessed in the sea, Commander, was likely a manifestation of some water-elemental law.”

The fishman youngster nodded slightly. “That makes sense.”

Cang Xu added, "Our knowledge in this area remains quite limited. Perhaps, during your next prayer, you could directly expend divine favor to make a targeted inquiry."

“Additionally, once we teleport back this time, I can consult Zhou Zhang.”

Outside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the Snow Pear Octopus had already slaughtered all the sea beasts and the few undead creatures, now pressing toward the praying Charming Blue believers.

Time was tight. The fishman youngster ceased communicating with Cang Xu, entering the chamber for the third time and sealing the hatch.

This time, both the prayer text and the altered memories had been personally fine-tuned by Cang Xu.

Moreover, unlike the previous two blasphemous rituals, this time he employed a hybrid transformation.

He possessed not only the Surging Wave fishman bloodline but also that of the Ghostface Fish.

"Great deity, oh Charming Blue Goddess! I gaze upon Your majestic countenance, feel Your omnipotent power, and revere Your unwavering compassion and mercy."

"Grant me abundant experience in wielding water magic, that I may command the power of water, make tides obey my will, and strike fear into sea monsters. Grant me Your magic, that I may dance through the waters as freely as You do."

"Lord, I also seek Your guidance and grace, that I may master necromancy magic and command the power of the undead and spirits. Grant me Your mercy and grace to break free from the curse of necromancy, to command the forces of the underworld, to communicate with spirits, and to learn more spells from You."

"Grant me an increase in my life level, making me stronger. Grant me Your protection and strength to eliminate sickness and pain, enhance my body, and infuse me with new vitality. Bless me with Your grace so I may continually surpass my limits, transcend myself, and become Your glory."

"As Your believer, I beseech You: hear my heart's plea and grant me Your response and blessing. Grant me Your grace to sail smoothly toward a bright future."

As the fishman youngster finished his prayer, the divine statue, recently elevated to silver level, suddenly radiated brilliant light.

A powerful divine force descended from the void, striking the fishman youngster squarely before permeating his entire being.

Outside the hatch, Cang Xu's eyes widened once more. “This defies logic!”

“The third divine power fluctuation is even stronger than before.”

"It's clear the commander has received more divine blessings this time than the second! Judging purely by the divine power fluctuations, it must be at least double."

“Could a mere ten units of divine favor truly amplify the divine blessing to this extent?”

Moments later, the hatch opened.

The fishman youngster appeared before Cang Xu, his expression both peculiar and delighted.

Cang Xu's mouth fell open. He recognized this look all too well. The previous two times had been exactly like this!

This time, without needing his prompting, the fishman youngster proactively recited the password to him.

The password was, of course, correct.

Cang Xu immediately inquired about the outcome of this blasphemous ritual.

The fishman youngster said nothing, instead simply opening his left palm.

The next moment, a tiny spring welled up from the center of his left palm.

Clear spring water gushed forth from the well.

As a magician, Cang Xu instantly recognized it: “Spring Well.”

This was an elementary spell, capable of condensing water elements from the air to produce pure, drinkable water.

Spring Well was the most fundamental of all water spells.

Before this teleportation blood sacrifice, the fishman youngster had already mastered this spell flawlessly, achieving a 90% success rate.

But this time, his execution displayed unprecedented mastery.

He didn't chant. He constructed the magic model for Spring Well within his mental space, accomplishing it in an instant.

Once formed, the Spring Well's water appeared precisely where he intended, flowing freely in any direction guided by his mental command.

Cang Xu recognized the breakthrough: “Silent casting, and nearly instantaneous!”

The clear spring water did not fall to the ground under gravity, but transformed into a tiny serpent, soaring through the air.

After landing on the palm of the fishman youngster's other hand, the serpent coiled into a ball and dissolved into a watery mass.

The watery mass hovered above the youngster's palm, suspended just inches away, maintaining an imperceptibly slight distance.

“Water-element spell - Water Sphere.” Cang Xu's gaze focused on the droplet, and he watched as it began spinning on its own.

The sphere's rotation accelerated, growing faster and faster until it whirled with a humming wind.

Yet no matter how rapid its spin, the sphere never shattered, nor did it deform in the slightest.

“Water-element spell - Water Sphere: Spiral Form!” Cang Xu exclaimed in awe.

Spring Water was merely a bronze level spell, while Water Sphere reached iron level, its magic model being several times more complex than Water Arrow. Water Arrow was a bronze level water spell.

Water Sphere: Spiral Form was even harder to cast, being silver level.

The fishman youngster sighed: "Although I practiced water spells previously, they were all elementary spells."

“My magician level was only iron level before, so I'd never practiced advanced spells like Water Sphere.”

“Of course, I'd seen its magic model, but only to broaden my horizons.”

“This should be my first time casting it.”

"But it feels effortless! As natural as breathing. My casting success rate must be approaching 100%."

Such progress was utterly astonishing.

Cang Xu exclaimed excitedly, “Commander, you've achieved another great success!”

The fishman youngster shook his head slightly. “It's not quite a complete success yet.”

“At least my life level hasn't increased at all.”

“And regarding undead magic experience, I haven't gained a single point.”

This time, in his blasphemous ritual, the fishman youngster made three requests in his prayer.

The first was to increase his water magic experience.

The second was to increase his undead magic experience.

The third was to elevate his transcendent life level.

In the end, only his first request was answered; the second and third remained unfulfilled.

Upon receiving this outcome, Cang Xu actually breathed a sigh of relief.

“This makes perfect sense.”

“The believers of the Charming Blue Goddess are almost entirely intelligent sea creatures, with many water magicians among them.”

“As these believers worship the Charming Blue Goddess, their spellcasting experience becomes her supplementary harvest.”

“Not to mention, the Charming Blue Goddess herself must possess profound expertise in water-elemental formations.”

“Thus, my Lord, you have gained this spellcasting experience.”

At this point, Cang Xu couldn't help but marvel: “Commander, when you cast these water spells, there's not a trace of artificiality, they flow with natural simplicity.”

"Silent casting, instant casting, and spell transmutation. these techniques have become as instinctive to you as breathing."

"Most magicians spend their entire lives striving to reach your level."

The fishman youngster sighed deeply. “Indeed. A single moment of prayer saved me at least eight years of grueling practice.”

“No wonder necromancers are so obsessed with blasphemy.”

The rewards were immense, far exceeding the fishman youngster's wildest expectations.

Crucially, avenues for gaining experience were extremely limited.

For ordinary individuals, beyond self-practice, reflection, and insight, the only other path was instruction from mentors or elders.

Certain rare artifacts could also impart experience. Take the Grand Arena Medal, for instance.

The fishman youngster had used one before, but compared to the blasphemous ritual, what did the Grand Arena Medal's gains amount to?

A squirrel nibbling at an elephant's leg?

Cang Xu shook his head slightly. “Commander, you're overestimating those necromancers' gains.”

“My situation is enough to make those necromancers envious, even jealous.”

“Because the main body of the Charming Blue Goddess lies dormant, perhaps due to the grim situation, She voluntarily accepted my necromancer identity.”

“Under normal circumstances, even if necromancers perform blasphemous rituals, they must disguise themselves to prevent their undead status from being exposed.”

“But you are different, Commander.”

"You possess the Bloodcore! Theoretically, it can transform into any god level bloodline."

“Imagine if you possessed a dark-type god level bloodline, then used the Deceptive Disguise spell to blasphemously worship a dark, evil god, what would happen?”

“Now consider this: if you possessed a beastman god level bloodline, bolstered by the Deceptive Disguise spell, Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, and fabricated memories, pretending to convert to those beastman deities' faith, what then?”

The fishman youngster fell silent.

Cang Xu's words had sent his mind reeling.

The Charming Blue Goddess was merely the beginning, far from the end!

With so many deities in the world, there's ample room to choose whom to desecrate with these rituals.

The fishman youngster drew in a sharp breath. “Who would have thought? The memory crystal making device, the Mermaid Fairy Tale, Deceptive Disguise spell, Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, all combined to make the success rate, risks, and rewards of a blasphemous ritual so incredibly tempting!”

“Indeed,” sighed Cang Xu.

He looked at the fishman youngster and continued his analysis: “The first prayer request received a powerful response and divine blessing.”

“The second prayer involved necromancy. The lack of divine blessing likely stems from the extreme rarity of necromancers who worship the Charming Blue Goddess, virtually nonexistent.”

“Who knows? I might be the first necromancer to worship the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Therefore, Commander, you gained no casting experience in necromancy.”

“As for the third request not advancing your life level, that was to be expected.”

“After all, advancing from silver to gold consumes far more than progressing from mortal to silver level!”

Cang Xu inquired in detail about the water-elemental spell experience, then added, “You still lack experience in numerous water spells.”

“But it's no trouble. As long as you keep earning divine favor and pray specifically for it, you'll easily make up for these spell experiences.”

“Speaking of which,” the fishman youngster interrupted, “my divine favor hasn't been depleted.”

Cang Xu froze, blinking in confusion, misinterpreting the fishman youngster's meaning: “You still have ten units of divine favor? And none of it's been used up?”

“Not quite,” the fishman youngster cleared his throat. “Not only have I not used any, but I've gained more.”

Cang Xu fell silent for a moment before asking, “How much more?”

The fishman youngster replied, “I now have 2400 units of divine favor.”

Cang Xu, who had painstakingly preached since the last teleportation blood sacrifice only to accumulate 1870 units of divine favor, fell silent once more.

Chapter 754: Cultivation Promotion is Too Fast, The Body Cannot Keep Up

“Commander, something feels off.” After a long silence, Cang Xu finally spoke.

“I've never heard of divine favor being given in excess.”

“And such a large amount!”

“Typically, only when a believer makes significant contributions to the church or the deity would they receive such substantial divine favor.”

“Yet your prayers haven't involved any such contributions.”

Ever since the fishman youngster performed the blasphemous ritual, Cang Xu had analyzed the situation repeatedly. But the fact that the Charming Blue Goddess was actively bestowing divine favor upon the commander was something he simply couldn't make sense of.

“In fact, the content of the divine blessing during the second prayer was also rather peculiar.”

"You weren't even in your hybrid bloodline form at the time, lacking the ability to cast undead spells. Yet the divine spell included that specific set of missionary divine spells."

“If we attribute all this solely to the god level bloodline, isn't that a bit self-deceptive?”

The fishman youngster fell silent, deep in thought.

Cang Xu looked at him, his tone grave: “Every gift bestowed by fate carries a hidden price.”

The fishman youngster nodded slightly, his expression also clouding with doubt.

He murmured thoughtfully, “What you say is precisely what troubles me.”

"I witnessed your entire blasphemous ritual. So, I know how things should normally unfold."

“Ordinary prayers cannot obtain divine blessings.”

“But here, with figures like Zhou Zhang conducting blood sacrifices and the arrangements of the Ocean Mother Nest, prayers consistently receive divine favor.”

“Divine blessings primarily depend on three factors.”

“The first is innate potential, essentially, the gods recognizing a believer's future value and making an early investment.”

"The second is depth of faith. Within the organization, this means assessing a member's loyalty."

"The third factor is divine favor. It's known that expending divine favor can guarantee the content and quality of divine blessings within certain limits. In essence, this evaluates a believer's past contributions and merits."

The fishman youngster spoke of his own situation: “I prayed three times consecutively, and each divine blessing I received was greater than the last. This defies all logic.”

“The scale and quality of the divine blessings far exceeded normal levels.”

“According to the intelligence you gathered, even gold level figures like Zhang Yu Wan and Jiu Hua Bi didn't receive as much as I did.”

“If we were to attribute this solely to the god level bloodline, I wouldn't be very confident.”

“Because my current Surging Wave bloodline is only at 11%.”

"There must be some secret information we're unaware of. "

“Or some other crucial factor we haven't considered?”

Silence fell between them once more.

When it came to divine spells and church, Cang Xu couldn't offer much assistance either.

After pondering for a while, the fishman youngster still had no clue.

Yet his expression hardened with resolve: "Even if we can't find answers or the truth right now, and even if the Charming Blue Goddess has some scheme, we still have to keep praying, right? "

“Only when our clerical position reaches a certain level will we find a way, perhaps we can use the Mermaid Fairy Tale to teleport out again.”

Cang Xu stared at the fishman youngster, shaking his head slowly.

His expression grew grave: “Commander, don't let the third divine blessing sway your heart.”

“If this is the enemy's lure, have you already fallen for it, your Lordship?”

“Advancing our clerical position to transmit our message is indeed the optimal plan we envisioned.”

“But it is not the only way to escape.”

“We could also destroy this Ocean Mother Nest, using the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the Deceptive Disguise spell, and other means to leave this place.”

“Currently, our tests indicate the Deceptive Disguise spell cannot fool the Charming Blue Goddess, but it remains effective against figures like Zhou Zhang.”

The fishman youngster's expression stiffened slightly.

Cang Xu's words prompted him to immediately examine himself.

“I cannot deny I was tempted.”

“But my desire to continue adventuring isn't mere impulsiveness. Don't forget, I still possess my Bloodcore.”

With that, the fishman youngster activated his Bloodcore, initiating its manifestation.

A crimson glow enveloped his entire body before slowly fading.

The fishman youngster vanished, replaced by the dragonman youngster.

"Bloodline, at its core, is life information. The soul is also part of life."

“When I transformed from fishman to dragonman, both my body and soul underwent profound changes.”

“Divine power can reshape believers, making them more aligned with the divine power of the deity they worship.”

“But by altering my own life form, I can significantly sustain myself and counteract the slow, gradual transformation of my body and mind wrought by divine power over time.”

Cang Xu perked up. “Commander, you're absolutely right.”

“I nearly forgot, the Bloodcore can be used this way too!”

The dragonman youngster sensed his own state.

After his partial transformation, the Surging Wave bloodline no longer flowed through him. The divine power's efficiency in reshaping his body and soul was noticeably lower than in his fishman form.

The youngster activated the Bloodcore once more, reverting to his fishman form.

The divine power's nourishment of his flesh and soul instantly surged a hundredfold, progress nearly visible to the naked eye!

The fishman youngster's face hardened with resolve: “Charming Blue Goddess has slumbered for years. This opportunity must not be missed.”

“We've delayed long enough. For the redemption plan, we need greater strength to safeguard our safety and interests.”

“As the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, I have no reason to retreat.”

“I previously had you lead the charge only because I am not a magician, while you are.”

“Now, I am better suited than you!”

“Truthfully, I relish this kind of adventure.”

“Lord Commander...” Cang Xu sighed deeply, gazing at the fishman youngster and sensing the sincerity in his heart.

It was precisely this kind of courageous and selfless leader that Cang Xu was willing to follow and pledge his loyalty to.

“For the fourth prayer, I intend to ask directly: why the goddess had bestowed such bountiful blessings.”

“Secondly, I intend to attempt using divine favor to elevate my life level. Even if it consumes all the divine favor, I shall not hesitate.” The fishman youngster declared his intentions.

Cang Xu nodded, preparing to draft the prayer for the fishman youngster.

Yet he also offered his own suggestion: “If the life level cannot be elevated and the divine favor remains untouched; you might instead want to pray for the deity to directly grant you the corpses of specific fishmen.”

“The gods are omnipotent. If they could transform an ordinary statue into a silver level one before, why couldn't they grant fishman corpses?”

What Cang Xu truly sought, of course, wasn't the corpses themselves, but the higher concentration of Surging Wave bloodline.

The fishman youngster swiftly accepted this sound suggestion: “Right, we can try that.”

Time was limited.

Off the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the Snow Pear Octopus had already examined nearly half of the believers. Countless Charming Blue believers had been devoured by it.

This meant this was the fishman youngster's final blasphemous ritual.

For Cang Xu was about to depart, returning beyond the Mother Nest.

Without Cang Xu's presence to hold the line, the fishman youngster dared not perform the blasphemous ritual alone. This was a safety measure they had long planned, their agreed-upon limit!

Yet, just as the fishman youngster stepped into the cabin, his legs suddenly gave way. He nearly collapsed on the spot!

“Lord!” Cang Xu cried out.

Fortunately, the fishman youngster caught himself against the wall.

He turned to face Cang Xu, his expression grim.

The next moment, unable to support himself against the wall, he collapsed and sat down.

Cang Xu was horrified and rushed forward to check the fishman youngster's condition.

“His physical condition is excellent!” Da Dou couldn't help but laugh.

Lying before him was the big guy.

He was snoring loudly, fast asleep.

Da Dou withdrew his gold battle qi, gazing at the sprawled giant as he took a deep breath.

His eyes burned with intensity, fixed on the big guy as if he were a priceless treasure.

“He devoured my specially seasoned fluttering boat fried rice, then consumed all those transcendent dishes from Ci Hai Shen...”

“Kid, your bloodline level is exceptionally high. You have a real shot at reaching the saint level!”

“Hehehe, the arena's packed with contenders, but who knows giants better than me?”

“They'd only guess you have gold level potential. None of them expected me to snatch you away.”

At this, Da Dou burst into a triumphant laugh.

Losing that gold level Meat Eye? No big deal.

Skipping the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's ceremony? No big deal either!

This was a future saint level expert!

After bringing him back to the island, the entire clan would see Da Dou in a whole new light. No one would think what he did was wrong!

“In the future, when this kid becomes a saint, I'll be the greatest hero of the entire clan. Ahahaha!”

As Da Dou laughed, the big guy slowly opened his eyes.

“You're awake.” Da Dou hurried to the big guy's side. “Look! What do you see?”

He held up a pot of transcendent level fluttering boat fried rice.

The aroma filled the air. Having just woken from hunger, the big guy instinctively snatched the silver level iron wok, tilted his neck back, opened his massive mouth wide, and dumped the entire contents inside.

The pot possessed spatial properties, holding far more rice than its outward appearance suggested.

The big guy ate a third of it and wanted to keep going, but Da Dou snatched the iron wok away.

The big guy glared at him.

Da Dou soothed him, "Stop eating. You've had way too much in that eating contest. All that energy is piling up inside you; it hasn't been properly digested yet."

The big guy turned his head and looked around, realizing he was stranded on a deserted island.

The barren island held no vegetation, only yellowish-brown earth and rocks.

The big guy jumped to his feet in shock. His elevated view still offered no sight of familiar ships, people, or belongings.

On the entire island, besides him, there was only Da Dou before his eyes.

“Home... take me home.”

“Xiao Guai wants to go home!” the big guy shouted.

“I'll take you home, to your real home,” Da Dou continued soothingly.

But the big guy suddenly glared back at him. “Bad guy! You're a bad guy!”

He remembered Da Dou suddenly appearing, attacking the mercenaries, and knocking him unconscious.

Bam!

The big guy swung his fist, landing squarely on Da Dou's chest.

Da Dou laughed heartily, stepping back without retaliating.

He wanted to gauge the big guy's fighting strength.

The big guy lashed out wildly, landing numerous blows on Da Dou, who just laughed heartily, showing no intention of retaliating.

After a short while, the once-vigorous big guy suddenly felt his legs give way, collapsing to his knees.

His eyes blazed with fury as he tried to rise and attack again, but in the next instant, his muscles convulsed violently. He fell face-first to the ground, utterly defeated.

Da Dou slowly approached him, looking down at the big guy with gentle eyes. "Your bloodline is too exceptional. After consuming so many dishes, your battle qi cultivation has surged dramatically. But your body hasn't adapted yet. After all, nourishing the body with battle qi takes time."

The big guy let out muffled grunts.

His jaw muscles trembled, distorting his roar into an eerie sound.

Da Dou crouched down, patting the broad, powerful back of the big guy, "So, you'd better stop messing around. You're definitely not going back home. Just come quietly with me back to Eruption Island!"

Chapter 755: Spatial Shuttle

White Hair Trench.

Ocean Mother Nest.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The fishman youngster scrutinized the state within his body.

Silver-hued battle qi gathered and fermented within him.

Equally silver level magic power coalesced within his personal magic pool.

Having activated his Bloodcore, he now existed in a hybrid form blending the Surging Wave and Ghostface Fish bloodlines. Within his pool, pale blue water magic coexisted with light gray undead energy.

The two forces shared the same reservoir, their boundaries not entirely distinct. At the dividing line, they intertwined and clashed, generating minor conflicts and internal friction.

Typically, dual-element magic pools underwent specialized modifications to their personal magic pools.

At minimum, distinct magic power elements were segregated into separate compartments.

Methods for constructing such compartments were archived within formal magician organizations. War Merchant's database naturally contains these techniques as well.

However, modifying one's personal magic pool demands extensive practice time and carries significant risks during the process.

With time so tight, the fishman youngster could only let his personal magic pools corrode and cancel each other out.

Fortunately, this internal friction remained mild.

Because all this magic power had been transformed through his personal meditation, bearing his deep, personal imprint.

Without this personal mark, direct contact between water magic power and undead magic power would cause severe magic conflict. In extreme cases, it could even cause an explosion!

Battle qi accumulated within every inch of his flesh and blood, while magic power was stored within his personal magic pool.

As for Charming Blue divine power, it was primarily concentrated within the youngster's soul.

Under the influence of the Bloodcore, the fishman youngster's soul had fully taken on the form of a fishman, matching his outward appearance. Enveloped by Charming Blue divine power, his soul received potent nourishment and transformation.

A small portion of the divine power also permeated the fishman youngster's flesh and blood. It manifested within his personal magic pool as well.

The fishman youngster observed: this divine power, intimately intertwined with his battle qi and magic power, was not static.

It would transform into his battle qi or magic power.

At times, it would devour his magic power or battle qi, adding a tiny bit to itself.

Divine power was an extremely high-grade form of energy.

It achieved perfect coexistence with battle qi and magic power. Even the closest contact posed no issue.

Divine power could effortlessly devour and transform the youngster's personal battle qi and magic power.

There was no internal friction whatsoever between them.

At least, the fishman youngster felt none.

He was about to perform the fourth blasphemous ritual when, upon reaching the hatch, his body lost control and he nearly collapsed to the deck.

Cang Xu immediately rushed over to support his commander.

The old magician used his spirit power to examine him and deduced the cause.

The fishman youngster, enlightened, examined himself before slowly opening his eyes to look at Cang Xu. “You're probably right.”

“These recent blasphemous prayers have elevated my magician level and caused my clergyman cultivation to surge to silver level.”

“Such a drastic increase requires both my body and soul time to adapt.”

"My earlier paralysis was a spontaneous protective mechanism, a coordinated effort between my body and soul."

At this point, the fishman youngster sighed. “Therefore, for the foreseeable future, I cannot pursue further advancement in my life level.”

Transcendent cultivation had always been a gradual process.

For the bodies and souls of transcendent beings must slowly acclimate to transcendent energy, allowing themselves to undergo subtle yet profound transformation.

Even the sacred sons and sacred daughters, who appeared to have low cultivation levels in ordinary times, could see their transcendent levels skyrocket instantly when infused with divine power during critical moments. Essentially, as vessels for the gods, they had undergone specific selection and cultivation. Their bodies and souls were already prepared, lacking only sufficient energy to nourish them.

The fishman youngster pondered, “It seems I must revise my blasphemous prayer. Seeking more spell experience would be more appropriate.”

Cang Xu shook his head slowly. “Lord Commander, you cannot do that.”

“Memory is a crucial element in shaping the soul.”

“Thus, when a person dies, though the body perishes, the soul retains its memories, remembering who it is.”

“It is precisely these memories that anchor the soul to its selfhood, preserving its wisdom.”

"But once driven by instinct to devour foreign spirits, absorbing vast fragments of others' memories, it leads to cognitive confusion and distortion. When quantitative change triggers qualitative transformation, the soul utterly loses its selfhood, degenerating into a beast-like existence."

“My Lord, the magic experience you gained from the blasphemous ritual is a special kind of memory, isn't it?”

Cang Xu's words made perfect sense.

The fishman youngster slowly nodded in agreement.

He looked up and sighed deeply. “So, my current situation is like overeating all at once. I'm so full that I need time to digest it.”

“Is there any other special method that could help speed up this digestion?”

“I recall War Merchant's database containing magic formations that accelerate the flow of time.”

Cang Xu once again dismissed the fishman youngster's idea.

“Even if a magic formation could accelerate the target's perception of time, it fundamentally relies on magic power to achieve that effect.”

“Any supernatural force still acts upon your body and soul, Commander.”

"And right now, your body and soul are being rapidly reshaped by silver level divine and magic power. Adding other magic training would be superfluous, it might even backfire."

“In this situation, we can look at the dragons as reference.”

“True dragons undergo a long period of growth. During this period, they often fall into deep, prolonged slumber."

"For instance, fire dragons seek out volcanic craters to hide and hibernate. Black dragons frequently conceal themselves in the deepest recesses of the underworld."

“In their slumber, they absorb external fire or dark elements, allowing the elemental forces to gradually reshape them.”

“If methods like time acceleration existed, the dragons would undoubtedly employ them extensively.”

"Yet, for millennia, the dragons have scarcely ever used such techniques. Before reaching adulthood, they simply sleep, refusing to use external forces to accelerate their growth."

The dragons' actions thus demonstrated that no truly effective method had ever existed in the world.

Even if one did, its conditions for use would be extremely stringent.

Otherwise, such methods would have long since become widespread across the world.

The fishman youngster sighed again: “Therefore, bloodline is the greatest foundation for transcendents.”

“In such cases, the superior the bloodline, the greater the capacity for assimilation.”

Cang Xu flattered: “My Lord, your words contain profound insight!”

With this delay, the time had nearly come.

Off the Deep Sea Monster Fish, over half the Charming Blue believers had already been snapped.

Cang Xu didn't even have time for prayer.

In the remaining moments, he could only hastily cast a divine spell on the Charming Blue believers captured aboard the ship.

Naturally, Cang Xu would also tamper with their memories.

By blurring the lines between truth and falsehood, blending reality with illusion, Cang Xu was confident he could deceive Zhou Zhang, Jian Gui, and the others.

Soon, Cang Xu completed all this. Before departing, he looked at the fishman youngster: “Commander, I intend to take the statue of the Charming Blue Goddess with me.”

The fishman youngster paused, then nodded: “Okay.”

The statue stored devotion and served as the deity's surveillance device. Leaving it aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish posed incalculable risks.

“Take it away. This statue will significantly influence Zhou Zhang and the others to your advantage.”

Though Cang Xu hadn't prayed this time, his bloodline concentration had risen, further refining his potential.

Combined with this divine statue, it would surely make Zhou Zhang and the others take notice once more.

As for Xiao Wang Zi, his treatment mirrored that of Ku Feng and Qing Xin, imprisoned within the longhorn cage, convinced he was already dead.

Igloo City.

The Li Jian Clan gifted Zi Di and her group the largest storefront on the city's most bustling street.

Zi Di established operations here, deploying reliable agents as tentacles to control the market in Igloo City.

A group of magicians stood quietly before the shopfront, awaiting Zi Di and her companions to finish their tasks.

Uniformed and led by five silver level magicians, the fifty-strong contingent bore the insignia of the Spatial Shuttle Squad.

This was the Li Jian Clan's magician squadron, possessing strategic-class capabilities.

They possessed the technique for collective spellcasting, wielding spatial magic to create a spatial shuttle capable of transporting both people and goods.

Because the spatial shuttle could traverse space, its movement resembled a teleportation effect.

However, the distance was limited.

The specific distance is directly proportional to the magicians' magic power reserves.

The higher the energy level required to transport passengers or cargo in the spatial shuttle, the greater the magic power expenditure for the magicians, ultimately resulting in a shorter travel distance.

However, when combined with a projection formation, the situation changed entirely.

Unlike conventional teleportation formations, projection formations were unidirectional magic formations that could supply immense energy to the spatial shuttle.

With these energy sources, the spatial shuttle's range skyrocketed by hundreds or even thousands of times!

This enabled the spatial shuttle to teleport directly from one city to another.

In times of war, mastering this long-range deployment capability could shift the tide of battle. For instance, executing decapitation tactics becomes remarkably convenient.

Undoubtedly, the Spatial Shuttle Squadron is the Li Jian Clan's trump card, and equally so for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!

Zi Di and Zong Ge's visit yielded the best possible outcome.

The Li Jian Clan spared no expense in deploying their trump card to facilitate Zi Di, Zong Ge, and others' swift movement throughout the kingdom.

Of course, this also leveraged the Li Jian Clan's influence and standing.

Merely possessing long-distance teleportation capabilities wouldn't grant unrestricted use within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. The Li Jian Clan itself was a privileged entity, a member of the ruling class, and thus could utilize it normally.

Naturally, when reaching strongholds controlled by other factions, they would notify those factions in advance.

“Let's go. Our next stop is Ice Prison City.” Zi Di was about to depart.

“Please wait. There's a confidential letter for you.” Just as they were leaving, a representative from the Li Jian Clan arrived, presenting a sealed letter.

Chapter 756: Not Saving

Zi Di took it and immediately recognized it as a letter from the Snow Bird Harbor branch.

In the past, letters from the branch would take at least several days to catch up with Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group.

But this time, the negotiations led by Zi Di had been a resounding success, allowing the survivors to deepen their cooperation with the Li Jian Clan. Consequently, communication between the branch and Zi Di and Zong Ge's group had been expedited many times over through the Li Jian Clan's channels.

Zi Di's brow furrowed slightly. She immediately moved to a corner of the shop, used an alchemy device to briefly treat the surrounding environment, and then began decrypting the message.

Zong Ge stood beside her, scanning the area for threats while keeping watch.

Both were deeply concerned about the letter's contents.

During Zi Di's negotiations with Mian Li Zang, they had learned from the latter that the branch had come under attack.

Although they had received urgent military intelligence from the Li Jian Clan after the successful negotiations, confirming the branch's victory, the Li Jian Clan couldn't accurately assess the specific losses and casualties.

Only a direct letter from the branch could provide Zi Di and Zong Ge with a proper understanding.

Soon, Zi Di successfully decrypted the message.

As she read, she transmitted her thoughts to Zong Ge: “The details are rather complicated.”

"Among the enemy forces was a gold level assassin disguised as a silver level fighter. His first target was our deputy commander, Shi Qi."

Zong Ge froze momentarily before realizing: Zi Di was referring to the alchemy puppet disguised as him.

Zong Ge feigned concern: “How is Shi Qi faring?”

"Shi Qi was critically wounded by a single strike and lost consciousness immediately. But don't forget, he possesses that powerful restorative battle skill. His life is currently preserved, and he's in a deep slumber repairing his body," Zi Di explained.

Zong Ge instantly grasped the underlying implication.

His heart sank as he thought: "The alchemy puppet disguised as me possessed considerable defensive capabilities, yet it was taken down in a single strike. The gold level assassin departed only after confirming ‘my’ death."

“However, this situation is advantageous.”

The alchemy puppet effectively drew fire, alerting the others.

Its fall also paralyzed the enemy to some extent. In their battle plan, “Shi Qi” commanding the branch office was the most crucial target.

Equally important was Bu Quan.

"Since the enemy assaulted the branch, they won't spare the alchemy workshop. What about Bu Quan?" Zong Ge inquired.

Zi Di replied, “The gold level assassin, Yi Jiu, attacked the ship. After succeeding, he moved to assault the small alchemy workshop within the city.”

"He infiltrated the workshop and captured the alchemist Bu Quan. However, at the critical moment, Shuang Lian intervened, capturing Yi Jiu alive and bringing him into the demi-plane, also temporarily taking Bu Quan away."

Zong Ge's expression remained unchanged as he continued surveying the surroundings, his brow slightly furrowed. "So Bu Quan's distress signal succeeded. But if her teacher took her away..."

Zi Di continued, “Bu Quan returned.”

“Though it was some time after the battle.”

“It seems my prior arrangements worked through Bu Quan.”

Shuang Lian intervened, rescuing only Bu Quan. For a time afterward, she made no further moves. Yet ultimately, she returned to the docks with Bu Quan in tow.

This sequence seemed contradictory, but Zi Di swiftly reconstructed the events.

"The alchemy materials from that great person are of extraordinary value. The Li Jian Clan values them, and so does Magician Shuang Lian," Zong Ge remarked.

He was no magician and knew little of alchemy. This time, he had finally witnessed its true power.

Zi Di, however, sighed, her expression far from cheerful.

Their original plan had never included deepening cooperation with the Li Jian Clan or leveraging Shuang Lian's influence.

Even if Zong Ge had underestimated the value of War Merchant's database, Zi Di and Cang Xu certainly hadn't.

They avoided such moves precisely because they carried immense risk.

This was the world of the strong.

When people saw a child holding gold in a bustling market, not everyone would trade fairly with the child.

If you could just take it, why bother with business?

Compared to the Li Jian Clan or Magician Shuang Lian, the survivors' strength was minuscule. Trading with them was tantamount to playing with fire. One misstep, and they'd be devoured whole, leaving nothing behind.

Such considerations kept the survivors from pursuing these plans.

Yet in practice, they had no choice.

The mysterious force was overwhelmingly powerful and relentlessly aggressive. Zi Di had considered backing down, but the sunk costs were too high to retreat now.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would suffer a devastating blow.

Zi Di knew for an organization to survive, it must turn a profit. At the very least, it must break even.

Abandoning the potion market plan would be an economic disaster, pushing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to the brink of bankruptcy.

This was unacceptable to her.

As an excellent leader, Zong Ge understood their situation well: "Our current alliance with both the Li Jian Clan and Shuang Lian is the optimal scenario. They can check each other's power."

“Exactly,” Zi Di nodded.

According to the intelligence gathered, Shuang Lian herself was a gold level magician. Possessing the Honey Snow Tower granted her formidable combat prowess.

Beyond that, she controlled a demi-plane, boasting immense wealth.

Her personal status was also extraordinary—she was an elder of the Alchemy Guild. Compared to most elders, her position held greater importance and uniqueness.

With the Alchemy Guild as her backing, Shuang Lian alone possessed the standing to challenge the Li Jian Clan.

Caught between these two major powers, the survivors had found a narrow space to survive, allowing them a moment's respite from their formidable enemies.

Zong Ge suggested, "Perhaps we could offer Shuang Lian a stake in our dragon rider potion business. Leveraging the Alchemy Guild would give us greater leverage against the Li Jian Clan, wouldn't it?"

Zi Di glanced at Zong Ge, thinking to herself: This is an incredibly foolish suggestion.

Yet she revealed no hint of disapproval, instead explaining patiently: “We cannot do that.”

“Both the Alchemy Guild and Shuang Lian possess formidable alchemy prowess. Bringing either into our fold would pose a grave threat to us.”

"In a three-way partnership, the dragon rider potion formula wouldn't withstand reverse engineering. Our alchemy capabilities are far inferior to the Alchemy Guild's."

"The Li Jian Clan isn't exceptional in alchemy—in fact, that was one foundation of our previous alliance. They need our alchemy skills; we need their shops and connections."

“Therefore, after introducing the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild, they'll unite for profit. The end result will be us being eliminated by their combined forces.”

Zong Ge paused, nodded slightly, and fell silent.

Zi Di continued, “In truth, within the potion market, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild is one of our primary competitors.”

“Once our plan makes them realize the enormous profits in mount potions, they'll definitely enter the fray and compete with us commercially.”

“It's a bit strange they haven't made a move yet.”

"However, other factions have already reacted. For instance, most counterfeit dragon rider potions appearing on the market originate from the Jing Xiang Clan."

"In the potion business, we only partner with the Li Jian Clan. This is because the Li Jian Clan ranks among the kingdom's top three major powers, possessing sufficient influence to protect this interest."

“Simultaneously, they lack expertise in potion-making, so they need our assistance to enter the potion market.”

“As you likely observed during negotiations with Mian Li Zang, the Li Jian Clan is a faction that maintains a vigorous spirit of enterprise.”

“For them, entering into a commercial potion partnership with us represents an opportunity and direction for growth and expansion.”

Zong Ge nodded again.

He hadn't fully grasped it before, but after Zi Di's detailed explanation, he finally understood: there were so many considerations behind her choice to partner with the Li Jian Clan.

Zi Di continued reading the final lines of the secret letter.

“Oh no.” Her expression darkened. “The big guy is gone!”

"What?!" Zong Ge couldn't help but gasp.

“Ah...” Zi Di stared at the decoded letter, letting out a deep sigh.

The decryption was complete; all the text on the envelope had transformed.

Zi Di handed the letter to Zong Ge: “Read it yourself.”

Zong Ge scanned the entire text at a glance.

He murmured to himself, quietly analyzing.

“Gold level magic chef, Da Dou... he took the big guy away?”

"The big guy, traumatized by the previous assassination attempt by Yi Jiu, went mad and was led straight into the arena. Then, through a series of misfortunes and feasts, he displayed extraordinary bloodline potential..."

“Based on his performance in the arena, it's easy to speculate he possesses gold level bloodline potential.”

“But why did Da Dou act so decisively?”

“By doing so, Da Dou violated the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's laws, openly abducting someone. He can no longer participate in the kingdom's grand ceremony.”

“Was it worth such a heavy price?”

“Is this revenge for Tripleblade choosing to eat Ci Hai Shen's food in the end?”

“Or could it be...”

Zi Di interjected, “Or could it be that Da Dou has already caught on, recognizing that the big guy's value far exceeds that of a gold level treasure!”

Zong Ge fell silent.

If that were the case, it would be disastrous.

“If he believes the big guy possesses at least saint level potential, snatching him away makes perfect sense.”

Zi Di voiced her concern: “When the commander returns and finds Xiao Guai missing, we won't be able to explain ourselves.”

“But if we rush back immediately...”

Zi Di's business plans were equally crucial.

Having just established deep cooperation with the Li Jian Clan, returning to Snow Bird Harbor now would undoubtedly severely hinder the progress of the business plan.

Zong Ge's expression grew grave. “I don't recommend rushing back to rescue the big guy.”

“If Da Dou seeks revenge out of resentment, his primary target can only be Ci Hai Shen.”

"Even if he wants to vent his anger on our mercenary group, it will only be through an attack, not kidnapping the big guy and fleeing using precious alchemy artifacts."

“Da Dou holds the big guy's future in high regard, which is why he abandoned the grand ceremony and acted so boldly.”

“Therefore, in the short term, the big guy's personal safety is not at risk.”

Chapter 757: Combat Practical Assignment

After discussing it, Zong Ge and Zi Di both agreed they weren't suited to rush back.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had been taken by the young commander.

Da Dou had already left the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. If Zong Ge and Zi Di wanted to pursue the big guy, they'd have to board other ships.

These vessels were far too conspicuous, placing their every subsequent move under constant scrutiny.

The chances of catching up to Da Dou were slim.

Even if they did catch up, confronting a gold level giant would be a troublesome affair.

Zong Ge and Zi Di had far more important matters to attend to. Not only did they need to execute their business plan, but they also had to travel to the royal capital. There, using the communication devices of the Wisteria shop, they would continue their contact with the Bai Zhen Clan.

Their decision wasn't to abandon the big guy outright, but to pin their hopes on Shuang Lian.

In the letter, they learned that Tripleblade had already gotten Shuang Lian's promise to help the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps get back the big guy.

Shuang Lian was a gold level magician, so her methods were far more varied than Zong Ge. She possessed a mage tower and a demi-plane, making her the most suitable candidate to rescue the big guy at present.

Even better, the big guy's value was secondary to Shuang Lian. What she truly coveted was the alchemy knowledge held by the survivors.

“Let's depart. Our destination is Ice Prison City.” Zi Di finally made the decision. ”With the Spatial Shuttle Squad's assistance, our journey will be swift."

“The arrangements for shops across all regions will be completed soon.”

“What we must accomplish is paramount for everyone.”

“Fulfilling our respective duties, that is the accountability we must demonstrate!”

Zi Di and Zong Ge jointly sent a secret letter back to headquarters.

The message was swiftly delivered to Snow Bird Harbor.

Without further delay, Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group boarded a spatial shuttle and departed Igloo City.

With events escalating to this point, the survivors found themselves caught between Shuang Lian and the Li Jian Clan, shouldering immense risks.

Driven to desperation by the mysterious force, they had no choice but to give their all, sprinting toward the future with everything they had.

The reply soon reached Tripleblade's hands.

After reading its contents, Tripleblade first breathed a sigh of relief, convinced Zi Di and Zong Ge's choice was correct.

Then, he immediately set off for the Honey Snow Tower.

Upon learning of Tripleblade's arrival, Bu Quan personally greeted him and escorted him into Shuang Lian's study.

“Come in,” Shuang Lian sighed inwardly as her beloved disciple Bu Quan pushed open the door.

Tripleblade was merely iron level, unworthy of Shuang Lian's personal audience. In truth, even the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was currently only a silver level group, far from matching an entity like Shuang Lian.

Even the dragonman youngster's feat of slaying Teng Dong Lang had merely earned Shuang Lian's respect, not true recognition at the same level.

Shuang Lian's personal audience with Tripleblade stemmed entirely from Bu Quan's influence.

Upon meeting, Tripleblade immediately bowed, expressing his profound honor. Having once served as a mercenary commander and fought his way up from the bottom, he knew this reception far exceeded standard protocol.

Shuang Lian's expression remained impassive as she addressed Tripleblade: “Within a day, the tower spirit will calculate the precise coordinates for Da Dou's teleportation.”

“Moreover, over the past two days, the mage tower has detected some unusual activity.”

Shuang Lian informed Tripleblade that vessels had departed the docks, both from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's faction and the Sea Bird Church. Some groups hadn't even left via the docks.

Tripleblade's heart sank as he instantly grasped the implications: “During the eating contest, the big guy demonstrated his potential. Most spectators believed he possessed gold level talent.”

“But then, Da Dou's direct attempt to snatch him away has sparked even more suspicion and curiosity among many.”

"Da Dou paid a heavy price for the big guy, abandoning the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's ceremony outright. This action, taken in reverse, suggests he has even greater expectations for the big guy's bloodline level!"

"And I have already publicly declared on behalf of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps that we will only collaborate with Shuang Lian. Others cannot obtain our endorsement, which is why these private maneuvers have emerged."

Regardless of which faction secured the big guy, they wouldn't report to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they'd strive to claim him for themselves!

Tripleblade's heart was filled with worry: “Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's schemes are vast, backed by formidable reconnaissance and intelligence-gathering capabilities.”

"The Sea Bird Church is no pushover either, they're a genuine, full-fledged church. Snow Bird Harbor is their largest diocese within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Crucially, their clergymen gain flight abilities at low levels. For maritime reconnaissance, that mobility is incredibly advantageous."

At this thought, Tripleblade suggested to Shuang Lian, “Perhaps, Lord Shuang Lian, you might consider tracking these individuals.”

“No one can predict the future.”

“Who knows? We might uncover clues about our corps' giant from this group.”

Shuang Lian gave Tripleblade another appraising look, thinking to herself: This short human fellow, no wonder he was secretly appointed to handle the branch affairs. His mind works rather quickly.

Bu Quan finally caught up: “Are you saying this group is after the giant belonging to our mercenary corps?”

Shuang Lian glanced at Bu Quan before replying to Tripleblade: "Rest assured, I've already taken care of it. Most of those setting sail have been secretly marked by me."

“If they locate the target first, I'll detect it swiftly too!”

Hearing this, Tripleblade immediately expressed gratitude and offered a small compliment.

Then he spoke: "Esteemed Magician Shuang Lian, I have one more request. I hope the potion production can be temporarily increased tenfold from the current output."

On her way here, Bu Quan had already learned of this strategic adjustment from Tripleblade.

She promptly offered, “I can leave the tower to oversee a small alchemy workshop.”

Bu Quan volunteered eagerly, brimming with enthusiasm.

Shuang Lian shot her a stern glance, her voice turning cold. “Bu Quan, have you completed all the assignments I gave you?”

“You've been away from the mage tower for so long, falling behind on much of your work.”

“Your top priority now should be to seize every moment for continuous study and cultivation.”

Bu Quan replied, “Teacher, all assignments are completed. I've already communicated with the tower spirit and submitted everything.”

“Thank you for your concern, Teacher, but I believe it's actually fine.”

"Even though I left you and came to Snow Bird Harbor, I never stopped studying. Moreover, I frequently corresponded with you, seeking your guidance on many alchemy questions."

Shuang Lian's expression grew even colder.

She secretly focused her mind to swiftly review Bu Quan's assignments. She discovered, just as she had claimed, that they were all correct!

In fact, for many problems, Bu Quan had provided multiple solutions, each showing unique innovation.

This left Sheng Lian with no grounds to erupt in anger.

“It is precisely because of your talent that you deserve stricter demands.” Sheng Lian stared at Bu Quan before her, sensing a hint of pride in her beloved disciple's demeanor.

Her voice turned icy. “Bu Quan, proceed to the top floor of the mage tower. You still have practical assignments to complete.”

Bu Quan let out a soft groan, casting a disappointed and resentful glance at her teacher. Seeing no softening in her expression, she resigned herself to fate, bowed her head, and left the study.

Tripleblade witnessed this exchange and promptly excused himself, not wishing to interrupt Shuang Lian's teaching.

Soon, Tripleblade emerged from the Honey Snow Tower.

He glanced back at the towering structure, which seemed to stand as a pillar between heaven and earth, shielding the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps from external pressures.

Tripleblade exhaled a heavy breath.

Deepening cooperation with the Li Jian Clan and Shuang Lian's intervention had significantly eased his burden.

“But the big guy went missing under my watch at the branch,” Tripleblade's expression remained stern, his gaze intense. “This is my responsibility!”

“Now, all hope rests on Shuang Lian's operation.”

"Da Dou may be gold level, but he's no magician. With Lady Shuang Lian involved, it should be fine, right?"

“Yes, the odds of success are high!”

If possible, Tripleblade desperately wanted to join Shuang Lian and the others on their mission.

But he knew requesting to accompany them would be presumptuous.

Besides, even if he did join, his contribution against a gold level giant would be negligible.

Not only that, he still had to oversee the daily operations of the Snow Bird Harbor branch.

Without him, the branch's routine tasks and functions would suffer greatly.

“In the end, I'm just a small fish,” Tripleblade sighed inwardly, overwhelmed by a sense of powerlessness and tinged with melancholy.

He was merely an iron level fighter, capable of influencing the current situation only minimally.

He could only align himself with a faction yet still felt like a leaf tossed by the wind.

Yet despite his personal weakness, he never gave up or lost heart, always dedicating his strength wholeheartedly to the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Shuang Lian swiftly reached the top floor of the mage tower.

Here, numerous magic formations converged, alongside crystal spheres and circular metal platforms covered in intricate magic runes.

This was the control room.

One of the most critical chambers within the Honey Snow Tower.

From here, Shuang Lian could not only manipulate the entire mage tower but also remotely oversee her demi-plane.

Her demi-plane, meticulously refined by her, operated in excellent condition. Shuang Lian expended vast resources to construct multiple colossal magic formations within the demi-plane.

These formations enabled her to harness greater combat power, effortlessly altering the demi-plane's environment.

Bu Quan, granted temporary authorization, proceeded directly to the main control room.
