Her movements were observed by other apprentices, stirring envy among them. They whispered among themselves: “Look, Bu Quan just entered the main control room alone.”

“She must have Teacher's permission. Nothing unusual about that.”

"Teacher favors her far too much, letting her enter such a critical area alone! We're always under strict supervision by others or puppets, even when cleaning routine tasks."

The murmurs soon ceased.

For they saw Shuang Lian also entering the control room.

Honey Snow Tower.

Within the control room.

“Come, stand beside me,” Shuang Lian beckoned to Bu Quan.

The teacher and student stood at the center of the circular metal platform.

Shuang Lian waved her hand lightly. Instantly, the various magic runes on the platform lit up simultaneously, radiating multicolored brilliance.

Shuang Lian explained each rune's function to Bu Quan, along with some control techniques.

When discussing the demi-plane, magic projections appeared, allowing Bu Quan to see clearly.

Finally, as Shuang Lian reached the conclusion of her instruction, the magic projection revealed a battlefield.

A blizzard swirled into a tornado, trapping Yi Jiu within its vortex.

"Your practical assignment today is combat. Manipulate these magic runes and attempt to kill this enemy!"

Shuang Lian declared, “Only when you slay him will you earn the right to leave the mage tower.”

Chapter 758: Bu Quan vs Yi Jiu

The Honey snow demi-plane.

Gale-force winds howled, and blizzards raged.

Yi Jiu's small form, lost in this vast expanse of ice and snow, resembled a fragile skiff struggling desperately amidst towering waves.

Hailstones the size of millstones, numbering in the thousands, pelted Yi Jiu under the cover of the blizzard.

Yi Jiu dodged relentlessly.

His extensive combat experience allowed him to maintain his reconnaissance battle skill. Without lifting his head, he could pinpoint the hailstones' trajectories.

Bang, bang, bang...

The hailstones missed their mark, exploding upon impact with the ground and releasing bursts of frigid cold.

Within seconds, the freezing air crystallized the surrounding atmosphere, forming a massive ice block two meters tall.

Hailstones detonated intermittently beside Yi Jiu, their frigid fury eroding his skin and leaving multiple frostbite wounds across his body.

Yet Yi Jiu had no time to tend to his injuries.

Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!

A barrage of ice spears erupted from the snow, piercing toward Yi Jiu.

He leapt into the air, soaring upward.

A massive figure burst through the blizzard, swinging fists toward Yi Jiu mid-air.

A gold level snow elemental!

Standing three meters tall, the snow elemental was burly and imposing. The fist's wind sliced through the air, amplified by the blizzard, making the threat even more intense.

At the critical moment, Yi Jiu flicked his arm, sending a dagger flying.

The handle of the dagger was connected to his gold level battle qi.

The battle qi converged into a durable long cord.

The dagger plunged into a distant ice block, instantly anchoring itself

Yi Jiu continued his battle skill, his entire body violently yanked forward by the gold level battle qi cord.

Bam!

The snow elemental swung wildly at empty air, sending muddy snow flying everywhere.

Unable to slow down, Yi Jiu slammed his face into the ice block.

Ignoring the dizziness, he scrambled to his feet, frantically pushing off the ice surface with a roll to evade further.

The next instant, the snow giant's ranged attack struck his previous position.

Boom!

The spell shattered the ice into fragments, its residual force slamming into Yi Jiu's back.

Propelled by the impact, Yi Jiu was driven headfirst into the snow.

The snow giant closed in, but its attacks into the snow missed their mark. Yi Jiu had vanished for the moment.

The snow giant pounded its chest in rage, then spotted “Yi Jiu” and resumed the chase.

Yi Jiu's true form lay hidden in a corner of the snowy terrain, urgently healing his wounds.

He downed three vials of potions in quick succession, then raced against time to refine his battle qi.

Within just half a minute, various frost spells descended upon him, forcing Yi Jiu to flee once more.

“So far, three gold level snow elementals have appeared.”

“Even using my strongest camouflage and concealment techniques, I can only maintain the illusion for about thirty seconds at most.”

“Clearly, Shuang Lian isn't trying to kill me outright. She's deliberately maintaining this level of combat intensity to drain all my battle qi, isn't she?”

“My battle qi reserves... still at two-thirds.”

Yi Jiu's mood grew heavy.

Gold level snow elementals were exceptionally troublesome, their magic crystals capable of shifting throughout their bodies. Unless Yi Jiu could pinpoint this fatal weakness, no matter how severely he wounded the elemental, its body would swiftly regenerate within this battlefield environment.

“Huh?” Yi Jiu murmured in mild surprise.

His keen senses detected a sudden pause in the relentless assault.

“Have reinforcements arrived?”

“Are they besieging Shuang Lian, causing disruptions here?”

Just as Yi Jiu speculated, the assault intensified to its previous ferocity.

“Damn it!” The gold level assassin cursed, forced to resume his desperate retreat.

His illusions shattered.

Honey Snow Tower.

Control room.

Bu Quan had officially taken over as the operator.

Shuang Lian stood beside her, guiding her step by step with patience and care.

It was precisely this switch between them that had caused the offensive to weaken.

In truth, even as Bu Quan gradually gained proficiency, the blizzard assault she could unleash still fell far short of Shuang Lian's.

“It's spirit power.”

“My spirit power can't compare to my teacher's. She can simultaneously control colossal ice storms. Even when I push myself to the limit, my storms don't reach one-tenth of hers.”

"My teacher can cast multiple ice spells at once, coordinating them to surround and attack Yi Jiu. But I can only control five spells simultaneously at most, and they're all elementary."

Operating personally against Yi Jiu, Bu Quan acutely felt the vast gulf between herself and her revered mentor.

Before long, Bu Quan voluntarily ceased her control.

The tower spirit took over her task, continuing to chase Yi Jiu into a frantic retreat.

Bu Quan didn't drain her magic power when controlling the tower, but she needed to use spirit power to manipulate various ice-element spells.

“Have some potions,” Shuang Lian said, pulling out over a dozen vials and handing them to Bu Quan.

They were all gold level spirit potions!

Bu Quan paused slightly. “Teacher, isn't it a bit excessive for me to use gold level potions right now?”

Shuang Lian replied, “I've diluted them. After you drink them, their efficacy will be 30% better than taking silver level spirit potions.”

Bu Quan felt a bit embarrassed. “But gold level potions cost over a hundred times more than silver level ones.” This feels like such a waste."

“Heh,” Shuang Lian chuckled softly. “This is far better than you giving away four alchemy workshops to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps outright.”

Bu Quan stuck out her tongue, obediently took the potions, pulled out a vial, and downed it in one gulp.

Her spirit power rapidly recovered.

Before long, she resumed manipulating the magic formation to attack Yi Jiu.

Many spells were beyond her silver level capabilities, but with the mage tower and the magic formation laid out by the demi-plane, she didn't need to construct magic models herself or expend her own magic power.

Bu Quan grew increasingly adept at the controls.

Shuang Lian's occasional guidance allowed Bu Quan to make rapid progress.

Purely practicing spells, her casting experience grew exponentially. Many things could only be deeply understood through actual combat.

After three rounds of rest, Bu Quan felt she had gained immensely.

“If given another chance to fight Yi Jiu alone, my performance would be at least ten times better than before!”

“I've learned so much about choosing which spells to use and when to cast them.”

“These decisions are crucial in combat!”

“Speaking of which, Yi Jiu makes an excellent training partner.”

“Teacher has been so kind to me...”

As Bu Quan silently expressed her gratitude, Shuang Lian nodded in approval within her mind.

She was thoroughly satisfied with Bu Quan's performance during the combat training.

“She's a prodigy!”

"Possessing extraordinary talent and brilliance in alchemy. Though slightly weaker in combat, she still surpasses other apprentices."

“Even after being away from me for a while, she never slacked off in her training. Her foundation is solid.”

"Excellent. It seems she truly took my parting advice to heart. Such a good girl!"

"Drink up. This potion contains something special. Not only does it restore spirit power, but it also raises your spirit power ceiling."

What Shuang Lian secretly invested in Bu Quan far exceeded what the latter perceived.

Shuang Lian did not reveal the hidden secret within the potion.

On one hand, she didn't want to place too much pressure on Bu Quan. On the other hand, though she cared deeply for Bu Quan, she had to maintain an appearance of fairness and impartiality. After all, Bu Quan wasn't her only student!

The effect of the heightened spirit power and mental capacity gradually manifested in Bu Quan's subsequent combat training.

She cast multiple spells simultaneously with increasing ease.

“Excellent. Continue training. Little Quan Quan's combat weaknesses will improve significantly.”

“Most crucially, this training method is extremely safe for her.”

“Training targets of the gold level are also rare and hard to come by.”

Shuang Lian had thought deeply for Bu Quan's sake, her intentions undoubtedly sincere.

“The real focus now remains the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

A sharp gleam flashed in Shuang Lian's eyes.

She had already discerned that the Li Jian Clan and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had deepened their cooperation, even deploying the Spatial Shuttle Squad!

Even though the current head of the Li Jian Clan, Mian Li Zang, was known for his humility and respect for talent, treating a silver level force this way was excessive.

“Perhaps what swayed the Li Jian Clan was alchemy knowledge of the same caliber I witnessed!” Shuang Lian speculated silently.

She harbored no affection whatsoever for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Given her temperament, she would likely have disciplined the survivors. Her earlier covert intervention, rescuing Bu Quan and departing without confronting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, stemmed primarily from consideration for Bu Quan's personal feelings.

“Regardless, Long Fu is a master of diplomacy, he even persuaded Mian Li Zang.”

“He successfully leveraged the power of the Li Jian Clan, and before that, he enlisted my help too!”

Shuang Lian was unaware of the truth: Long Fu, played by Zong Ge, had actually contributed little to the negotiations. The real negotiator was Zi Di.

Her loathing for the dragonman youngster deepened.

In her eyes, the dragonman youngster was a master manipulator. He'd wielded that skill against the innocent, pure Little Quan Quan, steering her beloved disciple down a path of ruin.

“We must proceed with extreme caution against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps now.”

Shuang Lian felt she needed to be more vigilant.

The presence of the Li Jian Clan was what truly made her wary.

She had freely disciplined Da Bei and Magician Cai Jing. But now, when dealing with the survivors, she was exercising restraint.

“Perhaps I could extract more alchemy knowledge from them?”

“That giant they snatched away is no ordinary creature, his bloodline level is at least gold level.”

“What reward will Long Fu offer me for helping them reclaim the giant this time?”

“Saving a future gold level talent should at least warrant alchemy materials of equal value to the one they previously gave, right?”

Plop.

Yi Jiu spat out a mouthful of blood, flecked with a dozen tiny ice shards.

His face drained pale, his body numb with cold.

His battle qi depletion had reached 48%, nearing half.

“This can't go on. I must contact them proactively!” Yi Jiu resolved, beginning his prayer.

Previously, he hadn't had the chance, but now that Bu Quan was in control, the external pressure on Yi Jiu had lessened considerably.

This finally gave him the time to pray.

He expended a large amount of divine favor, and his spirit was immediately drawn to the peak of Tranquil Hill.

There, discussions were already underway.

“It's Yi Jiu. His tombstone is glowing.”

“He's back!”

“I knew it! He must be okay, right?”

Yi Jiu's appearance instantly drew the attention of the other duelists.

"Did you escape? Where are you now?" Gu Dong asked immediately.

"Quick, save me! Shuang Lian is about to kill me!" Yi Jiu cried out loudly.

The scene fell silent.

Chapter 759: Divine Domain Descent

The Dueling Divine Domain.

The peak of Tranquil Hill.

Some gravestones glowed faintly as duelists from across the kingdom connected across distances through prayer.

Yi Jiu's cry for help stunned the crowd.

After a brief silence, they asked Yi Jiu where he was.

Yi Jiu reported his coordinates: "It's Shuang Lian's demi-plane! She ambushed me and dragged me into her demi-plane."

“I've been fighting for two days and nights straight, yet I can't find a way out.”

“Only outside help can break me free from this perilous situation.”

Yi Jiu steadied himself and swiftly explained the situation.

He hadn't originally planned to pray, but Shuang Lian had sent Bu Quan to gain combat experience, significantly easing Yi Jiu's burden.

This had finally given Yi Jiu the chance to contact others.

"Isn't Shuang Lian an alchemist? How could her combat prowess be this formidable?" Zhu Gan voiced his confusion.

Xue Tuan Zi replied, "I happened to learn some details. Shuang Lian left the royal capital a few days ago, clashing with certain magicians from the Alchemy Guild before her departure."

"She took the mage tower with her when she left.”

“Now, the Honey Snow Tower sits right on the docks of Snow Bird Harbor.”

Upon hearing this, Yi Jiu suddenly understood: “She has the mage tower, no wonder the spatial fluctuations were so swift and violent. I didn't have time to react before being dragged into the demi-plane.”

Zhu Gan pressed further: “Yi Jiu is currently within Shuang Lian's demi-plane and isn't facing the mage tower directly. By all logic, as a gold level assassin, he should be perfectly suited to counter magicians.”

Yi Jiu felt like crying. “To assassinate the magician, I need to find her true form!”

“But ever since I was trapped in this demi-plane, I've been under constant magic assault.”

“The spells are almost all gold level, and gold level snow elementals keep hunting me down.”

“I can't locate Shuang Lian's true body at all.”

“I strongly suspect her true form remains stationed in the mage tower, remotely controlling magic formations to attack me nonstop!”

With his extensive combat experience, Yi Jiu had guessed the truth.

He stated bluntly, “I can't break through the spatial barrier to find Shuang Lian's true form.”

“I can't hold out much longer. I need reinforcements!”

"Mei Lin, where the hell have you been? Get out here now!"

Gu Dong: "I know you're anxious, but please hold your horses. I've already urgently notified Lady Mei Lin through other channels."

Gu Dong, a military veteran, maintained communication channels with Mei Lin and had long been aware of Yi Jiu's assault operation.

In fact, he had volunteered to join the battle but was overruled by Mei Lin.

Gu Dong's identity posed too great a risk.

Exposing him would create immense complications.

Yi Jiu roared, "Hurry! Contact her immediately! This is nothing like our original agreement! I..."

Yi Jiu's mental fluctuations abruptly ceased. The connection snapped.

A chill settled over the summit of Tranquil Hill.

“Is he... dead?”

“Unlikely. Yi Jiu is an agile fighter. His voice sounded quite lively just now.”

“To be precise, he sounded furious!”

The next moment, Yi Jiu's mental transmission returned: “Did I just hear someone say I'm dead?”

"Ah! Who dared curse my death? Step forward!"

Just then, Yi Jiu had been attacked again, forcing him to focus his energy on combat and temporarily abandon the mental communication.

Everyone: “...”

“Alright.” Mei Lin's voice came through. “Yi Jiu, report your current situation.”

“You finally showed up!” Yi Jiu fumed. "The payment you gave me isn't enough to risk my life! The situation now is that I'm trapped."

“You must send someone to rescue me, or else I'll expose you all!”

Mei Lin snorted coldly. “Yi Jiu, watch your tone.”

“You're trapped because you fought carelessly and got pulled into a demi-plane.”

“Clearly, conventional methods won't let you send a message out. The only rescue force you can rely on now is us.”

That was true. Mei Lin displayed the unyielding resolve of a soldier.

Mei Lin continued, “However, when we made our deal, the intensity of this battle wasn't supposed to be this high.”

“After this fight, we'll double your original payment as compensation.”

Yi Jiu gritted his teeth, suppressing his rage. “More money won't matter if I'm dead to spend it!”

"Cut the nonsense, okay? Send help quickly!”

"How can we rescue him? He's trapped in a demi-plane," Zhu Gan said.

Gu Dong replied, "The rescue is indeed difficult, but not impossible. We can pray, beseeching the divine domain to descend!"

“You mean...” Xue Tuan Zi pondered, “Theoretically, this is entirely feasible!”

Zhu Gan gasped, "I see! Just like when Bing Xiao prayed not long ago, sending Teng Dong Lang's body back to be buried in Tranquil Hill. They could teleport into the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain precisely because the divine domain descended."

At that time, Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer's prayers succeeded, causing a portion of Tranquil Hill to descend upon Flower Skirt Island.

During this process, Shuang Tu's arrangements took effect, expanding the divine domain's scope to encompass Lan Zao and the others.

When part of the divine domain retreated, it carried many people back with it.

Mei Lin explained, "The divine domain is the domain of the gods. Theoretically, an active divine domain can descend anywhere within the Main Plane."

"After the divine domain descends, we can use it to return to Tranquil Hill. Or conversely, we can utilize the Divine Domain Descent to teleport elsewhere and provide powerful support!"

"Exactly! That's it!" Yi Jiu rejoiced, ”This method is truly excellent."

Zhu Gan hesitated, “The Divine Domain Descent isn't that simple.”

"Previously, Bing Xiao succeeded because returning Teng Dong Lang's corpse inherently benefited the divine domain. Thus, they expended almost no divine favor."

"But your support mission this time involves combat. It will consume a great deal of divine favor. Even after praying, the divine domain might not descend at all."

Prayer differed from divine spells, it was inherently unstable.

Yi Jiu grew impatient: “Is this about divine favor?”

“If I die, you won't get off easy.”

"Mei Lin, before I die, I'll expose you. You, Zhu Gan, and the rest, I’ll expose every last one of you! And the truth about Tranquil Hill too!"

These words sparked cold snorts and discontent among the crowd.

“Yi Jiu, you’d better understand what you’re saying!”

“Who cares? Even if it’s exposed, what difference would it make?”

“Hmph, Yi Jiu. I thought you were a man of honor. Never imagined you’d be so cowardly!”

Yi Jiu erupted: “I don't care what you say or how you see me!”

“I'm just saving myself!”

“I shouldn't die. I shouldn't have taken this damn job!”

“Calm down, Yi Jiu,” Mei Lin said coldly. “I'll organize a rescue.”

“Using the Divine Domain Descent method!”

“But before that, please hold on.”

“If you die, we won't have precise coordinates to descend directly into Shuang Lian's demi-plane!”

Yi Jiu took a deep breath: "Then you'd better hurry. The sooner, the better. I..."

This time, it wasn't Yi Jiu who lost contact. Mei Lin had directly withdrawn her spirit form and ended the communication.

White Hair Trench.

Cang Xu returned.

Accompanying him was a large number of Charming Blue believers.

Upon their return, the believers thanked Cang Xu profusely, some even kneeling before him on the spot.

In their memories, Cang Xu had guided their prayers and deepened their faith—he was the key figure in their survival and return, their savior!

"Rise, all of you. Preaching is the power bestowed upon me by our Lord, and it is my honor. "Cang Xu smiled, radiating pride in his role as a Charming Blue missionary.

Leveraging his believers, Cang Xu delivered a brief speech, effectively boosting his visibility and expanding his influence among the Charming Blue believers.

Jian Gui and Qi Ling stood apart from the crowd, observing from a distance.

Qi Ling remarked coldly to Jian Gui, “My Lord, Xiao Wang Zi did not return. He is dead!”

“I strongly suspect Xu Jia had the power to save him but chose not to offer genuine aid.”

Qi Ling and Cang Xu were bitter rivals. She seized this opportunity to sow discord.

Jian Gui wore his disappointment plainly.

Xiao Wang Zi hadn't returned.

This meant losing a capable subordinate, and the progress of memory modification would be significantly delayed.

Cang Xu glanced at the two figures of Jian Gui and Qi Ling, gave them a nod, then turned his gaze toward Zhou Zhang.

“Lord Zhou Zhang,” Cang Xu bowed before Zhou Zhang at the altar, “I have matters I wish to consult with you!”

Zhou Zhang was highly satisfied with Cang Xu.

The fact that so many Charming Blue believers had been preserved was undoubtedly Cang Xu's achievement.

Without Cang Xu, only one-third of those present would return through a normal teleportation blood sacrifice.

Xiao Wang Zi did not return, leaving Zhou Zhang somewhat disappointed.

After all, she had hoped other necromancers might emulate Cang Xu's success and become special missionaries within the Charming Blue Church.

Cang Xu could indeed achieve this, yet he imprisoned Xiao Wang Zi within the longhorn cage.

For if a second necromancer capable of missionary work emerged, it would inevitably diminish Cang Xu's own value.

Now, he remained the sole candidate; his worth in Zhou Zhang and others' eyes was consistently held at a high level.

Cang Xu requested a private consultation with Zhou Zhang, who agreed, leading him to a secluded corner.

Only the two of them remained.

Cang Xu then retrieved the Charming Blue divine statue from his robes and informed Zhou Zhang: Originally an ordinary object, it had undergone a transformation, becoming a silver level sacred artifact.

Zhou Zhang cradled the statue in both hands, examining it with delight for a long moment before returning it to Cang Xu: “What immense honor it is for you to possess this divine statue.”

“This is our Lord's reward for you. Continue your preaching, perhaps divine favor will be yours!”

Cang Xu pressed further: What exactly had happened? The concept of transcending the mundane far exceeded his comprehension.

As a magician, he understood clearly that the mundane and the transcendent were fundamentally different realms. Forging transcendent artifacts from ordinary materials was utterly impossible!

Zhou Zhang smiled, revealing a secret: “It's understandable that you wouldn't know.”

“Did you think being transported into the Ocean Mother Nest meant you were confined only within it?”

Cang Xu looked utterly perplexed.

Zhou Zhang asked, “Have you ever heard of ‘Divine Domain Descent’?”

Chapter 760: God's Chosen

“Divine Domain Descent?” Cang Xu's body and mind trembled.

He sensed this secret was no trivial matter and urgently pressed Zhou Zhang for more.

Cang Xu knew little about the divine domain.

Zhou Zhang explained, “Our Lord's divine domain does not soar high in the heavens but stands upon the earth—it is a divine domain within the mortal world.”

“Any divine domain existing in the mortal world can descend upon any location within the Main Plane.”

“When a divine domain descends, typically only a portion of it covers a segment of the Main World, transforming that area into an inner world, while the divine domain itself becomes the outer world.”

"Throughout this process, the divine domain remains a unified whole. Believers can travel from other parts of the divine domain to the manifested section. Upon leaving the divine domain, they return directly to the Main World."

Cang Xu's mind stirred: “So, through Divine Domain Descent, a church could deploy armies to any location instantly?”

Zhou Zhang shook his head: “Theoretically, yes.”

Cang Xu reflected for a moment before adding, “Yet history records few such instances.”

“Is the cost too great?”

Zhou Zhang nodded slightly. “Divine Domain Descent is a divine spell. It first requires spatial coordinates, a task often undertaken by deeply devout believers.”

"The casting of divine spells always incurs immense expenditure. Ordinary transcendent believers struggle to bear it, and expending a saint level being to transport a gold level army is profoundly unprofitable.”

“Additionally, the act of Divine Domain Descent itself carries inherent risks."

Zhou Zhang said no more.

Cang Xu understood perfectly.

Divine domains were famously easy to defend and difficult to attack. The god resided within them, making them exceptionally secure.

Once descended, a divine domain could teleport anywhere across the Main Plane. Conversely, enemies from the Main Plane could exploit this link to infiltrate the divine domain!

These factors combined made the Divine Domain Descent exceedingly rare.

Cang Xu gazed toward the direction of the Ocean Mother Nest: “Could it be... the Ocean Mother Nest was subjected to the Divine Domain Descent?”

Zhou Zhang replied, “This is classified information, but you now possess the clearance to know.”

Cang Xu opened his mouth to speak but ultimately remained silent.

He hadn't anticipated the Charming Blue Goddess' plan being so grand in scale.

He knew this place had undoubtedly been prepared, but hadn't imagined it involved the high-risk Divine Domain Descent.

Suddenly, Cang Xu understood why the saint beast Snow Pear Octopus resided within the Ocean Mother Nest.

Beyond screening believers, the Snow Pear Octopus' true purpose was likely guardianship.

Guardianship of this section of the Charming Blue Divine Domain.

Saint level beings were high-tier transcendent beings, national-level combat forces. Even the mighty Ice Sculpture Kingdom possessed only three saint beings.

Should any enemy attempt to breach the Charming Blue Divine Domain through this passage, they would inevitably face interception by the saint octopus.

Even if the octopus was outmatched, as a magic beast, its thick hide and tough flesh would undoubtedly buy time.

Just a moment's delay was enough to cancel the Divine Domain Descent, ensuring the Charming Blue Divine Domain remained safe.

“Why cast the Divine Domain Descent Spell here?” Cang Xu questioned inwardly.

“The Charming Blue Goddess needs to recuperate and requires sacrifices.”

“The vast resources, energy, and faith offered in blood sacrifices enter the divine domain when delivered to the Mother Nest.”

“The transportation costs here are minimal!”

“Simultaneously, the divine blessings the Charming Blue Goddess bestows upon her believers incur no transportation costs either, it's convenient and swift.”

"From this perspective, Charming Blue Goddess' condition is indeed concerning. To reduce these losses, they risked everything by invoking the Divine Domain Descent."

Cang Xu quickly thought of the Charming Blue divine statue.

“In that case, the Charming Blue divine statue must have been secretly enhanced with transcendent materials, forging it into a silver level sacred artifact.”

Cang Xu's conjecture had grounds.

Gods were omnipotent.

But it depended on the specific cost.

Cang Xu didn't doubt that the Charming Blue Goddess possessed transcendent abilities.

But elevating an ordinary object to the transcendent level would demand immense sacrifice. Using materials already of transcendent level for forging would undoubtedly be far less costly.

Given Charming Blue Goddess' weakened state, the choice should be obvious.

Of course, Cang Xu couldn't be certain of this answer.

“If I were Charming Blue Goddess, this is what I would choose.”

Cang Xu breathed a slight sigh of relief.

With that understanding, the fishman youngster receiving a silver level divine statue became somewhat easier to accept.

Next, Cang Xu carefully measured his tone before consulting Zhou Zhang about the accumulation of divine favor.

He circled around the topic, asking indirectly, until he finally posed the question: “Under what circumstances does divine favor increase automatically?”

Zhou Zhang answered without suspicion, smiling as she explained, “Of course there are situations where divine favor increases automatically.”

“The sacred sons and daughters of each church receive a fixed amount of divine favor after their daily prayers.”

Cang Xu nodded, continuing to listen.

Zhou Zhang continued, “Beyond the sacred sons and daughters, there are also god's chosen who receive divine favor.”

"Typically, these are descendants of the divine bloodline, even illegitimate offspring. Or they are exceptionally talented individuals whom even the gods themselves wish to recruit."

“The most renowned example is the Handsome Youth Mercenary Corps.”

Cang Xu's expression shifted slightly. He knew of this renowned mercenary group.

The mercenary group was small, consisting of only five members, all beautiful youths.

They displayed extraordinary valor during the Demon War, their outstanding performance lasting from the war's beginning to its end. In the final decisive battle, the five youths played a crucial role.

"Each of the five either descended from divine bloodlines or possessed extraordinary talents. All five bore varying degrees of divine favor. After daily prayers, their divine favor would naturally increase."

Zhou Zhang gave another example: “Have you heard of Qi Ci Lang?”

This time, Cang Xu shook his head, unfamiliar with the name.

Zhou Zhang explained, “He's from the Sheng Ming Empire, a gold level fighter possessing the Beloved bloodline.”

“Bearers of this bloodline often receive divine favor and affection.”

“With deep connections, Qi Ci Lang has participated in numerous major divine ceremonies, receiving blessings from various gods on multiple occasions.”

“Among these, the seven strongest blessings were all Immortality Blessings.”

“Seven distinct conditions must be met to break the blessing and inflict fatal harm upon him.”

"Thus, Qi Ci Lang is nicknamed ‘Seven Lives’ or ‘Beloved Child of Seven Gods.’ Some rank him among the strongest gold level fighters in the Main Plane."

“Though this ranking ultimately failed to become official, Qi Ci Lang is widely acknowledged as the most formidable gold level fighter in the Main Plane.”

"Qi Ci Lang possesses not just one type of divine favor. Moreover, his divine favor undoubtedly grows daily through prayer."

Cang Xu sighed softly: “I see.”

He relaxed considerably.

He had previously thought his own commander's situation was unique, but it turned out he was simply ignorant, similar cases were actually quite common.

Cang Xu genuinely hadn't heard of Qi Ci Lang.

The reason was because he had been a mere iron level, was wanted by authorities, forced to flee his homeland and escape to other continents.

His level was too low; Qi Ci Lang was as distant to him as the stars. Despite the other party being a major figure, Cang Xu remained unaware.

Much like how many citizens of the Sheng Ming Empire couldn't clearly identify the specific titles of the three saints of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

However, as Cang Xu's strength grew and the caliber of people he encountered rose dramatically, his understanding of figures like Qi Ci Lang, renowned for their charismatic lifestyles, gradually deepened.

Seeing Cang Xu sigh softly, Zhou Zhang mistook his expression for despondency and offered consolation: “For every gain, there is a loss!”

“Don't just focus on the increase in divine favor, they all paid a price.”

“I won't elaborate on the others, but you know about the Handsome Youth Mercenary Corps.”

“Think carefully, what became of those five peerlessly brilliant youths after the war?”

Zhou Zhang stopped there, leaving the matter unsaid.

Cang Xu’s expression turned solemn.

His thoughts drifted to the sacred sons and daughters.

“Such beings are the most divine power-aligned transcendents within a church.”

"They are often meticulously cultivated by the church, their faith deepened by every possible means. When the critical moment arrives, the sacred son or daughter becomes the vessel for the divine descent."

“Many sacred sons and daughters become crippled after the divine descent.”

“In truth, once the deity descends, they usually perish on the battlefield...”

Reflecting on this, Cang Xu gained deeper insight into why their own commander was so favored.

“It's still because of that god level bloodline, isn't it?”

“But the commander's bloodline concentration isn't high, gold level is the limit.”

“Perhaps that's it...”

A thought struck Cang Xu, prompting him to ask Zhou Zhang: “Lord, does our church have any sacred sons or daughters?”

Zhou Zhang gave Cang Xu a deep look: “That's a secret known only to the church's highest echelons. You're not qualified to know yet.”

Though Cang Xu received no direct answer, his suspicions only deepened.

“The Charming Blue Goddess is weak now, and the Charming Blue Church's situation isn't good either.”

“Perhaps they no longer have a sacred son or daughter!”

The Charming Blue Church had suffered a severe blow. Even a saint level powerhouse like Zhou Zhang, a kingdom-level combatant, could only secretly hold large-scale rituals deep beneath the ocean.

“Picking the best out of the available choices, though the commander's bloodline was diluted, it still earned favor.”

Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Splash, splash, splash...

The surrounding seawater swirled ceaselessly as the fishman youngster submerged deeper and deeper.

The next moment, he opened his eyes.

A puzzled expression crossed the fishman youngster's face: “What just happened?”

“Did I dream of that great whirlpool?”

His memory of that massive great whirlpool was vivid, for it was the divine manifestation he had glimpsed through the Spiritual Navigation divine spell.

After sending off Cang Xu, the fishman youngster, unable to perform blasphemous prayers, had diligently trained on his own.

Reaching his limit, he had fallen into a deep sleep.

Yet in his dream, he seemed to find himself within the great ocean vortex.

“Was it just a dream?”

The fishman youngster sensed it was more than that.

He had dreamed before, but the sensation he'd just experienced was distinctly different from a dream.

“My divine favor...”

“Increased by another 1000?”

After checking, the fishman youngster found no sudden changes in his body or soul, yet his divine favor had surged significantly.

“I... I didn't pray at all. Why did my divine favor increase?”

The fishman youngster was utterly perplexed.

Then, when he scanned his Bloodcore, he was utterly stunned, jolting upright in bed.

“What's going on?”

“The concentration of Surging Wave fishman bloodline has risen to 27%!”

Chapter 761: The Big Guy Jumps into the Sea to Search for His Father

The fishman youngster was utterly stunned.

Even if the divine blessing had been exaggerated before, he was still an outsider. Having just performed such a blasphemous prayer, his perception hadn't been as profound as Cang Xu's.

But this time, the fishman youngster was completely shocked.

He had been accumulating bloodlines for a long time and understood all too well the difficulty of increasing its concentration.

The lower the bloodline quality, the easier it could reach 100% concentration.

But for legendary bloodlines like the Flame Dragon King's, the fishman youngster had only managed to accumulate a little over thirty percent so far.

The Surging Wave fishman bloodline was god level, even surpassing that of the Flame Dragon King. Therefore, in the view of the fishman youngster, gathering this type of fishman bloodline proved far more challenging than obtaining the Flame Dragon King's bloodline.

He had already prepared for a protracted struggle, yet unexpectedly, upon waking, his bloodline concentration had surged to 27%!

He had done nothing yet reaped such immense gains.

“This is truly...” the fishman youngster mused, overwhelmed with emotion.

Once again, he deeply understood that saying, Gods were omnipotent!

“There is nothing the gods cannot do.”

“Even something like increasing bloodline concentration is no exception.”

“But what exactly was the cost?”

The fishman youngster had no idea what price the Charming Blue Goddess had paid.

He had an even greater puzzlement— “I didn't pray at all, so why did I receive divine blessing?”

The fishman youngster had prayed repeatedly before, but his power had surged too rapidly, forcing his body to constantly adapt.

Thus, he had been compelled to suspend his blasphemous prayers.

“I didn't pray this time either...”

“No, perhaps I prayed in my dreams.”

The fishman youngster's expression shifted slightly as he carefully recalled his recent slumber.

The more he recalled, the more distinct the difference felt, this dream had been unlike any before.

He surmised that praying in his sleep might have triggered the divine blessing.

“Thankfully, it didn't boost my life level or grant me any relevant experience.”

“Instead, it enhanced my bloodline concentration.”

“This is precisely what I needed most!”

From this outcome, it was simply fantastic!

The fishman youngster needed time to adapt and assimilate the previous enhancement.

Before this, he had discussed with Cang Xu and discovered that the best, and almost only way to quickly assimilate such an enhancement was to rely on his bloodline.

“The Surging Wave fishman bloodline concentration has more than doubled directly. This means the time I need to adapt has been cut at least in half!”

Yet alongside the delight, a shadow of unease crossed the fishman youngster's face.

“What is the price demanded by the deity?”

“What sacrifice must I make?”

Cang Xu's warning echoed in his ears—gain always comes with loss!

“Perhaps my previous divine blessing exceeded expectations because, with the Charming Blue Goddess, the concentration of god level bloodlines is actually raisable.”

"So even though my Surging Wave fishman bloodline concentration is low, it doesn't matter."

“The Charming Blue Goddess is investing in me as if I were a 100% pure Surging Wave fishman?”

The fishman youngster's thoughts churned, but after pondering for a while, he came up with nothing.

Playing it safe, he stepped out of the bedroom and approached the memory crystal making device, using it to erase a segment of his own memory.

This was a fabricated memory.

After conducting the blasphemous ritual, the fishman youngster hadn't actively erased it. Instead, he'd used the Memory Concealment Spell to hide this memory.

This spared him the trouble of creating another fake memory later, when performing another blasphemous prayer, and then implanting it.

But after this incident, the fishman youngster felt it was better to be cautious.

Performing such a dangerous act as a blasphemous prayer should only be undertaken once he was fully prepared.

The fishman youngster was deeply concerned that this dream prayer might recur.

Ultimately, he erased the fabricated memory.

On some nameless deserted island.

Da Dou was preparing a meal.

Firewood split from gold level torch trees had been ignited, emitting steady, enduring heat, the ideal material for grilling.

Large chunks of meat lay directly on the torchwood embers, sizzling as juices dripped onto the dry wood with audible crackles.

An intense aroma wafted through the air.

The big guy's nostrils flared, awakened by the scent of the feast.

Da Dou glanced at him before refocusing on the meat before him—this grilling had reached its most critical moment.

The gold level magic chef let out a low growl, unleashing a gold level battle skill. In an instant, it completely enveloped the grilled meat, compressing it.

Battle skill - Food Compression!

The grilled meat, originally as large as a carriage, had been compressed. Its mass remained unchanged, but its volume had shrunk to one-tenth of its original level.

Most crucially, the aura of the gold level grilled meat had dropped, stabilizing at silver level.

Da Dou wiped the sweat from his forehead. This time, downgrading the dish had required his utmost effort, and he'd even sacrificed expensive ingredients.

The big guy sat up, his upper body stiff, staring blankly at Da Dou, his expression still a bit hazy.

Da Dou took a few deep breaths, then used his gold level battle skill to deliver the roast meat to the big guy: “Eat.”

Da Dou urged gently, “Gold level cuisine is still beyond your current capacity.”

“Consuming silver level magic dishes won't fully unleash the potential of your bloodline.”

“Therefore, the optimal choice is to eat downgraded silver level dishes crafted from gold level ingredients!”

“Try it. It's delicious.”

Da Dou attempted to awaken the big guy's warm memories, hoping to capture his heart through his stomach.

But the big guy's heart had long since belonged to the young commander.

He stared at the grilled meat before him, his body already reacting involuntarily as saliva trickled down his chin.

Yet, as his consciousness fully returned, anger slowly crept onto his face.

Within seconds, that anger grew intense.

He shoved the grilled meat aside, leaped to his feet, and roared at Da Dou, “Home! I'm going home.”

"Father...”

“I need to find Father!”

The big guy stood, glaring at Da Dou.

Da Dou smiled.

The big guy suddenly turned and took off running.

He hadn't fallen into a frenzied state; he still retained his wits.

Earlier, when he'd tried to attack Da Dou, his entire body had convulsed, and he'd fallen to the ground, unable to get up.

This had left a deep impression on the big guy.

So, this time, instead of charging straight in, the big guy fled.

Da Dou smiled, watching the big guy scramble away. After he'd fled a considerable distance, Da Dou finally began to walk.

Using his battle skill, he effortlessly caught up to the big guy. Holding a piece of grilled meat, he casually persuaded him, "Hey, stop this nonsense. That mercenary group treated you like dirt. They didn't respect you at all."

“You're a giant, and so am I.”

“Come back with me to Eruption Island. That's where our home truly is!”

But the big guy wouldn't listen to Da Dou at all. He casually waved his arms, shoving Da Dou aside.

Right now, the big guy just wanted to run back.

Even though he had no clue which direction was correct.

Da Dou tried persuading him several more times, but the big guy remained stubbornly unmoved.

Seeing his pleas utterly futile, Da Dou sighed helplessly and called out to the big guy, “This isn't Ice Sculpture Island anymore.”

“Where do you think you can run to?”

By the time he finished speaking, the big guy had already sprinted onto the beach.

"See for yourself, we're surrounded by the ocean, not a single boat in sight. You'd be wise to come back with me..."

Before Da Dou could finish, the big guy plunged straight into the sea.

He began swimming the breaststroke, arms and legs churning.

Cold sweat beaded on Da Dou's forehead.

The distance from Ice Sculpture Island was already considerable. Relying solely on the big guy's stamina, he'd surely exhaust himself and drown midway.

Of course, if he could find a deserted island to rest on, it might be manageable.

But Da Dou observed that the big guy's intelligence was questionable, he'd likely swim single-mindedly.

“The problem is you're swimming in the wrong direction!”

Speechless, Da Dou could only take action.

But just as he moved to intervene, a sudden transformation swept over him.

He darted aside as the Flame Totem on his body erupted in a burst of fire, enveloping him entirely.

The flames vanished, exploding violently nearby.

Da Dou materialized there.

“Who goes there?!” Da Dou roared, momentarily forgetting about the big guy.

High above, a mage tower slowly materialized.

Honey Snow Tower.

It turned out the tower spirit had finally completed its analysis, obtaining Da Dou's teleportation coordinates.

Shuang Lian acted swiftly, directly bringing the mage tower along as she flew urgently, successfully locating both the big guy and Da Dou.

In the top-floor control room, Shuang Lian remained expressionless.

Having failed to ambush Da Dou, she immediately turned her attention to the big guy.

If she could seal him within the demi-plane, her mission would be considered a success.

Of course, capturing Da Dou as well would be even better!

Chapter 762: Shuang Lian vs Da Dou

Exhale, inhale, exhale, gulp, inhale, inhale, gulp gulp...

The big guy swam with all his might, completely unaware that Da Dou had been attacked.

His mind was set on returning to Snow Bird Harbor, but he hadn't considered what the correct direction might be.

His swimming skills were clearly poor too. His breathing rhythm was unstable, and he frequently choked on water.

But to be fair, given the big guy's intelligence, learning to swim at all was quite impressive.

Who knew how much time and effort the old shipwright had poured into teaching him?

Spatial fluctuations erupted once more.

This time, right beside the big guy!

Da Dou growled lowly, glaring at him.

Behind the big guy, a flame emblem suddenly ignited, erupting in intense heat.

The seawater couldn't extinguish the scorching flames at all.

The big guy was soon engulfed in flames, his consciousness fading into a haze.

The flames surged upward, like weary birds returning to their nests, and plunged directly into one of Da Dou's arms.

The tattoo on his arm was complete once more.

It turned out that after capturing the big guy, Da Dou hadn't been idle.

While the big guy slept, he had dismantled part of the Flame Totem on his own arm and implanted it into the big guy's back.

The big guy remained completely unaware.

Da Dou now activated his hidden maneuver, instantly bringing the big guy back to his side.

Honey Snow Tower, top-floor control room.

Witnessing this, Bu Quan couldn't help but gasp, her expression clearly showing disappointment.

She had desperately wanted to see the big guy captured by the spatial trap and drawn into the demi-plane. She had confidence in her teacher, Shuang Lian.

Yet, unexpectedly, Da Dou had once again managed to defend against it.

Bu Quan couldn't help but glance at her esteemed teacher.

Shuang Lian remained utterly serene, her composure unshaken by two consecutive failures.

The only fleeting expression was a slight arch of her eyebrow.

"What do we do now? The giant's vanished!" Bu Quan's heart raced with worry, but fearing she might disturb her teacher, she kept her concerns silent.

Shuang Lian glanced at her beloved disciple, as if hearing her thoughts, and offered reassurance: “Rest assured, neither of them can escape.”

Bu Quan seized the moment to ask about the big guy's whereabouts.

Shuang Lian explained: “That's a spatial artifact Da Dou carries - a gold level Packing Bag. This spatial storage device can contain living beings. Da Dou uses it to store ingredients.”

“This underscores the critical importance of intelligence.”

“However, the flames on his body exceed what our intelligence documented.”

"The Flame Totem originates from a beast deity, making it exceedingly rare. Da Dou only obtained it thanks to his teacher."

During preparations, Shuang Lian specifically gathered extensive intelligence on Da Dou through channels like the Alchemy Guild.

In multiple culinary duels, Da Dou demonstrated his mastery by wielding the Flame Totem, securing victories over numerous rival chefs. Yet he had never engaged in a true battle.

Or rather, even if he had, it remained unpublicized and unknown.

Da Dou's spatial awareness and masterful use of the Flame Totem caused Shuang Lian's first two strikes to miss their mark.

“But it doesn't matter!”

“The victor of this battle will still be me, and only me!”

Shuang Lian was utterly confident in this fight.

Spell - Spatial Anchor!

In the next instant, she unleashed the spatial spell she had been brewing through the mage tower.

A colossal phantom ship's anchor abruptly materialized mid-air before vanishing instantly.

The spell's model was constructed by the tower spirit, fueled by the tower's vast magic pool, expanding its radius to eight hundred meters.

Da Dou's expression twisted in shock.

He felt space itself freeze. Under normal circumstances, he perceived space like water, able to sense other worlds beneath its surface.

But now, the water's surface had become solid ground. He could no longer break through the surface to dive into the space beyond.

“Damn.”

“The Tunnel Staff is useless now.”

“And seriously, this spell's range is way too big, isn't it?”

Da Dou inwardly gasped.

He'd tried to stop it, but it was already too late.

While the mage tower was brewing the Spatial Anchor, Da Dou dodged Shuang Lian's two spatial grabs, causing him to miss the prime opportunity to interrupt the casting.

“But even if I had the chance to shake this mage tower...”

Da Dou himself didn't have much confidence.

If he were facing Shuang Lian alone, perhaps she might avoid his sharpest blows and be forced to interrupt her spell. But this tower, its defenses were clearly impregnable. There was no way Da Dou could breach it instantly.

He summoned his Flame Totem, only to discover with disappointment that the Flame Flicker divine spell he'd used earlier was also unusable.

Looking up at the tower, Da Dou shouted, "Who are you? If you're hired by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to retrieve the giant, I'll pay double the price!"

As a gold level magic chef, Da Dou certainly wasn't short on food or drink.

Even if he carried little cash, the ingredients he carried were absolutely priceless!

After all, he had come to Ice Sculpture Island to participate in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's grand ceremony. He had prepared a vast quantity of ingredients for this event, some of which were exceptionally high-end.

Shuang Lian chuckled softly. “Da Dou, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' bounty is far beyond what you could ever pay.”

“Release the hostage, and I might spare your life!”

Shuang Lian had no desire to utterly kill Da Dou.

For one thing, she and Da Dou shared no deep personal grudge. Had he kidnapped Bu Quan instead of the big guy, it would have been another matter entirely.

For another, she knew his background well—a gold level giant magic chef hailing from Eruption Island.

Though Eruption Island couldn't match Ice Sculpture Island in size, it housed legendary beings!

Hearing Shuang Lian's offer, Da Dou's heart stirred. He immediately replied, “Mighty magician, are you truly resolved to oppose my Eruption Island?”

“If you spare me and let me bring the giant back to Eruption Island, you'll gain not only my personal allegiance but the friendship of the entire island!”

“I swear on my personal honor, Da Dou, the rewards you receive will be beyond measure.”

Shuang Lian sighed.

Had this matter not involved Bu Quan's intentions, she might have agreed.

But now...

Shuang Lian's voice turned cold: “I am Shuang Lian, elder of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild and also the Honey Snow Tower Lord.”

“I don't believe you can speak for the entire Eruption Island.”

“Even if you could, I wouldn't fear it.”

“I don't believe the Eruption Island Lord would seek revenge with me over a mere young giant.”

“Would Eruption Island wage war against Ice Sculpture Island over this?”

“It's entirely possible!” Da Dou desperately wanted to blurt this out, but wisely kept his mouth shut.

The big guy possessed potential worthy of a saint level.

Igniting a war between the two islands was entirely feasible.

But now, Da Dou couldn't voice this so bluntly. If the big guy's true talent were exposed, the enemy would never let it rest.

Keeping it hidden was far more advantageous.

After pondering, Da Dou carefully chose his words to continue explaining.

He appealed to Shuang Lian, citing his and the big guy's shared giant ethnicity and his unwillingness to see kin exiled and bullied in a foreign land.

Shuang Lian scoffed. “Hmph. Even pure-blooded dragons wouldn't do such a thing. When did the giants on Eruption Island ever adopt such a policy?"

“Even if it's your personal stance, I haven't seen you act on it before.”

Da Dou tried to speak further, but Shuang Lian, growing impatient, resumed her actions.

She manipulated the mage tower with her mind, causing snow to drift down over the barren island.

The wind grew fiercer.

Soon, howling gales whipped through the air, carrying heavy snowflakes.

“Battlefield-class magic!”

“And she's casting two spells simultaneously.”

Da Dou sucked in a sharp breath, channeling his battle qi as he charged toward the sea.

He possessed no flight battle skill, but his movement battle skill allowed him to sprint directly across the water's surface.

Da Dou had no intention of fighting Shuang Lian.

If Shuang Lian were alone, his fighting spirit wouldn't be this low. The crux was the mage tower!

A full five-story mage tower gave him a headache just looking at it, let alone engaging in combat.

Since the big guy was already stuffed into the packing bag, Da Dou chose to flee directly.

So, Shuang Lian launched the pursuit.

Her advantage was huge. Stationed within the mage tower, she had no chance of losing.

Da Dou endured a brutal beating for a long time, subjected to all manner of spells that left him battered and bruised, suffering terribly.

He tried to run out of the Spatial Anchor's coverage, but the mage tower effortlessly followed him, keeping him perpetually near the center of its spell's range.

Da Dou's condition deteriorated severely.

Spell - Frost Flower Imprisonment.

Shuang Lian seized the moment, striking Da Dou.

Frost flowers instantly bloomed across Da Dou's body.

They shimmered like diamonds, radiant and crystalline, exquisite as works of art. Each frost flower was as large as an adult's palm, covering the Flame Totem on Da Dou's body in layers.

Da Dou channeled his battle qi, mounting a full-force defense, yet struggled to hold it.

The frost flowers quickly piled up thicker and thicker, gradually merging into a single mass.

Crack, crack.

Da Dou's movements grew increasingly sluggish as the frost flowers on his body solidified into a thick layer of ice, sealing him in place!

Chapter 763: Mei Lin Battles Shuang Lian with Wit

“Got it!” Bu Quan nearly leapt for joy upon seeing the large pouch frozen solid.

She gazed at her mentor with admiration.

From the recent battle, Bu Quan had witnessed the vast gap between herself and Shuang Lian, yet she had also learned much.

Her lack of combat experience was the sole reason for her inexperience in battle.

With her intelligence, she could achieve extraordinary results in many areas if she dedicated herself.

Shuang Lian secretly relished her disciple’s admiring gaze, though outwardly she remained composed, glancing at Bu Quan. “See? You still have much to learn.”

Bu Quan nodded repeatedly, voicing her most profound realization: “First, I need a mage tower!”

“Uh...” Shuang Lian froze in surprise.

Bu Quan burst out laughing: "Master, thank you for the demonstration. I feel much more confident facing Yi Jiu now."

“But... are we really going to kill him?”

“He's begged for mercy multiple times and offered a hefty price for his life.”

Shuang Lian's expression turned stern as she fixed Bu Quan with a steady gaze. “Kill. You must kill him!”

Bu Quan's heart jolted, and he looked at his mentor in confusion.

A faint pressure lingered in Shuang Lian's calm gaze: “Remember, he is your enemy!”

“Yi Jiu once sought to harm you. Had I not intervened in time, you might have lost your life.”

“Killing such an enemy is the proper course.”

“Your hesitation stems from two things.”

"First, you remain too kind-hearted. Seeing Yi Jiu tortured into a pitiful, wretched state, watching him beg for mercy, it softened your heart!"

"Such kindness is profoundly foolish. Do you believe me? Give Yi Jiu another chance to fight, and he'll strike you down faster, with even more ruthless battle skill."

"Second, you fear the repercussions of eliminating a gold level opponent. Is that it?"

Bu Quan nodded.

Shuang Lian understood her so well, striking right at the core of her thoughts.

Bu Quan said, “Teacher, yesterday you mentioned the Thieves' Guild inquired about Yi Jiu's whereabouts.”

“We've already discovered Yi Jiu is a key member of the Thieves' Guild.”

“You didn't reveal the truth to the Thieves' Guild.”

“I feared that if word leaked out after killing Yi Jiu, the Thieves' Guild would turn against us!”

Shuang Lian smiled faintly: “Your reasoning is sound.”

She gazed at Bu Quan; her heart filled with pity.

In her youth, Bu Quan had endured brutal beatings and oppression from the Bayonet Gang, which led to the death of her nanny. Though not related by blood, the nanny had raised Bu Quan from childhood, their bond as close as mother and daughter. This loss dealt Bu Quan an immense blow.

Fortunately, Bu Quan later joined the Alchemy Guild, escaping the Bayonet Gang's continued clutches.

Such life experiences profoundly shaped Bu Quan's character and mindset.

Bu Quan was acutely aware of the Alchemy Guild's formidable power, and when facing the Thieves' Guild, a rival organization of the same level, she inevitably felt constrained and hesitant.

“The Thieves' Guild differs greatly from our Alchemy Guild,” Shuang Lian explained patiently.

“Because Thieves' Guild members don't just gather intelligence or conduct reconnaissance; they also carry out tasks like assassinations.”

“Therefore, the Thieves' Guild finds it difficult to openly protect its members.”

“We are the aggrieved party. Yi Jiu's assassination attempt failed, and we killed him in return, it's only natural.”

“The Thieves' Guild contacted us because they offered a high price to attempt a ransom if Yi Jiu fell into my hands.”

“But if we killed Yi Jiu, the Thieves' Guild would never openly retaliate against us.”

“If they would do that, they wouldn't survive this long.”

“Those with needs will find them and pay for their services, but the vast majority dislike them.”

Shuang Lian stroked Bu Quan's head: “And one crucial point, we are members of the Alchemy Guild!”

“Even if we kill Yi Jiu in public, the Thieves' Guild won't dare speak up.”

“Remember, we have backing.”

Bu Quan nodded.

After Shuang Lian's guidance, she resolved: “Then I'll do my best to eliminate Yi Jiu!”

Shuang Lian nodded: Even with the mage tower and magic formations, taking down Yi Jiu remains challenging for you right now."

“This is your homework assignment.”

“You may only leave the tower once it's completed.”

“I'll finish it as quickly as possible!” Bu Quan declared fiercely.

Yet no sooner had she spoken than a shrill alarm pierced the control room.

The tower spirit immediately spoke: “The demi-plane has suffered a spatial breach; spatial defenses have been penetrated.”

“Detecting divine power. Analyzing divine power. Divine power unknown!”

“Decoding divine spell. Emergency decoding in progress...”

"What?!" Shuang Lian and Bu Quan were shocked.

Shuang Lian hastily summoned the demi-plane's magic projection. The teacher and student then witnessed Yi Jiu praying within the snow cave, as shifting light and shadow revealed the landscape of another realm.

"This is a divine spell for teleportation! Divine power that can't be analyzed? I specifically acquired the Divine Power Compendium; it catalogs nearly every deity's aura in the Main Plane."

Shuang Lian's eyes flashed with fury: "I never imagined Yi Jiu was a believer of an unknown deity! Using him as a coordinate, the enemy could open a portal and teleport directly into my demi-plane."

Within mere seconds, Shuang Lian had grasped the situation.

The situation was critical. Shuang Lian immediately acted with full force, manipulating the mage tower to enter the Honey snow demi-plane.

In a snow cave, Yi Jiu knelt, pouring his entire being into prayer.

The spatial fluctuations from the mage tower's arrival and the overwhelming aura sent shivers down his spine.

Yet he didn't even lift his head, praying with every ounce of strength, rapidly depleting the divine favor he'd painstakingly accumulated.

It wasn't just Yi Jiu exerting himself. Within the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, the duelists led by Mei Lin were also praying with all their might.

This caused the Tranquil Hill's space surrounding Yi Jiu to rapidly expand. What had been a constantly shifting blur of light and shadow quickly stabilized and sharpened.

Spell - Spatial Anchor!

After several seconds of brewing, the towering mage tower suspended overhead erupted with magic once more, casting the spell successfully.

A massive, translucent Spatial Anchor plummeted vertically, anchoring itself directly above Yi Jiu's head.

The anchor swiftly dissipated, but the spatial fluctuations triggered by the divine spell were rapidly suppressed.

The anchor's effect was immediate!

Yet it didn't halt the expansion entirely. The divine domain space triggered by the spell continued to expand slowly, though at a pace several times slower than before.

Shuang Lian continued manipulating the mage tower, casting spells.

Blizzards swirled, enveloping Yi Jiu.

Ice arrows and snowballs shot forth, ice elements rapidly coalescing to surround and attack Yi Jiu from all sides.

With no other choice, Yi Jiu halted his prayers and fought his way back.

"What should we do? Yi Jiu has stopped praying, and the divine domain space is being gradually pushed back!"

“Shuang Lian paid a heavy price in the demi-plane. The magic formation she constructed actually managed to block the descent of the divine domain.”

Gu Dong and the others began to panic.

The Divine Domain Descent failed to materialize, intercepted mid-cast by Shuang Lian's timely intervention.

Trapped within Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, Gu Dong and the others couldn't channel the spell sufficiently to force their way in.

Unable to join the battle, this group of gold level powerhouses could only watch helplessly!

Yet Mei Lin, the supreme leader of this operation, showed no signs of panic. Instead, a hint of anticipation flickered in her eyes.

Her beautiful eyes swept the room, taking in everyone's expressions before deliberately teasing them, “Rest assured, help will come.”

"Oh? Could Lady Mei Lin have planted someone close to Shuang Lian?"

"No, it's more likely one of Shuang Lian's students was bribed. After all, Shuang Lian favors Bu Quan the most, showing her considerable favoritism. This has already stirred discontent among many students."

Gu Dong and the others possessed solid military training. Before battle, they had memorized all relevant intelligence thoroughly.

But Mei Lin shook her head. “In such a short time, bribery would be difficult and risky.”

“If the bribery failed, word would leak out, alerting Shuang Lian and causing us to lose the element of surprise.”

“This time, the one aiding us is Shuang Lian herself!”

Mei Lin smiled confidently.

Gu Dong and the others froze in shock, then exchanged bewildered glances.

Had anyone else made such a claim, they would have voiced heavy skepticism immediately. But this was Mei Lin speaking.

Mei Lin wasn't just a magician, she was a commander in the First Navy.

Her leadership was exceptional. Under her command, the First Navy had crushed numerous powerful pirate factions. Her command abilities were universally recognized by the military.

"But getting Shuang Lian to help us against herself? How could that possibly work?" Gu Dong and the others couldn't wrap their heads around it.

Chapter 764: Falling Into a Trap

Honey Snow Tower.

The tower spirit reported: “Phase One testing complete. Divine power analysis inconclusive. Divine domain unknown...”

Without waiting for the tower spirit's full report, Shuang Lian summoned her focus and directly accessed all the test results.

The next moment, her expression changed.

The various indicators in the test reports revealed a monumental secret!

“An unknown deity?”

“No, perhaps... They haven't truly descended yet.”

“This divine domain is likely...”

Shuang Lian's heart trembled violently.

Her gaze shifted.

Earlier, when she looked at the divine domain space, she had wanted nothing more than to extinguish it outright.

But now, her burning gaze seemed intent on piercing through the very fabric of the divine domain space, seeking to verify the report's findings and uncover the truth!

“If it truly is what I suspect...”

“This divine domain will be the most crucial opportunity of my entire life!”

Shuang Lian had once stumbled upon a young demi-plane and captured it. It was the greatest fortune of her life.

She seized it.

Through years of cultivation, she reaped immense rewards.

It was precisely the prudent management of that demi-plane that established her worth, providing the foundational capital to leverage within the vast platform of the Alchemy Guild, unlocking even greater resources.

Ultimately, she built the Honey Snow Tower, amassing countless alchemy achievements and becoming one of the most influential elders in the Alchemy Guild.

But now, she felt the opportunity presented by encountering a demi-plane might pale in comparison to the one before her!

“A divine domain... that's not something a demi-plane can rival.”

“What's wrong, Teacher?” Bu Quan asked, puzzled.

She had been watching her mentor closely and was startled to see the tower spirit's report abruptly halt mid-sentence. Her mentor's eyes now blazed with an intense, piercing light!

Spell Conversion - Spatial Pendant!

Shuang Lian's contemplation lasted only an instant before she sprang into action.

She directly transformed the previous Spatial Anchor spell into a Spatial Pendant Spell.

These two spells served distinct purposes.

The Spatial Anchor fixed a space, restricting all spatial alterations. The Spatial Pendant, however, probed alternate planes and performed precise spatial localization.

Honey Snow Tower once again demonstrated its formidable power, effortlessly transforming the previous Spatial Anchor into a Spatial Pendant.

Spell conversion was a casting technique.

Magicians who mastered this skill could transform spells between two similar magic models.

Thus, one spell could be converted into another.

This technique found extensive applications in combat, alchemy, and other fields.

The Spatial Anchor coalesced and materialized once more, then instantly transformed into a giant water-drop pendant.

Without the Spatial Anchor's restraint, the divine domain space instantly shed its constraints and rapidly expanded once more.

Bu Quan was startled and wanted to ask her mentor, but wisely kept her mouth shut.

Shuang Lian was fully focused, manipulating the mage tower.

Spell - Ice Crystal Chain.

Soon, another spell was unleashed.

Frost elements coalesced into five slender chains, extending from the mage tower straight to the Spatial Pendant.

The pendant was hurled forth in the next instant.

The Spatial Pendant plunged directly into the divine domain space. As it penetrated, intense spatial ripples surged through the divine domain space.

Yi Jiu stared in shock and disbelief.

Spell - Frost Flower Imprisonment!

This spell had been used just moments ago.

Under its effect, Da Dou was utterly helpless, frozen solid like an ice block, unable to move.

Frost flowers materialized, first enveloping the space around Yi Jiu, then rapidly spreading to cover the divine domain space.

These frost flowers were over ten times larger than those on Da Dou!

Giant frost flowers interlocked, radiating frigid cold. Crystal clear and translucent, they formed a vast net, imprisoning the divine domain space.

No, more accurately, they were “reinforcing” it!

“Shuang Lian... What is she doing?!” Yi Jiu was stunned.

The other gold level experts within the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain shared his bewilderment.

“Shuang Lian is truly expanding the divine domain space!”

“Not only that, but she’s also reinforcing the spatial passageway, as if fearing we'll abandon Yi Jiu and retreat directly.”

“She actually did it!”

“Mei Lin, what exactly is happening?”

All eyes turned to the mermaid magician.

Mei Lin, however, only gazed silently at the Spatial Pendant entering the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, offering no explanation.

Suddenly, she declared, “It's almost ready. Xue Tuan Zi, activate the magic formation immediately!”

Xue Tuan Zi responded instantly.

The formation activated, unleashing an incredibly powerful pulling force.

The pulling force acted upon the Spatial Pendant, violently yanking it downward.

The Spatial Pendant and Honey Snow Tower were bound together by ice crystal chains. For the moment, they formed a single entity.

Everything happened too quickly.

Before Shuang Lian could react, the mage tower was dragged down.

Yi Jiu roared in panic.

The divine domain space used him as its spatial coordinate. In his field of vision, the mage tower plummeted from the sky, hurtling straight toward him!

Yi Jiu dodged with every ounce of strength.

He didn't evade completely, getting grazed by the tower's edge.

He flew through the air like a bouncing ball. In an instant, three ribs snapped—despite having activated his gold level defensive battle skill.

Yi Jiu crashed into the snow, coughing up another mouthful of blood.

He scrambled to his feet only to find the mage tower had vanished!

The divine domain space, violently impacted by the tower, now resembled a vigorously stirred paint bucket, the gradually crystallizing vision of the divine domain had coalesced into a multicolored vortex.

A single glance left Yi Jiu dizzy and disoriented.

Even more terrifying, the swirling vortex unleashed a powerful suction force!

Unable to evade, Yi Jiu was seized by the pull and violently dragged into the spatial vortex.

Within the next second, the vortex rapidly contracted into a single point before vanishing completely from Honey snow demi-plane.

With both Yi Jiu and the mage tower vanished, the once-bustling battlefield fell suddenly silent.

Tranquil Hill Divine Domain.

The magic formation used to drag the mage tower shattered with a thunderous crash.

“Success!” Xue Tuan Zi cheered, paying no further attention to the broken formation.

This formation had been a disposable, single-use item, having already fulfilled its tactical purpose.

"Damn it, I fell for it! Within the Honey Snow Tower, Shuang Lian's expression darkened considerably.

She instantly grasped the scheme.

The enemy had first exploited her spell to pinpoint the Honey Snow Tower's location.

Then, the magic force unleashed by the formation marked and dragged the entire unit, both the pendant and the mage tower.

That force alone wouldn't have been enough to subdue the mage tower. The crucial factor was spatial power.

The opponent had cleverly exploited the instability of the spatial passage to create a temporary spatial vortex. Using this vortex, they sucked the mage tower inside.

Originally, the Divine Domain Descent used the spatial power of the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain to counter the spatial power of the Honey snow demi-plane.

But just now, the duelists suddenly exerted force in the opposite direction, while the Honey snow demi-plane, striving to restore its original spatial order, also applied thrust.

The convergence of these two immense forces was something the Honey Snow Tower, caught off guard, could not withstand!

Thus, the mage tower was ultimately sucked into the enemy's divine domain.

A shrill alarm sounded within Honey Snow Tower.

The tower spirit urgently reported the extent of the damage.

The damage was extensive!

A spatial vortex was not a stable portal; it exerted crushing, grinding forces on anything it transmitted. Fortunately, the Honey Snow Tower was exceptionally sturdy, allowing it to pass through the vortex.

Even so, the tower's surface was now horribly twisted, with holes appearing in some places, exposing the interior to the outside.

"We pulled Honey Snow Tower inside?!"

“Weren't we supposed to attack the Honey snow demi-plane?”

"Mei Lin, this is so like you! What a tactical move!"

"Indeed, Shuang Lian has managed that demiplane for years, fortifying it like an iron barrel. Attacking from within puts us at a huge disadvantage. But now it's different. Here, we hold a massive home-field advantage."

The gold duelists all snapped to attention, showering Mei Lin with praise.

Yet Yi Jiu roared in fury: “Mei Lin, are you trying to kill me?!”

Yi Jiu's condition was equally horrific.

His nose had been sliced off, his left leg twisted 90 degrees forward, and his right forearm contorted into a twisted knot. The gravest injuries lay within, several of his internal organs had been displaced.

These were the costs Yi Jiu paid for his physical body traversing the spatial vortex!

In contrast, Shuang Lian, Bu Quan, and the others remained unscathed, protected by the mage tower.

Mei Lin spoke calmly: "Yi Jiu, you've been rescued, haven't you? I fulfilled my promise."

Yi Jiu spat blood in rage, his bloodshot eyes locking onto Mei Lin as if he wanted to devour her: "This is your idea of rescue? Making me pass straight through the spatial vortex?"

“Shuang Lian had already lifted the suppression. Why not wait until the spatial passage stabilizes before letting me return?”

Mei Lin shook her head. “I won't stake our chance on the enemy.”

“With every passing second, Shuang Lian grows more likely to sense this is a trap and alter the spell again.”

"Your injuries are not life-threatening. We simulated this tactic extensively beforehand and calculated every possible outcome."

"The mage tower entered before you, absorbing the bulk of the spatial grinding."

"Of course, you may also view these wounds as a warning from our side."

Earlier, Yi Jiu had threatened to expose Mei Lin and the others, even the entire Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, to provoke their rescue attempt. This had made the vast majority of duelists deeply resentful, even disgusted, toward Yi Jiu.

Yi Jiu trembled with rage at these words.

But seeing the cold stares directed at him by the other gold level duelists, he regained his composure.

"I'll remember this! Next time, Mei Lin, don't even think about hiring me!" Yi Jiu gritted his teeth and turned to leave.

Gold level battle qi enveloped his internal and external wounds, even coalescing into a battle qi prosthetic leg to support him.

"Wait." Mei Lin said.

Yi Jiu's heart shook as he turned around and stared at Mei Lin warily: "What else do you want?"

Mei Lin smiled lightly: "Don't you want revenge? Your true enemy isn't us—it's Shuang Lian."

Yi Jiu snorted coldly, glancing toward the mage tower looming in the sky nearby.

The desire for vengeance burned within him, but his injuries held him back.

Mei Lin suddenly tossed a bundle of potions his way.

Yi Jiu caught them, his expression shifting slightly.

He instantly recognized them as gold level potions, excellent healing items.

“Drink these, and you'll regain your fighting strength,” Mei Lin smiled. "This is my new hire for you. I'm only offering this much because the rest of the reward is over there."

Mei Lin's gaze shifted toward the mage tower, the implication clear.

Yi Jiu's expression flickered through a range of emotions before he finally drew a sharp breath and cursed, "I'll kill Shuang Lian!"

“Anyone else interested?”

The gold duelists exchanged glances and burst into laughter.

“We're all interested!”

“Can you handle it, Yi Jiu?”

“Let me make this clear: no one interferes with me. Or I'll take you all down too!”

Chapter 765: The Duelists Besiege the Magician Tower

Honey Snow Tower was forcibly dragged into the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain.

Both the tower spirit and Shuang Lian were conducting full-scale reconnaissance while rushing to repair the mage tower.

Bu Quan shouted nervously, “The enemy is attacking! So many of them, all gold level!”

Charging at the forefront was a burly, bare-chested man wielding a ram's-horn hammer.

It was Mo Gui Ji.

Mei Lin had only extended a simple invitation, and he immediately came to join the battle.

Next came Qing Hong Yan, the half rabbitman, half alchemy machine.

The other gold level fighters instinctively kept their distance from him.

Gu Dong and He Gai Tou formed the second wave.

Mei Lin and Xue Tuan Zi both rose into the air, beginning to channel their spells.

Yi Jiu, having consumed potions before engaging, lagged behind.

Yet one trailed even further: the wood elf Zhu Gan.

Ban Lan Gen, Qing Hong Yan, Gu Dong, He Gai Tou, Mei Lin, Xue Tuan Zi, Yi Jiu, and Zhu Gan. Eight gold level duelists united to assault the Honey Snow Tower!

But the tower struck first.

Beams erupted from the mage tower, dazzlingly colorful and encompassing all elemental forces except the negative energy of the undead.

The beams moved with astonishing speed. Mo Gui Ji and Qing Hong Yan were struck, each blown back by explosions of flame and lightning.

Gu Dong dodged decisively, while He Gai Tou hurled his round-brimmed straw hat as a flying shield to block the attack.

Xue Tuan Zi utilized frost magic to endure the impact.

Mei Lin, however, shattered upon contact, revealing she had merely projected an illusion as a decoy, her true form unknown to all.

Yi Jiu dodged with remarkable ease this time.

Zhu Gan proved even more effortless. Wielding a jade-green spear with a specially crafted tip, he lightly flicked the beams, sending them flying off course.

Honey Snow Tower's assault failed to halt the gold level duelists.

Soon, two magicians' spells were cast successfully. One spell turned into countless snowballs that slammed into the mage tower, exploding constantly. The other spell reinforced the other gold level duelists, making their attacks more ferocious.

As the two sides were too close in distance, Honey Snow Tower fell into a disadvantage.

After all, its attack range was exceptionally wide, far surpassing that of Snow Bird Harbor's mage tower. The latter's offensive and defensive reach encompassed the entire Snow Bird Harbor!

After withstanding a wave of attacks, the gold level fighters charged to the very front of the mage tower.

A barrage of gold level battle skills struck the mage tower.

At the gold level, battle qi could be condensed and projected independently, offering great flexibility. Even without flight capabilities, the battle skill itself could maneuver through the air.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

The mage tower endured the barrage while launching a full-force counterattack against the gold duelists.

Though bulky and unwieldy, the tower possessed exceptional defensive capabilities. The Gold level duelists, however, held the advantage in numbers. They divided their attacks and moved with agility, evading most strikes.

For a time, both sides fought fiercely, locked in a stalemate.
