"I never imagined Honey Snow Tower's defenses would be this formidable! This is no ordinary mage tower!"

"It's the newest among the kingdom's mage towers, but precisely because of that, advanced alchemy techniques were employed in its construction. Don't forget the kingdom's Alchemy Guild contributed its expertise to help Shuang Lian build it."

“The foundational structure alone surpasses most ancient mage towers.”

“Didn't you all see the intel? The funds Shuang Lian invested in this tower could have built half a naval fleet!”

The gold level duelists exchanged these remarks between combat bursts.

The engagement had been underway for a while now. They had surrounded the mage tower, attacking it fiercely from all sides.

Yet, they couldn't break through!

The Honey Snow Tower displayed remarkably strong defenses, though it wasn't even operating at peak defensive capacity. Remember, it had just traversed a spatial vortex.

Gu Dong voluntarily withdrew, secretly contacting Mei Lin and reporting, “Lord, may we unleash our full power?”

"We've followed your orders, holding back our true strength and familiar battle skills. But at this rate, breaching the mage tower's defenses is impossible!"

Mei Lin stood at the edge of the battlefield, gazing distantly at the mage tower and shaking her head slightly. “Be patient.”

“Since we've drawn the Honey Snow Tower into the divine domain, the outcome of this battle is already predetermined.”

“Victory will undoubtedly be ours!”

“Shuang Lian and Bu Quan are doomed.”

“The most critical task now is maintaining secrecy!”

“Though the mage tower cannot traverse space to escape, that doesn't mean information can't.”

“Once our identities are exposed, and Shuang Lian happens to possess such means of transmitting information outward, our situation will become quite troublesome.”

Gu Dong nodded, fully agreeing with Mei Lin's words.

But the others differed from Gu Dong; they lacked military backgrounds.

Bao Li Gen was knocked flying before regaining his footing. He roared at Mei Lin: “This turtle shell is such a nuisance! I can't use my full strength and just have to take the beating. What kind of nonsense is this?!”

Xue Tuan Zi frowned deeply and sent a magic message: "Mei Lin, what are we going to do if this keeps up? I have a crucial alchemy experiment pending, its success or failure determines my standing in the contest."

Zhu Gan had engaged in the least combat and bore not a single scratch, remaining as serene as ever.

He even yawned: "Attacking a mage tower is rather dull. Compared to that, fishing is far more entertaining."

Qing Hong Yan was the only one without complaints.

He grew increasingly frenzied as the battle progressed, his attacks fierce and relentless. His eyes turned deeper crimson as he chanted incantations under his breath. The duelists all kept their distance from him.

Yi Jiu drank the potion, receiving significant healing that restored his combat strength to 80% of its original level.

He was serious about facing Shuang Lian!

But seriousness alone wasn't enough. His prolonged assault yielded no breakthrough, only landing him in a beating time and again. It was frustrating and infuriating.

“If I unleash my Piercing Force and activate my gold level battle skill, I might break straight into the mage tower!”

“But I must find the weakest defensive spot.”

“Scouting alone makes timely detection nearly impossible.”

The key word here was “timely.” The mage tower was simultaneously under assault and undergoing repairs.

Even vulnerable sections could be patched up in short order.

Yi Jiu turned to glance at Mei Lin, considering leveraging her reconnaissance skills. But the thought shattered the moment it formed.

“Damn it, what am I thinking?”

“The interior of the mage tower is equally perilous. I have no idea how formidable its internal defenses are!”

"Besides, why should I take such a risk? Why should I go all out?"

Yi Jiu had been captured by Shuang Lian once before, and his previous collaboration with Mei Lin had been far from smooth.

He also recalled that even if he took the extraordinary risk and achieved the best possible outcome, defeating Shuang Lian and destroying the Honey Snow Tower, how much of the spoils would he actually receive?

Mei Lin now held the reins of power. How much could Yi Jiu truly claim under her rule?

The other duelists harbored unfavorable views of Yi Jiu. The likelihood of them banding together to ostracize him was quite high.

At this realization, Yi Jiu unconsciously slowed his offensive pace, beginning to coast through the fight.

Every move made by both sides on the battlefield fell under Mei Lin's watchful eye.

She sighed deeply within.

There was no other way. This wasn't the kingdom's navy. Mobilizing these experts had already required immense effort and resources.

Among them, some had been coasting from the start, biding their time, like Zhu Gan.

Most, under Mei Lin's orders, held back their true combat strength to avoid exposure.

Yi Jiu's identity was already out in the open. He was the only one on the field without that concern.

Yet their past collaboration, the recent rescue, and Yi Jiu's inherent character and values made it impossible to pin hopes on him launching a surprise assault to break the stalemate.

The mage tower's formidable defenses had even dampened the fighting spirit of brutes like Bao Li Gen.

The only one whose battle intent kept rising was Qing Hong Yan. But this guy, when he went berserk, would even kill his own allies!

“Honey Snow Tower...” Mei Lin's gaze fixed on the mage tower.

Her initial tactical plan had succeeded. Under her leadership, the duelists had successfully dragged the mage tower into the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain.

It was like a pitfall had been dug, and the prey had fallen in, unable to escape the peril.

But this prey was too formidable for the hunters to strike.

Still, Mei Lin had anticipated this outcome before the battle began.

Detailed intelligence gathered beforehand had already hinted at this possibility. And now, actual combat had fully confirmed her suspicions.

For this very scenario, Mei Lin had devised a solution.

“Use your divine favor! Let us unite in prayer!” Mei Lin commanded.

Gu Dong hesitated: “Are we really going to do this?”

Prayers rarely grant believers their heart's desire.

But expending divine favor could secure a targeted divine blessing. Especially here in the divine domain of Tranquil Hill, where prayers received direct answers.

The downside? It consumed a significant amount of divine favor!

Gu Dong said, “My divine favor isn't abundant, and accumulating it normally is quite troublesome.”

Mei Lin replied, “It doesn't matter.”

“Just do your utmost.”

"Whatever divine favor is expended, I will provide corresponding compensation after this battle. I have already made this clear to all of you."

“Indeed, that is so.” Gu Dong nodded, saying no more.

He wasn't defying orders, nor was he stingy with his divine favor. Rather, he feared others might react with extreme caution toward the expenditure of divine favor!

This was merely Gu Dong's reminder to Mei Lin.

Mei Lin knew this well, fully understanding that even generous post-battle compensation could never fully offset the value of divine favor.

For divine favor could not be bought or sold, but only earned through the personal actions of the duelists themselves.

As holy warriors of the Dueling God, their sacred duties made earning divine favor exceedingly difficult. Mei Lin's personal accumulation peaked at 2100. Most duelists hovered below 1000.

Holy warriors weren't like missionaries or bishops, who held advantages in divine favor acquisition.

They certainly weren't like the fishman youngster, who could earn thousands of divine favor simply by sleeping.

Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The fishman youngster awoke once more.

“Divine favor has increased again.”

“This time it surpassed 5000!”

“The concentration of the Surging Wave fishman bloodline... has reached 48%!”

Chapter 766: The Old King’s Crowbar

After just one night’s sleep, the fishman youngster’s gains already surpassed what duelists achieved through prolonged effort.

“Another increase... This means it’ll likely keep growing!”

The fishman youngster felt both elated and anxious.

His joy stemmed from the fact that growth had become a trend. Enhancing his bloodline concentration was his most urgent need. He had originally planned to use Zhou Zhang's blood sacrifices to achieve this. Unexpectedly, while sleeping on the ship, his concentration rose naturally! Safe, simple, and convenient.

His worry, however, was that he had dreamt again.

“This time, I dreamt of the great whirlpool once more.”

“And the speed and depth of sinking into the ocean floor were both faster and deeper than the first time!”

The fishman youngster retrieved the recorded footage.

Ever since that first incident, whenever he rested again, he had commanded the tower spirit to secretly monitor him.

Both the magic footage and the surveillance results showed: while the fishman youngster slept, divine magic fluctuations erupted from his body.

Analysis of these divine magic fluctuations indicated at least the presence of Spiritual Navigation.

With this, the mystery was solved.

“As Cang Xu said, the great whirlpool I saw in the sea must have been my confrontation with the true form of the Charming Blue Goddess!”

“The divine are unfathomable. I cannot comprehend them, and I may even misunderstand them. The great whirlpool is the manifestation of that comprehension.”

“Then, does my growing immersion within the great whirlpool signify a deeper understanding of the Charming Blue Goddess?”

“Or perhaps... that I'm drawing ever closer to the deity?”

This was the fishman youngster's deepest concern.

Had he been a pure, devout believer of the Charming Blue Goddess, he would have rejoiced at this moment.

But he wasn't!

He was desecrating the ritual. Only by invoking fabricated memories did his level of devotion barely pass muster.

The heightened bloodline concentration allowed the fishman youngster to assimilate his previous clergyman and magician levels more swiftly.

“Half a day. At most half a day, and I can pray again.” The fishman youngster calculated, his heart both anxious and hopeful.

The cost was unknown, but the benefit was tangible and imminent.

Most crucially, the pressure of his grand plan for atonement weighed heavily. After deep contemplation, the fishman youngster still leaned toward continuing his prayers.

Tranquil Hill Divine Domain.

The duelists chanted loudly, praying in unison!

“Supreme and mighty Dueling God, my Lord.”

“You are the protector of duelists, the witness of justice, the embodiment of fearless valor.”

“Your power is unmatched, Your wisdom unsurpassed.”

“We battle within Your divine domain, yet our foes' defenses surpass shields and armor.”

"We beseech Your might to shatter their defenses. We beseech Your radiance to illuminate our path to victory."

“Guided by You, our blades shall be razor-sharp, our shields unbreakable, our eyes discerning truth from illusion, our ears attuned to every sound!”

The entire divine domain trembled faintly as colossal divine magic coalesced above the battlefield.

This was the divine domain of Tranquil Hill.

It was the domain of the duelists, and they had once again invoked divine favor through prayer, prompting an immediate, powerful response!

"Te-te-teacher, what should we do?!" In the control room, Bu Quan panicked, his face drained of color.

Shuang Lian's expression was grim as ever.

She stared intently at the ever-expanding divine power aura, her tone more serious than ever before. “Bu Quan.”

“Now, you will witness my true trump card!”

“This is a severe challenge, but equally an unparalleled opportunity.”

“Open your eyes wide and watch carefully!”

With that, Shuang Lian summoned the greatest trump card of her life, an alchemy artifact from the deepest recesses of the Honey Snow Tower.

The legend level alchemy artifact—The Old King's Crowbar!

The crowbar's primary material was a leg bone. The bone came from the most notorious womanizer king in the Main Plane's history.

In his youth, caught stealing a kiss, he had his leg broken.

He fashioned this broken bone into a growth-type crowbar artifact.

His famous words echoed through the ages: “Give me a lever long enough and a fulcrum on which to place it, and I shall move the sun and moon!”

He ultimately ascended to legend level, and the crowbar became an legend level artifact.

The true point where Shuang Lian began to rise up was the accidental acquisition of this crowbar!

The crowbar's effect was immensely powerful, capable of prying open any object in the world, but it required at least one fulcrum.

In the present context, this fulcrum was Shuang Lian's understanding of this divine domain.

“Just as I suspected, this divine domain is ownerless!”

“Crowbar, pry it open with all your might!”

Shuang Lian screamed inwardly as the suspended divine power crashed down with thunderous force.

The mage tower was struck as if by a colossal divine energy waterfall spanning a hundred miles, violently crashing to the ground.

The divine magic, vast and overwhelming, pinned the mage tower down, inflicting devastating damage.

The gold level duelists retreated one after another, watching from afar, stunned by the breathtaking divine assault!

“Is this the power of a god?”

“Honey Snow Tower is finished.”

“If we'd known this would happen, we could have saved half our divine favor.”

Just as the duelists believed the battle was decided, the Old King’s Crowbar began to work.

The divine power washing over the Honey Snow Tower was successfully levered, causing it to flow back upstream. This portion of divine power not only collided head-on with the descending force but also formed a protective barrier.

The barrier shielded the Honey Snow Tower completely, top to bottom, left to right, with meticulous care.

The vast majority of divine power remained unshifted, crashing violently against the barrier.

The two forces collided violently, rapidly depleting each other!

By all logic, the divine power forming the surface barrier of Honey Snow Tower was vastly smaller than the opposing forces and should have been unable to sustain such a prolonged battle.

Yet the Old King's Crowbar continued its relentless work. It constantly leveraged the external divine power, converting it for its own use. Thus, the divine barrier enveloping Honey Snow Tower maintained its scale.

The divine power shockwaves generated by the gold level duelists, expending vast amounts of divine favor, were instead harnessed by Shuang Lian to shield herself.

Even so, the mage tower was paying a heavy price.

A shrill alarm echoed relentlessly throughout the tower's interior.

Shuang Lian's students were thrown into utter chaos.

The tower shook violently. Even the residual shockwaves from clashing divine energies were overwhelming. Large chunks of brickwork crumbled from both inside and out, while magic circles of all sizes shattered. The entire structure rapidly slid toward the abyss of collapse.

In the control room, Magician Shuang Lian gritted her teeth, clinging to her resolve.

Never before had she pushed the tower's power to such frantic extremes. Sparing no cost, she operated the tower at an unprecedented maximum output, forcing it to unleash its full potential!

Could Shuang Lian succeed?

When the divine power struck for the first time, Bu Quan instinctively closed her eyes in tension.

The tower's violent tremors made her stumble, nearly causing her to fall.

The shrill alarm almost pierced her eardrums.

The initial dozen seconds felt like an eternity, Bu Quan enduring each moment as if it were a year.

As she gradually adjusted to the tower's violent tremors and the maddening, piercing alarm, she summoned her courage and slowly opened her eyes.

The first person she saw was her beloved mentor.

Magician Shuang Lian wore an expression of solemnity and intense focus.

Her posture was perfectly straight, her figure commanding, the absolute anchor of the control room!

From her teacher, Bu Quan felt a powerful sense of security, drawing unexpected confidence from it.

Gradually, the alarm faded, the tower's tremors rapidly subsided, and it swiftly stabilized.

Throughout this time, the tower spirit had been overseeing critical emergency repairs.

Finally, the magnificent divine power that had swirled in the sky was completely exhausted.

The battered Honey Snow Tower crashed onto the land of the divine domain, struck by divine power.

Huge holes gaped in the topmost control room, offering a view of the open sky.

The Honey Snow Tower resembled a warrior who had fought a bloody battle, covered in wounds yet stubbornly holding its head high and refusing to fall.

Without a doubt, the Old King's Crowbar played the most crucial role!

Countering divine power with divine power, using force against force, this allowed the Honey Snow Tower to weather the storm. Had it relied solely on the mage tower's own strength, it likely wouldn't have lasted even the initial ten seconds.

Secondly, there was Shuang Lian's unwavering persistence. She refused to be intimidated by adversity, fighting back with everything she had until she narrowly escaped death.

There was also her unwavering investment in the mage tower, she never skimped on resources. It was precisely this foundation that allowed Honey Snow Tower to rush repairs and escape the brink of destruction.

Wealth was also a component of a magician's combat power.

Shuang Lian was wealthy and never hesitated to convert her riches into tangible strength.

The nature of divine power bombardment also played a role.

The divine power steadily weakened over time.

Its peak power lay in the earliest stage.

Failing to destroy the mage tower early made success in the mid-to-late stage far less likely.

“We made it!” Bu Quan cried out in disbelief, her voice trembling with joy, though traces of lingering terror still clung to her face.

Facing the divine power's assault head-on for the first time, she had felt a terror unlike anything she'd ever known.

Shuang Lian's body was actually trembling slightly, but with Bu Quan present, she maintained a facade of composure.

The gold duelists were utterly stunned!

“They actually held their ground!”

"What did Shuang Lian use? A legend level aura emerged from the mage tower!"

“They harnessed much of the divine power, so the divine strike didn't achieve its intended effect.”

"Such a blasphemous act against divine power, why doesn't our Lord punish them severely?"

“Could that conjecture be true?”

"Our Lord never truly appeared, is that it? Lady Mei Lin!"

The duelists swarmed around Mei Lin, demanding answers.

Under the scrutiny of countless gazes, Mei Lin offered no explanation.

Bao Li Gen pressed relentlessly: "Mei Lin! You were certain Shuang Lian would fall for the trap. The mage tower must have uncovered some secret of the divine domain. You must know something!"

Mei Lin nodded with a smile. "I did indeed discern some truth. Today's battle was a test to verify it. But the specific truth, I will not speak it aloud. You must all grasp it for yourselves."

“The most crucial task now is to decimate the mage tower.”

At this point, Mei Lin's expression darkened. “I fear I may have made a grave mistake!”

“I should never have lured Shuang Lian, along with the entire tower, into this trap.”

Chapter 767: A Persisting War of Attrition

Before the multitude of gold level duelists, Mei Lin openly admitted her mistake.

Gu Dong declared, “No harm done! When we unleash our true power, I refuse to believe the mage tower won't fall!”

Mo Gui Ji countered, “They used a legend level artifact, isn't that even better? I've never laid hands on such high-grade spoils before.”

Mei Lin shook her head slightly. "If I'm not mistaken, that legendary artifact is the Old King's Crowbar. We've lost the conditions for a swift victory."

“Victory is ours. that was sealed the moment the mage tower entered the divine domain.”

“But this will inevitably be a protracted war.”

"Therefore, do not reveal your identities. If she can produce a legendary artifact, she might possess communication methods capable of breaching the divine domain."

The ensuing battle proved Mei Lin's point.

The duelists' assault couldn't penetrate the defensive layers of the Honey Snow Tower.

The Old King's Crowbar remained potent, but this time it leveraged not divine power, but the duelists' attacks, even the divine domain itself.

“Our most powerful battle skills are being siphoned away, becoming tools for Shuang Lian to strike back at us!”

“The closer I get to the mage tower, the more I feel the very heavens and earth rejecting me. This is supposed to be our home turf!”

“The Old King's Crowbar... How did Shuang Lian come to possess such an artifact?”

The duelists' prolonged siege yielded no breakthrough, leaving them disheartened and restless.

None felt it worse than Yi Jiu.

He had once been stranded in the Honey snow demi-plane, fighting and fleeing endlessly, feeling the entire world conspiring against him. Now that damned feeling had returned!

The space surrounding the mage tower repelled the duelists, instead shielding the invader tower itself.

“A legend level artifact...” Mei Lin sighed.

Equipment of this caliber was exceedingly rare. Having witnessed its combat effectiveness firsthand, Mei Lin could easily guess its significance.

A piece of intelligence suddenly flashed through her mind: “In her youth, Shuang Lian successfully captured a young demi-plane. Was this true?”

“Or did she encounter a more mature demi-plane? Did she use the Old King's Crowbar to pry open its defenses and seize it?”

"Damn it! How dare she!!” Mo Gui Ji, frustrated by his failure to breach the defenses, grew increasingly enraged. He shouted accusingly, “This is blasphemy! Utter, blatant blasphemy! Why does our Lord not severely punish this blasphemer deserving of death?!"

The divine domain was the gods' home. Not only did Shuang Lian battle within it, she pried off a portion to serve her own ends.

This was nothing short of slapping the god in the face.

Yet throughout the conflict, the duelists had given it their all, while the Dueling God remained utterly silent.

This was highly unusual.

When deities suffered such humiliation, they almost always intervened to uphold their reputation.

Reputation was paramount to deities, the greater their renown, the easier their propagation. Conversely, allowing a gold level duelist like Shuang Lian to push them around like this would utterly destroy the Dueling God's reputation. Who would trust a deity too weak to eliminate the mage tower, demonstrating such feeble control over the divine domain?

This would be extremely detrimental to the Dueling God's future propagation of faith!

Yet, the Dueling God remained utterly still.

“It... seems as if He doesn't exist at all.”

Some duelists' eyes flickered, their expressions shifting between shadow and light, already grasping part of the truth.

“The will to fight has plummeted to rock bottom. Alas...” Mei Lin sighed.

Had she known Shuang Lian possessed the Old King's Crowbar, she would never have devised such a tactic.

Now, she was stuck between a rock and a hard place, forced to push forward regardless.

“A war of attrition is our best option moving forward.”

“This will be a protracted battle. I will assign each of you to maintain constant harassment and surveillance of the mage tower.”

"The Old King's Crowbar can handle mature demi-planes, but it cannot overcome the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain. Its leverage over the divine domain has limits."

“Moreover, the legend level artifact is a guaranteed energy hog!”

Mei Lin tried to rally the men.

But the other gold level members weren't fools. Immediately, someone retorted: “Give us a break.”

"Shuang Lian has her honey snow. Her demi-plane is the kingdom's largest honey snow production site."

Honey snow, a unique resource that could be consumed or used as a specialized elemental crystal to feed magic pools, converting into magic power.

Mei Lin's gaze darkened as she reiterated, “That's why I said this will be a protracted battle!”

The duelists' clash with the mage tower drew the attention of another group.

“What's happening over there?”

“It sounds like a fierce battle is underway!”

“Ridiculous! This is the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, how could any enemy possibly invade?”

This group was none other than the remnants of the Bayonet Gang.

The commotion from the divine power surge had drawn their attention.

As the divine power dissipated, the intensity of the battle plummeted, and they could no longer detect any signs of combat.

"Let's begin, Hen Fa.” Xiong Ju looked at Lan Zao, issuing a challenge. “Let me see what you've gained lately."

Lan Zao nodded and stepped into the center of the clearing.

Their sparring commenced.

Xiong Ju launched the first attack, aiming to draw Lan Zao into close combat.

But Lan Zao, the moment the sparring began, pulled back explosively, striving to create distance.

Bang, bang, bang...

As he retreated, he continuously fired bursts from his mouth.

Battle skill - Air Cannon.

Xiong Ju charged forward while taking the hits.

Each air cannon shell packed a solid punch, but the real issue was Lan Zao's increasingly precise shooting. His air cannons varied in power, mixing feints with real shots, making it impossible for Xiong Ju to react in time and significantly increasing his battle qi consumption.

Several shells slammed near Xiong Ju's feet, severely disrupting his advance.

Xiong Ju growled in frustration, momentarily unable to counter Lan Zao.

Watching the sparring match, Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer both shared the same thought: “He's gotten stronger again...”

Neither was the least bit surprised.

Since Lan Zao converted his faith, he received daily infusions of relevant experience during training.

This made wielding projectiles in combat second nature to him. Had he relied solely on his own efforts, it might have taken years to achieve such results.

Prayer, and receiving the divine blessing of the Dueling God in the Tranquil Hill Divine Domain, was an unparalleled shortcut to cultivation!

“Still, Hen Fa definitely isn't a match for Xiong Ju.”

“Xiong Ju's faith level is probably already at the pious level, right?”

Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer exchanged glances in secret.

Among everyone present, aside from Spring Boxer, the one who had made the most progress wasn't Lan Zao, but Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju's devotion to the Dueling God grew ever stronger, yielding increasingly abundant divine blessings.

“Ha! Gotcha!” Xiong Ju finally closed in on Lan Zao.

Unable to maintain distance any longer, Lan Zao had no choice but to raise his fist for a direct clash.

Xiong Ju's blow was powerful and heavy. Even in his fishman form, with bulging muscles, Lan Zao was no match.

In an instant, Lan Zao was sent flying by Xiong Ju's punch, instantly incapacitated for further combat!

“What a pity,” Spring Boxer sighed inwardly, watching Lan Zao lie defeated on the ground. “Your level of devotion, like the others, is simply too low.”

“If this continues, I'll have no choice but to leave all of you behind in Tranquil Hill.”

Chapter 768: Unaccounted For

"What the hell are you all doing? Are you weak from hunger?" Yi Jiu bellowed at the other duelists.

He was furious.

He’d assumed the Honey Snow Tower’s fall into the divine domain would make them easy prey, but then Shuang Lian produced a legend level artifact, forcing even Mei Lin to admit her mistake.

This left Yi Jiu, consumed by vengeance, with nowhere to vent his frustration. The more he fought, the more stifled he felt.

Earlier, within the demi-plane, he'd been bullied to the point of vomiting blood by Shuang Lian and Bu Quan remotely manipulating the magic formation. Now inside the divine domain, he still couldn't breach the mage tower's defenses and had taken numerous beatings.

Mo Gui Ji snorted coldly: "Of all of us, you're the only one without reservations. Go ahead, unleash your full power! Break through into the tower's core! Then we can finally breach its defenses."

Yi Jiu sneered coldly: “You think I'd be your vanguard, letting you reap the rewards?”

Yi Jiu himself was injured. Even after consuming healing potions, his combat strength was only at 80% of his peak.

He could infiltrate the alchemy workshop but lacked confidence in breaching the mage tower's inner sanctum.

Crucially, even if he succeeded, he'd face grave danger. Did the mage tower's interior lack defensive forces? That was clearly impossible.

If Yi Jiu proceeded, he alone would bear the greatest risk. The real issue lay in spoils distribution, he doubted he'd secure his fair share.

Therefore, even though Yi Jiu had exposed his identity, he had never fought at full strength.

Even Qing Hong Yan, the rabbitman who grew more frenzied the longer the battle raged, was restraining himself, suppressing his battle lust.

After all, he wasn't alone; his own rabbitman clan stood behind him.

Qing Hong Yan had dealt with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps before.

But this time, against Shuang Lian who was backed by the Alchemy Guild, it was an entirely different proposition from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

When not in a frenzy, Qing Hong Yan was quite sane.

He didn't want to bring major trouble upon his kin.

Honey Snow Tower.

In the control layer, Bu Quan exclaimed in surprise, “Teacher, these gold level fighters aren't much to speak of. They can't break through the mage tower's defenses!”

“We're safe now!”

But Shuang Lian shook her head, a faint worry on her face. “They haven't used their full strength.”

Shuang Lian's insight was far sharper and more precise than Bu Quan's.

Bu Quan froze. “Why?”

Shuang Lian gave a cold laugh. “They fear their identities being exposed.”

“How many gold level individuals are there?”

“The entire kingdom has only so many, and nearly every single gold level is documented on record.”

"They secretly worship an evil deity, formed a clandestine organization, and even attempted to eliminate me, an elder of the Alchemy Guild. They certainly wouldn't dare expose their true identities."

“I possess the Old King’s Crowbar, capable of prying open a portion of the divine domain. This terrifies them.”

“They dread the possibility that I might transmit information to the outside world, exposing them to the kingdom's pursuit!”

Having fought to this extent, Shuang Lian had also discerned the enemy's intentions.

Her gaze fixed intently on a corner of the magic projection, where Mei Lin stood.

Mei Lin concealed her features and employed commonplace spells, yet Shuang Lian sensed an uncanny familiarity in her posture.

“I've definitely seen this magician somewhere before!”

Of course, Shuang Lian had seen her.

Mei Lin enjoyed immense popularity within the kingdom. Not only was she exceptionally beautiful, but she also wielded immense power and held a position of great authority. Every public appearance she made drew attention, always commanding the spotlight.

This was the world of the strong.

Every gold level was a star, and Mei Lin was the star among stars.

Bu Quan asked, “Then, Teacher, can we send a distress signal to the outside world?”

Shuang Lian sighed. “If I could, I would have done so already. Unfortunately, it's impossible!”

“Ah!” Bu Quan gasped, stunned and disappointed.

Shuang Lian quickly encouraged her: “It's alright. Once I use the crowbar to pry open the divine domain sufficiently, I believe we'll be able to transmit a signal.”

Bu Quan hurriedly asked: “Then Teacher, when will we be able to pry open the divine domain enough to meet the standards for sending a distress signal?”

Shuang Lian shook her head slightly. “It's difficult to estimate the exact time...”

Truthfully, she herself didn't have much confidence either.

She had already made attempts, but the results had been dismal.

"This is clearly an ownerless divine domain. Why is it so incredibly difficult to pry open? At this point, we've already reached the absolute limit!"

Could it be that this divine domain possesses some unique quality?

Mei Lin scrutinized the Honey Snow Tower with intense focus, observing the divine domain itself.

“The Old King’s Crowbar lacks follow-through; the scale of the divine domain it leveraged is overall quite limited.”

“Is this deliberate suppression, or the limit of her power?”

"Our Lord is incomplete, yet His divine domain is unique. Because of Tranquil Hill's existence!"

“It's highly likely that even now, Shuang Lian hasn't succeeded in sending a distress signal.”

Observing this, Mei Lin's rising anxiety eased slightly.

If this operation ended up exposing the secrets here, Mei Lin would bear immense responsibility!

"Was my tactic too aggressive? I was too fixated on capturing Shuang Lian and Bu Quan."

“Truthfully, if we'd stopped after rescuing Yi Jiu, none of this would have happened!”

Mei Lin continued to reflect on her actions.

As an exceptional commander, self-reflection was an essential quality.

Mei Lin's tactical flaw wasn't due to poor command, who could have anticipated that Shuang Lian, a mere gold level, would wield a legend level artifact?

Any other legend item might have sufficed, but it had to be the Old King's Crowbar!

This artifact specifically countered spatial coordinates and planes.

Though the divine domain was unique, it too fell under its influence.

“Mei Lin, since you won't let us use our true strength, we can't breach Shuang Lian's mage tower anytime soon!” Mo Gui Ji stated bluntly. “If you have any other ideas, now's the time to share them.”

Mei Lin gave him a quiet glance, her tone flat. “I have no better solution.”

“We invoked divine favor for prayer, yet even that assault failed to breach the tower. Even if we exert our full strength, our chances remain slim.”

“You may use your full power. My earlier suggestion was merely a strong recommendation.”

Mo Gui Ji's expression darkened. He muttered a low curse but ultimately refrained from further action.

He fixed a final, contemptuous gaze upon the tower. "How boring! If I can't crack this turtle shell, staying here is a waste of time."

“Mei Lin, you're nothing special. You've disappointed me.”

“Let me know when you come up with a solution!”

With that, Mo Gui Ji knelt on one knee and began praying.

Then, visible ripples spread through space.

When the ripples vanished, Mo Gui Ji himself disappeared from the spot.

He had left the divine domain of Tranquil Hill.

His departure quickly triggered a chain reaction.

“We've already won. Shuang Lian can't leave the divine domain, and she must use that crowbar to maintain the defenses.”

“Yeah, I don't believe she can hold out for half a year.”

“Once her honey snow reserves are depleted, what power will the mage tower hold?”

“That's enough. Now is not the time for the final showdown.”

The duelists departed one after another.

“They're gone! Look, Master!” Bu Quan chirped excitedly.

Shuang Lian's expression remained composed: "Breaching the mage tower is meaningless without sufficient numbers. They'll leave one or two behind to monitor us. Once our magic power reserves can no longer sustain the current defenses, they'll swiftly regroup, and even bring more enemies, to surround and kill us."

Bu Quan's face paled.

Shuang Lian thought silently: Before that happens, we must save ourselves. At the very least, we must find a way to send out a distress signal.

“Too bad Da Dou didn't bring it in.”

“Perhaps he has a solution.”

Main Plane.

A deserted island.

Da Dou, frozen solid, suddenly let out a low growl. Hot flames erupted from his entire body.

The scorching fire blazed fiercely, rapidly melting the ice encasing him.

Seconds later, he shook his arms, breaking through the increasingly thin ice, and regained his freedom completely.

Bam!

He thrust his legs, shattering the sea beneath him, leaping upward before landing on the sand.

Kneeling, he gasped for breath as the flames on his body rose and fell with each breath.

Finally, the flames receded, coalescing into the Flame Totem on his body.

“What's going on?”

“Why did Shuang Lian suddenly stop!?”

Da Dou scanned the surroundings, confirming Shuang Lian had completely vanished.

“Did some unexpected change occur?”

“Or does she have some other devious scheme?”

Da Dou was utterly perplexed.

Because the duelists' ambushed Shuang Lian within her demi-plane, it was beyond Da Dou's perception.

“That was perilous indeed, but I still retained the strength for one final strike.”

"As long as she continued attacking me, the Flame Totem would sense the danger and unleash its divine power. That divine power would help me shatter the frost seal."

“Shuang Lian clearly had me under her control, yet she didn't strike immediately. Did she sense the divine power stored within the totem??”

“Regardless, I wasn't captured!”

“Nor did I lose the giant.”

“We must leave this place at once!”

Having learned his lesson, Da Dou no longer hesitated to use his Tunnel Staff.

He drew it forth immediately, casting spell after spell until every remaining use was exhausted.

Even so, he did not linger.

Instead, he activated the Flame Totem, his body transforming into a stream of fire as he soared across the sea at breakneck speed, continuing his retreat.

One day later.

Snow Bird Harbor, the docks.

“Still no word from Shuang Lian...” Tripleblade paced the ship's cabin, his face etched with anxiety and worry.

He paused at the porthole, scanning the horizon.

How he wished he could glimpse the mage tower's silhouette approaching across the sky.

But there was nothing.

Tripleblade had repeated this action countless times.

“Not only is there no word, but we've lost contact with the Honey Snow Tower altogether!”

“Something must have happened.”

“But how could that be?! That was a mage tower—a five-story mage tower, with a gold level magician like Shuang Lian!!”

“Could they have encountered a saint level foe?”

"Or perhaps Shuang Lian successfully captured Da Dou, only to discover the big guy's true potential? So they simply decided not to return?"

Chapter 769: Too Busy to Spare Time for Anything Else

According to Tripleblade's original assessment: Shuang Lian's chances of retrieving the big guy this time were quite high.

His greatest concern was the big guy's safety during the struggle.

Unexpectedly, Shuang Lian, who possessed a mage tower, had suddenly lost contact and vanished without a trace!

Just as Tripleblade paced restlessly, consumed by doubts, Men Shi knocked on the door and entered with a loud report.

"The alchemists at the workshop can't sit still anymore. They've completely lost focus on refining potions. What are we going to do?"

Men Shi brought back bad news.

The alchemists overseeing potion brewing at the workshops were almost entirely from the Honey Snow Tower, Shuang Lian's students.

Following Shuang Lian's orders, they had taken up residence in the four alchemy workshops, working tirelessly to brew the dragon rider potion.

The news of both the Honey Snow Tower and Shuang Lian vanishing together was impossible to conceal.

In fact, these alchemists were among the first to learn of it, even before Tripleblade.

Tripleblade lowered his head, silent.

“How can we get these magicians to continue brewing?”

Tripleblade realized he couldn't manage it.

He lacked the authority to mobilize sufficient funds to hire them.

Even if he wanted to spread false information to deceive these magicians, it would be difficult.

Because Tripleblade had nothing to offer that could persuade them.

“Forget it...”

Tripleblade sighed deeply within.

The situation was unclear now. If he tried to deceive these people...

Even if the deception succeeded, there would come a time when Shuang Lian, bearing the Honey Snow Tower, returned to Snow Bird Harbor. Once she learned of this matter, she would certainly make an issue of it.

Given the current strained relationship between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Shuang Lian, they weren't on such friendly terms that she'd overlook a false order.

If mishandled, this could turn potential allies into sworn enemies.

After much deliberation, Tripleblade concluded this risky maneuver was best avoided!

"Let them go. They're not part of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps; we can't control them." Tripleblade said to Men Shi.

Men Shi nodded and immediately left the cabin.

Since the battle at the alchemy workshop, Men Shi's regard for Tripleblade had grown significantly.

Despite only possessing iron level strength, the valor Tripleblade displayed in combat had greatly elevated his standing within the mercenary corps.

Consequently, Men Shi was now more willing than before to follow Tripleblade's orders.

However, Men Shi departed swiftly and returned even faster.

He brought back even worse news.

The magicians left behind by Shuang Lian in the alchemy workshop were now heading toward Snow Bird Harbor Dock, led by several silver level leaders. They sought out Tripleblade, demanding answers from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After all, their teacher had gone missing while searching for members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Upon hearing this news, Tripleblade immediately dispatched a mercenary messenger to notify Chi Lai to return as quickly as possible.

Among the alchemists left behind by Shuang Lian, there were three silver level individuals. This represented a formidable transcendent force. Tripleblade had to muster all his strength and prepare for battle!

But merely recalling Chi Lai was insufficient.

Chi Lai and Men Shi were only two silver fighters. Facing three silver level magicians, they would undoubtedly be at a disadvantage.

"I guess ‘Shi Qi’ succeeded in healing his wounds. Now, we need his return."

Currently, the role of Zong Ge was filled by an alchemy puppet, imbued with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

Yet, requesting the “Deputy Commander” to return after recovery did little to ease Tripleblade's pressure; instead, it intensified it.

For an alchemy puppet remained an alchemy puppet, lacking the agility of a living being.

The more the alchemy puppet was deployed, the greater the risk of exposure.

“If Lord Zong Ge could display his gold level cultivation...”

“At least the situation here at the branch would be much more stable.”

“There's a fundamental difference between gold level and silver level forces!”

“Lord Zong Ge has already reached gold level. He's more suited to be our leader than the commander.”

“Shuang Lian went missing, but our commander vanished even longer ago!”

“To rescue his confidant, our commander hasn't made a single move to this day.”

“This is utterly dereliction of duty!”

“And he dragged Lord Zong Ge into covering for him, forcing him to pretend to go to the Ice Sculpture Capital instead.”

As time dragged on, Tripleblade grew increasingly disappointed in the dragonman youngster, his impression of the latter steadily worsening.

Tripleblade steeled himself to face the alchemists' accusations, yet he did not neglect to compose a confidential letter detailing the branch's situation and dispatch it.

Tripleblade's letter was swiftly delivered into Zi Di's hands.

Following the deepened cooperation with the Li Jian Clan, survivors leveraged the clan's channels to achieve revolutionary progress in their mutual communications.

Zi Di effortlessly deciphered the coded message. After a quick scan, she handed it to Zong Ge beside her: “The situation has changed.”

Zong Ge's expression darkened upon reading the letter.

“Shuang Lian is a gold level magician, and this time she's deployed Honey Snow Tower.”

“That mage tower stands a full five stories tall.”

“According to intelligence we obtained from the Li Jian Clan, before leaving the royal capital, Honey Snow Tower clashed with three magicians from the Alchemy Guild.”

“Both Magician Da Bei and Cai Jing are tight-lipped about the details of that battle, clearly, they suffered losses.”

“Such combat power should be more than sufficient to handle the magic chef, Da Dou.”

“But now, both Shuang Lian and her Honey Snow Tower have gone off the grid...”

“Could it be... she discovered the big guy's innate talent?”

In this regard, Zong Ge and Tripleblade shared the same concern.

For now, the big guy's bloodline hadn't been precisely tested. But the survivors knew his power ceiling was at least saint level.

That alone was enough to make Shuang Lian change her stance, capture big guy privately, and make a clean getaway.

Zi Di didn't pursue the topic further.

She glanced at Zong Ge: “In the secret letter, Tripleblade proposed an idea. He suggested you directly reveal your gold level cultivation. What are your thoughts?”

Zong Ge shook his head slightly.

“I'm currently playing the role of the commander. Even if I exposed my gold level cultivation, it wouldn't provide immediate help in our current situation.”

“The enemy is so powerful. Shuang Lian and her mage tower are likely under attack.”

“And I believe that possibility is far greater than Shuang Lian discovering the big guy's talent.”

"It's simply not enough for just me or the commander to advance to gold level. It holds no deterrent for our enemies whatsoever."

“It would be better to keep this situation hidden, as a small trump card to play later.”

“In truth, we aren't lacking deterrence, are we?”

Zong Ge was naturally referring to his feat days prior: single-handedly defeating three gold level opponents, Jia Bing, the Axe Gang leader, and Lin, in rapid succession.

This achievement had genuinely shocked the duelists. It had bought Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group ample time and breathing room.

Otherwise, they would never have had the chance to negotiate in Igloo City.

And the outcome of those negotiations had secured them even more time and space.

Zi Di mused, "Tripleblade wrote in his letter: Shuang Lian's students can't be relied upon. Given that, we might as well shut down the alchemy workshop at Snow Bird Harbor."

“Under our new cooperation agreement with the Li Jian Clan, we can fully utilize their production lines to mass-produce dragon rider potions.”

When Zi Di negotiated with Mian Li Zang earlier, she hadn't known Shuang Lian would arrive.

Therefore, the condition of leveraging the Li Jian Clan's potion production capabilities was explicitly written into the cooperation contract between the two parties.

Zi Di had even planned for the mercenary company to secretly establish an alchemy workshop.

After Shuang Lian's arrival, this collaboration had yet to formally commence. With the four alchemy workshops ready to restart operations, there was no need to partner with the Li Jian Clan in this regard.

Proceeding with this collaboration would allow the Li Jian Clan to claim a share of the profits from dragon rider potions. They wouldn't work for free.

For the survivors, this meant reduced profits.

But given the current circumstances, a smaller share was negligible. Capturing this niche potion market segment was paramount.

Achieving this would mean Zi Di's business plan was more than halfway to success.

The survivors wouldn't lose out either.

Leveraging the captured distribution channels and War Merchant’s endless database, they were certain to achieve even greater commercial success.

And splitting a portion of the profits with the Li Jian Clan was well worth it.

This way, the survivors could use the Li Jian Clan as a buffer. If the duelists wanted to continue targeting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they'd have to go after the Li Jian Clan's alchemy workshop.

This would undoubtedly divert the majority of hostile fire away from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

By doing this, Zi Di indirectly protected the Snow Bird Harbor branch.

A thoughtful expression crossed her face: "Magician Shuang Lian is a powerful elder within the Alchemy Guild. She is currently missing. Could we leverage the power of the Alchemy Guild?"

Zi Di had previously sought the help of Shuang Lian through Bu Quan. Now that both Shuang Lian and Bu Quan were missing, she sought to repeat the tactic to harness the Alchemy Guild's strength.

Moreover, Zi Di had already handed over crucial intelligence regarding the mysterious faction to the Li Jian Clan. This information would be relayed upward through the Li Jian Clan's channels.

Both Zi Di and Zong Ge eagerly anticipated the Ice Sculpture Royal Family's next move.

For now, the strength displayed by the duelists far surpassed anything the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could muster.

Given this situation, both Zi Di and Zong Ge favored seeking external support wherever possible. The more external forces they could enlist, the less risk and pressure they would bear.

“We must press forward.”

"The store setups are already three-quarters complete. With the Spatial Shuttle Squad's assistance, we've saved immense distance and time."

Zi Di conveyed her assessment to Zong Ge.

The disappearances of Shuang Lian, Bu Quan, and the big guy, she could no longer concern herself with them.

This had long surpassed her limits.

Zi Di's choice revealed profound helplessness.

It was a choice born of necessity.

Yet Zi Di had no better solution.

Neither did Zong Ge.

The half-beastman showed no hesitation or deliberation, simply nodding in agreement to Zi Di's subsequent arrangements.

Chapter 770: Gold Level Fishman Youngster!

One day ago, in the divine domain of Tranquil Hill.

Most of the duelists had already departed.

“Sister Mei Lin, you sought me out?” Xue Tuan Zi approached Mei Lin.

Mei Lin handed her the material: “I heard you've been aggressively acquiring this type of material lately?”

Xue Tuan Zi took it and saw it was a shell from the Tide-Sound Conch. Each shell was a gold level material!

A flash of delight crossed Xue Tuan Zi's face, but it quickly faded into a troubled frown.

She looked at Mei Lin: “Sister Mei Lin, I'm sorry, but I don't have much time to stay here. I need to give my all for this Warm Snow Cup!”

Mei Lin nodded with a smile. “I understand. Your grandfather has high expectations for you.”

“Don't worry, I'm not assigning you to guard the Honey Snow Tower. I have another matter to ask of you.”

“Oh?” Xue Tuan Zi's eyes sparkled with curiosity. “Sister Mei Lin, tell me about it.”

Mei Lin said, “I wish to pay a personal visit to your grandfather.”

Xue Tuan Zi's grandfather was no ordinary transcendent. He was a Gold level alchemist who presided over the Snow Cake Tower and served as a powerful elder within the Alchemy Guild.

More crucially, relations between the Snow Cake Tower Master and the Honey Snow Tower Master, Shuang Lian, were strained.

Xue Tuan Zi paused slightly before grasping Mei Lin's intent.

They had lured the Honey Snow Tower into the divine domain of Tranquil Hill. As a pivotal figure in the Alchemy Guild, Shuang Lian's disappearance would inevitably trigger far-reaching repercussions.

The Alchemy Guild would undoubtedly come searching for her.

If this battle concluded decisively, Shuang Lian's death in combat might be acceptable. But the reality was that both sides were locked in a stalemate.

Without a definitive outcome, Mei Lin could consider the subsequent repercussions.

Thinking this through, Xue Tuan Zi nodded. "That's exactly right. Grandfather and Magician Shuang Lian have deep-seated conflicts. The rivalry between the two mage towers is significant, their relationship is quite hostile, to say the least."

“I can introduce you.”

“That shouldn't be a problem.”

Xue Tuan Zi welcomed the prospect of Shuang Lian being dealt with more effectively.

After all, she had become involved in this conflict herself.

In truth, it was precisely the animosity between the two towers that had motivated Xue Tuan Zi to contribute her strength to this battle.

“That's wonderful,” Mei Lin smiled.

She felt confident she could count on Xue Tuan Zi's continued support.

Mei Lin had made considerable efforts to expand her faction's influence within the divine domain of Tranquil Hill. Growing close to Xue Tuan Zi and becoming her friend was one such achievement.

After arranging the rotation of gold level duelists, Mei Lin departed the divine domain of Tranquil Hill alongside Xue Tuan Zi.

Half a day later, they arrived at the Ice Sculpture Capital via a teleportation formation.

The headquarters of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild resided within the capital.

The third position in the Honey Snow Tower remained vacant.

When Mei Lin arrived, the Alchemy Guild had yet to formally confirm Shuang Lian's disappearance.

She entered the Snow Cake Tower and was received by its master.

“Try some, Rear Admiral Mei Lin. This is a snow cake I made myself,” Xue Tuan Zi's grandfather warmly offered Mei Lin.

Though they had never formally interacted before, both had heard of each other.

Gold level individuals were neither numerous nor scarce.

As the master of a mage tower, Xue Tuan Zi's grandfather undoubtedly ranked among the elite of gold level magicians. Mei Lin, needless to say, was a beauty of the navy—a star among the gold level individuals.

Mei Lin knew the Snow Cake Tower Master was not only an alchemist but also moonlighted as a magic chef.

His greatest passion lay in crafting transcendent cuisine. His handmade snow cakes enjoyed a modest reputation throughout the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Having come with a request, Mei Lin naturally wouldn't refuse such kindness.

She extended her slender fingers, pinched up a snow cake, and examined it closely.

The snow cake wasn't thin; it was round, plump, and adorable. Frost flowers adorned both sides, releasing a sweet, fragrant aroma.

Mei Lin placed the snow cake in her mouth and took a gentle bite.

Its texture was delightfully crisp, no effort was needed to crunch it effortlessly.

As she chewed, the cake dissolved on her tongue, releasing an intoxicating cheese flavor. Following the rich cheese came an icy chill that made Mei Lin's mouth water.

The peak of that icy sensation sent a shiver through her very soul.

Mei Lin, with her high status and influence, had tasted countless delicacies. To be fair, while the snow cake was tasty, it wasn't exceptionally delicious. Among the delicacies she had tasted, it wouldn't even rank in the top fifty.

At best, it was a tasty dessert.

Yet after finishing one piece, Mei Lin's expression subtly shifted.

She consumed a second, then a third.

The Snow Cake Tower Master chuckled heartily.

After eating five pieces, Mei Lin thanked the tower master: “Your culinary skills are truly remarkable, tower master. I am deeply impressed.”

“I never imagined there could be snow cakes in this world that directly raise one's spirit power limit after eating them!”

The Snow Cake Tower Master smiled: "Rear Admiral Mei Lin, you are a dear friend of my granddaughter. Of course I must serve you my finest creations. I heard from Xue Tuan Zi that you also disapprove of Shuang Lian?"

Xue Tuan Zi had participated in the introduction, naturally informing her grandfather of certain matters beforehand.

The Snow Cake Tower Master had long been at a disadvantage in his rivalry with Shuang Lian.

Mei Lin was undoubtedly a powerful external ally, which was why he spared no expense, specially crafting the snow cakes at short notice to entertain her.

She said, "Tower Master, you might not know this yet. Shuang Lian has gone missing, and the Honey Snow Tower has vanished without a trace."

"Oh?!" The Snow Cake Tower Master's expression shifted slightly, his eyes flashing with sharpness. “Are you certain this is true?”

“Within half a day, the guild will receive the news as well.” Mei Lin continued, ”I don't care about the reason for her disappearance. What I want is for the Alchemy Guild to temporarily suspend the search and rescue efforts."

The Snow Cake Tower Master gave Mei Lin a deep look: “That's not so easy to arrange.”

“Shuang Lian's status is no ordinary matter. She's a powerful elder. Haven't you seen that even though the Snowflake Tower is gone, Pit No.3 has always been reserved for her?”

"You must understand, the lower the pit number, the greater the energy supply it provides to the mage tower. In a way, it represents the Tower Master's standing within the guild!"

“When such a heavyweight goes missing, the guild will go mad searching for her."

Mei Lin smiled: “After Pit No.3, Pit No.4 belongs to Snow Cake Tower, doesn't it?”

“Others might be helpless, but I believe in your influence, Tower Master.”

With that, Mei Lin handed the Snow Cake Tower Master a list. The list contained only a few items, but each was substantial enough to make the Snow Cake Tower Master's expression shift slightly upon seeing it.

The final item on the list was none other than a saint level alchemy material!

“Interesting...” Snow Cake Tower Master tucked away the list. “You've got good luck.”

“Under normal circumstances, this would be nearly impossible.”

“But now it is different. The entire Alchemy Guild's focus is on Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's inheritance.”

“The Alchemy Guild will inevitably dispatch personnel for investigation and rescue, I cannot prevent that.”

“But we can influence things to weaken the search team's strength.”

“I have an excellent idea.”

The Snow Cake Tower Master detailed his plan.

Mei Lin listened and let out a sigh of admiration.

The Snow Cake Tower Master knew that Da Bei, Cai Jing, and others had suffered losses at the hands of Shuang Lian. And now they were the leaders of the guild's organized inheritance group.

The Snow Cake Tower Master intended to exploit them by first transferring all alchemists skilled in investigation and search operations to the inheritance group.

This way, there would be no experts left to search for Shuang Lian, Bu Quan, and the others.

“Then, the task falls to you, Tower Master.” Mei Lin “backed out at the last moment,” avoiding contact with Cai Jing and Da Bei.

That very evening, news of Shuang Lian's disappearance had already been relayed.

The Snow Cake Tower Master invited Cai Jing and Da Bei to a banquet.

His snow cakes earned rave reviews from both guests. Da Bei even packed several slices to take home for Zhong Bei.

Neither Cai Jing nor Da Bei harbored any suspicion about the Tower Master's proposal.

After all, the feud between the two tower masters was common knowledge throughout the guild.

Both Cai Jing and Da Bei were eager to take on this task. Having suffered losses at Shuang Lian's hands, they saw this as their chance for revenge.

Upon hearing news of Shuang Lian's disappearance, they had already begun to stir with anticipation.

The Snow Cake Tower Master's invitation dispelled many of their reservations. Their actions signaled an alignment with Snow Cake Tower Master. Should Shuang Lian retaliate in the future, Snow Cake Tower Master would surely offer them refuge.

White Hair Trench.

Under the sea.

In a crude shack, Cang Xu knelt in prayer.

“Great Charming Blue Goddess, I praise Your greatness and mercy.”

“You are so mighty that when You rage, the seas tremble; when You show mercy, You grant us boundless care.”

“My Lord, oh Charming Blue Goddess, I look to Your power and trust in Your salvation.”

“I wish to ask: how much divine favor must I expend to elevate my life level?”

“To become silver, rising above iron and bronze?”

Since this prayer itself consumed several points of divine favor, Cang Xu received the answer swiftly—8,800!

To attain silver level, he had to expend 8,800 units of divine favor.

Cang Xu paled.

Ever since the fishman youngster's transcendent level ascension, Cang Xu had harbored this ambition. He never imagined advancing from iron level to silver level would require so much divine favor!

How long would it take to accumulate that much?

“With my current concentration of Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline, perhaps self-cultivation could accelerate my ascent to silver level.”

Cang Xu was only pretending to be silver level; his true level remained iron.

Meanwhile, aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster stood with solemn concentration.

Having carefully prepared his prayer, he began his invocation.

“Great Charming Blue Goddess, my Lord, I...”

Boom!

Divine radiance descended from the void, bathing the fishman youngster in its glow for a long moment.

Soon after, the fishman youngster opened the hatch, his expression oddly twisted.

“I barely started praying, I didn't even finish a sentence, yet I already received divine blessing.”

“I... just advanced to gold level?”

The fishman youngster felt as if he were in a dream.

This prayer had ended almost as soon as it began.

The life level of the fishman youngster officially ascended from silver level to gold level!

“My divine favor has surged to 8000!”

“I thought this prayer would drain it completely... yet it actually increased by so much...”

Chapter 771: Nurture

A single blasphemous prayer propelled the fishman youngster's life rank to gold level.

He attempted to summon his battle qi and discovered it now radiated a golden glow.

“This means I've caught up to Zong Ge.”

The fishman youngster had been away from Snow Bird Harbor for some time, unaware that Zong Ge had advanced to gold level fighter during his absence.

“No, more accurately, I've surpassed him. After all, my life essence is already gold level.”

“From here on, I won't even need to cultivate actively. Both fighter and magician levels will gradually undergo qualitative changes, ultimately advancing to gold level.”

The cultivation of clergymen differed from that of fighters and magicians; the enhancement of divine power required the blessing of the gods.

“However, if I actively cultivate, the advancement speed for my fighter and magician cultivation will be much faster!”

Having reached the next tier in life essence provides tremendous support for the cultivation of battle qi and magic.

“Of course, I have an even faster way to bring all three transcendent paths to the gold level.”

“That is—prayer!”

By now, the fishman youngster was certain his blasphemous prayers to the Charming Blue Goddess would yield terrifyingly immense rewards.

“For every gain, there is a loss...” Cang Xu's warning still echoed in the youngster's ears.

It was a lingering concern he couldn't ignore.

Yet this unease could not halt the fishman youngster's resolve to press forward.

In truth, he found himself unable to stop.

Even before, he would pray within his dreams.

Even after purging the fabricated memories, this phenomenon persisted.

“My life level has risen to gold level, time to digest it again.”

“I have a strong premonition that Charming Blue Goddess' divine blessings will continue to descend through my dream prayers.”

“And those blessings will still focus on enhancing my fishman bloodline, not further raising my level.”

Over the past few days, the fishman youngster's Surging Wave bloodline concentration had reached 57%.

“Breaking 60% will trigger a qualitative leap, granting me the potential to reach the saint level!”

The fishman youngster harbored immense anticipation for this breakthrough.

“8,000 divine favor... That's far too much.”

“I must utilize it more effectively!”

Cang Xu teleported away but remained connected to the fishman youngster through alchemy devices.

Based on intelligence Cang Xu gathered from Zhou Zhang and others, the fishman youngster was certain the divine favor within him represented a massive asset.

“I don't need all this divine favor myself. I can give some to Cang Xu to use.”

“Though divine favor can't be traded, I can arrange for Cang Xu to pray alongside me using specific invocations, letting him share in the benefits.”

Cang Xu's investigations revealed numerous successful precedents for such prayers.

A streak of fire streaked across the ocean.

Hovering above a sea reef, the flame descended and coalesced into a giant.

The giant, clad in a Flame Totem, was none other than Da Dou.

Ever since Shuang Lian caught up to him and inexplicably, incomprehensibly, let him off the hook.

Da Dou had grown intensely wary and tense.

Fearing Shuang Lian might track him down again, he used the Tunnel Staff until it was worn out, then fled relentlessly, journeying day and night without rest.

Breathing heavily, his face etched with exhaustion, Da Dou stood on the reef, scanning the horizon.

After a brief reconnaissance, he quickly determined that this sea reef only surfaced for a few hours each day, submerged beneath the waves for the majority of the time.

“The seawater can conceal my tracks to some extent.”

Da Dou felt a flicker of relief and began cultivating his battle qi technique.

He swiftly drew upon his battle qi, replenishing his reserves considerably.

Then, he performed his daily prayers, restoring some of his divine power.

Flying over the sea using his Flame Totem consumed not only his battle qi but also divine power from the Fire God.

Prayer demanded sincere devotion, while cultivating battle qi left Da Dou increasingly exhausted.

Yet he did not rest.

Instead, dragging his weary body, he retrieved his silver level iron wok.

He tapped his gold level storage bag, releasing the big guy.

The big guy lay motionless, eyes tightly shut, breathing naturally, still unconscious.

Da Dou looked at him, a smile spreading across his face. The aching muscles throughout his body seemed less burdensome.

Da Dou tapped the storage bag again. Flames shot out, landing on the reef and revealing a sack of rice.

This was gold level rice, each grain as large as a fist. It bore the peculiar name: How Many Floor Rice. (Note: Name sounds like Kanjiru, which is part of a meme involving "Itami Kanjiru" which sounds like "How many floors to bring up this rice?")

Its name was strange, and its effects were peculiar. Simply eating this rice would cause the eater profound suffering. Pain so intense it brought tears, unbearable agony!

Its yield was extremely limited, making it a truly unique ingredient.

On ordinary days, Da Dou wouldn't even treat himself to it, but now, for the sake of the big guy, he brought it out without hesitation.

He scooped a generous ladleful of rice into the iron wok and began stir-frying.

Fortunately, his iron wok possessed the ability to expand its capacity, otherwise it wouldn't have held such a large quantity of rice.

After stir-frying for a short while, Da Dou sprinkled stretchy cheese over the mixture.

This cheese emitted an intense, rich aroma. When pulled into strands, it formed beautiful stretchy patterns, making it a gold level ingredient.

After adding the cheese, Da Dou stopped stir-frying the rice and instead reduced the heat to simmer it.

Midway through cooking, Da Dou retrieved a dozen vibrantly colored mushrooms.

These mushrooms were called adventure mushrooms.

They grew clustered together, requiring simultaneous harvesting, picking just a portion would cause the entire cluster to instantly wither.

The peculiarity of these mushrooms lay in their random toxicity and medicinal properties.

A red mushroom in one cluster might taste like chili pepper, harmless. But a red mushroom in another cluster could be a bomb, one bite, and it explodes in your mouth!

A large cluster of adventure mushrooms went into the iron wok.

Soon, the aroma of cooking rice filled the air. When Da Dou finally lifted the lid, a cloud of white steam erupted, startling the big guy awake.

The aroma was overwhelming.

The big guy instinctively opened his mouth, drool streaming down his chin.

“Hold on a sec, it's not quite done yet,” Da Dou said with a laugh, noticing the big guy's intense stare at the rice.

Battle skill - Food Compression!

Gold level battle qi enveloped the entire pot of rice, then violently compressed it.

The intense gold level aura within the pot was forcibly compressed down to silver level.

“That should do it.”

“This is called brave baked rice. Downgrading it makes it the perfect dish for you.”

“Eat up, eat up. It's all yours.” Da Dou chuckled, reaching to hand it to the big guy.

But the big guy's expression changed. A memory surfaced, and he roared, lunging at Da Dou.

Da Dou sighed, forced to block the attack.

The big guy's transcendent level was lower than his, and his combat skills were laughable. Da Dou handled him effortlessly, one hand still holding the iron wok. Without exerting much force, the big guy collapsed from exhaustion.

He was worn out.

Earlier, he'd struggled to swim, expending a lot of energy and swallowing seawater.

Seeing he still couldn't defeat Da Dou, the big guy turned around again, plunged back into the water with a splash, and repeated his earlier actions, jumping into the sea to search for his father once more.

Da Dou sighed, punching out a ball of flame that struck the big guy squarely in the back, knocking him unconscious.

The flames enveloped the big guy, lifting him out of the sea and tossing him to Da Dou's feet.

Da Dou looked at the big guy, his eyes tightly shut, stubborn as ever, and couldn't help but frown.

“What am I supposed to do now?”

As Da Dou pondered solutions, he watched the unconscious giant open his mouth. Saliva began flowing toward the iron wok in Da Dou's hand.

Da Dou froze, then beamed with delight. “What a powerful bodily instinct!”

“Even unconscious, his body's instincts still drive him to eat ceaselessly, frantically absorbing nutrients to fuel his growth!”

“Is this the power of a saint level bloodline? It's incredible!”

Da Dou flipped his wrist, pouring out a portion of the brave baked rice.

The big guy's mouth filled with rice, and he immediately began chewing.

After a few bites, his Adam's apple bobbed as he swallowed the rice whole.

Then, his mouth opened again.

“He's practically starving.”

“Heh, kid, looks like I'm your new babysitter.”

Da Dou chuckled happily and continued feeding him.

After devouring most of the baked rice, the giant's body absorbed the nutrients and regained strength.

Suddenly, his eyes snapped open. Spotting Da Dou crouched beside him, he immediately swung a punch.

“Don't hit this pot of rice!” Caught off guard, Da Dou shielded the remaining baked rice. The giant's fist struck his right eye, turning it purple in an instant.

Da Dou leapt back, instantly creating distance between himself and the giant.

The big guy rose to his feet and gave chase.

“You little brat...” Da Dou fumed, “You ate my food and now you hit me!”

“I didn't eat it!”

“You're bad! You're the enemy!”

The big guy roared with righteous indignation, unleashing a halo.

Centered on him, the aura amplified his courage and slightly boosted his combat power.

Da Dou gasped, “Brave Aura?”

Though consuming the brave baked rice did carry a chance to generate a Brave Aura, Da Dou knew that after his compression, the probability of that happening was extremely low.

“Unless the consumer has truly exceptional digestive capabilities!”

“This guy, heh heh, your bloodline is top-notch! To digest it to this extent, you haven't wasted my meal!”

“Oh dear.”

Da Dou chuckled for a moment before the big guy caught up.

He sparred with the big guy, taking blows without retaliating, letting the big guy fight to his heart's content.

“Work it out, that's the way.”

“The more you expend, the more you'll digest and absorb!”

To boost the big guy's morale, fearing he might see the gap and jump back into the sea, Da Dou deliberately stopped defending this time.

Soon, his face was a bruised mess.

Seeing hope of defeating a formidable foe, the big guy fought with growing ferocity.

Even with his battered face, Da Dou beamed with delight.

Snow Bird Harbor Dock.

Ci Hai Shen visited the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch alone.

“Lord Ci Hai Shen, you're looking for me?” Tripleblade asked in surprise.

Ci Hai Shen fixed him with a steady gaze and stated his purpose plainly: “I've come to nurture you.”

“I reviewed the magic recordings. Your blade work during the alchemy workshop battle was exceptional.”

“Would you be interested in serving as my assistant? To accompany me to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's grand ceremony?”

Chapter 772: The Situation Develops

Due to Da Dou's destruction, Ci Hai Shen's plan to select assistants through the tasting event had failed.

However, during the battle where Yi Jiu and the other sacrificial warriors attacked the city's alchemy workshop, Ci Hai Shen unexpectedly discovered Tripleblade's talent.

At the time, Ci Hai Shen was still engaged in the eating contest. It was only after reviewing the magic footage of the battle that he learned of this.

“Who is that short human?”

“His sword technique is precise, swift, and accurate. He could barely qualify as my assistant to help process rare ingredients.”

Faced with Ci Hai Shen's recruitment offer, Tripleblade pondered for a long time before politely declining.

Ci Hai Shen initially assumed Tripleblade was playing hard to get and raised his offer.

Tripleblade's declaration of his true intentions left Ci Hai Shen utterly perplexed!

Having personally come to recruit him, Ci Hai Shen had naturally gathered some intelligence on Tripleblade.

He knew clearly: Tripleblade held no prominent position within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, merely an iron level.

Now, faced with an invitation from a gold level, he had actually refused!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn't even have a single true gold level member. Within the group, Tripleblade would have to participate in dangerous battles.

But if he became Ci Hai Shen's assistant, he'd only need to chop vegetables and prepare ingredients to earn far more than his current wages. Not only would it be safe, but also easy and pleasant.

Yet Tripleblade had turned down such an opportunity!

Rejected, Ci Hai Shen felt his face burn with shame. With a hint of irritation, he promptly departed Snow Bird Harbor's docks.

Intelligence regarding this incident swiftly reached Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The city lord pondered thoughtfully, his mind drifting back to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' arms smuggling background, “It appears their backers aren't confined to high-level operatives, they've also infiltrated mid-level positions, demonstrating significant control over this mercenary group.”

“Di Bi's true allegiance lies not with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps itself, but with the mysterious arms dealer behind them.”

“That explains his extraordinary loyalty!”

“I find this arms dealer increasingly intriguing.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord remained unaware that the primary reason for rejecting Ci Hai Shen's invitation lay in Tripleblade's own personal resolve.

“Had this been before the Mystifying Monster Island incident, I likely would have accepted Ci Hai Shen's offer.”

“But now, I have resolved to follow Lord Zong Ge!”

“Even if Lord Zong Ge hasn't ascended to gold level yet, he remains more worthy of my allegiance than Ci Hai Shen.”

Tripleblade's loyalty to Zong Ge stemmed not only from Zong Ge saving his life, but also because Tripleblade had pinned his life's dreams upon Zong Ge.

"The big guy is still lost."

“Shuang Lian, Bu Quan, even Honey Snow Tower have vanished.”

“But our business plan continues.”

“All thanks to Lord Zong Ge's efforts, and of course, Zi Di's.”

“The Li Jian Clan has taken over potion production; we no longer need to manufacture them here.”

“And according to their schedule, they should be nearing the royal capital soon, right?”

Within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' business strategy, Zi Di and Zong Ge's entry into the royal capital held extraordinary significance.

It signaled the preliminary completion of their shop network and the successful establishment of sales channels. This laid the foundational platform for their upcoming grand expansion.

Not only Tripleblade was monitoring Zong Ge and Zi Di's progress; Mei Lin was watching as well.

Despite their recent victories over Shuang Lian and Honey Snow Tower, Mei Lin showed no signs of relaxation or complacency.

“The tactic of attacking the enemy's weak points can no longer be sustained,” Mei Lin assessed the situation.

The Snow Bird Harbor raid she personally orchestrated had destroyed four alchemy workshops, achieving its objective.

However, Shuang Lian's subsequent reinforcements, bolstered by the Honey Snow Tower's formidable resources, swiftly rebuilt all four workshops. This rendered the sacrifices of the sacrificial warriors almost laughable.

It meant Mei Lin had traded the lives of her sacrificial warriors for a mere portion of the enemy's resources.

Such a costly exchange and outcome were not something Mei Lin was willing to bear.

“Besides, the Li Jian Clan now handles production of the dragon rider potions.”

Mei Lin had no intention of continuing raids on potion production facilities.

The Li Jian Clan ranked among the kingdom's top three clans. Targeting them would cause immense repercussions, creating complications too difficult to manage.

“What price did the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps pay to persuade the Li Jian Clan to set aside their pride and engage in such deep cooperation?”

Mei Lin was deeply curious.

Unfortunately, even she found it difficult to uncover intelligence on this matter.

The only participants in that negotiation were Zi Di, Zong Ge, and Mian Li Zang.

“Even without targeting the Li Jian Clan, we can still thwart the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' plans.”

Mei Lin had already envisioned two approaches.

The first was to continue leveraging the Alchemy Guild.

Earlier, Mei Lin had visited the Snow Cake Tower Master, successfully curbing the Guild's search efforts for Shuang Lian.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' attempt to monopolize the dragon rider potion market directly infringes upon the Alchemy Guild's interests.”

"The two sides are natural adversaries. However, the Alchemy Guild suffers from bloated personnel, a cumbersome structure, and rampant corruption, rendering it sluggish. At present, its response in the potion market lags behind even the Jing Xiang Clan."

The Jing Xiang Clan represented the second avenue.

Thus far, this family has shouldered the primary task of blocking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' commercial advance.

Though they haven't been completely stopped, they have certainly slowed the enemy's progress.

“Long Fu, Yao Ma, and their cohorts are nearly entering the royal capital.”

"Their strategy is indeed clever, overlaying operations on top of the Li Jian Clan's existing shops. This makes it extremely difficult for us to take action against these establishments."

"Their true weakness lies with Long Fu. Of course, that's also the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' greatest strength."

“To truly crush them, we must uncover how Long Fu single-handedly defeated three opponents, swiftly eliminating Jia Bing and the others without giving them a chance to fight back.”

“The other gold level members who know the truth refuse to confront Long Fu. I won't sacrifice lives for intelligence either.”

“Hua Tang... he's the ideal candidate for reconnaissance.”

“This guy vanished without a trace after setting sail from Snow Bird Harbor.”

“Where the hell did he end up?”

Achoo!

Hua Tang sneezed violently.

He found himself in a dimly lit cave.

“Keep it down!” Other transcendents nearby glared at Hua Tang in unison.

Hua Tang raised his arm and laughed heartily. “Sorry, I think... I've caught a cold!”
