“Don't speak so loudly!” The transcendents opened their mouths, silently shouting through lip movements.

But it was too late.

A shrill crow suddenly pierced the air.

The transcendents all wore horrified expressions and scattered in a panic.

“It's coming!”

“Run, quick!”

“Eat that guy instead, don't eat me!”

The transcendents fled frantically.

The caves here branched off in countless directions, forming a complex network of passages.

A colossal figure suddenly darted out, moving so swiftly it left afterimages on their retinas.

The massive form abruptly halted, snatching a transcendent in its jaws.

Simultaneously, it became visible to ordinary eyes.

It was a gigantic python, its body at least three meters in diameter. Its head resembled a rooster's, covered in fluffy blue-green feathers.

A massive comb, transitioning from red to yellow, stood erect atop the head, fiery and vivid.

The python's scales were black, interspersed with yellow markings.

The unfortunate transcendent, seized by the beak, froze instantly. In the blink of an eye, he was poisoned to death.

A Cockscomb Ration Python.

Saint level!

Hua Tang pressed his back flat against the cave wall, his breath utterly still.

Chapter 773: Hua Tang – Long Fu Killed My Friend?!

In the damp, dim cave, the Cockscomb Ration Python Snake's plump python body slithered before Hua Tang's eyes.

The distance between them was extremely close, the snake's scales nearly brushing Hua Tang's face.

Hua Tang held his breath, motionless.

After several seconds, the Cockscomb Ration Python finally slithered past him. Its plump tail appeared even more bloated than its body, clearly brimming with fat.

Hua Tang exhaled a cloud of stale breath.

As a gold level thief, his expertise lay in intelligence gathering and theft, not combat, certainly not confronting a magic beast like the Cockscomb Ration Python head-on.

Currently disguised as an unremarkable silver level transcendent, he had been exploring this cavern for roughly a month.

“Why can't I find the Thief Saint's legacy?”

“Where on earth is it hidden?”

After obtaining crucial intelligence from Teng Dong Lang, Hua Tang had set sail. Following a long journey, he arrived at a deserted island.

From the caves within this barren island, the piercing crow of roosters frequently echoed.

This peculiar phenomenon drew numerous adventurers and treasure hunters, Hua Tang merely one among them.

“The Thief Saint's hoard lies within the Rooster Crow Cave.”

“This is the closest I've come to the Rooster Crow Cave in all my years of searching.”

“But why can't I find the Thief Saint's legacy?”

“Not only can't I find it, but there's not even a single clue?”

Hua Tang watched the python's tail vanish into the shadows ahead, confirming the Cockscomb Ration Python had completely departed. Only then did he exhale a breath of stale air and peel his back off the damp cave wall.

His expression was grim.

Though the Cockscomb Ration Python possessed a saint level power, deceiving such a beast was no great feat for Hua Tang with his skills.

Not a trace of satisfaction graced his face.

He cared more about the Thief Saint's legacy, yet beyond the Cockscomb Ration Python, he held no other leads.

“This cave's terrain is complex, but I'm certain I've explored every inch.”

“Could it be that the true clues only reveal themselves when the cavern floods?” Hua Tang mused inwardly.

For the majority of the day, the cavern remained submerged in seawater. During the midnight high tide, the entire cavern was even completely submerged.

The only part Hua Tang hadn't explored was likely the cavern when it was flooded by seawater.

Just as he prepared to shift his search direction, the sound of fierce combat suddenly erupted from another area.

Hua Tang immediately activated his battle skill to sharpen his hearing.

He heard the agonized screams of transcendents, the whistling of cutting weapons swung at breakneck speed, and the shrieks of the Cockscomb Ration Python.

Hua Tang froze in shock: “There's a gold level transcendent here. Their aura is sharp and overwhelming. Who could it be? Actually holding their own against a Cockscomb Ration Python?"

Hua Tang instantly concluded that any gold level being here was extraordinary.

“But it's none of my business...” Hua Tang prepared to retreat, creating distance between himself and the battle.

Yet at that moment, the sounds of combat rapidly drew nearer instead.

Confused, Hua Tang immediately activated his battle skill, leaving an illusionary double behind.

His true form slipped away, while the illusion retained his previous aura, serving as bait.

A dozen seconds later, the charging gold level arrived.

Spotting the bait Hua Tang had left behind, his eyes flashed with sharpness.

Without hesitation, he chose a direction and charged into a side passage, precisely the route Hua Tang’s true form had taken.

Along the way, he encountered numerous transcendents, each one swiftly dispatched.

“That gold level is chasing me?”

“He's got a killer instinct; he's taken out all the other transcendents!”

“Hmm, wait...”

Hua Tang sensed something, a peculiar expression flickering across his face. Instead of continuing his escape, he halted and remained rooted to the spot.

Before long, the gold level pursued him from behind.

This gold level bore a wide white feather cloak, a bronze beast-face mask over his head, and wielded a scythe with a shaft nearly two meters long. The scythe's blade gleamed like a mirror, flashing a sharp, icy glint.

Hua Tang blurted out, “Feng Lian, it really is you!”

Feng Lian ignored him completely. With a light flick of his scythe, he unleashed a wind blade.

A gold level wind blade possessed formidable power, but its most terrifying aspect was its speed, so fast it was nearly impossible for ordinary people to react.

Hua Tang dodged it effortlessly, simultaneously shedding his disguise.

He wore a white robe adorned with intricate, vibrant floral patterns. His face was concealed by a silver-white fox mask, revealing only his pink pupils.

“Hold your attack, it's me!” Hua Tang shouted at Feng Lian.

Feng Lian had already withdrawn his blade before Hua Tang made a move.

The golden wind blade sliced through empty air.

Feng Lian's hoarse voice echoed: “Hua Tang, I've finally found you.”

“You came looking for me?” Hua Tang paused in surprise.

Feng Lian stated his purpose bluntly: “I need that Eye of Concealment you possess. I need it urgently!"

Hua Tang retrieved a small seal from his robe and tossed it to Feng Lian. “Here.”

Watching Feng Lian catch it, Hua Tang added, “Return it as soon as possible. I still need it to inspect this cavern.”

Their relationship was clearly unusual. The Eye of Concealment was an artifact of gold level, yet Hua Tang lent it without hesitation. When Feng Lian asked to borrow it, he showed no courtesy whatsoever, as if it were his right.

Feng Lian was somewhat surprised. “You've been here nearly a month. Haven't you finished exploring yet?”

Hua Tang gave a bitter smile. “You have no idea how complex the terrain here is.”

“The crux is that a saint level sea beast dwells here, severely disrupting my plans.”

“But I've found a new approach. I intend to explore again during high tide.”

Feng Lian grasped much from his words. Shaking his head, he stated bluntly, “You should give up.”

“In a month, you've made no progress, still stuck in the initial stage of searching for clues.”

"The likelihood of this being the Thief Saint's inheritance treasure is slim. Many transcendents arrived here before you, only to lose their lives without finding anything."

Hua Tang also shook his head. “No. Even if there's just a sliver of possibility, I'll strive for it. I won't let any chance slip away easily.”

“Staying here is a waste of your time,” Feng Lian said. “It seems you're unaware of what happened at Snow Bird Harbor.”

Hua Tang froze. “What happened?”

“Teng Dong Lang died.” Feng Lian's tone remained steady.

Hua Tang's voice shot up, nearly shrill: "What did you say?!"

“He died in a heroic duel, witnessed by tens of thousands.”

“The one who killed him was a dragonman named Long Fu. He's the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, an outside force.”

“Long Fu...” Hua Tang repeated the dragonman youngster's pseudonym, his expression blank.

Feng Lian continued, “Most crucially, during this heroic duel, Long Fu only displayed silver level strength.”

“He defeated a gold level opponent while only displaying silver level strength. Teng Dong Lang became the stepping stone for his nationwide fame.”

Hua Tang's expression shifted again.

Feng Lian watched him silently, his confidence unwavering.

He knew the depth of the bond between Hua Tang and Teng Dong Lang. He didn't believe Hua Tang would still have the heart to stay here treasure hunting after hearing such earth-shattering news.

Sure enough, Hua Tang gritted his teeth, his gaze fixed on Feng Lian. “You just used a term, ‘Long Fu only displayed silver level strength.’ What does that mean?”

“Could it be... he's concealing his true cultivation level?”

Feng Lian shrugged. “I believe so.”

“Let me share another shocking revelation.”

“After Teng Dong Lang's death, the remnants of the Bayonet Gang contacted me. I personally intervened, helping them escape Snow Bird Harbor.”

“After that, Long Fu traveled around the Ice Sculpture Kingdom with a group of people, opening shops everywhere and aggressively selling a magic potion called dragon rider.”

“This potion sold like hotcakes. Riding on Long Fu's fame, it practically monopolized the kingdom's market for mount potions.”

“Most crucially, the Axe Gang leader, Jia Bing, and Lin joined forces to ambush Long Fu mid-journey.”

"Yet Long Fu single-handedly defeated all three in swift succession. On-site investigation revealed almost no signs of resistance!"

Hua Tang's expression darkened: “All three were gold level, yet Long Fu ended them with such ease.”

“Long Fu must have concealed most of his strength!”

“What exactly is the background of this Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

Feng Lian shook his head: "On the surface, they're a mercenary group dabbling in potion sales. But most believe they're actually arms smugglers. Their arrival in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom is to sell their weapons."

“Their current operation is using potions as a front to establish distribution channels.”

"Oh, and they've formed a partnership with the Li Jian Clan. They now produce dragon rider potions for the corps."

Hua Tang gritted his teeth in fury: “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps... and the Li Jian Clan...”

“I smell conspiracy!”

“I'm heading back. Immediately.”

"Feng Lian, where's your ship? Lend me one!"

Hua Tang's tone contained no politeness.

Feng Lian replied, “Seven nautical miles away.”

“That far?”

Feng Lian sighed, “I can briefly hold my own against the saint level sea beast, but my fleet is too conspicuous a target to withstand them.”

Hua Tang waved impatiently. “Enough. I'm boarding the ship. We must return at once.”

“Such a catastrophe has occurred, claiming so many lives!”

"I'll see for myself what kind of man this Long Fu fellow is. What tricks does he have up his sleeve?"

“Where is he according to the latest intelligence?”

Feng Lian pondered briefly before replying, “Based on their itinerary, they've likely already reached the Ice Sculpture Capital.”

The Ice Sculpture Capital.

After the spatial fluctuations vanished, the Spatial Shuttle dissolved into nothingness, depositing a group of people.

First came all members of the Spatial Shuttle Squad who had deployed battle skills, followed by Zong Ge, Zi Di, and their companions.

“We've finally made it... to the Ice Sculpture Capital,” Zi Di murmured.

Only three companions remained by her side besides Zong Ge.

When they departed Snow Bird Harbor, their numbers were large. Yet as Zi Di deployed personnel across the kingdom and reorganized her businesses, most mercenaries stayed behind in various regions.

Arriving here meant her commercial plan was already more than halfway accomplished!

Chapter 774: The Royal Capital Is Under Attack

Zi Di surveyed her surroundings, her vision dominated by towering city walls.

The walls weren't built from stone bricks but rather ice bricks. They were incredibly high, Zi Di estimated at least 300 meters.

The crenellations along the crest undulated like the ridges of a shield, instantly reminding Zi Di of a gold level shield, the Thin Ice High Wall Shield.

Back in Snow Bird Harbor, Teng Dong Lang had wielded such a shield to withstand the fierce assault of the dragonman youngster.

Zi Di's gaze lingered on the walls, the resemblance growing clearer with each passing moment.

Unlike most castle walls, the Ice Sculpture Capital's walls were tall yet thin, exuding an ethereal, elven delicacy.

Zong Ge also studied the walls, silently assessing their defensive capabilities.

“Walls this high naturally offer excellent anti-air capabilities.”

“With a few ballistae positioned here, they'd provide formidable long-range firepower.”

Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group were not far from the walls.

The transcendents of the Li Jian Clan had been waiting for some time. Now, smiling, they approached to greet them: “Lord Long Fu, Lord Yao Ma, the carriage is ready. This way, please.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps boarded the carriage.

The carriage immediately set off, carrying them toward the location of the shops.

Seated inside, Zi Di looked around.

The first thing she saw were ice sculptures.

Ice sculptures of all kinds, large and small. Some as big as mammoths, others as tiny as ant colonies.

"This is the Wild Beast Road. The ice sculptures lining both sides depict beasts." Inside the carriage, the transcendent from the Li Jian Clan provided a timely explanation.

“Beyond Wild Beast Road, there's Element Road, where the ice sculptures along the path take the forms of elemental beings.”

“In the city center lies Portrait Road, subdivided into over a dozen sections. Each is designed based on the intelligent races of the present world.”

Zi Di nodded slightly, showing no hint of surprise. She had already learned about these details before arriving at the royal capital.

Still, the sensory experience of seeing them firsthand differed greatly from what she had read in intelligence reports.

Zong Ge inquired, “Are these ice sculptures mobile? Can they be used for combat?”

The receptionist from the Li Jian Clan nodded, his tone brimming with pride. “Indeed. This is a unique alchemy technique exclusive to our nation, considered exceptional worldwide.”

“Each ice sculpture is an animated alchemy puppet, possessing sentience and combat capability.”

“Of course, their levels of sentience vary, as do their combat strengths.”

Zong Ge pressed further, "I've heard the Ice Sculpture Capital houses at least a hundred thousand of these sculptures, including over three thousand silver level puppets and nearly five hundred gold level ones. Is this count accurate?"

The Li Jian Clan representative nodded, then shook his head slightly. “Based on the currently displayed sculptures, that figure is correct.”

“But in reality, the numbers are far greater.”

“Beneath our feet, vast numbers of ice puppets likely remain concealed.”

"These underground puppets generally possess superior combat capabilities to those above ground. As for their specific levels and scale, that falls under our nation's military secrets."

Zi Di followed up: “Is there truly a vast lake beneath the Ice Sculpture Capital?”

According to intelligence reports, the snow elf ancestors who first migrated here discovered the Ten Millennia Ice Lake at the island's center.

The lake was vast, rich in ice element resources.

Overjoyed, the ancestors decided to settle there.

Over time, the lake had been filled in. The snow elves built the Ice Sculpture Capital atop the lake.

The receptionist nodded in confirmation. “There's no doubt about it. We are currently above the ancient ice lake.”

“Rest assured, Lords. We are perfectly safe.”

“The ice layer covering the Ten Millennia Ice Lake is nearly a kilometer thick, growing harder the deeper you go.”

“The Ten Millennia Ice Lake is now fully under our nation's control and is undergoing sustainable development.”

"We have numerous laboratories and alchemy chambers established within the lake waters beneath the ice. Some renowned ice sculptors specialize in collecting water from the lake's depths, freezing it into ice blocks to serve as their primary material for carving and other artistic creations."

A fragment of intelligence flashed through Zi Di's mind.

The waters of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake held significant secrets. The surface waters were several years old, while deeper layers contained water decades old.

At a depth of one hundred meters below the surface lay century-old lake water. extracted, it became century ice.

By extension, there existed one-thousand-year ice and ten-thousand-year ice.

This resource was exceedingly rare. Legend had it that a god level time dragon once fell, causing spatial anomalies in the lake and transforming its waters.

The power of time condensed within the lake water, merging with the properties of ice and snow to form this unique resource.

Century ice already possessed a certain degree of sentience. By the thousand year mark, this sentience grew increasingly potent. Ten-thousand-year ice held profound sentience, surpassing that of ordinary humans.

Ice sculpture masters employ unique alchemy techniques and carving methods to shape this frozen time into forms, channeling its spirituality to awaken it—thus creating each ice sculpture.

These sculptures possess innate sentience, becoming ubiquitous statues throughout the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's capital.

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's alchemy techniques are built upon the super-resource site of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

"A significant portion of Jia Bing's ice sculpture construction methods involves ice sculpture craftsmanship. This craftsmanship holds a crucial position within the entire manufacturing process."

Zi Di gazed at the ice sculptures, her mind recalling Jia Bing's alchemy records.

They had seized numerous spoils from Jia Bing's alchemy laboratory and even discovered a secret passage to the legacy of Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

Ever since, Zi Di devoted every spare moment to studying Jia Bing's alchemy records.

She had long harbored a deep fascination with the ice-carving techniques of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

But the primary reason was that an unfinished gold level ice gargoyle belonging to Jia Bing had fallen into Zi Di's hands. Naturally, Zi Di yearned to complete this feat, to fully craft the gold level ice gargoyle!

“Jia Bing's personal alchemy knowledge and materials don't represent the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's most advanced ice-carving techniques.”

“Nor are they the pinnacle of its essence.”

“But how do I acquire that core expertise?”

“Lords, we've arrived at the shop.” The receptionist's voice interrupted Zi Di's thoughts.

Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the others dismounted from the carriage.

Zi Di entered the shop to inspect it thoroughly, reviewed the recent accounts, and held multiple discussions with key personnel.

After this round of investigations, she gained a comprehensive and profound understanding of the shop's operations.

“This is the Ice Sculpture Capital's shop.”

“As you've likely noticed, it's the largest establishment we've surveyed.”

“It holds immense importance; it will serve as our flagship store in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!”

…

After giving detailed instructions to the three mercenaries, Zi Di arranged for their accommodations.

With this, the preliminary phase of Zi Di's business plan was complete!

“Next, we'll select one or two alchemy weapons to distribute nationwide.”

“But before that, we must study the market and choose carefully.”

Zi Di looked forward to this phase, brimming with confidence in War Merchant’s database and full of ambition.

That evening, Zong Ge and Zi Di attended a banquet specially hosted by the Li Jian Clan.

Numerous high-ranking figures were invited to the feast. Attendants accompanied Zong Ge and Zi Di throughout, introducing them to influential individuals from various capitals.

Zong Ge remained reserved and taciturn, while Zi Di truly shone. With the Li Jian Clan paving the way, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' network began to take shape that very evening!

By late night, Zi Di, exhausted from the day's exertions, had depleted both her physical and mental energy, leaving her too drained for further activities.

After a good night's rest, Zong Ge gathered intelligence through channels like the Thieves' Guild the next day, focusing particularly on the national alchemy market.

Zi Di delved deeply into this intelligence while also paying visits to several relatively more important figures.

These high-ranking individuals held such elevated status that the Li Jian Clan had deemed it inappropriate to invite them to last night's welcome banquet.

After all, the evening's focal points were Zong Ge and Zi Di, figures insufficiently distinguished to warrant personally inviting such dignitaries.

The visits yielded no major breakthroughs, serving primarily to establish familiarity and underscore the close ties between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Li Jian Clan.

Thus, another day passed.

The arrival of Zong Ge and Zi Di was met with widespread welcome, greeted with warmth and curiosity.

Many gathered around Zong Ge, complimenting his feat of defeating the gold level Teng Dong Lang and asking about his experience during the battle, his reasoning behind his tactics, and so on.

By the third evening, the intense scrutiny had eased considerably, and the royal capital had largely accepted the arrival of “Long Fu” and his companions.

Zi Di finally found her opportunity. She quietly cast the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

She had already scouted the terrain and spent over ten minutes walking to a shopfront.

This location was no longer in the bustling downtown area.

The shop remained open late into the night, not passing up a single transaction.

It was the Wisteria shop.

Zi Di lowered her hood, wearing a mask as she entered the shop.

The clerk greeted her warmly upon her arrival: “Good evening, what might I assist you with today?”

Zi Di approached him and presented the Wisteria secret order.

The clerk's eyes widened instantly: “Level seven secret order?!”

He bowed deeply, asking Zi Di to wait briefly before scurrying off to fetch the shop manager.

The manager personally verified the authenticity of the Wisteria secret order before Zi Di, his demeanor shifting to one of profound respect. He declared his willingness to obey any command she might issue.

“Take me to the magic messaging unit,” Zi Di instructed.

Soon, she stood before the terminal.

This communication device was standard equipment for the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. While not every shop possessed one, every outpost was equipped with it!

The magic messaging unit stood as tall as a person, topped with a circular platform featuring a central groove. Simply place a stone tablet inscribed with messages into the groove, activate the tower, and the information would be transmitted outward.

Its transmission range was limited, but by installing multiple towers, messages could be relayed over long distances.

This method strikes a balance between performance and cost, offering excellent value.

Zi Di had previously used a messaging unit on Two Eyes Island to send a “blackmail letter” to the Bai Zhen Clan.

Yet, to this day, no reply had arrived.

Zi Di had no way of knowing what exactly went wrong.

This time, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had come to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom specifically to attempt another delivery using the local messaging unit.

However, Zi Di didn't immediately put down the long stone slab. Instead, she mentally accessed the station's historical records for inspection.

Seconds later, her eyes widened in shock and anger. "What's going on? This messaging unit never received my previous letter!"

“Where did it go wrong?”

“Two Eyes Island? Or... here?”

Just as Zi Di pondered this, four figures abruptly materialized, encircling her.

Alchemy chains shot forth, instantly binding Zi Di in a tight, inescapable knot!

“Gotcha, remnant of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance!” The enemy exclaimed, their expression brimming with excitement.

Chapter 775: The Empire's Investigation

The gold level warship cut through the night sea, riding the wind and waves.

The cube-shaped forecastle gleamed with gold and splendor, its opulent aura unmasked even by the darkness.

This was the Empire's warship, the Treasure Chest.

Captain Tan Mo, also one of the Empire's Rear Admirals, stood at the bow, gazing into the distance.

Within the thick night, an island gradually came into view.

“Two Eyes Island, we've finally arrived!” Tan Mo's heart swelled with joy.

In that moment, intelligence on Two Eyes Island surfaced in his mind.

The island's location was prime, sandwiched between the Magic Power Shipping Route and the Masked Shipping Route, making it a hub of thriving commerce.

Two Eyes Island was also unique, it was itself a giant living organism, a coral cage tree. It had grown from two branches of a single cage tree, an outstanding achievement of artificial intervention. The Blue Green Coral fishman clan had paid a tremendous effort and cost, while the Hun Tong had ultimately claimed the fruits of their labor.

The patrol fleet signaled and approached the Treasure Chest.

The fleet's leader, the silver level One Eye, wore a broad smile. "Lord Tan Mo, you've arrived! Our island lord has been awaiting you for quite some time. Please follow me into port."

Tan Mo leapt suddenly, transferring directly from the Treasure Chest to the patrol vessel, startling One Eye.

Tan Mo smiled, patting One Eye's shoulder. “Then let's hurry back to port.”

Though he was the captain, his days aboard the Treasure Chest had been grueling. He was carrying several important figures: a saint level shield guardian, a gold level Secret Gate Church believer, and one with even more formidable connections, a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution!

In their presence, Tan Mo could only bow and scrape, serving them diligently.

The patrol fleet escorted the Treasure Chest smoothly into port.

Two Eyes Island possessed no natural harbor, nor even a sandy beach.

This dock bore the unmistakable marks of human construction, with exposed tree roots, vines, and coral visible everywhere.

“Lord Tan Mo, welcome to Two Eyes Island.” The island lord, Hun Tong, opened his arms wide and embraced Tan Mo warmly as he leapt onto the dock first.

Hun Tong was deeply concerned about the arrival of this group.

The reason was the appearance of necromancer traces on Two Eyes Island, and his own defection during the fishmen offensive, where he had killed many of his own comrades.

All of this required investigation.

Thus, Hun Tong displayed both intense warmth and palpable tension.

Tan Mo returned the embrace tightly.

His thoughts, however, were elsewhere: "I made a fortune on Snake Rat Island, but Two Eyes Island is far wealthier. Despite the beast tide and the fishmen's offensive, Hun Tong struck it rich."

“Tan Qiu's spoils pale in comparison to his haul!”

With Hun Tong facing investigation, there was immense potential for extraction.

As this thought crossed Tan Mo's mind, he squeezed Hun Tong's back harder, as if trying to pull a pile of gold coins into his own embrace.

Tan Mo's heart burned with fervor.

Feeling this “heat,” Hun Tong's tension eased slightly.

He leaned close to Tan Mo's ear and whispered, "Lord, the banquet is ready for you. There's also an after-dinner entertainment program, guaranteed to immerse you in the exotic charm of Two Eyes Island."

Tan Mo's mouth split into a silent grin.

Hun Tong was so considerate!

“Hold on a moment,” Tan Mo said. “The key figure in this investigation is still aboard my ship.”

“No rush, no rush,” Hun Tong replied, his face plastered with smiles.

As the warship entered the harbor, Tu Ji of the Blood Light Punishment Institution stepped ashore and approached Tan Mo.

He flatly declined Hun Tong's invitation, his tone stiff and gaze icy: “Eat? Nothing matters more than the investigation.”

“Even a moment's delay could cost us the precious chance to hunt down the necromancer!”

“It's precisely because you've been so careless, complacent, and negligent that Two Eyes Island has become a haven for the necromancers!”

Tu Ji's words left Hun Tong stammering in protest.

Hun Tong instantly grasped Tu Ji's temperament and turned to Tan Mo with pleading eyes.

Tan Mo, however, averted his gaze, as if his earlier enthusiasm had been an illusion.

“Let Tu Ji take the lead first. There's nothing wrong with that.”

“The more Hun Tong grasps the gravity of the situation, the more money he'll cough up, right?”

Tan Mo had his own calculations.

Thus, when Tu Ji disrupted the lavish banquet, Tan Mo felt no regret.

What was a meal worth?

Even the most lavish “entertainment” couldn't compare to the glittering gold coins.

While inwardly cursing Tu Ji as a madman, Hun Tong had no choice but to lead them into the city government palace.

Following prior arrangements, he had already prepared the interrogation chamber.

As the city lord, he had arranged an interrogation chamber within his own residence.

Though Two Eyes Island lay a considerable distance from the Sheng Ming Empire, Hun Tong remained firmly under the Empire's control.

Separated by a desk, the two parties took their seats.

Tu Ji occupied the seat of authority, his gaze fixed on the standing Hun Tong as he demanded, “Island Lord Hun Tong, traces of a necromancer have appeared twice within your Two Eyes Island.”

“First question: When the first necromancer surfaced, why did you choose to conduct your own search instead of reporting it to the Empire, to the Blood Light Punishment Institution?”

Tan Mo occupied the secondary seat, his tone gentle and face smiling. “Lord Hun Tong, no need to be overly tense. Speak freely.”

“The Empire is just. It spares no villain yet wrongs no innocent!”

Tu Ji frowned slightly but ultimately refrained from contradicting Tan Mo.

He understood Tan Mo's role extended beyond that of a fleet captain, he was also an imperial military investigator.

Tu Ji had only one objective: to hunt down the necromancer. Tan Mo, however, was here to investigate and verify the allegations that Hun Tong had killed his own comrades during the fierce battle.

Hun Tong forced a faint smile at Tan Mo, then turned to Tu Ji with solemn gravity. “The circumstances are rather complex. I must begin with how I seized control of the Two Eyes Island.”

Tu Ji's brow furrowed deeper. He was about to snap, “Make it quick!”

But Tan Mo spoke first, his eyes crinkling in a smile. "No rush, Island Lord Hun Tong. The more detail, the better. It will help us assess and verify the facts. We might even uncover clues about the necromancer hidden within the information."

Tan Mo's last remark was clearly directed at Tu Ji.

Tu Ji snorted coldly but refrained from arguing. Instead, he fixed Hun Tong with an even sharper gaze.

Hun Tong took a deep breath, striving to make his tone steady and authoritative to bolster credibility. “Though I seized Two Eyes Island, I never fully eradicated the perpetrators.”

“The perpetrators are the Blue Green Coral fishman clan.”

“I once attempted an underwater assault, suffering heavy losses. Recovering from those injuries alone consumed months of my time.”

“Since then, I've understood that relying solely on my own strength to eradicate the fishman clan hiding deep underwater is exceedingly difficult.”

“And just when I was at a complete loss, an imperial spy approached me, seeking contact.”

“His code name is Shadow. He is an intelligence operative dispatched by the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, specifically assigned to handle matters concerning Two Eyes Island.”

Chapter 776: Secret Spy - Shadow

Two Eyes Island, city government palace, interrogation room.

“Shadow...” Tu Ji gave a slight nod.

He knew Shadow.

Before arriving, he had studied the reports submitted by both Hun Tong and Shadow.

“Where is Shadow now?” Tu Ji asked.

This time, before Hun Tong could answer, Tan Mo turned with a smile toward Tu Ji: He's waiting just outside the door, Lord Tu Ji. Should you wish to interrogate him, he can be summoned at any moment."

Tu Ji frowned again.

Tan Mo's apparent politeness was actually a declaration of sovereignty, telling him: Shadow is the Empire's secret agent. He cannot be touched without permission!

But Tu Ji still issued a firm order: “Bring Shadow in.”

The door opened and closed.

The imperial secret agent, Shadow, stood before Tu Ji and Tan Mo.

Tu Ji stared at him for a long moment before coldly interrogating, “Shadow, you possess only bronze level cultivation. Yet as far as I know, Yan Ding, also an imperial secret agent, holds the highest gold level.”

“Why isn't he Shadow? Why isn't he the head of this region?”

“Why is it you?”

“If you were brought here to be a scapegoat for Yan Ding, you can say so now.”

"Rest assured, no one can touch you. Because you are now a witness for the Blood Light Punishment Institution!"

Shadow raised his head, a bitter smile on his face, and shook his head. “Lord Tu Ji, I fully understand your confusion.”

“But this is indeed the truth.”

"I am the genuine Shadow, not some hastily appointed sacrificial pawn. Though Yan Ding is gold level, he still operates under my command."

Tan Mo interjected again: “Lord Tu Ji, here is Shadow's dossier.”

“To be honest, I once shared your doubts.”

“But after reviewing his records, I realized why the organization promoted him to lead an entire region.”

“This is internal intelligence from our Imperial Secret Spy Agency. You may examine it.”

“Sometimes we must acknowledge that certain individuals possess extraordinary talent, yet are unlucky enough to lack sufficient bloodline to attain the requisite transcendent level!”

Tu Ji took the documents, skimmed them quickly, and after a moment of silence, looked back at the Shadow. “Then tell me about yourself first.”

The young Shadow's expression grew solemn. “My true name is Yan Yi. I am a member of the Duo Mu Clan.”

"Once, I enlisted voluntarily, aspiring to be a vanguard in the Sheng Ming Empire's conquest of the Wilderness Continent. But ultimately, I was recruited into the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, becoming one of its agents."

“Eight years ago, I received orders from the organization to infiltrate the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.”

"Through careful maneuvering, particularly by emphasizing my relationship with the island lord, I successfully gained acceptance into the merchant alliance, becoming a stationed agent at the Wisteria store here. Thus, the initial infiltration mission was accomplished."

“After the Wisteria Merchant Alliance's reorganization, I remained as the stationed representative while secretly fulfilling my duty as an imperial secret agent, proactively eliminating all potential threats to ensure a stable environment for the Empire's subsequent troop deployments and supply lines.”

"Three years ago, under my command, subordinates infiltrated the Vinegar Jar Pirates. Through this intelligence channel, I uncovered the Vinegar Jar Pirates' close ties to the Blue Green Coral fishman clan. The latter had long been plotting to reclaim Two Eyes Island."

"Lord Hun Tong had been attempting to eradicate this fishman clan, his objectives aligned perfectly with my mission. There exists significant potential for cooperation between us."

“Therefore, I proactively revealed my identity as a secret agent and devised a strategy with Lord Hun Tong.”

“We would pose as traitors, leaking intelligence to the Vinegar Jar Pirates. This would enable them to repeatedly intercept merchant ships with precision for plunder, thereby earning their trust.”

"Once that foundation of trust was established, we would lure the pirates into a trap, thereby drawing the main force of the Blue Green Coral fishman clan into a direct landing assault. This would create a decisive opportunity for the island lord to annihilate the enemy in one fell swoop."

At this point, Tu Ji of the Blood Light Punishment Institution interrupted: “The report stated that you discovered the first necromancer?”

“Why did you not report it to the Empire first, but instead launched your own search operation?”

Tu Ji posed the same question to Yan Yi as Hun Tong had.

Yan Yi licked his dry lips and explained, "To be precise, it wasn't me who found the necromancer first, but the fishman Xi Dai Zi. This point is detailed in the report."

Tu Ji stared expressionlessly at Yan Yi. “Repeat the entire incident in detail.”

Yan Yi nodded hastily. “Yes.”

“At that time, Two Eyes Island was at the pivotal moment of its ebb and flow cycle.”

"This is the moment when Two Eyes Island's defenses are at their weakest. The island's residents must relocate, infrastructure requires rebuilding, and ecological shifts will trigger beast swarms.

Based on our analysis, the Vinegar Jar Pirates and the Blue Green Coral fishman clan will likely seize this opportunity to reclaim Two Eyes Island."

"Events have confirmed this prediction."

“The Vinegar Jar Pirates, under the pretext of raiding Two Eyes Island, blackmailed us into not only providing military layout maps but also serving as inside collaborators during their operation.”

“To lure both the Vinegar Jar Pirates and the main force of the fishman clan into attacking the island, we feigned cooperation with them.”

"A preliminary agreement was reached when Xi Dai Zi unexpectedly discovered a thief. This thief, named Xu Ma, belonged to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. He accidentally stumbled upon the secret meeting and overheard our covert collaboration."

"Xi Dai Zi naturally wouldn't let Xu Ma escape. He immediately attacked, which in turn drew the attention of a necromancer. As the fierce battle raged, the battlefield shifted."

“The necromancer unleashed undead spells, emerging victorious but thereby exposing his location.”

“Xi Dai Zi fled, wounded.”

“My men were nearly wiped out, and the substantial deposit, a large sum of demon gold coins, obtained from Xi Dai Zi vanished along with them.”

"The necromancer's traces immediately drew the utmost attention from our faction and Lord Hun Tong."

"Imperial law demanded we report this intelligence immediately, but practical circumstances forbade it."

"Reporting would cause too much commotion, inevitably alerting the fishman clan. If the Empire dispatched investigators, the fishmen's plans to invade the island would surely be thwarted."

“Lord Hun Tong and I engaged in a heated debate, ultimately deciding to take the risk. Our primary consideration was that the necromancer's displayed power hadn't reached gold level.”

“We launched a large-scale search but initially found no trace of the necromancer. A necromancer confident enough to land on the island possessed considerable stealth skills.”

"The first to discover the necromancer was Xi Dai Zi. He lay hidden deep beneath the Two Eyes Island, possessing the ability to command tree roots and vines to some extent."

“Xi Dai Zi also disliked seeing the necromancer's influence gradually expand, so he relayed this information.”

"Ultimately, the island lord personally slew the necromancer. We succeeded!"

Tu Ji remained silent.

Tan Mo glanced at Tu Ji and said to Yan Yi, "You have earned credit for eliminating the necromancer. However, specific rewards will require precise calculation after this investigation concludes."

Tu Ji snorted coldly, "Even if you eliminated one necromancer, the system still malfunctioned. While your merit outweighs the fault, what remains is meager. Don't expect too much."

He pressed further, “Then why did you deliberately allow the second necromancer to escape?”

“According to the reported magic footage, during that chaotic battle, both you and Hun Tong had multiple opportunities to intervene. Yet you ultimately stood by and watched as the second necromancer fled Two Eyes Island.”

“Regarding this matter, you two will explain yourselves.”

Island Lord Hun Tong glanced at Yan Yi beside him and immediately replied, “I followed Shadow's advice, deciding to let the second necromancer leave the island to catch a bigger fish later.”

“In fact, afterward, I even urged him to act immediately and arrest the second necromancer.”

“Shadow botched the entire operation.”

Tu Ji turned his gaze to Yan Yi.

Yan Yi's face was ashen, cold sweat beading on his forehead. After a moment of deliberation, he cautiously spoke: “The second necromancer is one of the level seven elders of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.”

“These two level seven elders appeared suddenly, but their identity verification was correct.”

“Though the Wisteria Merchant Alliance's reorganization succeeded, remnants of the old faction still lurk in the shadows, unwilling to accept defeat and biding their time.”

“I believe these two level seven elders belong to that faction.”

“As the regional head of Imperial Secret Spy Agency, my greatest concern is that they might disrupt order around Two Eyes Island.”

"But these elders move with extreme secrecy, making them nearly impossible to track. On the other hand, I fear stirring the hornet's nest prematurely."

"The two potions they released into the market have been selling exceptionally well. I used the Flint Merchant Alliance and others as a front to secretly spread rumors, slandering the potions' reputation, in an attempt to provoke a counterattack from these two magicians."

“But they showed no reaction whatsoever.”

“After the beast tide struck, the potions' efficacy became widely known, and sales surged even more. My provocation plan failed.”

“I feared these two elders might vanish entirely, so I hastily devised a new plan.”

"At that time, the auction on Two Eyes Island was about to commence. I boldly speculated: The reason these two Wisteria Elders suddenly arrived on the island and made a fortune selling magic potions was likely to attend the auction."

“So, on their way out of the Wisteria shop, I arranged for Ya Ma to provoke them.”

“Ya Ma successfully created friction and deliberately failed, presenting them with the auction invitation I had meticulously prepared.”

"My reasoning was that even if they had no intention of attending, the invitation itself served as a powerful lure."

"As it turned out, they were indeed there for the auction. Using the invitation, they entered Room 21 and proceeded to bid aggressively, sweeping up items left and right. They completely overshadowed the entire gathering of gold level powerhouses."

Chapter 777: Wisteria Remnants

“When the fishmen attacked the island, one of the Wisteria Elders intervened, successfully seizing the Iron Lump. Later, during the battle against Ya Ma, he was forced to unleash undead spells, thereby revealing his identity as a necromancer.”

“It was only then that we learned one of the two Wisteria level seven elders was a silver level necromancer.”

“But to be honest, I wasn't particularly surprised.”

“Because before its reorganization, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance did indeed have many secret dealings with these necromancers.”

“From another perspective, any remnant forces within the restructured Wisteria Merchant Alliance must have relied on the necromancer's covert abilities to hide and evade elimination.”

Tu Ji remained expressionless: “According to the report, during the battle against the fishmen's island assault, Hun Tong had demanded the execution of this necromancer, but you intervened to stop it.”

“After the necromancer fled by ship, Hun Tong again proposed pursuing and killing him, but you dissuaded him once more.”

"Shadow Yan Yi, since you deliberately let the necromancer escape, you must have had a follow-up plan. What actions did you take afterward?"

Yan Yi fell silent, glancing toward Tan Mo.

Tan Mo spoke: "Shadow, say whatever you have to say. We are both part of the Empire, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency and Blood Light Punishment Institution are comrades. Matters too sensitive to speak of can still be shared. You need not bear responsibility for them."

Yan Yi immediately grasped the subtext: sensitive matters could be discussed, but forbidden secrets remained off-limits!

Yan Yi replied, “After letting the Wisteria elder escape, I dared not mobilize the Imperial Navy, fearing it would alert the enemy prematurely.”

“So, I arranged for Lady Sea Snake to pursue the kill.”

“Lady Sea Snake is the captain of the Petal Snake Pirates; a crew affiliated with the Fire Beard Pirates.”

“Her true identity, however, is as an imperial secret agent—a mole embedded within the Fire Beard Pirates.”

Tu Ji's expression shifted slightly upon hearing this, revealing a hint of surprise.

But he quickly accepted the revelation.

The Sheng Ming Empire's large-scale offensive against the Wilderness Continent had never concealed its annexationist intentions from the outset.

The vanguard forces had already established a foothold on the Wilderness Continent, but the main army had yet to truly mobilize.

Since the Sheng Ming Continent and the Wilderness Continent did not share a border, the Sheng Ming Empire needed to establish supply lines across the vast ocean.

Against this backdrop, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency had extensively deployed agents. Their missions included gathering intelligence, carrying out assassinations, infiltrating enemy ranks, and conducting subversive operations to maintain stability and security across various regions.

Shadow Yan Yi was one such agent operating within this large-scale operation. Though only of bronze level, he possessed sharp logic, clear organization, and exceptional skill.

Through this interrogation, Tu Ji had clearly sensed Yan Yi's talent.

"If not for his bloodline holding him back, he would be able to reach a higher transcendent level and see a brighter future!'

Whether Lady Sea Snake was originally a member of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency or was recruited halfway, she was still a part of the covert operation.

Tu Ji had also heard of the Fire Beard Pirates' reputation: “The three legend level pirate crews represent the pinnacle of pirate power, and the Fire Beard Pirates is one of them.”

“It boasts the largest scale and the lowest recruitment threshold.”

“It's likely there are other secret agents besides Lady Sea Snake.”

Thoughts raced through Tu Ji's mind as he turned to Yan Yi: "According to your report, Lady Sea Snake's operation failed this time. Elaborate."

Yan Yi hastened to reply: “To be precise, it's not time to admit defeat yet.”

“For the sake of deception, Lady Sea Snake deliberately employed tactics to rally the Stench Pirates, the Applause Pirates, and the Lightning Pirates.”

Near Weeping Pine Island, she ignited the Weeping Pine Naval Battle."

"Following this engagement, the pirate crews retreated one after another. One of the Wisteria elders, aided by Jian Gui and others, fled in disarray. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps seized the Iron Lump and secured the dwarves' allegiance. Lady Sea Snake continued her pursuit."

“At this point, the necromancers departed these waters, beyond my sphere of influence.”

“Thus, I could no longer obtain intelligence regarding Lady Sea Snake's subsequent actions.”

At this, Yan Yi gazed at Tu Ji with utmost sincerity: “My Lord, this is the truth of the matter.”

"I have not lied, not a single word. I swear by my personal honor, my family's honor, and my loyalty to the Empire!"

“I know I am guilty.”

“I released the necromancer, setting a tiger free.”

Now I can only hope that the other secret agents taking over my mission will follow through successfully, ultimately eliminating all necromancers, so my merits outweigh my faults!

Tu Ji fell silent for a moment.

Yan Yi spoke with apparent sincerity, yet there was a hint of cunning.

He implied subtly: this mission, this matter, is far from over. Even if the Blood Light Punishment Institution sought accountability, the day of reckoning had not yet arrived. Everything depended on the result that followed.

After a long pause, Tu Ji broke the silence: “It's late. Rest now.”

“You two stay put. Sleep here.”

“All other relevant personnel are to be temporarily detained. Restrict their movements for the time being.”

The interrogation was merely the first step. Having reviewed the reports and heard the responses, Tu Ji still needed to gather evidence personally. He wouldn't accept one-sided accounts.

The Ice Sculpture Capital.

Wisteria shop.

"Wisteria elder, these are the Chains of Confinement. Surrender peacefully!"

“That's right. At least this way, you'll still have your life.”

Zi Di stood bound by alchemy chains, immobilized.

She struggled to break free, but the four ends were gripped tightly by ambushing transcendent beings, the chains stretched taut.

Her undead magic power rapidly drained.

"Chains of Confinement? They can even target undead?" Zi Di was astonished yet not despairing.

The next instant, the three ambushing transcendents were instantly slain.

One was cleaved in two at the waist, another had his head severed, and the third had his heart pierced clean through by a claw from behind.

“What the hell?!” The lone surviving transcendent was horrified.

But soon, he too met his end, slaughtered without mercy.

In an instant, all four silver level transcendents had been counter-killed.

The alchemy chain became ownerless, instantly slackening.

Zi Di shook her arms, flinging the chains from her body onto the ground.

“The Chains of Confinement... gold level equipment. Not a bad haul.” Zi Di turned to leave, simultaneously using her mind to command the ice gargoyle to retrieve the Chains of Confinement.

“The messaging unit here never received the ransom note. I was ambushed again.”

“This Wisteria shop was a trap.”

“Then, is the Wisteria Merchant Alliance on Two Eyes Island also a trap?”

“That ransom note was never sent out... something's wrong with Yan Yi!”

As Zi Di pondered this, her vision suddenly shifted.

In an instant, she was forcibly teleported, vanishing from within the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.

The next moment, she materialized in a pure white, sealed chamber.

Stunned by the sudden change, Zi Di's face darkened with gravity as she frantically scanned her surroundings.

A biting cold permeated the air. The walls were constructed entirely of ice bricks, devoid of any windows.

Just as Zi Di prepared to cast a detection spell, spatial fluctuations erupted again, revealing the presence of two gold level individuals.

Zi Di tapped her storage pouch, releasing three ice gargoyles that charged toward the source of the disturbance.

But by then, the teleportation had completed. The gold level fighters materialized on the battlefield, effortlessly blocking the three ice gargoyles.

Zi Di immediately retreated, creating distance between herself and her enemies.

She was merely an iron level necromancer, comparable at best to a silver level magician in mental attributes. Fighting two gold level opponents alone offered no chance of victory.

Therefore, her first choice was to send a distress signal.

She wasn't acting alone; Zong Ge remained outside the shop, prepared for any unexpected developments.

Distress signal transmission failed!

Three ice gargoyles were destroyed in succession, despite being premium puppets enhanced with ice magic.

The two gold level assassins lunged forward.

Zi Di had no choice but to unleash her ghost army.

She had been accumulating this force since leaving Mystifying Monster Island. By now, it was substantial in size. But the core remained the smelt rat souls and tide snake souls from Snake Rat Island.

The two gold level fighters remained silent. One scattered powdered potions, the other unleashed a battle skill.

The potion powder scattered upon the ghosts, causing them to dissolve like snow. The battle skill's light waves grew more intense, scorching every ghost in their path.

Zi Di's heart sank to the depths.

Her opponents were exceptionally well-prepared, possessing methods specifically countering the undead.

Zi Di was utterly powerless to fight back.

She reached into the leather pouch at her waist, pulled out two vials of magic potion, and smashed them open.

The toxic mist released by the potions temporarily forced back the two gold level foes.

Zi Di finally gained a moment to catch her breath.

These were gold level potions, purchased from the Li Jian Clan.

The poisonous mist posed little threat to undead like herself, yet proved highly lethal to other lifeforms.

Moreover, the fog began corroding the surrounding floor and ice walls.

Zi Di attempted to send a distress signal once more, only to fail again.

In her free hand not holding the staff, she clutched a handful of pearl bubbles.

But in this sealed battlefield, even if she used the pearl bubbles, could she truly prevail and escape?

Zi Di felt uncertain.

The Deceptive Disguise spell only worked on living beings, and using the pearl bubbles directly would be even less effective, forcing her to rely solely on the situation unfolding naturally.

Even with these tools, Zi Di doubted she could escape this place.

She didn't even know where she was!

“This time, I fear my fate is sealed.”

“Who would have thought entering the royal capital would lead to my end?”

“The pearl bubbles must be used, but it will likely leave many clues for the enemy.”

"May my disappearance serve as sufficient warning to Zong Ge! I hope he remains safe."

“Lord Commander... I might...”

Just then, with a thunderous boom, the floor suddenly shattered, and a torrent of cold water surged in.

The cold water met the air, instantly forming a layer of frost.

The ice shattered as a spindle-shaped micro-submarine emerged, its hatch opening.

“Get in! I'll get you out!” The hoarse voice sent a chill through Zi Di's heart.

"Elder Yuan Ci?"

Seeing the two gold level figures charging in despite the toxic mist, Zi Di hurriedly climbed into the submarine.

The gold level attackers lunged, striking the sub's hull with a force that left a deep dent.

But the sub's sturdy construction held. Though three layers of its defensive magic shield shattered, the final layer remained intact. It dove back into the breach and escaped successfully.

"Pursue!!" The gold level finally lost his temper, bellowing in fury.

Chapter 778: Giving Hun Tong His Poor Evaluation

Two Eyes Island.

Night gradually faded as dawn approached.

Tu Ji didn't rest. Instead, he went straight to a shop and found one of the witnesses.

He used the shop's main hall as an interrogation room, confronting the representative of the Clam Cloth Branch: “You participated in the fishmen's assault on the island and survived.”

“Tell me about your actions during that battle.”

The first question was relatively simple, designed to ease the witness' nerves.

Often, tension causes people to forget crucial details. And it was precisely these details that held the most valuable clues.

The representative of the Clam Cloth Branch was naturally nervous. The man before him was no ordinary gold level official, he was a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution!

The uniform alone was enough to strike fear into nearly every citizen of the Sheng Ming Empire!

"I observed that during the chaos, numerous merchant ships were the first to flee the harbor. Their desertion undermined the defenses of Two Eyes Island. Why commit such treasonous acts?"

This was Tu Ji's second question.

The representative of the Clam Cloth Branch hurriedly protested, “Lord, we're merchants, not soldiers!”

“When battle looms, our first duty is to protect the merchant alliance's assets and minimize losses.”

“Fighting is the soldiers' job.”

"At the time, our Clam Cloth Branch ships did participate, but honestly, we were in a daze. The situation was chaotic. The island lord severely neglected his duties, failing to organize us. I was swept up in the turmoil and sailed away in confusion."

“However, afterward, we analyzed the situation and waged a relentless, sacrificial resistance and battle.”

“Ultimately, through our efforts, we repelled the invading fishmen.”

The Clam Cloth Branch's representative deflected blame while claiming credit for his own protection.

Tu Ji didn't call him out but posed a third question: “What's your take on Island Lord Hun Tong?”

The Clam Cloth Branch's representative's eyes widened in indignation as he growled, " He's scum, a deadbeat! Devoid of any noble honor, a complete bastard!"

Tu Ji's surprise sparked his interest: “Elaborate.”

The representative immediately declared: “He slaughtered us!”

“During the battle, he used the Golden Flash to kill countless merchants.”

“Three representatives of the Six Great Merchant Alliances died in combat.”

"Even if he claims it was the Devil Gold Coins, who knows? We strongly suspect he did it deliberately!"

“After the battle, we analyzed the situation. Hun Tong actually had the time and capability to organize our defense.”

"But he let us act freely, betting we'd panic and scatter in disarray. This would breach our defenses, making us the ones at fault. He could then use this as grounds to challenge us and nullify the commercial contracts we'd signed with him."

“Why nullify the contracts?” Tu Ji asked.

“Because Two Eyes Island is set to become one of the central hubs for the transport lines,” the representative explained, “Hun Tong concealed this matter. We only recently learned this crucial information ourselves.”

“Information is often money, you know!”

“Hun Tong deliberately set us up to make mistakes so he could escape blame and smoothly transition Two Eyes Island, without paying any price!”

“He outmaneuvered us. Too clever!”

"But man proposes, heaven disposes. Ha! He fell into his own trap. The Devil Gold Coins ruined his scheme."

"He caused us immense losses. As the island lord, he bears undeniable responsibility. Demanding compensation from him is entirely justified."

“Yet he hasn't paid a single penny. Not a single coin has been compensated to us.”

“Not only that, he used this as leverage to force us to renegotiate our previous commercial agreements.”

“If he had no money, we could at least understand.”

"But Lord, do you know? Hun Tong struck it rich, he seized a colossal fortune!"

“That wealth was plundered from us by fishmen and pirates! They raided the entire island, and he claimed it all as spoils, shoveling it into his own coffers!”

“Tell me, isn't such an island lord utterly despicable?! He has the money, yet refuses to compensate. Instead, he uses this as leverage to force us into further concessions."

“Sigh... We're merely representatives sent by the merchant alliance, small-time representatives here.”

“We simply lack the authority to act. Even if we backtrack and renegotiate, the Chamber will hold us personally accountable.”

“Hun Tong is driving us to our deaths!”

The representative of the Clam Cloth Branch seemed to have opened a floodgate, pouring out his grievances in an unstoppable torrent.

Tu Ji, however, welcomed this development.

He could tell the man was speaking from the heart.

Testimony delivered in such a state carried high credibility.

Tu Ji listened patiently, occasionally asking questions.

After clarifying the details, he pressed on to the next merchant alliance branch.

By the time he had questioned several individuals, dawn had broken.

As Tu Ji stepped out of the Flint shop, he spotted a group gathered at the entrance.

“We are members of the Bandage Mercenary Corps! Lord, we come to report the island lord, Hun Tong!” they declared their purpose.

Tu Ji nodded. “If you hadn't come to me, I would have sought you out.”

"Fantastic."

“Head to the tavern over there and say everything you wish to say.”

The tavern was naturally still closed at dawn.

But the mercenaries forced the door open. The tavern owner, recognizing Tu Ji's attire, instantly perked up. He quickly shed his reluctant expression, opened the door, and let the mercenaries inside.

Tu Ji ordered, "A mug of beer for every mercenary. On me."

At the sight of beer, some mercenaries' Adam's apples bobbed, while others' eyes glistened.

Their recent days have been exceptionally harsh.

Two mercenary leaders stood beside Tu Ji and declared, “Island Lord Hun Tong is of vile character. Not only did he fail to reward merit, but he framed loyal subjects.”

“Our Bandage Mercenary Corps has sweated and bled for Two Eyes Island, paying a heavy price and making immense contributions during the fishmen's invasion.”

“Yet after the war, Lord Hun Tong not only withheld any rewards but imposed sanctions and oppression upon us.”

“He exploited our commander's death in battle by selling our flagship, the White Bandage, directly to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Since then, he has suppressed us at every turn, swallowing up our mercenary corps' meager remaining assets.”

“We received no compensation whatsoever. We gradually lost our livelihoods, and some of us were reduced to begging for survival.”

The two leaders spoke in turn, detailing the myriad crimes committed by Hun Tong.

Tu Ji remained expressionless throughout, occasionally interjecting with sharp questions.

The conversation lasted half an hour before Tu Ji rose to leave.

The mercenaries hurriedly stood to see him off.

“My Lord, we've stayed here all this time hoping you would come to investigate!”

"Your Lordship, you must severely punish that old bastard Hun Tong! He's damaged the reputation of the Empire, he's a parasite!"

Amidst the mercenaries' relentless pleas, Tu Ji left the tavern.

The sun rose, and the sea breeze blew.

Vines, coral, and branches covered Two Eyes Island, creating swathes of blue-green hues.

The sunlight was warm, brimming with vitality.

Tu Ji had not slept all night, yet he remained alert and energetic.

This was nothing to him. He had once gone seven days and nights without rest, solely to capture an escaped necromancer.

Past successes taught him: hunting necromancers demanded relentless urgency and unwavering persistence.

“I have gathered the evidence from the merchants and mercenaries. Next up are the Wealth Church members.”

The church members who'd once fought alongside Hun Tong now voiced fierce resentment toward him during Tu Ji's inspection.

“Our priests fell in battle, yet Hun Tong, as island lord, offered no condolences and refused to return our church's property.”

“Though these assets were plundered by pirates and ultimately became Hun Tong's spoils of war.”

“Hun Tong secretly cleaned up the battlefield, utterly dishonorable.”

“He's cast aside our order's friendship forged in battle and the shared memories of building Two Eyes Island's prosperity.”

“Lately, he's even been entertaining certain Justice Church members with ulterior motives!”

“Our order deems him an unworthy lord.”

While Tu Ji was gathering evidence from the Wealth Church, Tan Mo rose from bed.

After a cursory wash, he summoned Yan Yi alone to the interrogation chamber within the city government palace.

The moment Yan Yi entered, he warmly flattered Tan Mo and subtly expressed his curiosity, "Why pick such an early hour for a private interrogation? Lord Tan Mo, you've traveled far and should have rested well last night."

“After the investigation concludes, I humbly request you grant me another opportunity to properly host you.”

Tan Mo yawned, his voice thick with resentment. “Of course I'd like to rest. But who can blame me? Our esteemed Lord Tu Ji was so full of zeal, he didn't sleep a wink all night, rushing about gathering evidence.”

Yan Yi's expression shifted slightly. He sighed, his words carrying a hidden meaning. “I've long heard of the Blood Light Punishment Institution's formidable reputation. This time, I've experienced it firsthand.”

Tan Mo sat with his upper body leaning forward, elbows braced on the tabletop, staring intently at Yan Yi. “I've reviewed the intelligence. If you haven't concealed anything further, your chances of surviving this are quite high.”

“Now, it's just you and me here.”

“Yan Yi, representative of the Two Eyes Island under the codename Shadow. I am conducting this interrogation on behalf of the Empire's high command.”

“Regarding the departed Wisteria elder, how much have you actually uncovered?”

Yan Yi's expression turned solemn. He lifted his head, chest thrust out, his body straight as a spear. He reported with gravity and efficiency: “Reporting to you, Lord, the intelligence I submitted concerning the two Wisteria elders was nearly complete.”

Tan Mo nodded. "Per espionage protocol, what's reported constitutes only part of the information. The critical details must be conveyed through face-to-face communication before formal submission. Now, you may report that portion."

Yan Yi fixed his gaze on Tan Mo. “Lord Tan Mo, before proceeding, may this subordinate verify your identity?”

“Of course.” Tan Mo nodded.

Chapter 779: Secret Spy - Lost Return

Yan Yi retrieved an alchemy device from his robe, and Tan Mo produced one as well.

The two devices docked together. After a burst of magic light, Tan Mo and Yan Yi simultaneously focused their minds to activate the internal buttons.

The button sequence was classified, known only to headquarters and the secret agent personally, functioning as a dual-layered password.

Both passwords proved flawless, and the magic glow from the devices swiftly faded.

With a gentle tug, they separated the temporarily fused authentication devices into two halves, each retrieving their portion and storing it carefully.

Only then did Yan Yi report: “The two Wisteria elders are elusive as ghosts. There's simply no way to confirm their whereabouts.”

"My assessment is that their primary goal is to attend the auction. The sudden release of two potions for sale was merely to earn some gold coins and bolster their auction strength."

“Subsequent events have, to a certain extent, validated this assessment.”

"This subordinate owes his position as representative of the Two Eyes Island region entirely to His Highness the Seventh Prince's appointment. From the very beginning, I have kept the Seventh Prince's mission deeply engraved in my heart, never daring to forget it."

"These two Wisteria elders represent invaluable leads. After deliberating for a full day and night, I concluded that capturing them now would carry significant risks with little reward. Therefore, I resolved to focus on surveillance while prioritizing the infiltration of spies into this remnant faction!"

“Without seeking prior authorization, I directly deployed the secret agent codenamed ‘Lost Return’.”

Tan Mo frowned. "The secret agent codenamed ‘Lost Return’? Provide a detailed briefing."

Yan Yi nodded. “Lost Return, female, real name Qing Xin, a silver level wind magician.”

“She was originally a confidante of the previous president of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. Selected and sponsored by the merchant alliance, she was trained and cultivated into a wind magician.”

Before the Wisteria Merchant Alliance's restructuring, someone made a move against Qing Xin. She seized this opportunity to stage her own death, attempting to sever ties with the merchant alliance."

"The secret agent uncovered this truth and carried out a covert capture. The operation succeeded. After intense interrogation and psychological pressure, we successfully recruited Qing Xin."

"During her infiltration phase, she underwent secret agent training. It's worth noting Qing Xin excelled in her training."

“After arriving on Two Eyes Island, Qing Xin remained hidden behind the scenes, handling affairs and overseeing the daily operations of the secret agent organization.”

"The Wisteria elder appeared, showing signs of attending the auction. I first dispatched Ya Ma to deliberately create friction and deliver the auction invitation. Subsequently, I ordered Qing Xin to disguise herself as a slave and arranged for her to be listed as one of the auction items."

"During the auction, the Wisteria elder indeed recognized Qing Xin and purchased her. Thus, Qing Xin had successfully infiltrated the enemy's inner circle."

“But this level of infiltration was far from sufficient.”

"The remnants of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance would undoubtedly subject Qing Xin to rigorous scrutiny. To lend her a hand, I orchestrated Lady Sea Snake's small-scale military operation."

“In subsequent actions, Lady Sea Snake organized groups like the Applause Pirates to stage a naval battle around Weeping Pine Island. At the critical moment, they held back, allowing the necromancer temporary freedom.”

"I have no knowledge of the actions that followed. That segment of the mission was taken over by other secret agents. At that point, I also lost contact with Qing Xin."

Tan Mo nodded, scrutinizing Yan Yi from head to toe.

Despite being only bronze level, Yan Yi held some renown within the Imperial Secret Spy Agency.

His fame stemmed from his transcendent level.

Among regional representatives overseeing vast territories like Two Eyes Island, Yan Yi held the lowest level, likely the sole bronze level.

Typically, a representative's transcendent level should reach silver to be considered adequate.

Iron was already considered low, let alone bronze!

Only silver level could barely ensure personal safety and significantly mitigate the devastating effects of decapitation tactics.

As for the gold level, such transcendents were rarely assigned as regional representatives.

Their prominence made them too conspicuous, drawing sustained attention from factions of all sizes with every move.

This starkly contradicts the covert nature of secret agents and severely hinders espionage operations.

This was Tan Mo's first encounter with Yan Yi. Even this brief review had allowed Tan Mo to recognize Yan Yi's exceptional competence.

Yan Yi's various arrangements and preparations had proven remarkably successful.

“You are an exceptionally capable secret agent. It's a pleasure to meet you,” Tan Mo said with a smile.

Yan Yi hastened to express that he had been eagerly awaiting Tan Mo's arrival. After meticulously preparing numerous items, he sent them all to Tan Mo once the investigation concluded.

“That kid Yan Yi—he knows how to play the game!” Tan Mo silently praised inwardly.

Tan Mo's reputation far surpassed even Yan Yi's.

He was notorious within both the Secret Spy Agency and the Navy.

Yan Yi had long learned that Tan Mo was the investigator assigned to this case. The latter was notorious for his greedy nature, mastering all manner of extortion tactics.

Yan Yi genuinely welcomed Tan Mo's arrival.

This was no pretense, it was sincere.

Compared to other investigators, Tan Mo was remarkably easy to deal with—all it took was presenting him with a bribe that satisfied him.

One reassuring aspect was that while Tan Mo demanded bribes, he also valued integrity, once paid, he genuinely delivered results. The amount paid determined the extent of his service. It was a straightforward transaction, fair to all.

"Fantastic."

“This investigation concludes here.”

“One final note: the mission assigned by the Seventh Prince must be buried deep within your gut.”

“Neither the higher-ups in the Secret Agency nor the Blood Light Punishment Institution involved this time need to know.”

“Do you understand?” Tan Mo emphasized at the end.

“Of course!” Yan Yi bowed deeply. “Even if it costs me my life, this secret mission will never be revealed.”

Just as Yan Yi was about to leave the interrogation room, Tan Mo called him back: “Wait. Does Lady Sea Snake know Qing Xin's true identity?”

Yan Yi answered immediately: “She is unaware. Qing Xin also does not know Lady Sea Snake's true identity.”

“However, I cannot guarantee that Lady Sea Snake's personal speculations might not uncover fragments of the truth.”

Tan Mo nodded. “Alright, I understand.”

With a wave of his hand, Yan Yi turned and walked slowly out of the room.

White Hair Trench.

Ocean Mother Nest, Charming Blue Divine Domain.

Another teleportation blood sacrifice.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the Rubber Swallower disgorged an unconscious human woman.

She wore brown leather attire, her figure curvaceous and alluring. Even with eyes tightly shut, lost in unconsciousness, she radiated feminine charm.

It was Lady Sea Snake.

“Who would've thought this female pirate would end up our captive,” Cang Xu remarked with deep emotion.

During the battle at Weeping Pine Island, Cang Xu had witnessed Lady Sea Snake's formidable combat prowess. Amidst Wei Ying's surprise assault, Cang Xu and the others, including Jian Gui, fled in disarray.

Afterward, Jian Gui (Cang Xu) and his group engaged in a relentless pursuit battle with Wei Ying and Lady Sea Snake, which eventually dragged them into the demi-plane of the Ghost World.

Only with the support of Doctor Sui Shi, one of the Twelve Saints, did Jian Gui and his companions manage to escape the relentless chase.

After recuperating, the group returned to the main plane, heading to the White Hair Trench to rescue Qi Ling.

During the fierce battle, they were ambushed by Zhou Zhang. Like Jian Gui, Lady Sea Snake and others fell captive to the Charming Blue believers.

What should be done with Lady Sea Snake?

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu discussed it. Their conclusion: there was no urgency in killing this female magician.

As far as the fishman youngster and Cang Xu knew, Lady Sea Snake wasn't just a pirate captain, she was also a member of the Fire Beard Pirates. Such connections held immense value. Even without that, a silver level female magician like Lady Sea Snake was a formidable magic combat force in her own right!

Thus, another occupant found residence within the longhorn cage.

Just like Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, and Qing Xin before her, Lady Sea Snake was deceived into believing she was already dead.

The sharp horns within the alchemy longhorn cage slightly pierced her skin, preventing her magic power from accumulating.

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu were still unaware that among the four silver level captives they held, two were members of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency.

At this moment, their focus lay elsewhere.

“Gold level... This is truly a miracle!” Cang Xu circled the fishman youngster, feeling his life force, and praised him effusively.

After recognizing the extraordinary divine blessing within the fishman youngster, Cang Xu had already harbored expectations that the youngster might ascend to gold level.

Yet reality still surpassed his imagination.

He hadn't anticipated his own commander ascending to gold level so swiftly!

This was truly splendid news!

The old magician, loyal to the young commander, felt genuine joy for the fishman youngster.

“Zong Ge's battle qi now carries a golden glow.”

“But you, Commander, have now formally ascended to gold level.”

“He has no chance whatsoever to lay claim to the position of leader.” Cang Xu's spirit soared.

The fishman youngster shook his head slightly. “Enough of that. Come inside quickly. Let us pray together.”

“I am ready.”

Gazing at his captain's retreating figure, Cang Xu felt a momentary daze.

The fishman youngster, a devout believer in the Justice God, performed the blasphemous prayer with far greater fervor than Cang Xu, the necromancer.

Such seasoned demeanor made it hard to believe the fishman youngster had only recently been introduced to the practice of blasphemous rituals

Naturally, Cang Xu kept these thoughts to himself.

He obediently followed the fishman youngster into the prayer chamber.

The original statue of the Charming Blue Goddess had been replaced by an ordinary divine statue.

Cang Xu hastily retrieved the Charming Blue Goddess statue he carried with him, a silver level sacred artifact.

He replaced the statue, knelt upon the floor, and murmured prayers.

The fishman youngster knelt beside him, waiting a moment before beginning his own prayer.

“My Lord, exalted Charming Blue Goddess, You are the embodiment of boundless power. Hear my devout supplication.”

“In this boundless ocean, You are the tempest and the guardian of the seas.”

“You are the source of water, and the supreme master of deception.”

“You drive every soul seeking power and wisdom with illusion and deceit.”

“Today, we humbly beseech You.”

"Grant Your faithful servant, a pitiful, lowly necromancer, a higher magician level. Let him bathe in Your great bounty, instructed by Your magnificent magic power."

"Your loyal believer will expend divine favor to help the necromancer obtain a leap on transcendent level."

“Oh great Charming Blue Goddess, may You accept this sincere prayer. May You favor Your necromancer believer, guiding him onto the path of a higher magician, so that in the future, he may more fully proclaim Your radiance."

“Now I have spoken, praying with all sincerity.”

“Merciful deity, please grant my request.”

Boom!

The moment the fishman youngster's words faded, a pillar of divine light descended from the void, enveloping... the fishman youngster.

Bathed in the divine radiance, the transcendent level of his clergyman cultivation surged from silver level to gold level in an instant.

Hundreds of divine spells surged violently into his mind, accompanied by a wealth of experience in casting them!

The overwhelming flood of knowledge made the fishman youngster's head throb painfully, his expression contorting.

Beside him, Cang Xu's gaze went blank.

“This was a prayer meant for me. Why did the divine blessing land on the commander instead?”

Chapter 780: Archbishop!

A thick beam of light enveloped the fishman youngster entirely, its glare relentless and unyielding.

The beam's diameter spanned two meters, gradually shrinking as time passed.

When it reached 0.1 meters, the beam gently shifted onto Cang Xu.

Cang Xu immediately bowed his head, his prayers growing louder.

Then, his body trembled slightly—he had ascended to silver level!

The beam expended its last ounce of power and vanished completely.

Calm returned to the prayer chamber.

“Silver level...” Cang Xu remained kneeling for a long time, his heart surging with emotion.

Had he not already undergone cranial modification, becoming an undead being, tears would surely well up in his eyes now.

This marked a new frontier in his life, a vista beyond the limits of his bloodline.

His bloodline and innate talent had been inferior even to Zi Di's. Even after becoming an undead, he had expected to reach only the iron level at best.

Breaking through the iron level to reach the silver level, this was something Cang Xu had never dared to imagine.

“Now, I've done it.”

“I've become silver level.”

“And what's more, even greater things lie ahead!”

As these thoughts filled him, Cang Xu instinctively turned to the fishman youngster beside him, his gaze burning with fervor.

It was precisely by following the fishman youngster's path that he had reached this day.

What moved Cang Xu even more was the fishman youngster's willingness to expend his own divine favor to actively aid him!

The fishman youngster was not a necromancer, yet he chose to lavishly cultivate a necromancer. Such behavior wouldn't even be seen in the legitimate heir of the undead orthodoxy, Jian Gui.
