“Commander, this must have cost you a great deal of divine favor, right?” Cang Xu stood up, noticing the fishman youngster's body trembling, and helped him to his feet.

The fishman youngster was already very familiar with his own state—this was a massive advancement, and both his body and soul still needed time to “digest” it. Like after overeating, requiring time to rest.

The fishman youngster shook his head slightly, gave himself a quick check, then replied to Cang Xu with a hint of peculiarity in his voice: “Strange. My divine favor hasn't decreased. It's actually risen again. Now, it's surpassed 10,000 in number."

Cang Xu's body stiffened momentarily. He drew a light breath and quickly composed himself.

He was very familiar with this situation.

He inquired curiously about the fishman youngster's other gains.

The fishman youngster explained, “Through this prayer, my clergyman cultivation has advanced to gold level, granting me mastery over hundreds of divine spells. Moreover, each spell comes with corresponding usage experience.”

Cang Xu drew another breath. “So then, Commander, you've become an exceptional and powerful clergyman?”

The fishman youngster had already attained the gold life level, and now his clergyman cultivation had risen as well.

The key gain was over a hundred divine spells.

Casting divine spells was inherently far easier than casting magic spells. In a sense, divine spells could be considered a type of racial magic. Using them felt like moving one's limbs, almost like an innate instinct.

Of course, there were differences in experience and skill when using limbs. For instance, some could use their limbs to crawl, others to walk, while some could sprint with ease.

At this level, the fishman youngster would merely be a competent clergyman with diverse abilities.

But this divine blessing brought not only divine spells but also vast experience.

This meant the fishman youngster possessed rich, deeply ingrained familiarity with these divine spells before even using them for the first time. His first use of hands and feet skipped the crawling and walking stages entirely, reaching the level of running.

Cang Xu assessed the fishman youngster as an outstanding and powerful clergyman.

This evaluation was spot on.

The fishman youngster nodded in agreement with Cang Xu's assessment, then exclaimed excitedly, "Do you know? Among these divine spells is Resurrection!"

“With Resurrection, I can bring Zi Di back to life!”

The fishman youngster couldn't help but feel genuine joy.

He had always wanted to help his beloved, but had never found a way.

Now, possessing the power of resurrection and considerable experience in using resurrection magic, he was utterly confident he could revive Zi Di.

“Resurrection magic... this is truly wonderful!” Cang Xu exclaimed.

He had once harbored a similar plan, to master it himself and revive Zi Di.

Reality, however, had shown him the difficulty of obtaining divine favor and the strict standards for casting divine spells, making him realize his plan was far from achievable.

Then, he personally witnessed the absurd treatment bestowed upon the fishman youngster, watching him overtake him in the blink of an eye, leaving the pioneer far behind.

Now, the fishman youngster had actually mastered Resurrection!

Cang Xu inquired further: “What are the underlying requirements for casting Resurrection?”

"A divine spell with such extraordinary effects isn't something one can cast lightly. On one hand, it requires the expenditure of divine favor. On the other hand, there are clerical requirements."

The fishman youngster reined in his excitement slightly: “Resurrection consumes divine favor, and the amount required is also tied to one's clerical position.”

"For a monk, it demands 10,000 divine favor points through a directed prayer. If answered, Resurrection is granted."

“After this prayer, my clerical position has also risen.”

"As an Archbishop now, I don't need a directed prayer because I inherently possess the authority to cast Resurrection once per month. To cast it multiple times, additional divine favor must be expended. The exact amount varies based on the target's cultivation and quantity, some require more, some less."

"Zi Di's life rank is merely iron level, and she's the only one individual. The divine favor cost is only 200. I can easily afford that!"

At this point, the fishman youngster turned to Cang Xu: “Do you want to be fully resurrected?”

Cang Xu paused slightly, then quickly refused: “My body isn't mine anymore. Only my head is original. Resurrection like that would be meaningless.”

“My transcendent path lies in the undead. The Hanging Corpse Tree is a saint level bloodline, how could I abandon that?”

“At least not for now!”

The fishman youngster nodded. “For now, we also need a necromancer to maintain contact with the orthodox undead faction.”

“Yes.” Cang Xu nodded. “If I change my mind in the future, revival can always happen then.”

“Oh, I didn't catch that earlier.”

“Commander, you mentioned your clerical position has risen to a level of bishop, correct?”

The fishman youngster shook his head. “Archbishop.”

Cang Xu drew in a sharp breath again, staring intently at the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster looked puzzled. “What's wrong?”

“You know Zhou Zhang is merely an Archbishop of the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Your current standing within the Charming Blue Church is now equal to Zhou Zhang's.”

“But Zhou Zhang is a saint level!”

Hearing Cang Xu's explanation, the fishman youngster finally grasped a vague concept.

The latter nodded, his understanding slightly clearer: “So an Archbishop holds immense power and prestige!”

“Exactly,” Cang Xu affirmed. “If we liken the church to a kingdom, the Pope is the king, bishops are like city lords, and Archbishops are feudal lords, great nobles!”

“Bishops are typically held by gold level clergymen, while Archbishops often possess immense power, often reaching the saint level.”

“Whereas you, Commander, are only of gold level, and you've just recently ascended.”

The fishman youngster nodded again.

Cang Xu's expression grew complex. “Though Zhou Zhang didn't elaborate, I could sense her pride in her position as Archbishop.”

“She joined the Charming Blue Church at a young age.”

“Step by step, she rose to her current position. Even with the Charming Blue Goddess in such poor condition and the church's bleak prospects, she never considered leaving.”

“In truth, an Archbishop is firmly among the high-ranking members in any church.”

“More crucially, Commander, your promotion to Archbishop came after only a few prayers.”

“Compared to that, the sacrifices and efforts Zhou Zhang made are enough to bring tears to one's eyes.”

At that moment, Cang Xu couldn't help but feel sympathy for Zhou Zhang, finding her situation somewhat pitiable.

"Enough, enough, don't talk about me. Tell me about yourself, what else did you gain?" The fishman youngster wasn't interested in archbishop affairs; he was more concerned about Cang Xu.

Cang Xu held nothing back, replying with excitement: “As you saw, my magician level has risen to silver level.”

“Hmm, anything else?” The fishman youngster nodded, anticipation in his voice.

Cang Xu: ...

The fishman youngster: ???

They stared at each other for a moment, then the fishman youngster snapped out of it: “That's it?”

Cang Xu drew a light breath.

The fishman youngster asked: “So your bloodline concentration didn't increase either?”

Cang Xu shook his head: “No.”

The fishman youngster frowned slightly: “That's a shame. If we have to gather it ourselves, it'll be quite troublesome.”

Cang Xu sighed: “Commander, your situation is unique. In my view, even for a deity to enhance bloodline concentration, the cost would be steep.”

"Compared to your Surging Wave fishman bloodline, enhancing my Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline might demand an even higher price."

The fishman youngster looked surprised: “What do you mean?”

Cang Xu voiced his deduction: “Though deities are omnipotent, each possesses distinct specialties.”

“A deity's strengths are intrinsically linked to their divine nature, divine authority, inherent species, and the primary race of their believers.”

"Take the Sea Bird Church of Snow Bird Harbor, their deity was originally a Sea Bird, hence mastery of flight. Consequently, believers of the Sea Bird Church can commonly master flight spells even at lower transcendent levels.

"When it comes to the Charming Blue Goddess, being born from a mermaid, Her believers are primarily marine lifeforms. This conversely demonstrates that these domains are precisely where She excels. When the Charming Blue Goddess channels divine power, imparts experience, or enhances bloodline purity to marine life, the efficiency is undoubtedly higher than with other races."

"Especially regarding undead, most deities are inept with them, treating them with detachment or even disgust."

"So, in my case, for the Charming Blue Goddess, enhancing the bloodline purity of the Hanging Corpse Tree would likely be far more difficult than enhancing that of the Surging Wave fishman bloodline."

Chapter 781: Tan Mo Solicits Bribes

Cang Xu's reasoning was quite logical.

The fishman youngster listened, nodding slightly in agreement. “Your speculation is most likely correct.”

“But in specific circumstances, there are far too many influencing factors.”

"After all, your bloodline concentration is quite low, and the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline is only at the saint level. My Surging Wave fishman bloodline is nearly 60% pure, and it's a god level bloodline. Between saint level and god level lies the entire legend level."

“Next, let's try praying once more.”

Cang Xu frowned and waved his hands urgently: “We can't.”

He refused outright.

“This carries risks, Commander.”

“Your body and soul both require rest and adaptation.”

Cang Xu possessed considerable “experience” in this matter, having once borne not only Zhou Zhang's dual seals but also the deadly trap of Jian Gui.

The fishman youngster drew in a sharp breath and nodded solemnly.

Being held hostage would be disastrous.

Without looking far, Lady Sea Snake he'd just captured was the most obvious example.

Lady Sea Snake held the Da Ji Clan hostage, thereby seizing Qi Ling's Achilles' heel and forcing her into submission.

The fishman youngster didn't want himself, let alone the other survivors, to fall under the control of the Charming Blue Goddess!

“After all, this Resurrection divine spell, along with my understanding and experience of it, were all granted by the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Moving forward, we can seek opportunities to pray to other deities, learning their resurrection methods and wisdom.”

“By synthesizing this knowledge, we might avoid the pitfalls and reduce the risks.”

Cang Xu nodded repeatedly, praising the fishman youngster's prudent approach—truly worthy of a commander.

The fishman youngster, in turn, commended Cang Xu. This reminder from Cang Xu was exceptionally timely. No wonder he had sought to recruit him back on Mystifying Monster Island.

Almost simultaneously, on Two Eyes Island, Tan Mo was lavishing praise upon Yan Yi.

The first phase of interrogations concluded, granting temporary freedom not only to Yan Yi but even to Island Lord Hun Tong.

The two were secretly core members of their faction, so they could not be kept in the interrogation room for too long. Such detention would severely disrupt Two Eyes Island's daily operations.

Upon release, Yan Yi warmly invited Tan Mo to a feast.

After a hearty meal, Yan Yi presented Tan Mo with a substantial sum of money, carefully prepared by himself.

Satisfied and in high spirits, Tan Mo naturally showered Yan Yi with praise.

After all, saying nice things wouldn't cost a penny!

More importantly, Tan Mo needed to demonstrate his improved attitude. He had to become more enthusiastic, so others would know that the gifts he sent were effective!

Look, the results were immediate!

Rest assured.

Seeing Tan Mo's attitude shift, Yan Yi's face was plastered with smiles, his eyes subtly flashing with shrewdness.

He thought to himself: “Tan Mo, oh Tan Mo, this money won't be so easy to collect.”

Tan Mo, meanwhile, harbored even greater expectations: “If I gained so much from Yan Yi, a mere bronze level figure—”

“Then wouldn't I reap even more from Hun Tong, the gold level Two Eyes Island Lord?”

The height of his expectations matched the depth of his disappointment.

After their private meeting concluded, Tan Mo left the city government palace with a grim expression.

He carried tens of thousands of gold coins, yet his mood was foul.

The reason was simple: these tens of thousands of Devil Gold Coins were sacred artifacts and crucial evidence.

He absolutely could not lay a finger on them or embezzle them!

Hun Tong had certainly offered Tan Mo other gifts, but this sum was pitifully small.

“Not even two-thirds of what Yan Yi gave me!”

“Does Hun Tong look down on me? He thinks he can dismiss me so easily?”

“Take me for a beggar?”

"Hehe."

Tan Mo burned with humiliated fury.

At the same time, he seethed with resentment.

Hun Tong had ruled Two Eyes Island for years, amassing a fortune beyond measure.

Recently, he'd even raked in a massive war windfall!

So wealthy, yet so stingy! Had he forgotten he was still under imperial investigation?

Even for Tan Mo, finding such a prime target and opportunity for extortion was rare.

Burning with this intense resentment, Tan Mo secretly sought out Tu Ji and hinted around, “Lord Hun Tong is utterly incompetent. He's never properly handled disputes with the major merchant alliances, and the conflicts keep escalating.”

"He even dared to attack the Bandage Mercenary Corps! They've contributed on the battlefield, shedding blood for the cause. They're his comrades-in-arms! How could he bring himself to do that?"

“And his relationship with the Wealth Church has deteriorated drastically too...”

Tu Ji looked at Tan Mo and cut him off bluntly: “Lord Tan Mo, my assessment of Hun Tong is the exact opposite of yours!”

“He's been actively managing the disputes with the major merchant alliances, fiercely protecting the island's interests.”

“He likely has no money.”

“I've reviewed his intelligence reports over twenty times. If he had funds, his personality wouldn't allow such actions.”

"It's clear he didn't secure any spoils of war. The notion of him profiting massively from the conflict is a fog he deliberately released. And that, precisely, is one aspect I admire about him."

In this regard, Hun Tong had always strived to maintain a facade. For a short time, it worked well. But over the long haul, it wouldn't hold up.

After all, no matter how stingy he was, surely he hadn't neglected to refurbish the light cannon towers on Two Eyes Island or his magic flying ship, the Golden Flash?

On this point, Tu Ji was certainly not the only one to see through it; the major merchant alliances were also gradually coming to realize that Tan Mo was merely feigning ignorance.

Tu Ji sighed. “Hun Tong was simply unlucky. His scheming and maneuvering were actually quite skillful.”

“Though he repelled the fishman clan, it was a pyrrhic victory that severely depleted his forces.”

“Therefore, he decisively absorbed the Bandage Mercenary Corps and others. This allowed him to replenish his strength, restore part of his power, and simultaneously eliminate potential troublemakers before they could stir up trouble.”

“Those mercenary groups have always been ungrateful dogs that can't be tamed.”

“Finally, seizing the opportunity presented by the death of the Wealth Priest, he actively introduced the Justice Church.”

"His aim was to balance the Wealth Church by bringing in other churches, ending their previous dominance. By fragmenting religious authority, the greatest beneficiary would be himself, the Two Eyes Island Lord."

“In summary, Hun Tong is an exceptional noble, truly an empire-building elite who rose from nothing to establish the foundation of Two Eyes Island!”

During evidence collection, Tu Ji heard countless negative assessments of Hun Tong. Yet he remained unshaken, guided by his own clear and rational judgment.

He met Tan Mo's gaze with calm eyes, and the latter backed away awkwardly.

Chapter 782: Hun Tong Raises Resources

“That bastard Tu Ji...” Tan Mo had hit a brick wall with Tu Ji, and recalling it afterward made him grind his teeth in frustration.

If Tu Ji had come from any other background, Tan Mo might have had room to maneuver. But Tu Ji hailed from the Blood Light Punishment Institution, that was far too formidable. Tan Mo felt completely hamstrung.

As for the claim that “Hun Tong hadn't looted any spoils and made a killing from the war,” Tan Mo had his suspicions.

He preferred to believe the theory that Hun Tong had struck it rich.

“Even if he's strapped for cash, a leaky ship still has three tough nails. I refuse to believe I can't squeeze more out of him!”

“If I let this chance slip away, I'll regret it for the rest of my life.”

“How should I go about extorting him?”

Tan Mo began racking his brains.

If Tu Ji cooperated, he could easily manipulate Hun Tong.

But without Tu Ji's cooperation, it would be difficult.

Though Tan Mo was in the Imperial Secret Spy Agency and secretly bore the expectations of the Seventh Prince, the current situation involved investigators from the Blood Light Punishment Institution. With both sides conducting a joint investigation, neither the Blood Light Punishment Institution nor the Imperial Secret Spy Agency would believe his one-sided account.

If he resorted to outright slander or fabricated evidence, it would be pure recklessness, Tan Mo courting disaster and jeopardizing his career as a Navy Rear Admiral.

“What should I do?” Tan Mo pondered incessantly, while Hun Tong also felt the pressure mounting.

Hun Tong sought out Yan Yi and confided, “Tan Mo won't give up like this, Shadow.”

Advanced in years and seasoned by experience, Hun Tong understood well that the bribe he had offered Tan Mo would never satisfy him.

Hun Tong had long shed his youthful impetuosity. Otherwise, he wouldn't have allied with Shadow years ago, enduring sacrifices, like losing merchant ships, to gradually lure the fishman clan into attacking the island.

Hun Tong sought out Yan Yi this time specifically for assistance.

Yan Yi spread his hands helplessly. "Lord Hun Tong, I'm truly sorry, but I can do nothing more. I've practically emptied my coffers in bribes to Tan Mo, begging him to show mercy this once."

Yan Yi's refusal was delivered with tactful restraint.

This left Hun Tong seething with frustration.

He suppressed his rage, fixing Yan Yi with a piercing stare: “I know exactly how much you've given Tan Mo!”

“In a way, you raised his expectations, which is why Tan Mo is even more dissatisfied with my gift to him.”

“You should understand this logic.”

Yan Yi shook his head and sighed bitterly: “Lord, I am truly afraid.”

“I am not like you!”

“You are the lofty gold level, the de facto ruler of Two Eyes Island, your position unshakable.”

“And me?”

“I'm merely a bronze level. Being the representative of this sea area already feels like walking on thin ice.”

"This time, Tan Mo, as a secret agent, can't easily act against you. But if he harbors ill will toward me and reports it to higher-ups, I'm finished!"

“Tell me, in this situation, what choice do I have but to give it my all?”

“If I hold back and fail to satisfy Tan Mo, leading to my dismissal or even imprisonment, I'll regret it for the rest of my life!”

Yan Yi's almost tearful plea softened Hun Tong's expression considerably.

He continued, “On the contrary, if I satisfy Tan Mo this time and earn his favorable evaluation—”

“Who knows? My current standing might solidify considerably. Perhaps I could even advance to the next level in the future.”

At this point, a hint of longing flickered across Yan Yi's face.

Hun Tong inwardly sneered, thinking: You're just a bronze level, and you want to climb higher? What are you dreaming about? Don't you have any self-awareness?

Hun Tong had calmed down. He thought: I shouldn't push Yan Yi too hard. After all, Yan Yi and Tan Mo are members of the same organization.

But he was desperately short of money.

There was simply no other way.

Hun Tong fixed Yan Yi with a fierce glare, his voice low and menacing. “My predicament stems directly from heeding your advice and letting that necromancer escape.”

“You guaranteed me you could eliminate him.”

“And what happened?”

“Tan Mo needs a pretext to strike against me, my failure to stop the necromancer is perfect!”

“Shouldn't you be held accountable for your flawed command?”

Yan Yi hurriedly protested, “Lord Hun Tong, you mustn't speak recklessly.”

“I was no longer responsible for the necromancer's subsequent actions.”

“How could I have known the new representative would act this way?”

"Besides, war is unpredictable. Anything can happen on the battlefield. A minor mishap can even sway the final outcome."

“On this point, your Lord should understand it best, and feel it most keenly.”

“The primary reason for your investigation wasn't just the necromancer, but the Devil Gold Coins.”

Hun Tong snorted coldly, cutting him off: “The issue now is that Tan Mo won't let me off the hook.”

“We are one. If he won't spare me, how could he spare you?”

"He intends to make things difficult for me. He'll certainly tamper with the final investigation report. If he can give me a low evaluation, do you think you can escape unscathed?"

Yan Yi bowed his head in dejection. Raising his hands in surrender, he relented: "Lord Hun Tong, what assistance do you require from me? I shall do my utmost."

Hun Tong shook his head. “It's not about me. We must weather this storm together.”

“The real problem now isn't Tu Ji.”

"We both share Tu Ji's information. That man is upright. The true issue lies with Tan Mo."

“Hmph. That greedy bastard will never be satisfied with the payment I offered.”

"He won't let this rest. He'll twist the investigation results to his advantage!"

Yan Yi glared: “Then what can we do?”

“Feudal Lord, he's an investigator. He has a magic ship, and it's guarded by a saint level shield guardian!”

“Since we ran into him, we'll just have to accept our misfortune. Better to pay the price and avoid disaster.”

Hun Tong also glared: “What do you think I want to do?”

“Take out Tan Mo?”

“Impossible!”

“Tan Mo himself is a gold level. He made it to Navy Rear Admiral—you think he can't fight?”

"Even if we handle Tan Mo, even without the saint level shield guardian, there's still the Tu Ji. He's from the Blood Light Punishment Institution!"

“This is a joint investigation. We have no choice but to bow our heads.”

“Brother Yan Yi, you've got some nerve!”

Yan Yi was startled and waved his hands frantically. “Feudal Lord, you can't say that! I never had any dangerous thoughts!”

“I'm a member of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, the regional representative! My loyalty to the Empire is absolute!”

“Enough.” Hun Tong waved him off wearily. “Money. I need money now.”

“I plan to raise another sum to give to Tan Mo.”

“Consider this round a loan from me.”

Yan Yi wore a pained expression. “Feudal Lord, to weather this crisis, I've already used every last coin I own for bribes.”

“Where would I possibly find more money for you?”

Hun Tong's expression darkened instantly. “Stop spouting such nonsense.”

“We're fighting this together, you owe me at least this much!”

With that, Hun Tong held out his right hand, fingers splayed wide.

Yan Yi's eyes widened. He couldn't sit still any longer and sprang to his feet. “My Lord! Even if you killed me now, I couldn't produce that much!”

“It's simply impossible.”

Hun Tong sneered, convinced Yan Yi must be hiding something.

Yan Yi retorted fiercely without hesitation.

After a tense standoff, Yan Yi finally bowed to pressure, reluctantly agreeing to “lend” Hun Tong 1,600 gold coins.

This fell far short of Hun Tong's initial demand.

Yet Yan Yi volunteered to mediate, offering to facilitate friendly negotiations between Hun Tong and Tan Mo.

Considering Yan Yi's status as a secret agent, Hun Tong didn't want to push too hard.

Judging by Yan Yi's demeanor, Hun Tong also sensed he couldn't squeeze any more money out of him. Reluctantly, he nodded in agreement.

“1,600 gold coins alone won't solve the problem.”

“We'll have to think of other ways.”

Still, Yan Yi's attitude satisfied Hun Tong.

At times, Hun Tong felt fortunate that the secret agent assigned to oversee the Two Eyes Island region was only bronze level in cultivation. This allowed him to maintain absolute initiative throughout their negotiations!

Had Yan Yi been gold level, no, even silver level, Hun Tong would never have wielded such effortless leverage and coercion.

Solutions always outnumbered difficulties.

Hun Tong devised a new plan.

He sought out another helper.

After some general conversation, Hun Tong revealed his true intent: borrowing money!

“Bishop Nei Dou,” Hun Tong said, “if you wish to establish a Justice Church parish on Two Eyes Island, now is actually the best opportunity.”

“The Treasure Chest at the dock, and this joint investigation, you should be well aware of both.”

“I myself have no issues, but to navigate this investigation, I'll need some assistance from the Justice Church...”

“As the Justice Church's bishop personally establishing a new diocese, I find it hard to believe you lack startup funds.”

Hun Tong then bluntly demanded 50,000 gold coins. Such an exorbitant sum instantly stirred resentment among the eavesdropping pilgrims.

Nei Dou, an unremarkable middle-aged man, pondered briefly before shaking his head slightly. “Fifty thousand gold coins, we simply cannot produce that amount.”

“It's far too much.”

“We're establishing a new parish; we couldn't possibly carry that much gold with us.”

“But we do understand your current predicament.”

"We are willing to assist you in overcoming this crisis. However, we can only lend a maximum of 20,000 gold coins."

“Furthermore, we demand priority missionary rights, the right to build churches anywhere, and a reserved parish territory.”

Priority missionary rights were clearly intended to counter the Wealth Church.

The right to build churches anywhere was an even more potent offensive weapon.

The right to a reserved parish was the heaviest demand, within this area, only the Justice Church may be professed.

Hun Tong made exorbitant demands of Nei Dou, and Nei Dou returned the favor.

But that's how negotiations work, as the saying goes, ask for the moon and settle for the stars.

Do not be afraid of high demands; but fear a lack of sincerity in negotiations.

Chapter 783: Tan Mo Makes Things Difficult

Both Hun Tong and Nei Dou approached the negotiations with genuine sincerity.

A fierce verbal battle ensued, with both sides trading arguments and shifting tactics. After over an hour of back-and-forth, an agreement was finally reached.

The final outcome was as follows:

The Justice Church would provide 12,000 gold coins in sponsorship in exchange for the Hun Tong's future support for missionary activities.

No priority preaching rights were granted, and church construction sites were restricted to designated areas on Two Eyes Island.

Yet the preservation of the diocese was secured.

In short, this was a victory for the Justice Church!

After all, Hun Tong had revealed his vulnerability, he desperately needed to weather this investigation. This external pressure had placed him in a severely passive position.

Of course, the Justice Church paid a price too.

The 12,000 gold coins they contributed were sponsorship fees, not loans. This meant—it was a gift. Hun Tong would never repay it.

After the negotiations concluded.

Guang Ke privately inquired of Nei Dou: “Lord, the total funds we brought for developing the new parish amount to only 20,000 gold coins.”

“Now that half has been given away, how much remains for missionary work?”

“Don't forget, constructing the church is also a massive expense.”

Nei Dou smiled: “Rest assured, this investment will absolutely be worth every penny.”

“With Hun Tong's inclination and support, we're certain to carve out a piece of the Wealth Parish.”

“As for funds, they are indeed insufficient.”

“But it doesn't matter. I've already devised a solution.”

“Oh? What is it?” Guang Ke inquired.

Nei Dou looked at him: “This is thanks to you. Before this, we secured the island lord's consent on Snake Rat Island and established a parish there.”

“Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island are quite close. Our two dioceses can be considered one entity.”

“In the future, we can raise some religious taxes from Snake Rat Island to fund the construction of the Two Eyes Island diocese.”

“Of course, funds will still be scarce. We'll endure a rather arduous pioneering period.”

“But no matter how difficult it gets, what does it matter for the cause of our Lord?”

Guang Ke's expression grew solemn: “Indeed. As long as it expands our Lord's cause, I shall always serve with utmost loyalty and wisdom. What hardship is worth mentioning?”

Nei Dou chuckled heartily, patting Guang Ke's shoulder: “Actually, there are other solutions.”

“Didn't you personally convert a mercenary commander?”

“His name was Long Fu, correct?”

“From what I understand, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the most prominent force in the fishmen offensive.”

“These men have deep connections and considerable wealth. They profited from the fishmen offensive and even purchased the magic ship White Bandage when they left the island.”

“See if you can get in touch with them?”

“Have them contribute some funds?”

Guang Ke instantly recalled the dragonman youngster's appearance. He smiled faintly: "It wasn't just the fishmen offensive. Back on Snake Rat Island, we defeated Rou Zang's pirate crew together. Their performance was already exceptionally outstanding."

“In fact, without their participation in that naval battle, we would have lost!”

“Long Fu is an extraordinary young man. He joined the faith voluntarily, possesses great insight, and has his own understanding of the doctrine.”

“I believe that with proper guidance, he will become a devout believer and a capable member of our Lord's cause!”

“If they're still active nearby, I can try to contact them.”

The dragonman youngster had left a deep impression on Guang Ke.

A very favorable one.

Besides, it hadn't been long since they parted ways.

But what Guang Ke hadn't anticipated was that despite the brief separation, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had undergone tremendous growth and transformation during that time.

The dragonman youngster in his mind had already transformed into the fishman youngster.

Clatter, clatter, clatter.

The roar of churning seawater filled his ears.

The fishman youngster sank deeper and deeper into the ocean.

“The place…”

“I dreamt of this place again.”

He surveyed his surroundings, now quite familiar with this dreamscape.

Sea water, whirlpools.

“So I must have prayed in my sleep once more?”

Though the fishman youngster was aware of his actual situation, his thoughts moved sluggishly, far slower than usual.

“Wait, what's that?”

The fishman youngster sank to a new depth on the ocean floor, discovering a massive shadow lurking within the great whirlpool.

The shadow loomed like a mountain, and he was drifting steadily toward it.

The next moment, the fishman youngster snapped open his eyes and struggled to his feet, jolted awake from his dream.

Gasp, gasp...

He gasped for breath as his racing heart gradually calmed.

“The Surging Wave fishman bloodline... its concentration has surpassed 67%.”

The fishman youngster had anticipated this moment.

This was an extraordinary achievement!

According to the characteristics of the Surging Wave fishman bloodline, once the concentration reached 60%, one could cultivate to the saint level!

Originally, the fishman youngster's level cap was at the gold level, but now his future possibilities had expanded once more.

Though his expectations had been fulfilled, the fishman youngster felt little joy in his heart.

He couldn't help but recall that enormous, mysterious shadow lurking in the depths of the great whirlpool.

“What exactly is that?”

“According to the magic images recorded, whenever I pray in my sleep, divine spells like Spiritual Navigation are cast upon me.”

“Could it be that the immense, mysterious shadow is the true form of the Charming Blue Goddess?”

“I am steadily drawing closer to Her!”

At this thought, all joy vanished from the fishman youngster's heart. Instead, a creeping chill took its place.

Tan Mo’s gaze toward Yan Yi also grew icy.

He pondered for a long time, unable to devise a good solution. Tu Ji felt like a boulder blocking his path, severely limiting his options.

It wasn’t impossible to bypass Tu Ji, but Tan Mo wasn’t satisfied with the expected results of using such methods to extort bribes.

Just as Hun Tong perceived him, his nature was greedy, he would never be content with such meager gains.

Thus, he privately sought out Yan Yi.

“Tell me everything you know about Hun Tong.”

“You've been in charge here for years and are one of his kin. You must understand him well.”

“How much leverage do you hold over him?”

Tan Mo dropped all pretense, stating his demands directly with an overbearing tone and expression.

After all, he was a gold level, while Yan Yi was merely a bronze level.

There was no need for politeness!

Excessive courtesy, for a gold level being, was tantamount to lowering one's status and inviting humiliation.

Yan Yi looked troubled. “Lord Tan Mo, as you just mentioned, Lord Hun Tong and I are kinsmen of the same clan.”

“What use would evidence against him be to me?”

“Even if I gathered it, I couldn't possibly sell it as merchandise.”

“If I betrayed my own kin, how would those around me, my subordinates and my superiors, ever look at me again?”

“Such betrayal is something I absolutely will not do!”

Yan Yi's refusal was blunt and resolute.

“Heh heh heh.” Tan Mo let out a cold laugh, glancing out the window to gaze at the sea for a moment before turning back. “Yan Yi, you have strong principles. I admire that.”

"You're also quite resourceful. The secret agent Qing Xin, codenamed ‘Lost Return,’ was originally slated to succeed you. Yet you managed to place her within the necromancer’s inner circle. The entire process was flawless, not a single issue to be found."

“But Yan Yi, though this operation adhered perfectly to protocol, do you truly believe those above will accept it without question simply because it followed the rules?”

Yan Yi’s expression shifted.

Tan Mo's voice continued to echo in Yan Yi's ears, icy cold: “You're merely a bronze level agent, yet you've wielded immense power all this time.”

“The taste of power is so sweet, you've become utterly addicted, haven't you?”

“Perhaps it has even blinded you, making you mistakenly believe you're powerful?”

“Can you truly recognize your own situation? Can you see reality clearly?”

“Let me give you a small example.”

Tan Mo's eyes flickered with murderous intent. “I could kill you now. It wouldn't take a second!”

Yan Yi clenched his teeth, fists tight, his face drained of color.

Within a minute, cold sweat beaded on his forehead.

Seeing his stubborn resolve, a flicker of surprise crossed Tan Mo's eyes.

However, the warning had been delivered. There was no need to push further. A pause would yield better results.

"Think it over carefully. I'm a reasonable man." Tan Mo eased his grip.

Yan Yi left, his face etched with anguish.

Two or three hours later, he sought out Tan Mo of his own accord, his face grim. “I've reviewed past cases. There are indeed some areas where we could make adjustments.”

“But I don't want to hand them over directly to you.”

“Lord Tan Mo, you know I'm only a lowly bronze level agent.”

“If I give you the intelligence outright, my career would be ruined.”

“Because I not only exploited the intelligence in my hands for personal gain but also framed my own kin.”

This was the world of the strong.

The foundation of strength lay in the bloodline.

Bloodline naturally forged powerful bonds between kin and a deep sense of belonging among relatives.

Thus, betraying one's own clan was utterly despicable, a vile act despised by all.

It was perfectly natural for Yan Yi to harbor such concerns.

“You wish to employ some clever method to make me ‘discover’ this intelligence myself?” Tan Mo chuckled lightly.

He made no effort to conceal his mockery.

In his view, this was utterly pointless, like taking off one's pants to fart.

Such concealment was fundamentally useless. Those in high positions judged by actions and outcomes. Yan Yi's concealment was at best a transparent veil, likely deceiving only himself and fooling no one else.

“Fine. Let me ‘discover’ that intelligence myself.” Tan Mo nodded, deciding to play along to secure Yan Yi's cooperation.

Tan Mo had no intention of aiding Yan Yi.

Yan Yi was merely a bronze level cultivator, and the two shared no bond. Yan Yi held no value worth his aid.

Besides, if he showed sympathy and spared Yan Yi, how could Tan Mo later extort Hun Tong?

Yan Yi's face darkened as he desperately pleaded his case: “I need to serve as an intermediary to facilitate future negotiations between you and Lord Hun Tong.”

Tan Mo agreed to this arrangement, inwardly sneering: No matter how much you involve yourself or how innocent you pretend to be, it won't make a difference.

Chapter 784: Yan Yi Talks Things Over

As the intermediary, Yan Yi sought out Hun Tong, concealing the fact that he had contributed intelligence. He merely stated: “I was forced by Tan Mo to act as a go-between. That damned Tan Mo, utterly despicable! Just because I'm only bronze level, he dares bully me like this!”

Hun Tong felt a surge of joy inside. He patted Yan Yi's shoulder and tried to show empathy: "You and I are on the same side. Back then, I teamed up with you because we're from the same clan."

"That bastard Tan Mo cares about nothing but money. And he's got powerful connections in the Imperial Secret Spy Agency. He's been extorting bribes for years, and no one can bring him down!"

Yan Yi stated bluntly, “Tan Mo's attitude this time is exceptionally brutal and unyielding. He demands at least 30,000 gold coins before he'll let it go.”

Hun Tong's rage boiled over: “He really dares to ask for that much!”

He'd painstakingly raised funds, yet still fell short of the 30,000 gold coins. But the gap wasn't insurmountable. To reach 30,000 gold, he'd have to dip into his own savings to make up the difference.

Yan Yi pleaded, “My Lord, circumstances are beyond our control. A step back opens up a world of possibilities.”

Hun Tong fought to suppress his rage. Under Yan Yi's imploring gaze, he finally chose compromise. “30,000 gold, truly, not a penny less?”

"Truthfully, I can produce 30,000 gold. But I fear this might only fuel Tan Mo's greed further."

“Bargaining is essential, he must understand my absolute limit!”

Yan Yi hastened to respond: “My Lord, your stance is truly most wise.”

“Rest assured, as the intermediary, I will exhaust every effort to negotiate with Tan Mo. I'll push and pull until he comprehends that 30,000 gold is the absolute bottom line.”

Hun Tong nodded. "Shadow, I have complete confidence in your work. If anyone else were the intermediary, I'd worry their words might provoke Tan Mo and worsen the situation. But you would never make such a mistake, correct?"

Yan Yi's body trembled slightly, understanding this was Tan Mo's warning. He hastily assured, "Of course not! Rest assured, Lord."

Yan Yi left Hun Tong and approached Tan Mo for negotiations.

Returning with the outcome, Yan Yi reported to Hun Tong.

Hun Tong roared in fury: "What? Tan Mo has his eye on my alchemy components? Beyond the 30,000 gold coins, he also wants the Golden Eye thrown in as a bonus?"

Yan Yi replied, “Tan Mo suggested that since Lord lacks the means to repair the Golden Flash, why not part with the Golden Eye instead?”

“He also remarked that the name alone would suit his Treasure Chest perfectly.”

Hun Tong laughed in anger.

“The Golden Eye reconnaissance component aboard the Golden Flash is an alchemy artifact of gold level! It's practically a sacred relic of the Wealth Church!”

“Because it is augmented with Appraisal!”

"It can appraise the value of everything within its scouting range. It's a uniquely effective reconnaissance tool with exceptional results."

"Its market value is conservatively estimated at 4,000 gold. And in Tan Mo's words, it's merely a ‘bonus’?"

At this point, Hun Tong fixed his gaze on Yan Yi, his expression darkening as he made no effort to conceal his anger.

Yan Yi took a step back, cold sweat pouring down his brow. “Lord, I have already negotiated the price to the absolute limit.”

“Tan Mo's greed is truly staggering.”

"Upon my return, I explicitly warned Tan Mo that this asking price would be nearly impossible to meet. I told him not to harbor any expectations."

“Perhaps, Lord, if you were to confront him directly and state your firm stance, it might make him truly recognize the reality of the situation.”

Hun Tong did not nod at Yan Yi's suggestion.

He needed an intermediary to mediate this matter.

He was reluctant to confront Tan Mo directly, partly out of distaste and pride, partly because he held the weaker hand in such negotiations. To maneuver effectively, he needed room to maneuver. Yan Yi was that room, his forward-deployed defense line.

He was well-suited for this role.

On one hand, Yan Yi held significant power and influence as the head of the secret spies in the Two Eyes Island region. On the other hand, Yan Yi was also easy to manipulate. A bronze level individual was truly insignificant in the presence of a gold level one.

After pondering for a moment, Hun Tong stated, “I won't agree regarding the Golden Eye, but I can offer a bit more in gold coins.”

“30,000 gold coins is out of the question, but 3,200 gold coins is feasible.”

"Remember, this is my absolute bottom line. Go negotiate again!"

Hun Tong dismissed Yan Yi.

Yan Yi then sought out Tan Mo. After some discussion and negotiation, he returned to Hun Tong with results.

After several rounds like this, the negotiations showed no signs of ending, instead, they deteriorated significantly.

Tan Mo's demands grew ever more exorbitant, while Hun Tong, constrained by circumstances, could only concede step by step.

But Hun Tong wasn't an emotionless puppet. Anger and fury swelled within him.

“That scoundrel Tan Mo!”

“I, Hun Tong, a feudal lord, am actually being threatened like this by him!”

"He utterly despises me, thinking I'm old and easy to bully!"

“I'm starting to envy the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. If customs here allowed it, I'd challenge him to a heroic duel!”

Yan Yi desperately tried to reason with Hun Tong.

“Take one step back and your choices will expand, my lord.”

“The future belongs to you. This setback will merely serve as a footnote in your life, highlighting your admirable flexibility and resilience.”

"We weathered the fishman invasion. We survived the crisis caused by the Devil Gold Coins. We endured such hardships. There's no need to let it all come crashing down now."

Beyond pleading, Yan Yi offered Hun Tong strategic advice.

“My Lord,” he suggested, “you could leverage external forces.”

“Those merchant alliances possess considerable capital.”

Hun Tong refused.

Yan Yi pressed on: "Since you wish to bring in the Justice Church, these groups could also be utilized. Borrow a substantial sum from them! They must have funds, especially for developing new districts."

But Hun Tong had already exhausted that avenue.

At a loss, Yan Yi suggested again: “My Lord, perhaps you could sell off some furniture, wine collections, or antiques from the city government palace?”

Hun Tong shook his head, dismissing the idea outright as utterly ridiculous. Such a move would utterly destroy his reputation as a noble.

He had truly reached his limit. Refusing to back down any further, he asked Yan Yi if he could provide some support.

Yan Yi stated he was completely penniless, the gold coins he had given earlier being his entire fortune.

Hun Tong angrily rebuked him, saying such deceitful nonsense was unnecessary in his presence.

Yan Yi reiterated that he truly had nothing left.

Hun Tong retorted, “As the head of the Secret Spy Agency here, can't you access the organization's operational funds?”

Yan Yi was alarmed: At this critical juncture, tapping into the organization's funds would be handing over weaknesses to others. Tan Mo would be delighted to learn of this.

Hun Tong's face remained cold: “I'm already in this state. Do you still have such reservations?”

“Besides, we are on the same boat. This is us weathering the storm together.”

“After this, I will compensate you tenfold!”

With no other choice, Yan Yi reluctantly agreed to try allocating some funds.

After being pressed once more, Yan Yi sought out Tan Mo and declared, “I have a solid plan to secure at least 50,000 gold pieces for you, sir!”

Tan Mo paused, then grew intensely curious.

Like Hun Tong, he too had reached his limit in this grueling negotiation, his patience nearly exhausted.

He’d expected concessions, but jumping from the agreed-upon 20,000 gold coins to 50,000? There was definitely something fishy going on!

Yan Yi looked at Tan Mo’s astonished expression, spread his hands, and said, "The one offering this 50,000 gold isn’t Hun Tong. Lord Tan Mo, allow me to introduce you."

Tan Mo's expression steadied as he fixed a deep gaze on Yan Yi, “Lead the way.”

The submarine plowed through the icy-blue lake water, cutting a path through the frigid depths laced with shards of ice.

The vessel was remarkably compact; its interior cramped even with just two occupants.

Clearly, though this small submarine was a magic ship, it was a specialized craft, nothing like the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

It was only suitable for certain specific scenarios.

Like the escape they were undertaking now.

Hidden within the submarine were Zi Di and Elder Yuan Ci.

This elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance had pale skin, a bushy beard, and a large frame, though he was very thin.

This differed significantly from Zi Di's recollection of Elder Yuan Ci.

In her memory, he had been tall and robust, boasting a booming voice he used to drown out rivals during negotiations.

Most crucially, he had been an ordinary human. Yet now, he radiated the aura of a silver level magician.

While investigating the Wisteria shop in the Ice Sculpture Capital, Zi Di fell into an ambush. Just as defeat and death seemed imminent, Elder Yuan Ci suddenly piloted a submarine, breaking through the floor's ice layer to rescue Zi Di in the nick of time.

The enemies lying in ambush for Zi Di naturally wouldn't let it go, pursuing them relentlessly.

Meanwhile, the submarine, under Elder Yuan Ci's control, sped along at breakneck speed, desperately fleeing until it finally shook off the pursuers.

During this time, Zi Di and Yuan Ci exchanged a few words, establishing a basic level of mutual trust.

“Once we pass this shattered ice layer, we'll reach my alchemy laboratory,” Yuan Ci stated.

“I never imagined the underground of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom would look like this,” Zi Di marveled.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was a magnificent capital city built upon the Ten Millennia Ice Lake. Beneath the city lay not soil, but thick, massive ice.

Yet it was more than just a solid ice layer.

As Yuan Ci explained: The Ten Millennia Ice Lake comprised four distinct ice layers.

The uppermost layer was decade ice. Yet, fortified over countless millennia by the kingdom and reinforced by numerous magic formations, it had become exceptionally resilient and thick.

Beneath lay the century ice layer, from which century ice could be mined.

Deeper still was the millennia ice layer.

And the final layer was the ten-thousand-year ice.

This ten-thousand-year ice encased the god level remains of the Ten-Thousand-Year Dragon. Even after countless millennia since its demise, its immense power continues to permeate the entire Ten Millennia Ice Lake and the Ice Sculpture Capital.

Near each ice layer lay the shattered ice layers, where fragments of frozen ice intertwine with the lake water, undergoing constant transformation

Beneath the ice lay flowing lake water and a unique cold freshwater ecosystem.

Almost all life that thrived here was magic beasts, with many species found exclusively in the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

Beyond this lay the alchemy chamber.

Chapter 785: Ice Lake National Policy

The ancestors of the snow elves who first migrated here settled near the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

The Ten Millennia Ice Lake stood as the largest and highest level resource point on the entire Ice Sculpture Island—without a doubt!

The exceptionally dense and uniquely concentrated ice element, combined with the temporal laws emanating from the Millennium Dragon's remains, had together forged this peerless natural wonder.

Clergymen worshiped here, constructing temples and spreading their doctrines.

Fighters hunted and populated here, for the flesh and blood of ice beasts serve as potent tonics for snow elf fighters.

Magicians established alchemy labs and mage towers, absorbing the abundant natural elements to convert them into magic power. They studied the unique ecology here, gaining knowledge. They harnessed the rich resources to build their homes.

Even before the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was founded, numerous magicians had already made their mark here.

Around the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, countless battles raged, countless schemes unfolded. Countless souls shed blood and tears, even sacrificing their lives.

Ultimately, the barbarians retreated, and the snow elves became the masters of this land.

A kingdom was founded.

From its inception, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's royal family balanced all factions, announcing a policy for the joint development of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

This became national policy.

The influence of this policy had been pivotal in shaping the Ice Sculpture Kingdom into what it was today. To put it dramatically, without this policy, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom of today would not exist.

The policy stipulated that the Ten Millennia Ice Lake did not belong solely to the royal family, nor would it ever be monopolized by any powerful clan in the future.

The Ten Millennia Ice Lake belonged to everyone.

Each year, permits were issued, allowing magicians, fighters, and clergymen to establish themselves within the lake.

Naturally, magicians were the most numerous.

Conservative estimates suggested over three hundred new alchemy laboratories were established on the lake each year.

Over the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's history, these laboratories had accumulated into an immense number. The royal family could not even keep an accurate count, partly due to the lake's teeming magic beasts making comprehensive oversight difficult, and partly because numerous facilities predated the kingdom's founding.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's unique ice-carving techniques were the fruits of research conducted within these laboratories.

Nearly every year, these alchemy chambers and mage towers yield groundbreaking new alchemy techniques, either refined from existing knowledge or born from entirely novel innovations.

These achievements continuously propelled the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's advancements in alchemy.

Consequently, within the frost domain of alchemy today, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom stands unparalleled, surpassing even the vast Sheng Ming Empire.

"Our merchant alliance established its flagship store in the capital long ago. At our peak, we maintained branches across the major cities of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

“After the former president was assassinated by imperial conspirators, factions like the kingdom's Alchemy Guild seized the opportunity to attack, seizing the market share we had painstakingly built here.”

Caught between these internal and external pressures, we could no longer sustain operations. Only the flagship store in the capital clung to life."

"This alchemy chamber is a remnant of the merchant alliance's former achievements. Truthfully, securing a permit to establish an ice lake alchemy chamber here wasn't difficult."

Elder Yuan Ci explained as he piloted the submarine, slowly docking alongside a colossal ice formation.

The ice mass was at least the combined volume of three blue whales.

As they drew near, a corner of the ice underwent mechanical deformation, revealing an access passage.

Elder Yuan Ci maneuvered the submarine into the passage, entering a short underwater metal tunnel that led to the landing pod.

The exit sealed shut as the chamber drained, filling with breathable air.

The submarine's hatch then opened, and both Elder Yuan Ci and Zi Di stepped out.

They entered another metal passageway. Elder Yuan Ci led the way, explaining, “This is the ice-skating laboratory constructed by the merchant alliance.”

“Its exterior is camouflaged to resemble a giant ice block, but it's actually a gold level magic ship.”

“Unlike conventional magic ships, it lacks an internal power source. Instead, it harnesses the abundant ice elements in its surroundings through elemental density flow technology to generate propulsion.”

“This makes its disguise remarkably natural. Even drifting within an ice lake, it remains undetectable to those without specific intent.”

“Imperial spies never discovered this location.”

"The place you were trapped in earlier was the Empire's secret alchemy chamber. These chambers are fixed installations, anchored atop the millennium ice layer."

Zi Di nodded slightly. “So we're currently positioned between the millennium and century ice layers.”

Clang!

No sooner had she spoken than two metal barriers slammed down, sealing her in from both ends. The metal walls on both sides closed in, forming a prison cell.

Zi Di was momentarily startled but quickly relaxed.

This reaction from Elder Yuan Ci was one she had anticipated.

Zi Di wasn't particularly nervous.

If he had wanted her dead, he wouldn't have risked saving her in the first place.

Since he had rescued her and brought her here, his sincerity was evident.

Of course, it could also be a third party with ulterior motives.

But it didn't matter.

Bringing her here had been a significant effort; there must be a purpose.

Zi Di was safe for now, far better than hanging between life and death as before.

Elder Yuan Ci said, "I'm sorry, but I cannot truly trust you yet. Even though you were the one attacked."

Zi Di nodded, her tone calm. “I understand.”

Elder Yuan Ci narrowed his eyes. "For now, I lean toward believing your intentions are genuine. Prove yourself going forward. A friendly warning: our next conversation will be crucial, it will determine whether you live or die."

Zi Di shrugged. "Truthfully, I need to verify your identity too. Let's use that method. It yields the most immediate results."

Though unspoken, Elder Yuan Ci raised an eyebrow, scrutinizing Zi Di once more. “You can refine a level zero Wisteria secret order too?”

Zi Di nodded. “Of course.”

Moments later, Elder Yuan Ci retrieved alchemy materials and slipped them through the metal bars into Zi Di's hands.

Before his eyes, Zi Di refined the Wisteria secret order.

The organizational structure of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was divided into ten levels: Level ten president, level nine vice president, level eight senior elder, level seven elder... level three representatives...

On the surface, there was no such thing as level zero.

Yet in reality, the level zero Wisteria secret order existed. To forge this order, the creator had to first sign a special Wisteria Pact and had never violated it.

Elder Yuan Ci witnessed Zi Di forge a level zero Wisteria secret order before his eyes, his expression softening considerably.

“Only a trusted confidant of the President could sign the Wisteria Pact,” Elder Yuan Ci marveled. “Yet I've never seen you before, nor heard your name.”

Zi Di shrugged slightly. “Well, I am a necromancer, after all.”

Elder Yuan Ci nodded lightly, accepting Zi Di's explanation.

“Now it's my turn,” Elder Yuan Ci declared. He proceeded to personally forge a level zero Wisteria secret order in Zi Di's presence.

As the two secret orders touched, they both emitted a purple magic glow.

Initially two separate glows, they swiftly merged into one. This fusion, involving both divine spells and alchemy techniques, served as compelling proof that both Wisteria secret orders were genuine.

Witnessing this, Elder Yuan Ci exhaled in relief, his face lighting up with joy.

He laughed heartily, waved his hand, and the metal bars rose again, restoring Zi Di's freedom. “Hahaha! Looks like I bet right this time, I didn't rescue the wrong person!”

“I knew it, I'd never seen those bastards so frantic, making such a huge fuss.”

Zi Di looked at Elder Yuan Ci, her gaze softening considerably as her earlier wariness diminished.

Stepping before the elder, she introduced herself: “I am Gui Teng. I've maintained direct contact with the former President but handled no daily affairs.”

“My cultivation relies on undead resources supplied through the merchant alliance's channels, a power secretly cultivated by the former President.”

“My journey to the sea was to search for the missing Miss Zi Di.”

“Little Zi Di...” Elder Yuan Ci nodded, sighing. “I know. She has become the new President and vanished at sea several months ago.”

“I too attempted to dispatch personnel. After over a month of investigation yielding no results, I decisively called it off.”

“I suspect this was a deliberate trap set by our enemies to lure out the remnants of our Wisteria Merchant Alliance.”

“Consider this: Magician Gui Teng, you came to the sea upon receiving this news, only to be ambushed by imperial spies and nearly killed.”

Zi Di fell silent. Thoughts churned in her mind as she pondered rapidly before finally speaking: “In truth, my investigations confirm that Miss Zi Di has indeed vanished.”

"Vanished alongside her were Zhen Jin, the next head of the Bai Zhen Clan, and Fei She, a level eight elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.”

Elder Yuan Ci shook his head. “Though the Bai Zhen Clan was once a great noble house in the Empire's south, it has now fallen into decline.”

“The current clan head, known as ‘Iron Knee’ and ‘The Unbowed,’ possesses only gold level cultivation. He represents the last vestiges of the Bai Zhen Clan's glory and dignity.”

“The next generation's clan head, Zhen Jin, though promoted to the Emperor's templar knight, cannot conceal his profound shortcomings, inadequate ability and a timid disposition.”

“The future of such a family is bleak. Little Zi Di's alliance with them is nothing but a desperate, misguided attempt to save a sinking ship.”

"Zhen Jin cannot serve as her protector. In fact, this maritime disaster was likely orchestrated by a major imperial faction targeting the Bai Zhen Clan. And Little Zi Di may have been caught in the crossfire."

At this point, Elder Yuan Ci sighed deeply: “Little Zi Di has fallen into a trap.”

"She voluntarily took over the position of the old President, becoming the new head of the merchant alliance. This was actually the result of the enemy deliberately letting her take the lead."

“They intended to use Zi Di as a trail to track down our remaining forces, and ultimately find what they covet most—the Wisteria Cache!”

Zi Di's heart trembled.

She had long suspected she'd fallen into a trap, so this revelation held little surprise.

Yet hearing the name “Wisteria Cache” again sent shockwaves through her heart.

The first time she'd ever heard of the Wisteria Cache was from Qing Xin. Later, she sought confirmation from Fei She, who admitted he'd only heard rumors and knew no details.

Chapter 786: The Wisteria Cache is Here?

Elder Yuan Ci was also aware of the Wisteria Cache, which didn't surprise Zi Di.

But considering her ambush and the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's arrangements at the Wisteria shop, she formed another theory!

Why would the Imperial Secret Spy Agency expend such effort to establish a presence within the Wisteria shop in the capital? The teleportation formation they set up was exceptionally sophisticated, capable of both transmission and aura suppression, undoubtedly costing a fortune.

And the alchemy laboratory within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake was guarded year-round by a gold level fighter.

Gold level!

This was no ordinary individual.

Within any faction on the Main Plane, such a level signified an extraordinary pillar of strength.

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency had invested such immense effort in this setup here, they must have sensed something, formed some conjecture. The actions of such a massive organization, even when based on conjecture, followed logic and had grounds for it.

Though Zi Di wasn't an outstanding commander like Zong Ge, as an exceptional merchant, she excelled at discerning her opponents' intentions.

From the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's actions and deployments, she deduced numerous implications.

Immediately, Zi Di turned to Yuan Ci: “I understand!”

“Elder Yuan Ci, you are the person appointed by the old President specifically to oversee the Wisteria Cache, correct?”

“The Wisteria Cache... Could it be within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake?”

Elder Yuan Ci paused, then shook his head with a smile. “What on earth are you thinking?”

Zi Di's gaze remained steady as she continued her analysis: “The Imperial Secret Spy Agency wouldn't deploy such heavy forces here for random placement or ambushes.”

"The late President's selection of the Wisteria Cache's location faced many constraints. It must be both hidden and secure, satisfying both requirements is extremely difficult."

“If placed on some deserted island or remote wilderness, we'd have to build the chamber from scratch.”

"A low-grade chamber couldn't conceal the transcendent aura of the treasure. Since it's the Wistoria Cache, it must contain vast quantities of extraordinary artifacts and precious resources."

"A high-grade chamber would require immense manpower and resources to construct. But this makes the construction process less covert, inevitably leaving traces behind."

"The ideal location would be a pre-existing, high-level secret chamber. One that's already been excavated, its contents taken by discoverers, and subsequently abandoned."

"Such a chamber could be repurposed with minor modifications and repairs. But while this approach is discreet, it isn't secure. The previous discoverers represent the greatest vulnerability. Even if those individuals are dead, they may have left behind records. Even without records, the manpower and resources expended during the renovation would still be a telltale sign."

Ever since learning of the “Wisteria Cache,” Zi Di had spent her free time pondering its location.

These were conclusions she had reached through her own independent reasoning.

Zi Di continued, “Now that I've seen the Ten Millennia Ice Lake and the ice-skating alchemy chamber, I believe the Wisteria Cache is most likely here!”

“Because this place is both secure and concealed!”

"This is beneath the Ice Sculpture Royal Capital. Even secret agents wouldn't dare excavate here openly. Any saint level entering the kingdom must be registered. If infiltration is discovered, it would cause a severe diplomatic disaster."

"This ice layer alone has four layers, teeming with magic beasts, offering limited visibility. Most crucially, alchemy chambers are as numerous as stars. Whether we build our own or find abandoned structures left by predecessors, it's incredibly easy."

“I trust the alchemy chambers our merchant alliance has established here aren't limited to just the one we're in, right?”

Though Zi Di phrased it as a question, her expression betrayed absolute certainty.

Elder Yuan Ci stared at her blankly for a moment before nodding with a bitter smile. “Since you've already deduced this much, I won't pretend anymore.”

“Indeed, I am the elder appointed by the President to oversee the Wisteria Cache!”

Zi Di's pupils contracted slightly.

“No wonder you've been hiding here all this time...”

“Precisely because the Wistoria Cache is here!” Zi Di sighed deeply before pressing, "The Imperial Secret Spy Agency is the Sheng Ming Empire's intelligence agency. I refuse to believe the entire Empire is searching for the Wistoria Cache. It must be some high-ranking figure or faction leveraging the agency to pursue their own agenda!"

“Who is this person or faction? Are they the true masterminds behind the Wisteria Merchant Alliance's downfall?”

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency was a vast organization serving the Sheng Ming Empire.

The Sheng Ming Empire's current strategy is to invade the beastman continent. A minor target like the Wistoria Cache would never be the primary objective for the entire secret spy organization to mobilize its full strength toward.

Yuan Ci sighed deeply once more and nodded. “On this point, your guess is correct.”

“Who?” Zi Di's gaze sharpened to a piercing intensity as she pressed immediately.

“The Seventh Prince.” Yuan Ci replied.

Zi Di's body and mind shook violently.

The Seventh Prince...

The son of Emperor Sheng Ming, the Son of God?

Immediately, immense doubt surged within Zi Di: "Why? Why would the Seventh Prince, such a lofty figure, come searching for a mere Wisteria Cache?"

Two Eyes Island.

A hidden location, within a secret chamber.

Guided by Yan Yi, Tan Mo secretly arrived here, where he encountered someone already waiting in the chamber.

“The bishop of the Wealth Church?” After this person introduced themselves, Tan Mo raised his eyebrows in sudden realization.

He glanced at Yan Yi and instantly understood why Yan Yi had claimed he could offer Tan Mo a full 50,000 gold in return.

The reason was simple: this man belonged to the Wealth Church!

The Wealth Church... the name alone hinted at its immense financial resources.

As for why the Wealth Church sought him out, Tan Mo had long been aware of Two Eyes Island's situation—it was all about the parish struggle.

Two Eyes Island had originally been the Wealth Church's territory. After the fishmen attacked, Two Eyes Island managed a narrow victory, but the true winner was only one: the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In that battle, the Wealth Church's top leader had been killed.

Leaderless and in the midst of chaos, this presented Hun Tong with an opportunity.

Hun Tong's disguise was flawless, convincing everyone he'd plundered the ocean floor's spoils, amassing staggering wealth until he was rolling in riches.

Seizing his moment of dominance, Hun Tong wielded political maneuvering to intimidate opponents, prying open cracks and shifting the balance. He brought in the Justice Church.

Once the Justice Church's dominance was solidified, Two Eyes Island would become a battleground between the two factions. Hun Tong, the so-called secular island lord, would then hold the upper hand, standing on the sidelines to effortlessly balance the religious powers and emerge as the ultimate winner.

This undoubtedly constituted a grave infringement upon the Wealth Church's interests.

Thus, this time, the Wealth Church secretly dispatched a bishop to take charge of the situation, leaving Hun Tong completely unaware.

“To bring a gold level Wealth Bishop to Two Eyes Island, your capabilities are truly impressive, Shadow,” Tan Mo probed.

Yan Yi hastily waved his hands, offering a few modest words.

The Wealth Bishop stated, “Major Tan Mo, with your profound wisdom, you must surely understand our position.”

Tan Mo frowned. “I grasp your motives, but I haven't seen any sincerity yet.”

The Wealth Bishop laughed heartily. “Our Wealth Church may lack manpower, may lack land, but we absolutely never lack money!”

“Fifty thousand gold. If you agree to cooperate, we can deliver thirty thousand into your hands right now.”

Tan Mo's heart skipped a beat, but his frown deepened.

Such sincerity must conceal a grand scheme!

Thus, Tan Mo did not agree immediately. Instead, he asked, “Tell me your plan.”

The Wealth Bishop and Yan Yi exchanged a glance before the former spoke: “We want to replace the island's ruler.”

Tan Mo's brow knotted into a tight knot. He hadn't expected them to play such a high-stakes game.

In his own scheme, he'd merely intended to force Hun Tong into submission and extract a sum of money.

But the Wealth Bishop aimed to remove Hun Tong entirely!

This involved the replacement of a local ruler!

In an instant, Tan Mo realized he'd been swept into a vortex.

A vortex of political struggle.

He'd become a pawn being manipulated!

His first impulse was to flee this chamber, to extricate himself from the vortex.

Yet hesitation gripped him.

After all, the promise of 50,000 gold pieces was no small sum.

Upon reflection, he began to see this as an opportunity!

“If they truly intend to eliminate Hun Tong, assassination won't suffice.”

“That's not standard practice in imperial political struggles!”

"Especially now, when the Empire is pouring all its resources into conquering the beastman continent. If internal strife became this extreme, the Wealth Church would face the wrath of Emperor Sheng Ming!"

Tan Mo quickly deduced: the intensity of this political struggle wouldn't be too great; it would remain within the bounds of established rules.

Further evidence for this deduction was himself.

If the Wealth Church intended to eliminate Hun Tong through assassination, they wouldn't have revealed this plan to an outsider like him.

After a moment of silence, Tan Mo looked at the Wealth Church Bishop: "You want to replace Hun Tong? Who do you intend to install in his place?"

This was a crucial question.

Because this concerned a fundamental founding principle of the Sheng Ming Empire, the hereditary system of nobility.

If the Wealth Church were to support an outsider, Tan Mo would turn and leave immediately.

But if the Wealth Church sought a candidate from within the Duo Mu Clan, then there might be room for discussion.

Two Eyes Island was the domain of the Duo Mu Clan; the new ruler had to be a Duo Mu Clan noble.

This was the law of the Sheng Ming Empire, the rule of the nobility!

Facing this question, the Wealth Bishop merely smiled without speaking.

Beside him, Yan Yi stepped forward slightly and bowed slightly to Tan Mo: “Lord Tan Mo, do you think I am suitable?”

Even Tan Mo, greedy as he was, was utterly stunned at the sight of Yan Yi and the Wealth Bishop's calm expression.

“Are you serious?” he snapped, glaring at the Wealth Bishop, who gave a slight nod.

Tan Mo turned to Yan Yi and declared bluntly, “You're merely a bronze member—and you dare covet the position of ruler?”

"You're even more greedy than I am!"

Tan Mo scrutinized Yan Yi as if seeing him for the first time, his eyes darting up and down in disbelief.

Yan Yi chuckled. “Precisely because I'm only bronze level that the Wealth Church finds me suitable, wouldn't you agree?”

Chapter 787: The Imperial Higher-ups’ Games

Yan Yi spoke with sincere earnestness: “I hesitated at first too.”

“But upon careful consideration, isn't it thrilling and deeply rewarding to serve as a bronze level ruler with real authority?”

“Besides, I've weighed this decision thoroughly.”

Yan Yi laid out his reasoning: “First, I am of the Duo Mu Clan. I possess the inherent legal right to inherit this domain."

"Second, I lead the secret agents in this region. I command considerable covert forces. My identity as a secret agent allows me to maintain mystery, few outsiders know my true appearance or level."

"Finally, I have backing. I have powerful support! The Seventh Prince stands behind me."

“Shut your mouth!” Tan Mo's face darkened as he roared at Yan Yi.

The mission concerning the Seventh Prince was highly classified. The Seventh Prince had issued strict orders: the search for the Wisteria Cache must be kept under the highest level of secrecy!

Yet now, Yan Yi had violated that principle, openly declaring the Seventh Prince as his backer!

How dare he?

“Your audacity knows no bounds. You're courting death, do you understand?” Tan Mo narrowed his eyes, his murderous intent unmasked.

The Wealth Bishop, however, smiled soothingly. "Lord Tan Mo, please do not overreact. We only know the Seventh Prince is your supreme leader. We know nothing else."

Yan Yi chimed in, “Lord Tan Mo, I'm a veteran spy too, overseeing this region for years. How could I make such a low level mistake?”

“I merely skirted the edge a little.”

Tan Mo sneered, taking a step back. “You're playing with fire, Yan Yi. Heh, believe me or not, a single word from me reported up the chain, no embellishment, just the plain truth, and you're finished. Completely finished!"

Yan Yi shrugged. “It doesn't matter. I'm just a lowly bronze level, a nobody so insignificant they couldn't be any smaller.”

“In the eyes of gold level powerhouses like yourself, I'm as tiny as an ant.”

“To a figure like the Seventh Prince, I'm not even dust.”

“So if I die, I die.”

“But you, Lord Tan Mo? This time, you'll likely be implicated by me. Not only will you gain nothing, but you'll never be employed by the Seventh Prince again, right?”

Yan Yi's threat caused Tan Mo's expression to darken abruptly.

Yan Yi was absolutely right!

For the Seventh Prince, countless gold level beings actively sought to attach themselves to him.

Gold level beings formed the backbone of the transcendents. Men of this caliber were never in short supply under the Seventh Prince's command.

In truth, Tan Mo had bribed higher-ups to secure this assignment. It was all to serve the Seventh Prince, to make a name for himself.

If this mission went awry, the impact on his standing with the Seventh Prince was all too clear!

“That bastard Yan Yi must have dug up a lot of dirt on me.”

“He set me up!”

“And here I am, threatened by a mere bronze level...”

Tan Mo found it unbelievable, yet he was genuinely intimidated!

Yan Yi produced a letter and handed it to Tan Mo: “I am of the Duo Mu Clan. I possess the right to inherit this place.”

“I have gained the recognition and support of the Wealth Bishop.”

"Not only that, but here is a joint letter from several major merchant alliances. Lord, please take a look."

Tan Mo unfolded the letter and scanned its contents swiftly, his gaze darkening further.

Numerous merchant alliances, large and small, operated on Two Eyes Island. The dominant ones were the five major merchant alliances: Clam Cloth, Flint, and others.

This joint letter bore many signatures. The five major merchant alliances were listed first, followed by a long string of other small and medium-sized merchant alliances.

It was no surprise that Yan Yi had secured their support.

After the fishman offensive, Hun Tong practically alienated himself from these merchant alliances. Driven by the Devil Gold Coins, he had slaughtered numerous merchants without offering any compensation afterward. He had also attempted to renegotiate many crucial contracts previously signed with the merchant alliances. Such actions severely violated commercial ethics, prioritizing his own interests above all else.

These practices had sparked intense resentment among the merchants.

“But these shrewd, cunning merchants, how dare they jointly sign their names, leaving such obvious evidence?” Tan Mo wasn't doubting the authenticity of the joint letter.

After all, that could be easily verified.

He was merely curious: “Shadow, how did you persuade these merchants to sign and produce this joint letter?”

Yan Yi replied, “Lord Tan Mo, you overestimate me.”

“How could I possibly persuade these people to put their names on it?”

“They signed it voluntarily, or more accurately, the higher-ups behind them demanded it.”

Seeing Tan Mo's lingering doubt, Yan Yi continued his explanation: “Lord Tan Mo, I assume you know that Two Eyes Island has been selected by the Empire to become a hub for future maritime transport routes, correct?”

Tan Mo nodded. “Correct.”

Yan Yi stated, “Per the Emperor's decree, Two Eyes Island will become a military-controlled zone in the future. All merchant alliances must be expelled and cannot remain.”

“But the Five Great Merchant Alliances wish to stay here.”

Tan Mo's eyes flashed with understanding. “I see!”

In the Main Plane, the Sheng Ming Empire was the most powerful force. Within the Empire, the Emperor held supreme authority.

Yet the Empire operated under a feudal system. Beyond the Emperor and the imperial family, other living gods and major noble houses held sway.

A vassal's vassal was not my vassal.

Each grand noble's domain functioned as an autonomous mini kingdom.

Compounded by the dioceses of the living gods, power became fragmented, intertwined, and mutually checked, preventing true unification.

In the Emperor's vision, Two Eyes Island was to become a militarily controlled zone, a transportation hub, serving as a pivotal point along one of the Empire's vital maritime supply routes.

This was the Emperor's vision.

Yet the imperial elite behind the Five Great Merchant Alliances, the patriarchs of those great noble houses, and the living gods themselves harbored their own ambitions.

They too sought to establish maritime transport nodes, leveraging these hubs to continuously shuttle supplies and maximize their share of the war's spoils.

Logistics proved a critical endeavor.

On one hand, transporting military forces from the homeland maximized the gains of war. On the other hand, returning spoils of war allowed for more efficient assimilation, fueling further growth in strength that could then bolster frontline forces—creating a virtuous cycle.

However, spatial teleportation came at an exorbitant cost. Alchemy technology had not yet advanced to the point of enabling the mass transport of armies and supplies.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's alchemy was undoubtedly world-class. Yet even within their own territories, teleportation formations were never the preferred method for large-scale transportation. Their primary means of transport remained the primitive caravan system.

The Emperor's vision for Two Eyes Island was rooted in the interests of the Empire and the imperial family. Meanwhile, the great entities behind the Five Great Merchant Alliances pursued their own distinct agendas. A subtle distinction existed between the two.

The higher-ups' power struggles trickled down, ultimately crystallizing into the political conflicts of Two Eyes Island.

As Yan Yi stated, he never coerced or persuaded the merchant alliances to sign the joint letter, it was an entirely voluntary act by the Five Great Merchant Alliances.

Upon grasping this truth, Tan Mo realized: “Should the struggle succeed and Hun Tong be ousted, they could genuinely preserve the fruits of their victory!”

For this falls within the bounds of legitimate political competition among nobility.

Should the Five Great Merchant Alliances prevail and elevate Yan Yi to power, it would fully comply with the established rules of the game.

Even the Emperor would find it difficult to intervene forcefully.
