This concerns the internal affairs of the Duo Mu Clan. If the Emperor wished to intervene, the optimal approach would be to undertake a similar maneuver—installing a Duo Mu Clan's member as a proxy to pursue and contend for power within the clan's internal hierarchy.

Naturally, the Emperor's might and prestige could also compel compliance through force.

Should he resort to such tactics, the Five Great Merchant Alliances and the deities backing them would recognize the situation and voluntarily step aside.

But Tan Mo remained convinced the Emperor would not act thus.

As the Empire's supreme leader, the Emperor would naturally prioritize upholding imperial order rather than violating it.

Moreover, the overall interests of the Two Eyes Island were insignificant to the Emperor. Forcing the issue would be like picking up sesame seeds while losing watermelons, no, more accurately, like picking up pebbles while losing a castle.

Ultimately, the Emperor would tacitly acknowledge the victory of the Five Great Merchant Alliances and the Wealth Church.

After grasping the situation, Tan Mo was utterly numb!

He inwardly lamented: “Heaven have mercy, I only wanted to embezzle and extort a little pocket money!”

“I never intended to get entangled in this political struggle.”

“Especially since I was aiming to align myself with the Seventh Prince, hoping to gain favor right under his nose.”

The Seventh Prince was a member of the imperial family, Emperor Sheng Ming's own son. Tan Mo had been seeking to cozy up to the royal faction.

If he accepted the invitation from the Wealth Bishop and the Five Great Merchant Alliance, what would that make him? How would the Seventh Prince view him?

Yan Yi had been carefully reading the room.

Seeing Tan Mo's shifting expression, he chuckled. “Lord Tan Mo, you may be overthinking this.”

“Truthfully... there's no need for such grim considerations.”

“Because even if you were involved in this matter, it wouldn't amount to anything in the eyes of the Seventh Prince.”

“What significance could the Two Eyes Island hold in his eyes?”

Tan Mo froze, jolted awake by Yan Yi's words.

Yes, what the Seventh Prince truly valued was the Wisteria Cache, never Two Eyes Island.

Besides, under this current plan, he could easily pose as someone “used as a pawn by others.”

After all, his reputation for greed was already widely known.

Fifty thousand gold coins.

Fifty thousand gold coins!

At this thought, Tan Mo's expression gradually steadied.

He looked at Yan Yi: “If you're not afraid of being forced into the limelight, I'm happy to help.”

"I'm not like you. I'm timid. Dragging me into such a whirlpool? I'm scared."

“You'll have to pay more!”

“Fifty thousand gold... isn't enough.”

Tan Mo shook his head firmly, his eyes gleaming with unabashed greed.

Yan Yi and the Wealth Bishop exchanged a glance, both breaking into smiles.

The hardest part of the negotiation—the first step—was already done.

What followed was merely bargaining over benefits.

Chapter 788: Zong Ge vs Hua Tang – Battle Preparations

Ice Sculpture Capital.

“Something's wrong!”

“The Wisteria shop is in serious trouble.”

Zong Ge's expression was grim.

He was well aware of Zi Di's secret mission to the Wisteria shop within the capital.

Not only that, but he was also tasked with providing backup.

Yet when Zi Di fell into an ambush and combat erupted, not a single sound reached him.

It wasn't until spatial fluctuations erupted that the disturbance could no longer be concealed, alerting Zong Ge outside the shop.

His expression changed instantly, and he was about to rush in to help.

Fortunately, in the next moment, he forced himself to suppress that impulse.

Since the spatial teleportation had already occurred and no further sounds of combat followed, it meant Zi Di had been teleported out!

"We must rescue her immediately!!" Zong Ge turned and left immediately.

He had no idea where the spatial teleportation had taken her. But he knew that the more urgent the situation, the more crucial it was to remain calm.

Truthfully, he and Zi Di had considered the possibility of an ambush before their operation.

Both had deemed it highly unlikely.

After all, this was the Ice Sculpture Capital!

Part of their confidence stemmed from intelligence.

Through direct and indirect channels like the Thieves' Guild and the Li Jian Clan, they'd purchased substantial intelligence, finding no suspicious activity related to the Wisteria shop.

Secondly, there was Zi Di's rapidly growing combat prowess.

During their journey to the capital, Zi Di's power had surged dramatically. Not only had she ascended to iron level necromancer, creating rat eye ghosts and snake shadow ghosts, but she had also seized Jia Bing's alchemy puppets. Apart from the unfinished gold level ice gargoyle, each of the twelve silver level ice gargoyles was a masterpiece. Working in tandem, they displayed remarkable synergy.

To prepare for any contingencies, after consulting with Zong Ge, Zi Di decided to imbue three of the silver level alchemy puppets with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, rendering them “invisible.”

It was with these three “invisible” ice gargoyles that Zi Di entered the Wisteria shop.

It could be said that by taking these precautions, Zi Di and Zong Ge had already anticipated the worst-case scenario.

These three “invisible” ice gargoyles indeed proved invaluable, instantly eliminating the first enemies to appear, freeing Zi Di from danger, and even seizing several gold level chains.

But the subsequent spatial teleportation was utterly unexpected.

Through the alchemy communication device, Zong Ge quickly confirmed Zi Di had fallen out of range.

“This is bad!”

“Zi Di's combat strength exceeded the enemy's expectations, forcing them to resort to spatial teleportation.”

“This was a reckless move. The spatial fluctuations can't be concealed; it will inevitably trigger an investigation from the royal capital.”

“Zi Di is also a necromancer. That alone might create an unexpected advantage.”

“Stay calm, don't panic!”

Zong Ge swiftly returned to their temporary lodgings.

He inspected and organized his equipment, preparing for the upcoming battle at a moment's notice.

First, he opened his luggage and retrieved a mass of metallic liquid.

The liquid shimmered with a golden hue, radiating a gold level aura.

This precious item had been procured by Zong Ge and Zi Di from the Li Jian Clan's vault, part of their deep cooperation agreement.

Striking Shifter!

That was the name of this metallic liquid.

It was an alchemy weapon.

Zong Ge plunged both hands into the liquid, simultaneously infusing it with gold level battle qi.

Thanks to his Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, the golden aura of his battle qi appeared as silver to outsiders.

Even when Zong Ge projected his battle qi outside his body, achieving the breakthrough where silver level energy couldn't be separated, no one could detect this flaw.

For battle qi itself is an extension of the fighter's body, still benefiting from the blessings of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

Before obtaining this molten metal, Zong Ge avoided letting his battle qi leave his body whenever possible.

Doing so would undoubtedly greatly diminish his combat effectiveness.

After acquiring this molten metal, Zong Ge's disadvantage in this regard vanished.

Under the infusion of battle qi and Zong Ge's mental manipulation, the molten metal reacted instantly, rapidly reshaping itself.

In less than a second, it transformed into two short blades, each gripped by Zong Ge's left and right hands respectively.

Zong Ge maintained the battle qi infusion while mentally manipulating the blades, transforming them into daggers, one-handed swords, short staffs, boomerangs, and more.

Then, bringing his hands together, the molten metal coalesced into a single mass, reshaping into a long-handled broadsword, then a staff, and even a musket.

This was the Striking Shifter.

Under the user's mental control, it could transform into over twenty different weapons.

Not conventional weapons, but alchemy artifacts.

It complemented Zong Ge exceptionally well, allowing him to fully leverage his prowess as a weapons master.

Thus, when Zong Ge acquired the Striking Shifter, it immediately became his primary weapon.

Zong Ge began donning armor.

Compared to his weapons, the armor fell short by a level, being only silver level.

This wasn't because the Li Jian Clan lacked gold level armor, nor because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lacked funds, it was simply unsellable.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom enforced a crucial national policy: the Armor Prohibition!

Gold level armor had a strictly limited annual production quota, with every transaction on the market requiring official documentation.

The Li Jian Clan also had no desire to sell to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The corps had complex backgrounds and mysterious, powerful patrons. Should they sell to them, any heinous acts committed by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would inevitably bring misfortune upon the Li Jian Clan.

Zong Ge first donned a silver level inner armor, then began putting on the outer armor.

Additionally, there was a chainmail.

All three pieces were silver level. The inner armor covered only the torso, while the outer armor included shoulder guards, arm guards, knee guards, leg guards, and more.

The chainmail extended over his entire body and limbs, including a hood.

Without any gold level armor, he had to stack multiple silver level pieces.

It was a necessary compromise.

Because there were compatibility issues between the three silver level pieces. Even when functioning perfectly, the combined defense of three silver level pieces still fell short of a single gold level piece.

As he donned the silver outer armor, a flicker of hesitation crossed Zong Ge's face.

He was debating whether, under these circumstances, to request aid from the Li Jian Clan.

Doing so would make it difficult to explain why Zi Di had secretly visited the Wisteria shop late at night.

Even if he concealed that fact and only requested aid, it would create a strong connection between the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. This would bring unforeseeable risks.

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance itself was trouble, and Cang Xu exposing his necromancer identity on Two Eyes Island was an even greater hidden danger!

If he concealed his identity, it would be difficult to leverage the cooperative relationship to borrow strength and influence from the Li Jian Clan.

Simply reporting it to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom authorities would also be unlikely to succeed.

Zong Ge knew the reason: he lacked crucial evidence! Even if he reported it, it wouldn't garner serious attention.

“Intense spatial fluctuations occurred within the Wisteria shop.”

"Mysterious, unexplained spatial fluctuations, such phenomena are taboo not only in the Sheng Ming Capital but in any major noble family's city! It cannot be tolerated."

“This alone should warrant an official investigation, right?”

"This secret faction attacked the caravan, sabotaged the Snow Bird Harbor alchemy workshop, targeted Shuang Lian, and finally struck against us in the capital. One crucial question remains: how exactly was Zi Di exposed?"

Zong Ge was unaware that it was the secret agents, not duelists, who had assaulted Zi Di.

His intelligence was severely lacking in this regard, leading to a misjudgment.

After pondering, Zong Ge decided to hold his position for now, waiting for an opportunity to strike.

There was little else he could do.

He had no idea where Zi Di had been teleported to.

The safest course was to wait for the official investigation. Perhaps it would spark a battle, and then Zong Ge could seize his chance.

Zong Ge's hand drifted to his right breastplate.

Beneath the triple layers of armor lay the inner lining.

Within its pockets, several pearl bubbles were concealed.

Besides this spot, others were hidden beneath his soles and near the wrists of his armguards.

Zong Ge channeled his spirit, transforming the fluid metal Striking Shifter back into its liquid state.

The liquid flowed over Zong Ge's entire body, seeping into the armor and lining, gently enveloping every pearl bubbles.

This way, even if subjected to violent impacts during fierce combat, the pearl bubbles would remain intact.

When the time came to use them, Zong Ge needed only mentally command the metallic liquid to deform, crushing the pearl bubbles. Convenient and discreet.

With this completed, the next crucial phase of battle preparations commenced.

Zong Ge entered the side chamber.

The chamber was cramped and dimly lit.

Kneeling with head bowed, Zong Ge remained silent, his heart filled with prayer.

“Great War God.”

“You are my Lord, You are my Father!”

"I heed Your teachings, follow the Way of War, and pursue the glory of victory. Now, the enemy is mysterious and unpredictable, their strength formidable."

“The battle approaches, and I am stirred, excited, and eager.”

"In this hour of preparation, I humbly beseech You: Grant me extraordinary strength, let this power flow through my blood, my soul, and my weapon. Let me battle in Your name, make me ever-vigilant, keenly perceptive, unbreakable, and unstoppable!"

"Great War God, mighty Military God, for You I shall raise the banner of victory high, I shall…"

Almost every survivor held their own distinct faith.

Zong Ge worshipped one of the living gods of the Sheng Ming Empire—the War God.

Before every crucial battle, or whenever Zong Ge was about to enter a major battlefield, he would offer such a prayer.

As he prayed earnestly, Hua Tang stood by the window, gazing across the street toward the inn where Zong Ge resided.

“Are Long Fu, Yao Ma, and their group there?” Hua Tang inquired softly.

A small, brooch shaped alchemy communication device was pinned to his collar.

Mei Lin's voice came through the device: “Yes, I can confirm it.”

“This inn is owned by the Li Jian Clan.”

“After deepening their cooperation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the Li Jian Clan holds the latter in high regard.”

“This inn ceased normal operations two days ago.”

“After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps members checked in, they appeared unremarkable on the surface but had secretly reinforced defenses extensively.”

“Hua Tang, are you truly ready to act now?”

“You've just returned, passing through numerous teleportation formations along the way. Resting overnight and adjusting your condition before moving would be more prudent.”

Hua Tang shook his head slightly. An unmistakable glint of hatred flashed in his eyes as he gritted his teeth, “But I can't wait any longer.”

“Teng Dong Lang's murderer is right before my eyes. I can't wait any longer!”

Mei Lin fell silent for a moment. “Then go. May the winds of battle be favorable to you.”

“Long Fu, I'm coming!” Hua Tang gritted his teeth, gently pushed open the window, and leaped out.

To outsiders, all that could be seen was the third-floor window opening, followed by a few pink petals drifting down.

Chapter 789: Zong Ge vs Hua Tang – Divine Blessings Bestowed

Hua Tang drifted like a feather, floating above the inn.

Few other gold level fighters mastered flight battle skills. Yet Hua Tang possessed three distinct flight battle skills.

These three battle skills covered three distinct application scenarios. The one he employed now was most suited for covert infiltration.

Hua Tang landed lightly on the inn's roof, the entire process utterly silent.

Magic formations constantly protected the inn's perimeter, and the roof was no exception.

Yet when the magic light swept over Hua Tang's feet, it naturally bypassed him without triggering a single alarm.

In his stealth state, Hua Tang stood openly on the roof, surveying his surroundings.

From his vantage point, he easily observed the patrol teams changing shifts and numerous hidden sentries crouched in corners.

With a dismissive snort, Hua Tang activated his battle skill, vanishing from his spot in an instant, leaving only a few petals drifting behind.

The next moment, he was inside the inn.

The communication device pinned to his collar had been deliberately deactivated.

The interconnected state of these alchemy devices could be detected by certain magic formations, creating a vulnerability.

With his extensive infiltration experience, Hua Tang naturally avoided such minor missteps.

Though Mei Lin's spies couldn't probe the inn's interior, the relevant structural blueprints had already been delivered to Hua Tang.

With just a glance, he etched the layout deeply into his memory.

Recalling the blueprint as he moved, his exploration was swift.

He easily evaded any patrols he encountered along the way.

“This level of personnel and security? To me, it's practically nonexistent.”

“Found it!”

Hua Tang's eyes lit up as his reconnaissance battle skill revealed Zong Ge's blurred silhouette.

“A silver level aura, and the form of a dragonman, it must be Long Fu!” Hua Tang drew a sharp breath, his eyes blazing with intensity.

Little did he know, he had been deceived by a combination of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

Hua Tang first located the room's main door. Just as he prepared to use his battle skill to push it open, the dozen or so micro-candy pellets in his mouth suddenly began to vibrate.

The vibrations were incredibly subtle, yet Hua Tang immediately sensed the anomaly.

“The shock candy has activated!”

“This means that if I continue to push through the door, I will trigger grave danger.”

“Finally, things are getting interesting.”

Hua Tang gave a cold chuckle as he attempted to decipher the trap.

Yet he found himself utterly unable to locate the crucial defensive warning mechanism.

This setback only fueled his fighting spirit further.

“My current strength and experience should be sufficient to discern any defensive devices within the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“Who would've thought I'd encounter something unprecedented while with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

"But that fits their identity, they are arms smugglers, after all. Without any real capability, how could they stir up trouble within the kingdom's market?"

“They clearly aim to break into the market and aggressively sell alchemy arms within the kingdom!”

Hua Tang cautiously tried a second time, only to fail again.

He decisively abandoned the attempt and chose a wall instead.

The opposite side also had defenses, but they were far simpler and easier to bypass than the door.

It took Hua Tang only a moment to slip through the wall.

“This is the Li Jian Clan's inn. They can't make sweeping changes or renovations here.”

“So even with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' powerful surveillance systems, they couldn't establish a complete defense network. There's always a loophole to exploit.”

Hua Tang took a deep breath and quickly scanned his surroundings.

The room's layout matched the blueprint exactly, with only a few simple pieces of furniture added.

Glancing toward the side room, Hua Tang wore his silver fox mask, his pupils constantly glowing pink. In his vision, he could make out the blurred silhouette of a dragonman kneeling in prayer.

Hua Tang didn't rush to search for Long Fu. Instead, he first ransacked the main hall, bedrooms, and bathhouse.

“Where are Long Fu's weapons and equipment?”

“Strange...”

Hua Tang had intended to steal Zong Ge's weapons and gear before the battle.

After searching thoroughly, he found nothing.

“Where did Long Fu hide these things?”

"According to intelligence, they possess gold level storage equipment. During the naval battle at Snake Rat Island, they once stored gold level cannons."

All relevant intelligence had been obtained by Hua Tang.

This was all thanks to Mei Lin's support behind the scenes.

Every action left a trace.

Since escaping Mysterious Monster Island, the survivors' movements had been meticulously tracked by those with ulterior motives. These traces had been compiled into intelligence reports for analysis and interpretation.

“Could that gold level storage equipment actually be portable?” Hua Tang couldn't help but speculate.

Typically, gold level storage equipment was massive and heavy, resembling safes anchored in hidden locations within rooms or ships.

Portable gold level storage gear, capable of being carried, generally had limited capacity.

This was dictated by the current limits of alchemy technology.

“I've been infiltrating this room for so long, yet Long Fu still hasn't detected me.”

“I can afford to be more conspicuous now.”

Hua Tang concluded it would be wiser to first steal Long Fu's equipment.

Thus, he employed another battle skill, one that keenly sensed transcendent auras, enabling him to pinpoint the locations of transcendent items.

Even when these items were concealed, he could detect faint traces.

However, the formidable detection power of this battle skill also meant it couldn't conceal its own aura.

Most of Hua Tang's equipment was designed to suppress, mask, and disguise his own aura. Even so, it could barely cover up this particular battle skill.

Battle skill - High-Energy Scent Detection!

The moment Hua Tang activated it, his nostrils immediately caught an intensely potent transcendent aura.

Not only was it potent, but multiple strands intertwined, delivering a powerful olfactory assault.

The stimulation nearly made Hua Tang sneeze violently!

He swiftly channeled his battle qi to shock his nasal passages, halting the sneeze.

Then, with a look of resentment, he turned toward the side chamber.

He had identified the source.

The source of the powerful aura emanating from the side chamber was incredibly potent.

“Did Long Fu actually equip all his gear onto himself?”

Hua Tang approached the side chamber.

Gradually, he heard the sound of Zong Ge's prayer drifting out from within— Great War God, mighty Military God, for You I shall raise the banner of victory high, I shall...

Hua Tang was suddenly filled with doubt: "According to intelligence, wasn't Long Fu a believer of the Justice Church? Yet this prayer invokes the War God."

“True, the War God's power far surpasses that of the Justice God. It wouldn't be surprising if Long Fu had converted.”

“Or perhaps he worships both deities simultaneously.”

"But regardless, both of these deities belong to the Sheng Ming Empire. These mercenaries likely hail from the Empire!"

As Hua Tang pondered this, a sudden surge of divine power rippled through the air.

A beam of divine light followed, illuminating Zong Ge's form.

“Divine blessing?!” Hua Tang gasped in shock.

Blessed by the deity, Zong Ge was now enveloped in a faint halo of divine power.

Divine blessing - Reveal Enemy Presence!

He abruptly turned, glaring toward Hua Tang's true form and bellowed, “Who goes there?!”

Empowered by the divine blessing [Reveal Enemy Presence], Zong Ge instantly detected Hua Tang.

He halted his prayer, spun around, and lunged forward, fists flying toward Hua Tang.

Hua Tang had no choice but to dodge urgently.

Agile and swift, and having prepared himself, he evaded successfully.

But Zong Ge's follow-up attacks came relentlessly, one after another.

Hua Tang continued to focus on evasion, but Zong Ge's relentless pressure forced him to resort to battle skill to counter.

Bang, bang, bang...

Zong Ge's sweeping strikes created deafening clashes, their shockwaves tearing apart the room piece by piece.

Numerous patrolmen from the Li Jian Clan rushed over to intervene.

“Don't interfere!” Zong Ge bellowed in advance, cutting off their support.

The battle scene was peculiar.

Amidst billowing dust, Zong Ge fought relentlessly, swinging his fists alone.

Amidst such overwhelming pressure, Hua Tang still maintained his invisibility. This scene was transmitted to Mei Lin’s eyes, prompting the Navy Rear Admiral to marvel: when it came to the art of stealth and concealment, Yi Jiu simply couldn’t compare to Hua Tang.

Soon, Zong Ge reduced the second floor of the inn to rubble.

Fortunately, the inn only had two stories, preventing the collapse of higher floors.

Chapter 790: Zong Ge vs Hua Tang – Flower Willow

Battle skill - Charge.

Battle skill - Fast Fight.

Battle skill - Heavy Punch.

…

Zong Ge unleashed his battle skills in rapid succession. Though each battle skill was simple and unadorned, in his hands they meshed seamlessly into a suffocating barrage of overwhelming force.

Hua Tang was forced on the defensive, dodging relentlessly as he desperately sought an opening to strike back.

Suddenly, the Striking Shifter attached to Zong Ge's body activated, instantly forming a long spear.

Battle skill - Lance Blossom!

Grasping the spear's tail, Zong Ge channeled his battle qi into the shaft, causing the razor-sharp tip to vibrate at a high frequency and thrust forward, creating a shower of golden blossoms.

Hua Tang hadn't anticipated this shift and was caught off guard.

He desperately activated a defensive battle skill to intercept.

The golden blossoms collided with the pink light wall.

The wall held for several seconds before shattering with a thunderous crash.

Hua Tang was wounded, tasting metallic sweetness in his mouth. He nearly spat out a mouthful of blood, but forced it back down.

A cold gleam flashed in Zong Ge's eyes as he pressed his advantage.

Hua Tang snorted coldly, his entire aura surging violently in that instant.

Gold level artifact - Spatial Disconnector!

Battle skill - Piercing Flower Finger!

He joined his index and middle fingers together, rapidly tapping the air before his chest.

With each tap, a crimson flower materialized out of thin air.

The flowers bloomed, revealing their centers.

Each time, Hua Tang's sword finger pierced precisely through the heart of the flower. The petals were thin, but the center was a weak point in space.

Hua Tang's sword finger attack pierced directly through the space, vanishing without a trace.

Zong Ge had already heightened his vigilance the moment he saw Hua Tang unleash his offensive battle skill. Upon witnessing this scene, he immediately surged his gold battle qi, shielding his body inside and out.

The next instant, Hua Tang's attack manifested repeatedly, striking both within and without Zong Ge's body.

Zong Ge grunted in pain, severely weakened, barely able to hold his spear.

He resolved to transform his metal spear into a massive five-bladed boomerang with a flick of his mind.

Battle skill - Auto-Target Seek.

He didn't aim and hurled the boomerang with all his might.

He took a step back, kneeling halfway on the floor.

Empowered by battle qi, the boomerang traced an arc, striking precisely toward Hua Tang.

“Don't linger in battle, the city guard are rushing this way!” Mei Lin's voice suddenly crackled through the communication device pinned to his collar.

Hua Tang's pupils contracted sharply. Mid-air, he ignored the incoming boomerang attacks and took a deep breath.

Battle skill - Flower Willow!

The next instant, five boomerang blades struck his abdomen, slicing through his gold level battle qi barrier and tearing open a long gash.

Blood spattered everywhere.

He himself coughed up a large mouthful of blood.

The blood transformed into a swirling mass of light, enveloping Zong Ge.

Though Zong Ge was entirely enveloped in battle qi, the light imprinted itself onto his protective barrier before swiftly seeping through.

Startled, Zong Ge immediately expelled his battle qi, determined to sacrifice that portion of gold level battle qi.

But most of the light had already touched his skin, etching a pattern of intertwined willow branches and blossoming buds.

“Long Fu, you've fallen victim to my battle skill. Your days are numbered.” With a sinister grin, Hua Tang vanished into thin air before his feet even touched the ground.

Zong Ge remained on high alert, his vision still clearly fixed on Hua Tang.

Hua Tang insisted on retreating, moving with astonishing speed until he vanished from Zong Ge's sight.

This clash erupted abruptly and ended just as suddenly.

Only now did the patrol personnel arranged by the Li Jian Clan begin to cry out in shock.

“What... what did I just see?”

"It was Hua Tang, right? It was definitely Hua Tang!"

“How could Hua Tang ambush Lord Long Fu? This is bizarre! Why?”

"Lord Long Fu is only silver level, yet he defeated another gold level opponent. Hua Tang isn't just any gold level fighter!"

…

Zong Ge swiftly retrieved his five-bladed boomerang and rushed to where Hua Tang had spilled his blood.

He helplessly discovered that Hua Tang's blood, scattered across the ground, had transformed into petals.

Hoping for the best, he carefully gathered and collected these petals.

The city guard captain immediately leaped to the second floor.

The upper floor of the inn had been nearly leveled.

Being only a silver level, the captain dared not adopt a superior tone toward Zong Ge, who was of equal rank. His voice carried genuine respect as he asked, "Lord Long Fu, what exactly happened? Was that gold level who just departed Hua Tang? Why did he attack you?"

A barrage of questions.

Zong Ge sighed. Though reluctant, with Zi Di absent, he had no choice but to field the city guard captain's endless questions.

At the same time.

Beneath the royal capital, deep within the frozen lake.

Inside the ice-skating alchemy chamber, Yuan Ci also questioned Zi Di: “Magician Gui Teng, after searching for so long, have you found Miss Zi Di?”

Though Zi Di had been rescued because of Yuan Ci, and the two had recently verified the level zero Wisteria secret order, Zi Di still did not place complete trust in Yuan Ci.

Gui Teng (Zi Di) shook her head and replied bitterly: "Precisely because there's no clue, I decided to find the others and mobilize more manpower for the search. The ocean is too vast. Even with more people, I have little confidence. Elder Yuan Ci, you mentioned sending people to search earlier. Any leads?"

Yuan Ci shook his head. "None. Not only did I find nothing, but after the incident, the Bai Zhen Clan also expended great effort, issuing a bounty notice and mobilizing part of the Imperial Navy's forces. Judging by their demeanor, they likely haven't made any progress either."

Zi Di was aware of the bounty notice, issued by the Bai Zhen Clan through the Sheng Ming Empire's Bounty Hunter Guild.

Even here in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, rumors had spread widely.

Unfortunately, the Bounty Hunter Guild was an organization of the Sheng Ming Empire, and such entities rarely existed in other nations. Even if they did, they operated through highly secretive offices or outposts.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom was no exception.

It maintained its own Bounty Hunter Guild, Hunter Guild, and Mercenary Guild. At most, it permitted certain commercial organizations to establish limited personnel and material networks within the kingdom. For instance, a major merchant alliance from the Sheng Ming Empire was stationed in Snow Bird Harbor.

Thus, Zi Di couldn't directly contact the Bai Zhen Clan through the Bounty Hunter Guild channel.

Even if she could, it would be highly unreliable.

Zi Di's initial choice had been the messaging unit of the Wisteria shop.

“The messaging unit is no longer usable.”

"Both Snake Rat Island and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's capital are under enemy control. The Wisteria shops on other islands are also extremely dangerous."

“We must find a new means of communication.”

"Still, the Bai Zhen Clan truly lives up to its reputation as a great noble house of the Empire's south. Though it has fallen now, its connections remain. It could even mobilize a portion of the Imperial Navy!"

With this thought, Zi Di looked at Yuan Ci, her face clouded with worry: “The Imperial Navy is formidable. Did their investigation yield anything?”

Yuan Ci gave a cold laugh: “Of course not.”

“The Sheng Ming Empire's naval might is indeed formidable, but the oceans of the Main Plane are vast beyond measure.”

“The naval forces the Bai Zhen Clan can influence are but a tiny fraction.”

"Most crucially, the Imperial Navy itself faces its own struggles. Since the Pirate King's Throne emerged, piracy has grown increasingly rampant across the Main Plane."

“The Imperial Navy scrambles to contain threats left and right, barely maintaining control and temporarily securing the stability of the Empire's vast coastline.”

“But how long can one guard against thieves?”

“Pirates launch proactive attacks, while the navy remains on the defensive, utterly passive.”

“Not to mention, the three strongest pirate crews each possess legend level combat power. Such tough nuts to crack demand extreme caution even from the Imperial Navy. One misstep, and not only might they fail to break them, but they'd end up losing teeth in the process.”

As Yuan Ci spoke these words, his eyebrows arched with a hint of schadenfreude.

As a former elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, he harbored nothing but hatred toward the Sheng Ming Empire.

"Forget about this." Elder Yuan Ci looked towards Zi Di: "You're interested in the Wisteria Cache too, right? Shall we collaborate to unlock it?"

Zi Di paused, then inquired, “Wasn't the Wisteria Cache left by the late President specifically for Miss Zi Di? Can we unlock it too?”

Elder Yuan Ci shook his head repeatedly. “No, no, no, you misunderstand.”

“The late President established the Wisteria Cache to preserve our vital energy, ensuring the Wisteria Merchant Alliance could swiftly rise again when opportunity arose.”

"The Wisteria Merchant Alliance isn't just the late President's life's work, it's the collective legacy of us founding elders."

Elder Yuan Ci continued, “The Wisteria Cache is indeed within the ice lake, but not here.”

“To truly unlock it, five level zero Wisteria secret orders are required.”

“Of course, if it is Miss Zi Di's level zero secret order, hers alone can substitute for three others.”

A flicker of emotion stirred within Zi Di, as gratitude and fond memories of her father welled up in her heart.

Outwardly, however, she remained composed, her voice barely betraying her inner turmoil as she inquired, “Counting you and me, we only have two level zero secret orders. Have you contacted others as well?”

“Indeed, I have,” Yuan Ci nodded. “But we still fall short of the five required.”

“If you're interested, we should sign a contract first. When the time is right, we can open the cache together.”

Zi Di remained silent, deep in thought.

After a moment, she finally spoke, “I need to return first. I must consider this matter carefully.”

Yuan Ci's expression shifted slightly, but he quickly regained his composure, nodding with a smile. “Alright, I'll see you off immediately!”

The departure method was teleportation.

The convenience surpassed Zi Di's expectations.

Yuan Ci explained, “Every alchemy chamber beneath the ice lake uses teleportation to connect to the surface.”

“Of course, the teleportation formations and spatial fluctuations are all meticulously recorded by the authorities.”

“Any unfamiliar spatial fluctuations would trigger an immediate investigation.”

Zi Di's first thought was the teleportation incident at the Wisteria shop.

“So that teleportation at the Wisteria shop won't trigger a kingdom investigation?” she asked.

Yuan Ci nodded. “Not unless there were unfamiliar spatial fluctuations.”

“I don't recommend reporting it to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom immediately.”

“They're from the Imperial Secret Spy Agency. Losing one base is hardly a significant loss for them.”

“And you are a necromancer, despite your clever disguise.”

“Moreover, if the Wisteria Cache is implicated, it would create a major problem for us!”

Chapter 791: Zong Ge and Zi Di Converge

“Oh, right.” Before departing, Yuan Ci offered a final reminder. “I nearly forgot to mention one small matter.”

“You know that the divine dragon's remains within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake have created an exceedingly precious temporal resource within it.”

“Within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, there exists a phenomenon where the flow of time is inconsistent.”

“Particularly during our journey through the shattered ice layers, time accelerated significantly.”

“So, when you, Magician Gui Teng, return to the capital and find time has passed much faster than you anticipated, don't be surprised.”

After hearing these instructions, Zi Di stepped onto the teleportation formation.

The next moment, the formation activated, spatial ripples rapidly forming and then vanishing.

Zi Di vanished from her original spot.

Gazing at the empty teleportation formation, Elder Yuan Ci sighed softly, murmuring, “At last, a level zero secret order has arrived.”

Zi Di's vision shifted dramatically, a wave of dizziness washing over her before fading away.

Essentially a spectral being, she differed from ordinary lifeforms, yet teleportation involved drastic shifts in spatial perception, still requiring adaptation and adjustment.

Zi Di found herself in a compact alchemy laboratory.

The crude, outdated alchemy apparatus and the dilapidated window frames and door jambs all revealed the decay of this alchemy chamber.

The spatial ripples from the teleportation gradually subsided, leaving dust swirling in the air.

The laboratory was empty, which brought Zi Di a slight sense of relief.

The room wasn't large. She spread her mental energy, easily mapping the structure, and swiftly reached the laboratory door.

Pushing it open, the light immediately became blinding.

Stepping formally outside, she found herself on a street saturated with ice and snow.

Looking up, she saw several ice sculptures lining the road, a castle in the distance, and towering ice walls beyond.

“So I'm back in the capital after all,” Zi Di murmured inwardly.

She began walking and soon merged into the flow of people.

At this early hour, the streets weren't crowded, just small groups here and there, almost all dressed as magicians.

Most of these magicians were snow elves, though occasionally humans, dwarves, or goblins could be seen.

Zi Di deliberately slowed her pace to observe them. After a moment, a thoughtful expression appeared on her face: "This street is almost entirely lined with alchemy laboratories. These magicians must be the staff here."

Alchemy labs like the one she had just left seemed to be quite numerous.

Zi Di never stopped walking, passing through several streets as the number of alchemy labs gradually decreased.

When she finally merged onto a main thoroughfare, the crowd grew dense, filled with the clamor of voices as people bought and sold, haggling over prices.

“There's a market here, mainly selling alchemy materials.” Zi Di quickly identified the market's name and pinpointed her location on the mental map she'd stored.

Of course, she had already committed the map of the Ice Sculpture Capital to memory.

Having narrowly survived an ambush, Zi Di didn't rush to reunite with Zong Ge. Instead, she entered the bustling market.

Only after observing the market, surveying prices, and chatting with numerous vendors while haggling did her mind truly settle.

“Yuan Ci really let me go.”

"Being able to craft a level zero Wisteria secret order likely means they're an ally. But it still can't be completely confirmed, especially after I was ambushed."

Having learned from her mistake, Zi Di grew more vigilant than before.

She feared this might be a scheme within a scheme, that Yuan Ci had orchestrated the rescue to gain her trust.

If Zi Di rushed to meet Zong Ge now, they could follow her or plant tracking devices on her, which would play right into their hands.

“I can't rush to meet him.”

“First, retrieve the three abandoned ice gargoyles!”

After a moment's thought, Zi Di decisively changed course.

Moments later, she approached the vicinity of the Wisteria shop once more.

She did not reveal herself immediately, halting three streets away.

This distance was sufficient for her to remotely control the three ice gargoyles.

As she had anticipated, the enemies lurking within the Wisteria shop remained oblivious to these three ice gargoyles.

Earlier, when Zi Di had been ambushed inside the shop, it was these three ice gargoyles that had instantly killed the enemies, unexpectedly yielding several gold level Chains of Confinement.

The key to this outcome lay in the ice gargoyles being imbued with a layer of Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

The synergy between these two spells yielded extraordinary results.

Even after Zi Di teleported away and lost contact with the three ice gargoyles, they remained frozen in place without alerting the enemy.

Even when the enemy swept the battlefield and searched the shops multiple times, bumping their heads against the ice gargoyles, they failed to detect them.

To be precise, they were deceived.

Despite deploying numerous alchemy reconnaissance tools and spells, they remained unable to penetrate the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell combination.

“This spell combo is truly reliable and effective. Along the way, I can't even count how many crises it helped us avoid!”

Zi Di marveled after successfully retrieving the three ice gargoyles to her side via mental remote control.

"It's a pity I lost several ice gargoyles during the battle beneath the ice lake while buying time. I can no longer assemble the full twelve gargoyle formation."

With a hint of regret, Zi Di inspected the three recovered ice gargoyles, finding them intact.

She proceeded to assign them.

One ice gargoyle remained by her side for protection, while the second was dispatched back to the Wisteria shop.

Finally, she programmed the last one to seek out Zong Ge.

“Yuan Ci told me the ambushers were imperial spies.”

“That's one side of the story. While likely true, I still need to verify it myself.”

Zi Di moved away from the Wisteria shop yet maintained constant contact with all three ice gargoyles.

Soon after, the third ice gargoyle broke free from her control and continued forward according to its pre-programmed instructions.

Zi Di had programmed its route along rooftops. Though lacking sentience, the ice gargoyle bore numerous advanced magic circuits installed by Jia Bing, enabling it to execute relatively complex temporary commands.

The enchanted Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell functioned flawlessly, guiding the ice gargoyle safely to Zong Ge's vicinity.

Zong Ge immediately spotted the ice gargoyle.

A crucial trait of Deceptive Disguise spell was its inability to deceive those with insider knowledge.

Thus, even surrounded by people, the ice gargoyle approached Zong Ge unhindered.

A silver level transcendent from the Li Jian Clan was apologizing to Zong Ge.

The ice gargoyle slammed him straight to the ground.

The silver level felt nothing, rising immediately to return to his spot—only to smash his face into the ice gargoyle's frigid, frost-covered armor.

The silver level was knocked back, his head jerking upward.

Zong Ge couldn't bear to watch. He pulled him aside, anchoring him in place to keep him from crowding the ice gargoyle.

The silver level remained oblivious, repeatedly assuring Zong Ge that he would strengthen defenses and thoroughly investigate the reasons behind Hua Tang's actions.

Zong Ge had intended to apply pressure in order to leverage more support from the Li Jian Clan, but the arrival of the ice gargoyle made him decisively end the conversation.

He descended to the inn's ground floor, entered his new room, closed the door, crushed another pearl bubble, and only then initiated communication with the ice gargoyle.

Zi Di perked up instantly, activating her alchemy communication device. Soon, Zong Ge's voice transmitted through it.

Zong Ge was direct and to the point, opening with the code phrase.

Zi Di successfully matched the code and relayed the events unfolding around her.

Zong Ge's brow furrowed. “Imperial spies ambushed you?”

“They deliberately employed the Chains of Confinement. And during your battle beneath the ice lake, your ghosts were similarly suppressed.”

“This proves they've been waiting for you all along.”

Zi Di nodded, voicing her conjecture: “The flying messaging unit here contained no trace of the ransom note. This means we never actually sent it out.”

“The station on Two Eyes Island was tampered with. The shopkeeper there, Yan Yi, is likely compromised.”

“I was ambushed only after checking the communication logs.”

"This proves they couldn't confirm my identity beforehand. It wasn't until I arrived here that they confirmed it, which is why they moved against me."

“I believe they might have mistaken me for Cang Xu.”

During the Two Eyes Island battle, Zi Di and Cang Xu had disguised themselves as unfamiliar Wisteria elders, participating in the auction and making quite a splash. Later, due to the Devil Gold Coins, Cang Xu exposed his necromancer identity while fighting Ya Ma.

Following this, under the deliberate leniency of Yan Yi, codenamed Shadow, Cang Xu boarded the Iron Lump and successfully departed Two Eyes Island, participating in the subsequent Weeping Pine sea battle.

Zi Di's conjecture was reasonable and plausible. After pondering, Zong Ge acknowledged its validity.

“You did the right thing.”

"For now, avoid meeting up with me. This prevents the enemy from discovering your connection to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

“Your priority is confirming whether those who ambushed you were imperial spies.”

“This is of utmost importance.”

Zong Ge sighed again. “I was actually hoping the royal capital would investigate the Wisteria shop, since spatial fluctuations were detected inside.”

“But now that you mention it, and considering no one from the capital has come to investigate, those spatial fluctuations must be on record.”

“Frankly, I never imagined a nation's capital would maintain such lax restrictions on spatial teleportation!”

“It poses far too many security risks.”

Zi Di, however, understood: “It's a historical legacy, I suppose.”

“When the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was founded, this became national policy. Overthrowing it would be exceedingly difficult.”

"Most crucially, too many people benefit from this policy. The kingdom's alchemy itself has flourished unrivaled, constantly innovating through countless laboratories."

“Furthermore, since spatial teleportation has never caused catastrophic failures, even those within the kingdom who recognize the risks are either isolated or beneficiaries themselves. Overthrowing this policy is exceedingly difficult.”

Chapter 792: Dangerous Situation

Zong Ge added, “Unless some powerful individual acts on their own accord.”

"Unfortunately, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's strongest are three saints. Centuries ago, the Snow Elf King was legend level. Though his strength was sufficient, his political acumen fell short. Even his own subjects privately deemed him weak-willed. He never accomplished this task."

Zi Di continued: “This incident has once again exposed a major weakness in our organization.”

“We are sorely lacking in intelligence operatives.”

“Alchemy puppets and undead creatures do offer advantages in reconnaissance, especially when enhanced with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.”

“But genuine intelligence requires living agents to gather, analyze, and orchestrate.”

"We need someone to oversee intelligence operations. Up until now, we've primarily relied on purchasing intelligence."

“This situation desperately needs improvement!”

Zong Ge immediately voiced his strong agreement.

Even now, they still knew very little about the duelists, only that they were a powerful, mysterious force.

This incident at the Wisteria shop was a costly lesson learned from insufficient intelligence. Had they detected even the slightest hint of trouble beforehand, had it raised Zi Di and Zong Ge's suspicions, the situation would never have become so perilous!

Zong Ge sighed, “None among us survivors possess the talent to lead intelligence operations.”

"If we hire outsiders, loyalty is the most critical factor. We cannot guarantee that."

“Indeed,” Zi Di sighed. “If we hired an outsider to handle our intelligence gathering and espionage, they could easily use the information we collect to reverse-engineer many of our secrets.”

“This position is critical, too important!”

"We must ensure this person's loyalty. Relying solely on magic or divine spells is ultimately unreliable."

After Zi Di finished explaining her situation, it was Zong Ge's turn to detail his circumstances.

“The attackers were formidable!” Zong Ge sighed.

"To be honest, I got lucky. At the time, I was actively preparing to rescue you. Had I not been prepped for battle, wearing armor and such, allowing the enemy to secretly steal my gear beforehand, this fight would've been brutal."

"With my strength alone, I never would've detected Hua Tang. But by chance, I received a divine blessing. My Lord granted me a divine spell at the last moment, allowing me to see through the hidden enemy."

"I wore three pieces of silver armor, yet the enemy still bypassed my defenses. He used alchemy tools combined with battle skill to directly tear open space and strike at my internal organs."

"However, I've become highly proficient in defending my vital organs. In that sense, I should perhaps even thank Blood Vengeance for its persistence."

Zong Ge had previously launched surprise attacks against the Axe Gang leader, Lin, and Jia Bing, slaying all three in a resounding victory. Yet at the final moment, he was struck by the Axe Gang leader's counterattack, falling victim to the enchantment upon the Blood Vengeance Axe—Blood Vengeance.

Blood Vengeance activated periodically, striking any part of Zong Ge's body. Through battling this curse, Zong Ge had honed his internal defenses to a high level of proficiency.

This solid foundation rendered Hua Tang's peculiar techniques ineffective.

“Our clash was actually quite brief.”

"I sensed he hadn't fully unleashed his true strength. Most likely, he withdrew voluntarily, concerned about the city guards and such.

“I had no way to keep him.”

“Moreover, before leaving, he left me a ‘gift’.”

Zong Ge's tone grew heavy as he spoke these words.

He didn't know the name of the battle skill Hua Tang had used, Flower Willow, but after examining it post-battle, he had already discovered its troublesome nature.

The black shadow formed from the flower bud and willow branch seemed branded onto his skin. It drained his vitality and battle qi every moment.

“It's merely battle qi, yet it feels alive.”

“It's taking root and sprouting within me, using my body as soil.”

“I have a premonition, when that bud blooms, it will be the day I die.”

“Therefore, before that happens, I must find a way to break free!”

Zong Ge was no stranger to peculiar abilities, yet he had never witnessed such a battle skill.

But as an exceptional commander, he possessed the composure to remain unflappable even as a mountain crumbled before him. When it came to matters of his own life and death, he discussed them as if they concerned some unrelated outsider.

Zi Di frowned deeply upon hearing this: “The Blood Vengeance afflicted upon you remains unresolved. Now another such battle skill has been added...”

“The tower spirit resides within the Deep Sea Monster Fish. We cannot access the database for the time being.”

“The most reliable approach is to find the enemy who attacked you and extract solutions from him.”

Zi Di then posed a crucial question: “Zong Ge, can you confirm it was Hua Tang?”

“According to our intelligence, Hua Tang has never demonstrated such techniques.”

Zong Ge fell silent for a moment, then spoke with deliberate gravity after deep contemplation: "I cannot confirm with certainty."

“In terms of strength, he does fit Hua Tang's profile.”

“After all, this gold level thief is quite legendary, having been active in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for so many years.”

Zi Di instantly understood what Zong Ge meant. She sighed, “Yes, you are already gold level and wield a gold level weapon.”

“Compared to that, the enemy who attacked you seems more powerful than the ‘Hua Tang’ we encountered in Snow Bird Harbor.”

“The one who attacked you is more likely Hua Tang!”

"But if that guess is true, then our previous speculation in Snow Bird Harbor was wrong. Who, then, was that gold level thief Snow Bird Harbor City Lord killed?"

Back in Snow Bird Harbor, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had faced an assassination attempt by a gold level thief.

Fortunately, at that time, the roles of Lao Zhong and Hong Teng were played by alchemy puppets.

Moreover, these puppets were imbued with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell. Consequently, the gold level thief remained convinced his assassination had succeeded.

The gold level equipment he stole bore tracking devices discreetly installed by the survivors. Yet, under the cover of Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, the thief remained utterly unaware.

Through precise tracking, the survivors pinpointed his location and alerted Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

This led to the subsequent battle where Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, leading magic squadrons and coordinating with the Sea Bird Bishop, ultimately utilized the mage tower to surround and eliminate the gold level thief.

Zong Ge attempted to analyze: “We did indeed judge this thief to be Hua Tang earlier.”

“But upon careful reflection, back in the mage tower at Snow Bird Harbor, we only detected that he possessed a Silver Fox Mask.”

“That is indeed a signature trait of Hua Tang, but beyond that, the other characteristics did not match the intelligence we had on him.”

Zi Di nodded. “At the time, Commander speculated this was Hua Tang's deliberate strategy.”

“His goal was to use these inconsistent traits to distance himself from any connection to Teng Dong Lang.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was aware of the secret alliance between Hua Tang and Teng Dong Lang.

This knowledge came courtesy of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Back then, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had proactively provided the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with two magic recordings. His aim was to draw them into action, granting him a legitimate reason to strike against the Bayonet Gang. One of those recordings depicted Teng Dong Lang and Hua Tang engaging in a friendly, private sparring session.

Zi Di sighed. "I always believed Hua Tang perished in Snow Bird Harbor. Now a new Hua Tang has emerged, and his physical features perfectly match the intelligence."

“With the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's national ceremony imminent, his timing to attack you aligns with his characteristic flamboyance.”

“Yet the techniques he used against you were never documented in our intelligence.”

Zong Ge interjected: "Techniques alone aren't conclusive. Many keep secret weapons hidden, only unleashing them when absolutely necessary."

“Given that Hua Tang has operated freely in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for so long without capture, it's entirely plausible he possesses one or two lethal techniques.”

Zi Di could tell Zong Ge leaned toward believing the attacker was the real Hua Tang. Or rather, compared to the one who died in Snow Bird Harbor, this one felt more like Hua Tang.

Recalling now, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had also mentioned something in the mage tower back then.

The gist was that even the city lord himself couldn't, or dared not to, confirm that the one who attacked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was Hua Tang.

Because many before had claimed to have slain Hua Tang, using it to make a name for themselves. Only for Hua Tang to reappear afterward, continuing his crimes and slapping many in the face. Leaving some powerful figures utterly humiliated.

“Perhaps the thief we killed belongs to this category too.”

“He acted under the guise of Hua Tang.”

This thought sparked a sudden insight in Zi Di. She voiced a new conjecture to Zong Ge: “It's possible that the gold level thief who ambushed us in Snow Bird Harbor originated from that mysterious faction.”

"So it wasn't just the commander who slew Teng Dong Lang. Earlier, we enlisted the aid of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to ambush and eliminate members of their faction."

Zong Ge listened to Zi Di's analysis through the communication device and nodded slightly. “That's certainly a possibility.”

The two continued discussing for a while, but made little progress.

“We're too short on crucial intelligence. Speculating like this is pointless.” Zong Ge cut in.

His voice grew solemn. "Right now, we need to address the issues at hand. The most troublesome situation is yours. Frankly, I'd prefer it if the Imperial Secret Spy Agency had been framed. I wish the ones who ambushed you were actually our ‘old friends’."

Zi Di sighed deeply, “I wish that were true too, but reality isn't shaped by our fantasies.”

“Judging by the current situation, even if it wasn't the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, it was likely another enemy.”

"After all, before heading to the Wisteria shop, I had reapplied the Deceptive Disguise spell. My current identity is completely severed from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

Zi Di understood Zong Ge's concern, she shared it.

The survivors' predicament was dire.

They faced annihilation by a mysterious force of immense power; a struggle the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were barely holding their ground against.

The cost had already been heavy—not only had the alchemy workshop been destroyed, but even Bu Quan, whom they'd recruited, along with her teacher and the Honey Snow Tower, had vanished.

This forced Zi Di to take a risk, offering up some of the War Merchant's alchemy creations in exchange for greater support from the Li Jian Clan.

Under these circumstances, if they provoked the Imperial Secret Spy Agency as well...

There was no doubt that under the pressure of two colossal organizations, the survivors would be crushed into dust.

“Is this the pressure Father once faced?”

“No. My situation now seems even more dire than what he endured back then.”

“How did it come to this?”

Zi Di sighed, her spirits sinking.

Once more, she found herself thinking of the dragonman youngster: "Commander, where are you now? If it were you, how would you confront this predicament?"

Chapter 793: Divine Domain Descent Spell

The roar of the tide thundered in his ears.

The fishman youngster slowly opened his eyes, finding himself once again within the great whirlpool.

The colossal vortex swirled around him yet exerted no pull whatsoever.

He descended steadily.

The deeper he went, the dimmer the light grew.

A colossal shadow, like a mountain, loomed in the depths of the ocean floor, waiting for him.

"Once again, I prayed in my sleep..."

"This is a vision guided by the divine spell, Spiritual Navigation..."

The fishman youngster's consciousness was unclear, his thought processes slowed to a fraction of their normal speed.

The shadowed mountain silhouette was swallowing him whole.

A sense of dread and terror deepened within the fishman youngster's heart.

“No, I refuse to sink any further...”

“This means I'm drawing closer to the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Wake up! Wake up now!”

He roared inwardly.

The roar began feeble and sluggish, but as he kept shouting, it rapidly intensified, soon becoming deafening.

Amidst his own roar, the fishman youngster in reality suddenly snapped his eyes open.

He jolted awake.

Huff, huff, huff...

He gasped for breath, a trace of terror still lingering on his face.

His breathing gradually steadied, his pupils slowly returning to their normal size, and his vision cleared.

Before him lay the simple furnishings of the captain's cabin.

The fishman youngster had lived here for many days. This familiar scene brought him a faint sense of security, quickly easing the lingering fear in his heart.

No drowsiness.

The fishman youngster entered the bathroom.

Warm water cascaded over him from head to toe, while he examined his own body and sorted through his thoughts.

“The concentration of my Surging Wave fishman bloodline has risen to 79%.”

“Just as I anticipated, when both my body and soul are in a state of digestion, the divine blessing transforms into an enhancement of the god level fishman bloodline's concentration.”

Even so, the fishman youngster wouldn't risk praying again.

He'd taken Cang Xu's warnings to heart, committing them to memory.

"According to the bloodline chart, the legend level requirement for the Surging Wave fishman bloodline is 90% concentration. The god level concentration benchmark is 97%."

There was no way around it, the fishmen were a lower level race.

In contrast, most upper level races could reach legend level at a 80% concentration standard, while the threshold for saint tier level was even lower.

This disparity manifested externally: upper level races produced saint and legend individuals far more frequently than lower level races.

The fishman youngster's mind stirred, and the warm water cascading over his head shifted course. It transformed into countless threads, enveloping his entire body and solidifying into a full-body armor.

It closely resembled the effect of the Flowing Wave Armor he encountered during his duel at Snow Bird Harbor.

But fundamentally, he was wielding the racial magic, Water Current Manipulation.

Having surpassed the standard concentration required for the saint level, the Surging Wave bloodline granted the youngster this entirely new racial magic.

It allowed the fishman youngster to freely manipulate any liquid around him. The range of control was vast and would continue to expand as the youngster's level increased.

Notably, this control extended beyond merely altering flow direction or speed.

Temperature, for instance.

Just now, with a mere thought, the warm water enveloping the fishman youngster rapidly cooled to room temperature.

The fishman youngster took a deep breath.

He didn't breathe through his mouth or nose, but through gills.

The heightened bloodline concentration also brought physical changes.

Gill lines now spread not only across his cheeks, but also across his chest and back, each featuring a pair of massive gill arches.

The water's temperature continued dropping, quickly shifting from room temperature to icy cold, even carrying a hint of chill.

Yet the fishman youngster felt increasingly comfortable, his mood naturally lightening into ease and joy.

It was like an exhausted traveler, after a long journey, returning home covered in dust and dirt, finally lying down on his own bed in his own bedroom.

Safe, at ease.

“In water, I feel as though I've come home.”

A resolute gleam flashed in the fishman youngster's eyes.

Suddenly summoning his focus once more, the water enveloping his body shattered violently the next instant, dispersing into countless tiny droplets.

The droplets continued to evaporate, transforming into wisps of air currents.

Within the bathroom environment, the air currents continuously liquefied, becoming visible mist.

The earlier relaxation and pleasure vanished.

The fishman youngster's eyes were filled with cool composure: “The higher the bloodline concentration, the more complete this fishman life form I transform into becomes. The sensations it brings me are unlike those of the human race."

“Take this affinity for water currents, for instance.”

“If I linger too long in this affinity, my nature will gradually shift toward that of a Surging Wave fishman.”

“This is something I must remain vigilant against!”

The fishman youngster stepped out of the bathroom. He didn't need to dry himself; a mere thought caused the water droplets clinging to his body to flow away effortlessly.

His Surging Wave fishman bloodline concentration was nearing legend level standards, yet this time, the fishman youngster felt little anticipation or excitement.

Clearly, continuing this way would likely push his bloodline concentration straight to 100%!

Even if it didn't, it didn't matter.

For now, it was sufficient.

“What needs consideration now is the direction of my next active prayer.”

“Should I go big and pray directly, letting me leap to the saint level?”

The fishman youngster had already become a gold level clergyman, but gold level and saint level were entirely different matters.

He was filled with hesitation over this choice.

Cang Xu advised him to focus on advancing his fighter and magician levels. Both of these levels were still at the silver level.

Unlike the clergyman cultivation, the battle qi and magic power gained from fighter and magician levels were the fishman youngster's own innate strength.

Should the unexpected occur, should the Charming Blue Goddess awaken and attempt to seize control of his body, these two sources of power would be his most crucial bargaining chips!

The fishman youngster found Cang Xu's reasoning compelling.

The aggressive path was to push forward relentlessly, striving for the saint level.

The prudent path was to elevate his fighter and magician levels.

“There is another option: advancing my clerical position.”

“I currently hold the position of Charming Blue Archbishop, mastering over a hundred divine spells and wielding them with proficiency.”

“Among these spells is the Resurrection Spell.”

“But I lack the Divine Domain Descent Spell!”

“And what we need most urgently right now is precisely Divine Domain Descent.”

The fishman youngster hadn't forgotten his predicament—he and his ship were trapped within the Ocean Mother Nest.

Intelligence gathered through Cang Xu revealed that the Ocean Mother Nest was actually part of the Charming Blue Divine Domain.

This situation had been deliberately created by the Charming Blue Goddess herself, who had cast Divine Domain Descent.

Therefore, to slip away unnoticed, he would need to use Divine Domain Descent to teleport himself and his ship out.

“Best to master the Divine Domain Descent first, ensuring I can leave at any moment, that's the safest bet!”

“Time's been ticking since I left the mercenary corps.”

“Wonder how much progress Zi Di and Zong Ge have made?”

“With their skills and the Deceptive Disguise spell, they should be fine.”

The fishman youngster lacked concrete, accurate intelligence and misjudged Zong Ge and the others' situation.

He remained optimistic. Even after mastering the Divine Domain Descent, he didn't plan to return immediately.

“If I can become a saint, I'll have more leverage in future atonement negotiations with the Empire. The interests I can protect will be far greater!”

The Ice Sculpture Capital.

A luxurious mansion.

In a dimly lit chamber, a magic recording played on loop.

The subject of the footage was a magician clad in robes, hood drawn to conceal their features.

“He” was bound in place by several gold level Chains of Confinement, unable to move, gradually being suppressed.

But at that very moment, the silver level magician wielding the Chains of Confinement was instantly killed on the spot.

The magician protagonist immediately broke free, even picking up the Chains of Confinement as his spoils of war.

The magic footage abruptly froze, capturing the moment the magician protagonist bent down to retrieve the Chains of Confinement.

Had Zi Di been present, she would have recognized the magician protagonist instantly, it was none other than herself.

Seated within the chamber was none other than the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's supreme commander in the Ice Sculpture Capital, codenamed [Usurper].

[Usurper] had replayed this footage over a dozen times. His brow remained furrowed, growing ever deeper with each viewing.

“What method could instantly eliminate my agent?”

“Afterwards, my men combed every inch of the shop, literally digging up the floorboards, yet found not a single clue.”

“Compared to that, the methods used after the target was teleported are crystal clear.”

Having planted traps within the Wisteria shop, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency naturally recorded the magic footage.

Yet they remained deceived.

This time, it was again the work of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

The Deceptive Disguise spell alone couldn't fool lifeless alchemy recording devices. But when combined with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, it also countered these investigative and recording methods.

“So far, every available investigative method has been employed, yet no flaws have been uncovered.”

“If we can figure out this technique, we might just crack the method Wisteria remnants used to conceal themselves.”

"It's a shame, it's not that I lack more powerful means. But using them would cause too much commotion, inevitably drawing an official investigation from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

The leader, [Usurper], hadn't truly grasped the full potential of combining the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell. This left him with many regrets.

He sighed, relinquishing his efforts, and began observing the second segment of the magic footage.

This footage depicted the harrowing struggle Zi Di faced after being teleported away. Shortly thereafter, Yuan Ci piloted a submarine to break through the ice and rescue her, the very sequence of events captured here.

“The remnant Wisteria elder, Yuan Ci, has reappeared!”

“That water rat truly knows how to hide.”

[Usurper's] gaze darkened as he recalled a particularly unpleasant experience from his past.

“Just wait a little longer. How much longer can you hide?”

“The Warm Snow Cup is about to begin. Then I'll be able to truly unleash my power, deploy ample manpower, and conduct a massive search of the lake bottom.”

“I don't believe you can stay hidden!”

Chapter 794: Ice Sculpture King

Ice Sculpture Capital.

Within an alchemy laboratory.

The coffin-like healing pod opened its lid, immediately releasing a thick cloud of mist.

From the pale green mist, Hua Tang slowly sat up, then rose and stepped out of the chamber.

“Cough, cough.” He coughed twice, a faint metallic taste of blood immediately filling his mouth.

“Your injuries require two more treatments before full recovery,” Mei Lin's voice came from the alchemy broadcast device in the corner.

Hua Tang frowned. “Long Fu is clearly only silver level, yet his attack power is entirely at the gold level.”

“This contradicts the intelligence.”

Hua Tang had reviewed the duel footage between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang.

Mei Lin dismissed this with indifference. "He also killed the Axe Gang leader, Jia Bing, and Lin. Personally, I suspect his fight with Teng Dong Lang was a carefully staged spectacle, performed under the spotlight."

“You genuinely believe he's only silver level?”

Hua Tang nodded slightly. “Perhaps you're right.”

“He possesses remarkably sophisticated disguise techniques. His true strength extends far beyond this.”

“Who knows? He might publicly announce his ‘promotion’ to gold level soon enough.”

At this point, Hua Tang sighed deeply. “I acted recklessly this time.”

“I should have heeded your advice, rested, adjusted my condition, and then launch a surprise attack.”

“Now it's too late. The mistake is done, and the commotion I've stirred will inevitably trigger a major investigation by the Kingdom's authorities.”

Mei Lin tried to console him: “Hua Tang, you really shouldn't blame yourself so much.”

“Truthfully, you didn't have many opportunities.”

"Even if you rested, could you have fully unleashed your combat power? In that surprise attack just now, you simply didn't have enough time."

“And I cannot mobilize military forces to help you breach the Li Jian Clan's defenses or pinpoint Long Fu's exact movements.”

“So, waiting longer won't grant you a suitable moment to strike either.”

"You know as well as I do that the National Ceremony is imminent. The capital's defenses will only tighten, and your status severely limits your options."

“That's precisely why I agreed to your attack.”

Hua Tang raised an eyebrow. "Oh? Wasn't it because I was seething with rage at the time, and you knew you couldn't force me to obey?"

Mei Lin chuckled softly. “Of course, I admit that played a part too.”

She continued, “I'm quite satisfied with the outcome of this mission.”

“You uncovered crucial intelligence.”

“For instance, Long Fu secretly worships the Empire's War God, not the Justice God he ostensibly serves.”

“This faith deepens his suspicion. I now strongly doubt the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is backed by arms dealers, it might be the Empire itself.”

Hua Tang's expression hardened. “You mean they could be secret agents of the Sheng Ming Empire?”

Mei Lin replied, “My suspicions began when I saw they landed at Snow Bird Harbor.”

“Within the kingdom, the political faction led by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is explicitly pro-Empire.”

“Now, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are collaborating with the Li Jian Clan, their partnership is unusually close.”

Hua Tang pondered for a moment, then nodded slightly in agreement. “Indeed, their involvement in this matter is highly suspicious.”

“Once my injuries heal, I will continue my pursuit!”

Mei Lin countered, “Hua Tang, among all duelists, you are unique.”

“Cease your reconnaissance efforts.”

“Judging by the severity of your wounds this time, Long Fu possesses the capability to eliminate you.”

“With the National Ceremony approaching, the capital's defenses and surveillance will reach peak levels. It's not the time for you to continue operating so openly.”

“Your gains from this operation are already substantial.”

“Long Fu has been struck by your Flower Willow battle skill. That's not something easily dispelled.”

Hua Tang immediately protested, unwilling to accept this: “You want me to stop now?”

“Then what was the point of me rushing back through all this hardship?”

“While the Flower Willow battle skill is unique and hard to counter, killing Long Fu outright is the most reliable solution, isn't it?”

Mei Lin sighed deeply. “We can't solve problems too directly.”

“Based on current intelligence, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is likely the Imperial Secret Spy Agency's representative force abroad!”

“If we eliminate Long Fu and wipe out the mercenary corps outright, we'll likely draw fierce retaliation from the Empire's spies.”

“And with the Empire's vast resources, dispatching another mercenary corps like this would be effortless for them.”

Hua Tang fell silent.

Their assumptions were wildly off the mark.

But it wasn't surprising.

For up until now, the strength and methods the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had displayed to the outside world had clearly surpassed those of ordinary arms smugglers.

In Mei Lin's view, which clandestine faction possessed such capabilities?

Within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, aside from the duelists, only the Imperial Secret Spy Agency possessed such capabilities.

Zi Di could never have imagined that during her moments of tension, her adversary, Mei Lin, whom she perceived as the mastermind behind the mysterious forces—was equally anxious, even hesitant to act for fear of unintended consequences.

Mei Lin stated, “To counter the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, we must employ subtle tactics.”

“We can leverage the Alchemy Guild or use Mi Fang to influence the Jing Xiang Clan, launching a full-scale blockade against the dragon rider potion trade.”

“I also plan to deploy a large number of silver level fighters to challenge Long Fu.”

Most gold level duelists were unwilling to risk challenging Long Fu. Mei Lin couldn't command them effectively either.

Besides, Mei Lin didn't want to take excessive risks, these gold level fighters were precious assets.

Her side had already suffered heavy losses.

Hua Tang's injuries served as a stark reminder to Mei Lin. Even a formidable fighter like him was now gravely wounded. What would happen if more gold level fighters arrived?

Gold level fighters were unusable, but silver level fighters were viable. Even if they suffered casualties, it wouldn't be a major issue. Such losses were bearable.

After pondering, Hua Tang approved Mei Lin's follow-up plan: “This plan is sound. The National Ceremony is imminent.”

“The National Ceremony Grand Duel will draw all aspiring warriors.”

“Long Fu has been the most prominent figure in the kingdom lately.”

"Facing a silver level challenge, he won't back down. If he refuses the duel, his reputation will plummet, losing his current favorable position."

“The reason Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' potions sell so well is largely because buyers recognize Long Fu's strength.”

“Should he avoid the fight, potion sales will inevitably plummet.”

“But if he accepts the duel, he'll have even less time to find a solution for the battle skill, Flower Willow.”

“Exhausting him with constant battles, weakening him step by step until the Flower Willow blooms, that will be his end!”

Mei Lin smiled. “There's no need to wait that long.”

“Once he's weakened sufficiently, other gold level fighters can step in and publicly challenge him.”

“Perhaps we won't even need our own people to intervene.”

“A national duel of this scale, has any ever failed to attract outsiders of gold level?”

The Ice Sculpture Castle served as the residence of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's royal family and the political heart of the entire realm.

It stood atop the Ten Millennia Ice Lake, at the very heart of the Ice Sculpture Capital.

White ice bricks formed towering walls and soaring turrets, embodying the architectural aesthetic of the snow elves.

A study.

The Snow Elf King slowly placed the intelligence report before him on the desk.

As ruler of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, he possessed the striking beauty of a pure-blooded snow elf alongside the sternness befitting a ruler.

His snow-white eyebrows were slender yet straight as swords.

Beneath them, his eyes radiated a cold, piercing light.

This cold light fell upon the figure standing across the desk, an elderly snow elf clad in white robes, his temples frosted with silver.

“Teacher,” the Snow Elf King inquired, “should we continue to retain the Wisteria Merchant Alliance?”

After the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was divided among the major factions of the Sheng Ming Empire, most of their holdings within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were likewise seized by local powers.

Only one Wisteria shop remained, the one in the royal capital.

The royal family had withdrawn.

They had detected the movements of imperial spies.

Facing the king's inquiry, the snow elf elder replied slowly, “Enemies in the open are always easier to deal with than those lurking in the shadows, Your Majesty.”

This snow elf elder was none other than the Royal Family Grand Magician.

Like the king, they were both transcendents of the saint level.

"The imperial spies struck within the shop. Who were they targeting? Do you have any idea, Teacher?" the king pressed.

The elder shook his head slightly. “With the National Ceremony approaching, it's no surprise rats have infiltrated.”

The king grew impatient, his voice rising slightly. “Teacher, you know what I mean.”

The royal grand magician sighed softly. “Your Majesty, you wish to ask: since they are enemies of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, might they be useful to us? Is that correct?”
