“But I believe, with the National Ceremony approaching, it is unnecessary to abandon the main objective for minor details.”

The king shook his head. “I want to achieve success in both the minor details and the main objective!”

“Intelligence indicates that combat didn't occur solely within the Wisteria shop. Subsequently, the secret agents initiated a teleportation, continuing their clash within the alchemy laboratory at the ice lake.”

“The target that could provoke such a major operation from the Imperial Secret Spy Agency is no ordinary matter.”

“Follow-up investigations revealed the alchemy lab was breached by a micro-submarine, followed by a chase sequence.”

“The Imperial Secret Spy Agency failed to intercept the micro-submarine. They likely withdrew voluntarily, concerned about causing too much commotion.”

"To survive an ambush, evade the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, and still receive backup, I find this faction highly intriguing."

The Royal Grand Magician nodded. “A force capable of clashing with the Imperial Secret Spy Agency is indeed formidable.”

“Your Majesty, you suspect they might be an enemy nation opposing the Sheng Ming Empire, correct?”

“But forming alliances and shifting partnerships is no simple matter.”

“Your thirteenth brother is about to return.”

The Snow Elf King's expression darkened slightly. “With the National Ceremony approaching, he ought to make an appearance.”

He turned back to the subject at hand, “I hope I can contact this power as soon as possible.”

“I have a premonition; this faction is far from ordinary.”

Chapter 795: Framing Hun Tong

“It's difficult,” the royal grand magician stated bluntly. “Your Majesty, you must be aware of the chaos within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

“Even before our nation's founding, numerous alchemy laboratories, sacred temples within the lake, and even mage towers were constructed within its depths.”

“Due to the altered flow of time within the ice lake, the concentration of ice elements and the rampant presence of magic beasts make it exceedingly difficult for us to fully survey the lake's interior.”

“In truth, detecting this much information this time is already considered quite fortunate.”

The king sighed deeply. "The Ice Lake National Policy has brought us too many hidden dangers. As the ruler of a nation, I cannot even grasp the situation within my own capital. How utterly ridiculous! For so many years, this threat has remained unresolved, only growing more formidable with time."

“Teacher, what are we to do?”

The Royal Grand Magician's expression remained impassive: "Your Majesty, the Ice Lake National Policy is the very foundation of our nation. Without it, we would not possess the unrivaled alchemy prowess that sets us apart from the world."

“This is a strategy for national strength, a sound strategy indeed.”

"Every system has its merits and drawbacks. As rulers, we must weigh all the pros and cons."

“For now, this National Ceremony is paramount—it will determine our nation's future course.”

"Your grandfather considered resolving this threat during his reign. But after deep deliberation, he feared it might undermine our very foundations and abandoned the idea."

The Snow Elf King's grandfather was the previous Snow Elf King, a legendary figure of legend power.

“Ah!” Mentioning his grandfather, the Snow Elf King sighed deeply, his expression desolate, “Then let it remain as it is for now.”

“Teacher, upon your return, do your utmost to engage with this faction.”

The Royal Grand Magician nodded. “This old servant will spare no effort!”

Watching the grand magician's retreating figure, the Snow Elf King's gaze grew profoundly somber.

The reconnaissance had yielded results. The royal grand magician's response was as expected, yet it also left the Snow Elf King with a chill.

Altering the Ice Lake National Policy would face immense resistance!

It touched upon the interests of far too many, including the royal grand magician himself.

Though the grand magician was the Snow Elf King's teacher, he was also the head of the Han Yu Clan. The Han Yu Clan was the second-greatest noble house, surpassed only by the Ice Sculpture Royal Family.

According to incomplete records, they possessed as many as 280 alchemy laboratories within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

In the past, even the legend level magician who had been the previous Snow Elf King had failed to accomplish this task. Much less the current Snow Elf King, who possessed only saint level strength.

Changing the Ice Lake National Policy was utterly impossible!

Two Eyes Island.

"You think he can topple me? Impossible! Absolutely impossible!" Hun Tong bellowed before Tan Mo.

Hun Tong possessed the composure of a lord who remained unflappable even as mountains crumbled before him, yet now he displayed a rare loss of poise.

Understandably so.

For the current situation was exceptionally dire. Hun Tong was under imperial investigation when Tan Mo suddenly ordered his arrest, accusing him of treason and collaboration with the enemy.

This charge was monumental!

"Why would I collaborate with the enemy? How could I, Hun Tong, ever betray my country?" Hun Tong shouted.

Tan Mo sneered coldly, “For your own gain, of course.”

"Two Eyes Island's strategic location makes it a prime candidate for an imperial maritime transport hub. Yet you, Hun Tong, had previously signed contracts with numerous merchant alliances."

“Once it's converted into a military transport hub, you'll be in massive breach of contract and liable for enormous gold coin compensation.”

“But unwilling to pay a single coin, you seized the opportunity during your clash with the fishmen. You feigned possession by the Devil Gold Coins and slaughtered the merchants.”

"That wasn't enough. After the war, you harassed the merchants, accusing them of desertion for breaching our defenses during the offensive and forcing them to renegotiate their commercial contracts."

Hun Tong retorted, “This is a dispute between me and that merchant alliance. How does that equate to treason?”

Tan Mo laughed heartily, “Because you have connections with certain fishmen and secretly formed an alliance with them!”

“What?” Hun Tong's eyes widened. “This is pure slander!”

“I have a witness!” Tan Mo swept his arm dramatically across the interrogation room, shouting with righteous fervor, “Bring him in!”

The next moment, two expressionless naval soldiers escorted a silver level fishman into the chamber.

This was a silver-scaled fishman.

Slender in build, he bore fins along the outer edges of his forearms, the backs of his calves, his entire fish head, and his back. These fins glowed dark silver, their edges razor-sharp like blades.

He was the blade fin fishman, Dao Hen!

During the great battle at Two Eyes Island, he was invited by the Blue Green Coral Clan to join the fight, where he performed remarkably well.

Even when Ku Feng fought him in peak condition, he still suffered defeat.

Undoubtedly a formidable fighter among the fishmen, he ultimately fell on Two Eyes Island and was taken prisoner.

Hun Tong certainly knew about Dao Hen. In fact, he had nearly executed him publicly after the battle to boost morale. But upon reflection, he spared him, keeping him as evidence to withstand the Empire's investigation, while also using him to claim border defense merits for his application to become a ruling lord.

Just as Hun Tong was about to speak, Dao Hen cut him off, pointing a finger at him, “It's you! You traitor! You despicable, shameless scoundrel!”

“You pretended to cooperate with us, but it was all for your own gain! You even tried to double-cross us...”

This tirade was clearly rehearsed, logically coherent and delivered at breakneck speed, leaving Hun Tong no chance to interrupt.

Only when he finished did Hun Tong glare at him with bloodshot eyes, fixing his gaze on Dao Hen: “I truly regret not beheading you publicly back then!”

He then turned his furious stare to Tan Mo: “You didn't think that stealing my prisoner from my dungeon and persuading him to falsely testify for me would allow you to frame an actual ruling lord, did you?”

Tan Mo smiled. "Still playing dumb? Give it up. Besides the witness, I've gathered plenty of evidence."

“Open your eyes wide and take a good look!”

Tan Mo tossed a pile of documents at Hun Tong.

Hun Tong scooped them up and scanned them furiously. " Brilliant trick! These documents were originally letters I exchanged with Shadow to contact the Vinegar Jar Pirates. You altered them slightly, and now they're evidence to frame me!"

“How bold! Don't you fear the Empire's investigation?”

Just as Hun Tong was questioning, the interrogation room door burst open. Yan Yi barged in.

Tan Mo and Tu Ji immediately frowned deeply.

Yan Yi looked panicked, quickly scanning the interrogation room before forcing himself to calm down. He squeezed out a smile and said to Tan Mo, “Lord Tan Mo, you really shouldn't be doing this.”

“I am utterly loyal to the Empire. I hold you, and every member of this Imperial Investigation Team, in the highest regard and faith.”

“You cannot act recklessly. This evidence must not be tampered with...”

“Insolent!” Tan Mo snapped, his voice laced with thin anger, cutting off Yan Yi. “Shadow, watch your tongue!”

“I want you to be clear about what you just said.”

Hun Tong's eyes lit up. He thought: “Right! If these fabricated testimonies hold up under investigation, Yan Yi will be implicated as a party involved, he won't be able to shake it off!”

Hun Tong also spoke up: "Yan Yi, be mindful of your position. If Tan Mo takes me down, do you think you'll escape unscathed?"

Tan Mo addressed Yan Yi by his Imperial Secret Spy Agency codename, aiming to leverage the organization's authority to intimidate him.

Hun Tong, however, addressed him directly by name, reminding him they were both members of the Duo Mu Clan, blood kin.

Caught between them, Yan Yi soon broke into a sweat, panic etched deeper on his face.

His gaze darted between Hun Tong and Tan Mo. He opened his mouth to speak, but time and again, no words came out.

“Coward!” Hun Tong muttered under his breath at the sight.

Tan Mo offered a mix of reassurance and warning: “Shadow, look closely. None of this evidence implicates you.”

“So don't stir up trouble where there is none.”

“What do you think you can accomplish, mere bronze level?”

“You weren't involved before, but meddle now, and you will be!”

“Hmph! A bronze level? Weak and feeble? Who cares? Get lost.”

Yan Yi's body trembled. Beyond the panic on his face, a flicker of struggle and hope flickered.

Hun Tong observed the situation and felt something amiss, so he quickly voiced out and defended Yan Yi.

Tan Mo observed Yan Yi, engaging Hun Tong in a verbal duel. For a moment, neither gained the upper hand.

Fishman Dao Hen, watching from the sidelines, felt a surge of satisfaction.

Hun Tong was undoubtedly his sworn enemy. It was Hun Tong who had seized Two Eyes Island, forcing the Blue Green Coral Clan to later offer Dao Hen a hefty sum to join their fight.

Beyond Hun Tong, figures like Tan Mo and Tu Ji from the Sheng Ming Empire were certainly no better.

The Sheng Ming Empire boasted vast influence and formidable strength, firmly establishing itself as the dominant power of the main plane. For centuries, its external actions had been relentless. Along its coastlines, countless fishman clans had been exterminated or driven into exile. Numerous fishman warriors were targeted and suppressed; many vanished mysteriously or met inexplicable deaths.

The enmity between the two sides had long been deeply entrenched.

As a member of the fishman clan, Dao Hen's stance toward the Sheng Ming Empire was inherently hostile.

“Never imagined I'd witness such a spectacle before my death.”

“Dogs fighting among themselves, heh heh heh.”

"They even made me slander and frame Hun Tong. The Sheng Ming Empire is nothing special after all, rotten and corrupt from within. Good. Absolutely perfect!"

Weak and powerless, Dao Hen was tightly guarded by naval forces on both sides, yet his heart swelled with satisfaction.

The only flaw was that this was a human race's internal strife. If he could have been the one to strike down his enemy with his own hands, the thrill of vengeance would undoubtedly have been far more satisfying.

But witnessing this scene was enough to satisfy Dao Hen.

Therefore, even though he knew he had been used by Tan Mo, he accepted it willingly and welcomed the outcome.

Hun Tong glared at Tan Mo with savage intensity, his hatred and fury plain for all to see.

He had gone to great lengths to satisfy Tan Mo's greed. He never expected Tan Mo to suddenly turn on him with such ferocity, especially since they had been bargaining just moments before.

At most, tensions had been simmering between them.

Truthfully, Tan Mo’s sudden attack had caught Hun Tong off guard, leaving him on the defensive ever since.

“Lord Tu Ji, you alone are a trustworthy investigator.” Hun Tong turned to Tu Ji, seeking his support. “You personally conducted fieldwork and inquiries. Could you possibly believe that I, Hun Tong, would betray my country?”

Chapter 796: Yan Yi's Performance

Hun Tong looked at Tu Ji, his eyes filled with expectation. He hoped Tu Ji would uphold justice, not necessarily speak on his behalf, but simply maintain impartiality.

After all, this was a joint investigation; Tan Mo's judgment wasn't the only answer.

Yet Tu Ji's expressionless face made Hun Tong sense trouble.

Tu Ji looked at Hun Tong, his tone utterly calm: “As a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, I am solely responsible for matters concerning necromancers.”

“Lord Hun Tong, whether you colluded with the fishmen and committed the crime of treason is none of my concern.”

“Lord Tu Ji!” Hun Tong gasped, his heart sinking.

Seeing Tu Ji stand idly by, Tan Mo turned his gaze to Yan Yi, a quiet respect for this bronze level figure rising within him.

After Yan Yi and the Wealth Bishop joined forces to persuade Tan Mo, Tan Mo had stated that without neutralizing Tu Ji, framing Hun Tong would be nearly impossible.

Yan Yi countered: Though Tu Ji was impartial and refused bribes from both him and Tan Mo, he still had a weakness.

That weakness lay in his identity and mission.

As a member of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, his foremost duty was hunting down necromancers.

Now, a second necromancer had escaped the chaos of Two Eyes Island and vanished without a trace.

Tu Ji failed to find the second necromancer on Two Eyes Island, meaning his mission remains unfulfilled. He had to continue the pursuit.

To continue the pursuit, he needed reliable, accurate intelligence.

And coincidentally, Yan Yi held precisely such crucial intelligence.

“If I present this intelligence as leverage, Tu Ji will surely be tempted,” Yan Yi told Tan Mo. “At that moment, with Lord Tan Mo present, please subtly imply that you could assist Tu Ji in the hunt through covert agents.”

Tan Mo agreed with Yan Yi's assessment of Tu Ji, but he was more concerned with the intelligence itself.

“The second necromancer possesses a Wisteria secret order. He's undoubtedly a remnant of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance and likely holds clues to the Wisteria Cache.”

“If we sell out his intelligence, how will we explain it to the Seventh Prince?”

Yan Yi immediately assured him, “This intelligence has absolutely nothing to do with the second necromancer.”

“Lord, you've seen the battle footage from Two Eyes Island. The Wisteria elder fought alongside Hong Zhu against Ya Ma and Yue Ban, revealing undead techniques."

“During the final chaos, Hong Zhu evacuated Two Eyes Island and vanished without a trace.”

“My intelligence reveals Hong Zhu's current whereabouts!”

“As a treasure hunter, she is currently on the Magnet Archipelago.”

“I estimate that after Lord Tu Ji obtains this intelligence, he will board your vessel for a portion of the journey before parting ways with you.”

“Such an arrangement would also facilitate your subsequent investigative mission, would it not?”

Tan Mo's face lit up with delight. He patted Yan Yi's shoulder warmly and praised sincerely, "Your plan is both logical and ingenious. Were you not merely bronze level, I'd consider recruiting you to my side."

Subsequent events unfolded precisely as Yan Yi had foreseen.

Yan Yi and Tan Mo coordinated their efforts to persuade Tu Ji.

On one hand, Yan Yi presented him with an offer he couldn't refuse. On the other, this constituted legitimate competition among imperial nobles, entirely within the established rules of the game.

Had Yan Yi not succeeded in his bid, Tu Ji would have been compelled to report the matter.

Yan Yi didn't demand Tu Ji's active cooperation either, merely asking him to stand aside and observe the power transition.

This undoubtedly eased Tu Ji's resistance considerably.

Hun Tong realized Tu Ji could no longer be his ally, a chill running through him. Suddenly it clicked: “Of course! Tan Mo suddenly framed me and severed ties, he must have laid extensive groundwork beforehand.”

"Tu Ji has been won over by them. Damn it!"

“You damned imperial parasite, you greedy devil! Do you know the crime of framing a ruling lord?” Hun Tong growled, threatening Tan Mo.

“You will pay for this! I refuse to believe the Empire will take only your word for it!” Hun Tong shouted.

Tan Mo sneered coldly: “You colluded with bandits, nurturing enemies for personal gain. Such a ruling lord is a threat to the Empire.”

“What crime have I committed?”

“I've purged a menace for the Empire, securing future maritime supply routes. I deserve credit, not blame!”

The two argued fiercely, soon red-faced and neck strained.

Yan Yi watched for a moment, then, seeing the moment was ripe, hurriedly stepped between them.

His voice trembled, high-pitched, as if he'd mustered all his courage for this: “My Lords, please stop arguing!”

“Quarrelling won't resolve your differences.”

Yan Yi turned to Tan Mo: “Lord Tan Mo, might you grant me a private word with Lord Hun Tong?”

A silence fell over the room.

Yan Yi pressed on, pleading, “Lord Tan Mo, let me work my influence. You might just get what you desire!”

Hun Tong snorted coldly behind him, but offered no rebuttal.

Tan Mo pondered for a moment before saying, "Very well. You two may discuss matters in the adjacent room. But you have only five minutes."

Yan Yi and Hun Tong then entered the adjoining private chamber.

Only the two of them remained in the room.

Yan Yi pleaded earnestly with Hun Tong: “My Lord, at this point, surely you must see Tan Mo's true colors clearly?”

“Bite your tongue and the storm will pass; step back and the sky opens wide.”

Hun Tong paced along the wall in the cramped room, growling repeatedly: "This is outrageous! Absolutely outrageous! On my own turf, he dares to humiliate me like this!"

“His appetite is too big, his audacity too great.”

“I couldn't produce gold coins meeting his standards, so he outright framed me?”

“I have my limits too! If pushed far enough, I'll strike down this wretched trash!”

Hun Tong's eyes blazed with murderous intent.

Yet Yan Yi sneered inwardly, unconcerned.

In his view: Hun Tong had truly grown old; his eyes dimmed by age. He still failed to grasp his true predicament, mistakenly believing Tan Mo was orchestrating everything.

The truth was Hun Tong had been dragged into a higher-level, larger-scale political struggle.

Yan Yi certainly didn't believe Hun Tong would resort to violence when cornered. If he intended to fight, why hadn't he done so earlier?

Yan Yi understood Hun Tong's current state and emotions perfectly, utterly enraged and humiliated after being provoked, consumed by extreme hatred toward Tan Mo, and so on.

After waiting two minutes and seeing no sign of Hun Tong calming down, Yan Yi deliberately remarked, "Lord Hun Tong, you cannot lay a hand on the Empire's investigators. If you do this, regardless of the truth, you will certainly not fare well. Do you wish to betray your country like Fei Hong?"

Fei Hong, a saint level powerhouse, had openly rebelled against the Sheng Ming Empire after protesting its tax increases. Within a day, he was captured and publicly beheaded.

The Sheng Ming Empire possessed overwhelming might; even a saint level traitor could be swiftly suppressed and executed.

At the word “treason,” Hun Tong shook his head vehemently. “How could I possibly betray my country?”

"What nonsense are you talking about? You know the truth better than anyone, I was framed!"

“Colluding with enemies? Betraying my nation? I never did any of that.”

"At most, you and your men contacted the Vinegar Jar Pirates. What does that have to do with me?"

At this point, Hun Tong paused, realizing his previous remark had been too far.

Yan Yi was now his sole ally; he didn't want to push this last remaining ally away with careless words, yet apologizing was something he simply couldn't bring himself to do.

After all, he was a mighty gold level, while Yan Yi was merely a lowly bronze level.

In the end, Hun Tong didn't apologize. Instead, he sighed deeply, his face darkening as his voice grew desolate: “I truly never imagined Tan Mo would strike so ruthlessly!”

“Nor did I expect to be bullied to this extent by outsiders on my own turf!”

Yan Yi sighed in sympathy: “Lord Hun Tong, from what I know of you, you're not the type to be stingy with money.”

“Remember when you personally subsidized merchants out of your own pocket to organize that clearance sale?”

"After the fishmen's island assault ended, you never made any major investments again. To this day, the Golden Flash and the light cannon tower remain incompletely repaired."

“You've publicly claimed to have salvaged countless treasures from the ocean depths, but in truth, that's not the case, is it?”

Hun Tong fell silent, then let out a bitter laugh. “Since you've already guessed, I might as well be frank.”

“It's true. I haven't obtained any treasures at all.”

“When I thought I'd reap a rich harvest, all I ended up with was a few copper coins.”

"Can you imagine? A few copper coins!"

By this point, Hun Tong's voice had grown sharp and intense, as if venting pent-up frustration.

He had indeed been bottling it up for far too long.

Such matters couldn't be spoken of openly. He had to put on a brave face, projecting an image of “financial strength” to maintain stability.

Only when merchants knew he had the means to compensate them would they continue negotiations. Had they known he was powerless, they would have long since pursued other avenues for compensation.

Yan Yi stepped forward, clasping Hun Tong's hand. “Lord, you have endured so much these past days.”

“Only now do I truly grasp your predicament, the weight of pressure and hardship you bear.”

“You've been through so much!”

Hun Tong instinctively tried to pull his hand back, but Yan Yi held it tightly.

He cleared his throat. “I know... you blame me deep down...”

Yan Yi cut him off. "Lord, I am deeply ashamed. You're right, I did think you should have some awareness, pay a price, and send the Imperial investigation team away."

"With your wealth, why covet the operational funds of my organization? You know, if this were discovered, I'd bear responsibility too."

“Now I see you were driven to desperation, with no other choice.”

"I only just learned Tan Mo intended to do this to you! That's why I rushed here immediately."

"To be honest, on my way here, I still harbored resentment, resenting you for not letting go, forcing Tan Mo to make such a bold move. It could easily end up dragging me into trouble too."

"If that happened, I'd be utterly wronged. After all, I emptied my entire fortune to present gifts to Lord Tan Mo."

Chapter 797: Deputy Two Eyes Island Lord!

Under normal circumstances, Hun Tong would have been displeased to be interrupted by Yan Yi like this.

But now, Hun Tong wanted nothing more than to pull Yan Yi closer, to have him speak up.

Hun Tong patted Yan Yi's shoulder. "You truly are one of our own. You're more reliable than outsiders. I'm deeply relieved you can speak your mind to me. I truly never anticipated Tan Mo would be so audacious. Framing me carries significant risks for him too."

Yan Yi replied, “At this point, Lord, we must focus on navigating the immediate crisis.”

“Since you cannot provide sufficient gold coins, perhaps... perhaps we should abandon the Golden Flash?”

Hun Tong's expression darkened instantly as he flatly refused: "Impossible! The Golden Flash cost me immense effort and sacrifice to acquire. How could I abandon it so easily?"

“Don't let Tan Mo's tactics intimidate you, he's only trying to scare me.”

Yan Yi hurriedly urged, "My Lord, Tan Mo has already moved against you! He intends to take you away. Do you still intend to resist?"

Hun Tong shook his head. "Of course I won't fight back. Resisting would only play right into Tan Mo's hands. It would only solidify the charge of treason against me."

“I will endure captivity for a time!”

“Suffer the hardship until the Empire conducts another investigation.”

"Tan Mo made a colossal mistake, he framed me for treason! Such a grave crime requires multiple investigations under Imperial protocol to verify."

“I refuse to believe Tan Mo can single-handedly cover everything up and bribe every single investigator from here on out!”

Yan Yi asked, “Lord, why must you do this?”

“Playing the prisoner, even if Tan Mo doesn't eliminate you, he'll certainly make your life miserable and subject you to torture.”

Hun Tong sneered coldly, "What's a little suffering? I am innocent! Whatever torment Tan Mo inflicts, I will repay him tenfold someday!"

Without Yan Yi's persuasion, Hun Tong still wavered. But this very argument only strengthened his resolve.

Yan Yi continued, "Lord, perhaps we should consider another approach. Since you refuse to sell the Golden Flash, why not negotiate a contract with Tan Mo? Agree to allocate a portion of Two Eyes Island's profits for the next few years, paying Tan Mo a fixed sum annually at set times."

“This way, you're essentially using future income to resolve your current crisis.”

“Not only would you avoid captivity and torture, but you'd remain ruler of the island throughout, wouldn't you?”

Hun Tong glared. “You want me to compromise?”

“The future profits of Two Eyes Island, are they so easily relinquished?”

"What about its future development? I'm short on funds now, will I be even more strapped later?"

“Besides, did I toil to build this empire just to generate profits for Tan Mo?”

Yan Yi knew Hun Tong's temperament well; such persuasion only fueled his rage.

Hun Tong snapped, "Besides, I'm not alone. I have my family too! Tan Mo has connections, do you think I don't? Does the Duo Mu Clan not have any?"

Yan Yi agreed, "You're right. We have our clan. Our clan won't abandon us. As one of the clansmen, Lord, I'll do everything in my power. Just give me your orders. Shall I send word to the clan now?"

Hun Tong raised a hand to stop him. “Wait. Don't be so hasty.”

He had reservations.

If he could have sought help from his clan, he would have done so long ago. Why endure this torment?

The truth was, Hun Tong's relationship with the Duo Mu Clan was far from harmonious.

Otherwise, he wouldn't have led his own forces out onto the vast ocean, carving out his own territory from the fishman clan.

If he could rely on the clan for help at any time, he wouldn't have strived to be self-sufficient, nor would he have leaned toward collaborating with Yan Yi during his conflicts with the fishman clan.

In truth, ever since he established himself on Two Eyes Island, the Duo Mu Clan had actively expressed their intent to send reinforcements to Hun Tong on multiple occasions.

Yet each time, Hun Tong refused.

The reason was simple.

Such familial aid and reinforcements came at a price.

Every time Hun Tong accepted, every benefit gained, his territory would gain more members of the Duo Mu Clan. These clan members obeyed his commands, but more often, they answered to the factions behind their clan.

Over time, it would become unclear whether the territory of Two Eyes Island belonged to Hun Tong personally or to the Duo Mu Clan.

This time was no different.

Why didn't Hun Tong seek help from his clan? He hadn't salvaged any loot, and he was desperately short of funds.

Once he revealed the truth to the clan, the reinforcements dispatched by the clan would be too overwhelming, and Hun Tong would have to pay the corresponding price, losing many of his privileges.

But if he could endure this difficult period, Hun Tong's future and prospects would be bright. After all, his former major threat—the Blue Green Coral fishman clan, had suffered heavy losses and had completely migrated away.

Yan Yi's direct declaration to deliver the message to the clan struck like a needle into the most sensitive part of Hun Tong's heart.

A question arose naturally: “During my forced exile from the island, Two Eyes Island is in grave danger!”

“None of my subordinates are capable of leading independently. Two Eyes Island is practically leaderless.”

“Once news of my escort spreads, the clan will certainly intervene!”

“Then my holdings, the foundation I built with blood, sweat, and tears, will fall into others' hands.”

“What am I to do?”

The urgency hit him. Even if he didn't fear Tan Mo's accusation, the fate of his territory terrified him. What if the imperial investigation cleared him, only for him to return to Two Eyes Island and find his home stolen?

“I must find someone to hold the island lord's position in my stead.”

“First, this person must be capable, able to stand alone and stabilize this place.”

“Second, this person must be ‘one of us,’ trustworthy enough for me to rely on.”

"Finally, this person must have a weakness, something I can hold over them. In short, someone I can control. Remember, lofty positions breed ambition in people's hearts."

After pondering, Hun Tong's gaze involuntarily settled on Yan Yi.

Wasn't this man before him the ideal proxy?

First, Yan Yi's talent was undeniably sufficient. He was the regional head of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency organization for the entire Two Eyes Island. Even if his personal strength fell short, he commanded a network of Imperial Secret Spy Agency operatives at his disposal.

Second, Yan Yi was a member of Hun Tong's clan, having collaborated with him all along. Though he'd been somewhat deceitful, it was ultimately driven by pursuit of his own interests. It was merely that events had spiraled beyond his control.

Finally, this fellow was weak! He was merely a bronze level. Even if he harbored treacherous thoughts in the future, as a gold level, could Hun Tong not handle him?

Reaching this conclusion, Hun Tong made his decision.

He clasped Yan Yi's hand firmly: “Yan Yi, my clansman, I entrust you with a crucial mission.”

“My Lord, please instruct me!” Yan Yi replied.

Hun Tong: “I am entrusting you with the position of Island Lord.”

"Ah?!" Yan Yi was stunned.

Before he could speak, Hun Tong waved him off. “Time is critical. That bastard Tan Mo has given us only five minutes. It's almost up.”

“I'll make this brief. Before I depart, you will temporarily assume the duties of Island Lord.”

"Do not refuse. You've worked with me long enough to know my men lack competence. Moreover, the fishmen's assault has depleted our forces."

“You are the best choice!”

"While acting as island lord, stabilize the situation. If the merchants cause trouble, blame it all on me. I'm already under arrest anyway. Let them scramble to bail me out, then I can compensate them for their losses."

"Be wary of clan members reaching out. Two Eyes Island is my domain! These clansmen arriving will be troublesome to handle."

Hun Tong finished speaking, patting Yan Yi's shoulder. “When I return, I'll hand over the Deputy Island Lord position to you!”

“The Imperial Secret Spy Agency isn't a place for the faint-hearted.”

“Two Eyes Island is your home. When you have children, I can even petition the Empire for a knighthood for you, carving out a portion of territory as your knightly fiefdom!”

"My Lord!!" Yan Yi trembled slightly with excitement, utterly moved by the bright future Hun Tong had laid out.

Yan Yi was so eager he could have pounded his chest in assurance: “I will devote every ounce of my strength to guarding this place fiercely!”

“Even if it means death, even if I'm assassinated, I will die at my post!”

Hun Tong immediately frowned: "No, you cannot die so easily. You are the Deputy Island Lord. This is what we'll do: I'll also entrust the Golden Flash to you."

“I'll grant you the authority to command the Golden Flash.”

"In peacetime, you'll remain hidden aboard the Golden Flash, stationed in the skies above Two Eyes Island. You'll strictly oversee all personnel entering and exiting the vessel. This will significantly safeguard your life!"

Yan Yi was overjoyed, lavishing praise on Hun Tong's brilliant arrangement.

Half an hour later.

Two Eyes Island's dock.

Yan Yi bid farewell to Tan Mo.

Tan Mo gazed intently at Yan Yi. “Congratulations. With events unfolding this way, you stand to gain the most.”

Yan Yi offered a wry smile. “Lord Tan Mo, this is a mutually beneficial partnership.”

“I'm merely a tool, a puppet propped up by the merchant alliance and great nobles behind me.”

Tan Mo snorted coldly. “Recognizing that fact shows you have some self-awareness.”

He still harbored resentment. After all, this time, he had been used as a pawn.

The next moment, Yan Yi produced a piece of intelligence and handed it to Tan Mo. “My Lord, I humbly request you speak favorably of me in your investigation report.”

“This intelligence concerns the whereabouts of the underwater spoils from the great battle of Two Eyes Island.”

“Lord, if you proceed to Ice Sculpture Island to secure these spoils, you will be the greatest victor.”

"Oh? You've been thoughtful." Tan Mo's cold expression melted away.

Yan Yi bowed deeply: “Then I wish you smooth sailing on this journey, Lord.”

Chapter 798: The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps Took Away the Spoils of War?

Tan Mo nodded and turned to board his Treasure Chest.

The Treasure Chest had long been prepared for departure and immediately set sail at a leisurely pace.

Within the ship's hold, in a prison cell, the imprisoned Hun Tong felt the vessel's movement. He opened his eyes, revealing hatred and resolve, then closed them again.

The Treasure Chest gradually left the dock, its speed steadily increasing.

Tan Mo looked at Yan Yi who was still watching him from the shore, his expression complex.

“This guy... he's actually quite something.”

At first, Tan Mo hadn't seen through Yan Yi's true nature, even using him as an intermediary to facilitate his extortion of Hun Tong.

Little did he expect that Yan Yi would deceive both sides, stoking the flames, fueling Tan Mo's greed, and intensifying Hun Tong's humiliation and rage.

Step by step, Tan Mo was led down this path. By the time he sensed something was amiss, the momentum of the negotiations had already solidified. The conflict between him and Hun Tong had reached a boiling point, impossible to resolve in the short term.

Moreover, Yan Yi immediately seized the opportunity, luring Tan Mo with a lucrative offer. Ultimately, swayed by this financial onslaught, Tan Mo joined Yan Yi and the major merchant alliance's scheme to “replace the island lord.”

Having framed Hun Tong, Tan Mo now looked back on the events with a chill running down his spine.

Yan Yi had skillfully maneuvered between the investigation team, the Two Eyes Island Lord Hun Tong, and the major merchant alliances, exploiting every faction to near perfection. He even used the Seventh Prince as a figurehead to establish trust for the merchant alliance's cooperation.

And he was merely a bronze level!

Such maneuvering was like a newborn infant dancing gracefully amidst a sea of giant crocodiles.

The crucial part? This infant actually pulled it off.

The audacity and vision Yan Yi displayed left Tan Mo, a gold level secret agent and naval commander, in awe.

Especially that final moment, when Tan Mo publicly arrested Hun Tong, Yan Yi stepped in. His performance left Tan Mo secretly stunned!

Yan Yi ultimately deceived Hun Tong, becoming the acting island lord and gaining control of the Golden Flash. With the endorsement of the previous ruling lord, the backing of major merchant alliances and the Wealth Church, plus his own network of secret agents... these three factions strongly supported him, securing his seat as ruler of Two Eyes Island.

"The only trouble is the Justice Church. They've dispatched a bishop to Two Eyes Island, along with a rather notorious Guang Ke. Now that Hun Tong is captured, their investment has gone down the drain, making them aggrieved parties. They're likely to cause trouble. This poses a challenge for Yan Yi."

But Tan Mo believed Yan Yi would overcome it.

Before arriving at Two Eyes Island, he would never have imagined a bronze level cultivator could resist such formidable enemies.

But now, Tan Mo had profound confidence in Yan Yi.

Tan Mo silently asked himself, if the roles were reversed, could he, in Yan Yi's place, have achieved what he had?

He considered his own courage and vision to be in no way inferior to Yan Yi's.

“The only thing missing is acting!”

Undoubtedly, this was a crucial skill that allowed Yan Yi to navigate smoothly among major factions and powerful figures.

Though he was only a bronze level transcendent, under his manipulation, it no longer seemed a disadvantage but had transformed into an advantage.

At bronze level... everyone naturally underestimated him.

The bronze level Yan Yi was looked down upon, yet at the same time, he was seen as harmless.

Major merchant alliances, the Wealth Church, even Hun Tong, all of them underestimated Yan Yi. Even he, Tan Mo, hadn't he looked down on Yan Yi?

"Magicians have a popular adage: ‘Wisdom is power.’ Seeing Yan Yi, I understand that saying more deeply."

"If I ever face such an enemy in the future, I'll kill him outright. No nonsense. Never play games with such people. He seems to have eyes in the back of his head!"

“Thankfully, Yan Yi's bloodline peak is only bronze level.”

Thinking this, Tan Mo exhaled a breath of stale air, feeling a surge of relief.

Tan Mo cast one final glance at the docks of Two Eyes Island.

As the distance grew, Yan Yi shrank into a blurry mass within his vision.

He turned and walked toward his captain's cabin.

Stepping onto the deck, his gaze lowered, as if seeing through the planks to the dungeon where Hun Tong was imprisoned.

Tan Mo neither abused nor tortured Hun Tong.

After all, Hun Tong remained uncondemned and retained his status as an imperial noble, a courtesy he deserved.

Simultaneously, this was Tan Mo’s method of lulling Hun Tong into complacency.

“Such treatment will make Hun Tong feel at ease, but he is already as good as dead!”

According to the plan, once Tu Ji wore off, agents dispatched by the major merchant alliances would step in to deliver a dignified and just end to Hun Tong.

Meanwhile, in another detention cell, a fishman remained imprisoned.

It was Dao Hen.

After being swayed by Yan Yi and recanting his testimony, he had become a crucial witness for Hun Tong's conviction. Consequently, he too was to depart aboard the ship.

“He's as good as dead too.”

Dao Hen knew secrets; his fate was equally sealed.

“A silver level fishman is one thing, but Hun Tong is gold level, with his own fiefdom. To end up like this... what a waste.”

“He had business acumen, adapting to circumstances and revitalizing Two Eyes Island.”

“But alas, he failed to recognize true talent, blinded by age!”

Hun Tong had always lamented the lack of capable subordinates. Yet viewed another way, wasn't this precisely proof of his own shortcomings, an inability to discern potential and nurture promising candidates?

“His greatest mistake was misreading the situation.”

"He thought Two Eyes Island was a faraway outpost, but the entire Main Plane is the Empire's chessboard! Who could possibly escape?"

Hun Tong misjudged the situation. Even now, he still believed this was merely a straightforward frame-up. He was innocent. The real culprit was Tan Mo, greedy and audacious beyond measure.

Little did he know, the tide of the times had already swept him underwater.

All that awaited him was drowning!

Thinking of Hun Tong, Tan Mo silently warned himself: “The Empire's advance into the Wilderness Continent has already unleashed the colossal waves of this era.”

“On the Beastman Continent, the ever-rising castles and the battles, great and small, are merely the surface of these waves.”

“Beneath the surface, however, rages a turbulent undercurrent.”

“These currents aren't confined to the Beastman Continent. They're spreading across the Sheng Ming Continent, permeating the entire Main Plane.”

“No one is spared.”

“Even gold levels, the ruling lords of the Empire, with just a single lapse in vigilance, and they'll be swallowed by the currents.”

“Hun Tong is a sacrifice of this era, right before our eyes.”

“His example is far from unique.”

“In this era's relentless tide, even gold level individuals can be shattered to pieces with the slightest misstep.”

Tan Mo's expedition to Two Eyes Island had netted him a massive windfall.

Yan Yi and the merchant alliance representative invited Tan Mo to intervene. After some bargaining, the latter secured 80,000 gold coins on the spot.

Combined with previous bribes from Yan Yi and Hun Tong, Tan Mo's total earnings this time exceeded 100,000 gold coins!

“I wasn't mistaken. This operation truly was a lucrative deal.”

“It was well worth spending 30,000 gold coins on gifts to secure such an assignment from higher-ups.”

Tan Mo demanded bribes, but he also funneled benefits upward.

The 30,000 gold coins represented his largest expense for this operation, with minimal expenditures elsewhere.

Supplies consumed by the Treasure Chest during its voyage could be reimbursed through the Imperial Navy.

Nearly all aboard the Treasure Chest were members of Tan Mo's clan, and their salaries were still paid by the Navy.

Tan Mo had also prepared a bribe for Tu Ji.

But Tu Ji never accepted it; he couldn't stand Tan Mo.

Tan Mo wisely withdrew, sparing himself further embarrassment.

Two other key figures were aboard Tan Mo's vessel: a member of the Secret Gate Church and a saint level shield guardian specializing in defense.

Both held critical roles yet remained deeply secluded, rarely appearing in public.

During stops at both Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island, they never left their respective quarters.

Tan Mo had made a fortune, yet his mood wasn't as joyful as he'd anticipated.

With mixed feelings, he returned to the captain's cabin.

Alone, with no one else present.

He unfolded the documents and carefully examined the intelligence sent by Yan Yi.

Moments later, he withdrew his gaze, lost in thought.

"The second necromancer once used alchemy puppets to seize the Iron Lump. Yes, I've seen the magic footage of that battle, that scene is there."

"After the Battle of Weeping Pine Island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps captured the Iron Lump. That is an indisputable fact."

“According to intelligence, upon arriving at Snow Bird Harbor in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suddenly became flush with cash, frequently selling high-value art pieces like antiques and calligraphy.”

At this thought, Tan Mo's eyes flashed with sharp brilliance.

“Interesting... Yan Yi's theory holds merit.”

“So, it seems likely that the necromancer deployed alchemy puppets during the naval battle to secretly salvage the island's entire treasure hoard.”

“Then the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps swept them all away.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps... Tan Qiu mentioned them briefly back on Snake Rat Island.”

“If they really got their hands on that loot, imagine how many gold coins that would be!”

“That's the entire island's accumulated wealth!”

"Especially since Two Eyes Island is a thriving commercial hub, many pirates come here to fence their loot!

Just speculating on the value of this haul made Tan Mo's breathing grow heavy.

“If this treasure really falls into the hands of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, what am I supposed to do?”

"Their background is complicated, likely arms smugglers operating under false identities. Perfect! This gives me the ideal cover to investigate them."

"No wonder Yan Yi said I might be the biggest winner of the Two Eyes Island naval battle. Haha! If fate smiles upon me and I truly get my hands on that loot, it would be utterly fantastic!"

Tan Mo's innate greed swiftly eroded the fleeting sense of awe and caution stirred by the staggering bounty. In its place surged an overwhelming impulse!

His current role and mission had already granted him follow-up intelligence regarding the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' arrival at Ice Sculpture Island.

“Hmph! So what if Long Fu can defeat gold level opponents with silver level cultivation?”

“He's still just a silver level!”

“Tu Ji will disembark midway. Without that constraint, my movements will be far more free.”

“Even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has some hidden cards, I have a saint level shield guardian by my side.”

"Even that bronze level Yan Yi managed to thrive. I'll ride their coattails and borrow their might, it's entirely possible!"

Tan Mo muttered to himself, his greedy nature fueling wild fantasies. He couldn't contain himself, he had to thoroughly test this Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

“Transmit my orders: increase speed.”

“I demand maximum speed to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!”

As Tan Mo issued the command, he thought to himself: "We must hurry. If the spoils are truly in their hands, I must get there quickly."

“Before these scoundrels squander every last coin.”

“That money belongs to me!”

Chapter 799: Snake Shadow and Rat Eye Builds Another Achievement

The Ice Sculpture Capital.

Wisteria shop.

A spy posing as a store clerk was inspecting the goods.

A massive wooden crate was pried open with an iron bar, revealing it filled to the brim with stones.

The inspecting secret agent immediately covered his nose and exclaimed, “Volcanic itch stones again!”

These stones varied in size, ranging from grayish white to black in color.

Inner and outer layers alike were riddled with countless minute, densely packed cavities.

Submerging the stones in water caused it to boil instantly, reaching a rolling boil within seconds.

Left exposed to air for extended periods, the stones emitted an overpowering, persistent stench.

Unprotected skin touching the stone's surface would cause unbearable itching, scratching until bleeding offered no relief.

"Count them carefully. This is a strict order from above. Don't say I didn't warn you, if you mess up and the higher-ups find out, you're dead meat!" warned the cargo-handling spy.

“Of course, of course!” The inspection clerk nodded frantically, his face etched with seriousness and caution.

While meticulously counting, he chatted casually, “Did you hear?”

"Just a few days ago, this place was attacked. Three silver level group leaders were killed!"

“Though the enemy was dealt with, who knows if any accomplices remain?”

“If enemy allies come seeking revenge, we'd be finished!”

The delivery agent's expression darkened instantly: “Keep your voice down! Is this something we should be discussing?”

“Can't you just focus on the inspection?”

“Shut your mouth. I don't want to get punished for listening to your nonsense.”

After being scolded, the inspector pouted but ultimately said nothing more.

This caused Zi Di, observing from a distance, to frown slightly in disappointment.

The silver level ice gargoyle she controlled stood silently beside the deliveryman. Even at such close range, neither of the two living people could see the ice gargoyle.

The combined effects of Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell made the ice gargoyle's comings and goings in the Wisteria shop as effortless as drinking water for an ordinary person.

“Ever since their ambush failed, the enemy has replaced nearly all the shop staff.”

“The old hands vanished through teleportation.”

“The new recruits are highly skilled too. This inspector is the most accessible source of intel I've found.”

After infiltrating the vicinity of the Wisteria shop, Zi Di's primary task was directing the ice gargoyle to conduct surveillance.

But her gains were meager.

The undercover agents were highly skilled and discreet, maintaining silence that made it difficult for Zi Di to uncover any secrets.

The shop manager and others only handled the surface-level business operations.

With the shop's dismal sales, the tasks assigned to the manager and other higher-ups were equally unremarkable.

Any truly confidential matters were relayed directly through communication devices.

Interactions among shop staff were minimal. Even work-related exchanges strictly adhered to confidentiality protocols.

"The only clear thing is that the enemies behind the Wisteria shop have been secretly transporting volcanic itch stones. This is already the sixteenth crate I've seen."

As a magician who had dabbled in alchemy, Zi Di possessed basic knowledge despite her limited skill level.

As far as she knew—volcanic itch stone was a common ingredient for bomb-making.

This made Zi Di vaguely sense: the enemies lying in wait for her were quietly brewing something, and it was likely to be big!

After the inspection was complete and the signature obtained, the deliveryman left.

The inspector struggled to carry the wooden crate toward the depths of the warehouse.

Zi Di immediately commanded her ice gargoyle to follow close behind.

A metal door swung open. The inspector stepped inside, and as his second foot crossed the threshold, the door slammed shut.

But the ice gargoyle had already squeezed through.

Though the inspector stumbled and nearly fell during the squeeze, he remained completely unaware.

Zi Di also directed the ice gargoyle to steady the wooden crate in his hands, preventing the entire load of itch stones from spilling onto the floor.

Of course, even if that had happened, Zi Di’s ice gargoyle would never have been detected.

The inspector would simply have blamed it on his own carelessness.

Struggling under the weight of the crate, the inspector descended to the first basement level, where he encountered yet another metal door. The difference was that, alongside the metal door, there was also an inspection device.

Basement 1, Basement 2, Basement 3...

The barriers set up by the Imperial Secret Spy Agency along the way grew increasingly numerous, and the inspection procedures became ever more stringent.

Metal doors were merely standard equipment. There were five or six different types of manual password responses, and elemental suppressors of various types appeared one after another. What Zi Di found most troublesome was the mental wave counter device.

This alchemy device severely interfered with Zi Di's control over the ice gargoyle.

Magicians controlled alchemy puppets by channeling their own spirit power. Zi Di's manipulation of the ice gargoyle was no exception.

Spirit power manifested as mental fluctuations.

This principle also applied to the long-distance communication between the duelists.

The mental wave counter device emits mental fluctuations at various frequencies to counteract external mental waves, preventing enemies from remotely controlling alchemy puppets to infiltrate this area.

Each mental wave counter device continuously alters its countering frequency bands over time.

Therefore, even if the frequency of Zi Di's mental fluctuations was unknown to the enemy, it was still suppressed by this defensive measure.

Each time the ice gargoyle advanced further, Zi Di's control over it weakened incrementally.

By the time the ice gargoyle reached the fourth underground level, Zi Di's connection to it had faded to a faint, indistinct trace.

Meanwhile, the inspector accompanying Zi Di arrived at the warehouse carrying wooden crates.

The warehouse door stood wide open.

Two secret agents addressed the inspector: “You're just in time. Help us carry these stones down.”

“Yes, sir!” The inspector nodded immediately.

This turn of events delighted Zi Di.

Previously, when she followed them here, the inspector would always abandon the wooden crates and head straight back.

Without his lead, the ice gargoyle couldn't pass through the metal door. Zi Di didn't want to rashly blast an opening either.

The inspector and the other two carried the wooden crates as they continued onward.

Zi Di immediately commanded the ice gargoyle. Its chest suddenly burst open, releasing several snake shadow ghosts and rat eye ghosts.

The mental wave counter device could disrupt conventional alchemy puppet control, but it couldn't fully counter a necromancer's summoned ghosts.

Though these ghosts also operated under a magic power's mental control, their bond with the necromancer was far deeper—a soul-level connection had already formed.

Much like how Jian Gui in the White Hair Trench secretly communicated with Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi, and others through their soul ties. Zhou Zhang was mere inches away yet remained completely unaware.

The snake shadow ghosts and rat eye ghosts continued their pursuit.

Moments later, they followed the three secret agents to the fifth underground level, where they saw the teleportation formation at the far end.

One by one, wooden crates were carried to the center of the formation.

Guarding the formation was none other than a gold level fighter.

“That's one of the two gold level fighters who attacked us before!” Zi Di recognized him immediately.

The rat eye ghosts climbed onto the gold level fighter's shoulders, scrutinizing his face closely.

The snake shadow ghosts coiled around the gold level fighter's thigh, clinging tightly to memorize his aura and probe deeper secrets within him.

Enhanced by the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, the gold level fighter sensed nothing amiss.

He sternly urged his subordinates to work faster.

Once all the wooden crates were moved, he teleported away amidst a cloud of volcanic itch stones.

“They've entered an alchemy laboratory.”

“It should be somewhere within the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.”

Moments later, a sharp gleam flashed in Zi Di's eyes: “Indeed. This is where they ambushed me before.”

Moments later, Zi Di's snake shadow ghosts and rat eye ghosts observed two gold level figures conversing.

“The Thirteenth Prince will return soon.”

“Besides him, there's also Lord Qi Ci Lang.”

“You mean the one called the Beloved Child of Seven Gods?”

“Precisely!”

“Excellent. He truly is the most suitable candidate for this Ice Sculpture Kingdom Tournament.”

"Long Meng is the top duelist in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. But Lord Qi Ci Lang possesses the blessing of immortality. Who among the gold level on Ice Sculpture Island could defeat him?"

"Once this mission is complete, I can retire. To be honest... I've truly had enough of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's bitter cold. Lately, I've been dreaming of the red bean banana trees back home night after night..."

…

The status of these two gold levels was worlds apart from those shop clerks.

Though their casual conversation didn't touch on Imperial Secret Spy Agency's secrets, Zi Di overheard enough sensitive terms to raise alarm bells.

For instance, their reference to the “Thirteenth Prince,” and the mention of “red bean banana trees”, a fruit unique to the Empire's southern regions.

“So, they really are imperial secret agents?”

Half an hour later, Zi Di confirmed this suspicion.

She told herself to stay calm, but her heart sank nonetheless.

All along, the survivors had pursued their goal of atoning to the Sheng Ming Empire. The hostile relationship with the Imperial Secret Spy Agency undoubtedly clashed violently with this objective.

In a way, the weight of antagonizing the Sheng Ming Empire's Secret Spy Agency felt even heavier and more oppressive than opposing the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

“Why is the Imperial Secret Spy Agency targeting us?”

“The Mystifying Monster Island incident is unlikely. The most probable target... is the Wisteria Cache!”

"Sister Qing Xin told me about it, that's how I learned of the Wisteria Cache. The Wisteria shop in Ice Sculpture Capital has already been infiltrated by the secret agents. The letter from Two Eyes Island never made it out. That means Two Eyes Island is likely compromised too. The most suspicious figure is the shopkeeper, Yan Yi."

"My identity was exposed while I was on Two Eyes Island, drawing the secret agent's attention. At the time, I attended the auction using the identity of a Wisteria elder. Then, during the naval battle, Cang Xu exposed his necromancer abilities."

"My ambush at the Wisteria shop was likely a follow-up from Two Eyes Island. They've been waiting for me."

Zi Di's thoughts scattered as she comprehensively analyzed her situation.

According to her reasoning, nothing had happened on Mystifying Monster Island, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' identity remained undiscovered for now. Otherwise, the secret agents could have easily struck them at sea.

Chapter 800: The Business Plan is a Great Success!

"The Imperial Secret Spy Agency is targeting the necromancer of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance."

"The mysterious force, however, is targeting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

"Therefore, these two forces are distinct."

After pondering this, Zi Di's trust in Yuan Ci grew significantly.

“Next, we must investigate two individuals in detail.”

“One is the ‘Thirteenth Prince,’ likely the hostage prince.”

“The other is ‘Qi Ci Lang,’ currently known to be an exceptionally powerful gold level individual possessing the ability of immortality.”

Zi Di did not recall the rat eye ghosts or snake shadow ghosts, leaving them temporarily within the alchemy laboratory.

The ice gargoyle also couldn't be recalled immediately.

It had to wait for the metal door to open again to withdraw from the Wisteria shop with minimal risk and cost.

Once Zi Di withdrew her primary focus, her mental connections with the ice gargoyle, the rat eye ghosts, and snake shadow ghosts immediately grew faint and blurred.

The link with the ice gargoyle was the weakest, while the connections with the snake shadow ghosts and rat eye ghosts remained somewhat stronger.

This was despite the latter being farther from Zi Di and separated by the transcendent environment of the Ten Millennia Ice Lake.

“Though the undead school is increasingly targeted and countered, it still retains certain advantages as a new magic faction.”

Zi Di contacted Zong Ge immediately.

The intelligence she had gathered was too crucial, fundamentally impacting the survivors' development strategies and upcoming operational plans.

Once the alchemy communication device connected, Zi Di and Zong Ge swiftly exchanged their secret code.

“There's bad news,” Zi Di said gravely, relaying all the intelligence she'd gathered to Zong Ge.

After a moment of silence, Zong Ge analyzed: The Imperial Secret Spy Agency was likely targeting the remnants of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance. This view coincided perfectly with Zi Di's.

“One is a mysterious force producing gold level individuals one after another, the other is the Sheng Ming Empire's secret spy organization...”

Zong Ge sighed. Even someone as tough as him felt the weight of the pressure.

"I have some good news at least." He said to Zi Di.

"Not long ago, the Li Jian Clan came with our profit assessment for this period of time."

“Sales of the dragon rider potion have been excellent. We've made over 200,000 gold coins in total profit!”

Zi Di paused slightly upon hearing this, then exhaled a quiet breath.

Her eyes stung, reddening.

It had not been easy!

This business venture had nearly collapsed midway. Fortunately, Zi Di had weathered the immense pressure and persevered.

She hadn't charged ahead recklessly, but instead considered reality, made decisive compromises, and sacrificed some profits to secure the Li Jian Clan's deep cooperation.

With this powerful ally, the business plan she spearheaded finally gained traction.

Now, it has yielded substantial interim results.

Zi Di took a deep breath, steadying her breathing, determined not to let Zong Ge on the other end of the communication device detect her fluctuating emotions.

She was thrilled.

She couldn't help it.

The recent barrage of bad news had been overwhelming!

Zi Di had been ambushed and attacked at the Wisteria shop. Had Yuan Ci not rescued her, her fate would have been grim. Even if she hadn't died, she would have been captured.

Afterward, Zi Di conducted covert investigations and formally confirmed that the enemy who ambushed her was indeed the Sheng Ming Empire's Secret Spy Agency.

This revelation brought immense psychological pressure!

The survivors' numbers and strength were simply too insignificant to compare with the empire's Secret Spy Agency.

Not only that, but beyond the Secret Spy Agency, they faced another mysterious and powerful enemy faction. This faction possessed an extraordinary number of gold level individuals!

Were it not for the support of the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-Investigation and Prophecy spell, and the cautious habits honed by the survivors on Mystifying Monster Island, they would have been crushed into dust by these two organizations long ago.

“This is indeed rare, good news,” Zi Di said calmly. “What are the specific details of the gains and losses?”

“I'll teleport them to you immediately,” Zong Ge replied.

Through the magic alchemy device, Zong Ge swiftly transmitted a magic image to Zi Di.

This magic image recorded the Li Jian Clan's commercial envoy visiting “Long Fu” (Zong Ge), delivering detailed written materials and giving a brief in-person report.

At that time, not only Zong Ge was present, but also an alchemy puppet impersonating Magician Yao Ma.

Whenever Zi Di acted alone, Zong Ge would deploy the alchemy puppet disguised as her.

Neither the Li Jian Clan nor anyone else detected the slightest irregularity.

The Deceptive Disguise spell proved remarkably reliable. However, this significantly accelerated the consumption of pearl bubbles.

When Zi Di departed Snow Bird Harbor, she carried over sixty pearl bubbles.

During the journey, Zong Ge ambushed the Axe Gang leader and others, consuming a considerable amount.

Upon reaching Igloo City, consumption skyrocketed and never decreased.

Arriving at the Ice Sculpture Capital, Zi Di faced another ambush and had a strong need for reconnaissance, causing the pearl bubble consumption to surge dramatically.

The pearl bubbles were depleted as they were used, and neither Zong Ge nor Zi Di had any means or channels to replenish it.

This was now their most critical strategic resource!

Zi Di desperately wanted to conserve it, but there was simply no way! The situation forced her to increase the pearl bubble usage in many areas.

She reviewed the magic projections and mentally accessed the textual records within the alchemy apparatus.

She studied them twice, gaining a deep understanding of the detailed income and expenditure this time.

dragon rider potions were selling like hotcakes nationwide across the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. This was a blue ocean market, demand remained largely unsatisfied, with vast untapped potential.

“Our primary competitor right now is the Jing Xiang Clan.”

“The Alchemy Guild hasn't reacted at all. This is beyond my expectations.”

Originally, Zi Di had assumed the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild would be their primary rival. Yet even now, this colossal entity remains silent.

“It's precisely this situation that made the first round of profits far greater than I anticipated.”

While the Jing Xiang Clan also held significant market share, it paled in comparison to the Alchemy Guild.

“The benefits of our deep cooperation with the Li Jian Clan are evident here.”

“Relying solely on Snow Bird Harbor's four alchemy workshops, even working day and night, couldn't meet the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom's potion demand.”

“The Li Jian Clan operates alchemy studios, workshops, and factories nationwide. Their mage towers also participate in potion production.”

“This allowed them to suppress the Jing Xiang Clan and securely reap the first wave of dividends brought by the commander's nationwide fame.”

As for expenditures...

The largest portion went toward acquiring the various shops under the Li Jian Clan's name during the initial phase.

Next came transportation costs.

The final category was reconstruction expenses. This primarily involved rebuilding the four alchemy workshops in Snow Bird Harbor that had been destroyed. However, thanks to Tripleblade's timely coordination and arrangements, these losses were far less severe than they appeared on the surface.

“Tripleblade played no small part.”

“Though he's only an iron level fighter and a staunch supporter of Zong Ge, his extensive experience as a mercenary captain allowed him to manage the sub-unit's operations.”

“During our absence, Tripleblade temporarily led the group and handled everything quite well.”

“If not for that major blunder, he would have earned significant credit.”

The major blunder Zi Di had in mind was, of course, Tripleblade losing the big guy.

At a critical moment, Tripleblade ordered Chi Lai to retrieve the big guy, never anticipating Chi Lai would act on his own initiative. This resulted in the big guy being taken away by Da Dou.

Of course, looking back at the entire sequence, Zi Di also realized: even if Chi Lai had retrieved the big guy in time, given the will and decisiveness Da Dou had demonstrated, he would still have forcibly taken the big guy away. The silver level Chi Lai couldn't have stopped Da Dou.

In fact, Tripleblade's order and Chi Lai's last-minute decision actually reduced the risk for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Had Chi Lai clashed with Tripleblade, he likely would have been killed in battle.

But none of this could obscure the error in Tripleblade's decision-making!

A mistake was a mistake.

On this point, Zong Ge also acknowledged flaws in Tripleblade's command.

Zong Ge enforced clear rewards and punishments, refusing to shield Tripleblade in spite of their personal connection.

"For this major dragon rider potion sales campaign, we invested over 600,000 gold. A significant portion of this capital was allocated to acquiring shops across various regions."

“We've now generated roughly 200,000 gold in profit, but the Li Jian Clan must receive approximately 80,000 gold from that revenue.”

“I plan to hand over 120,000 gold coins directly to the Li Jian Clan to expand production of the dragon rider potion,” Zi Di told Zong Ge.

Zong Ge didn't hesitate: “You make the call on this matter.”

Ever since the survivors emerged from Mystifying Monster Island, Zi Di had continuously demonstrated her business acumen.

Even Zong Ge, with his high expectations, found himself impressed.

Had this venture failed, Zong Ge might have hesitated, yet he would still regard Zi Di as the most business-savvy survivor.

Reality proved otherwise, Zi Di's plan achieved resounding success.

This was merely the first round of profits, already reaching 200,000 gold coins.

The foresight, tactics, and decisiveness Zi Di displayed throughout the process earned Zong Ge's quiet admiration.

Having commanded troops, Zong Ge understood all too well the immense importance of provisions to an army!

Therefore, he immediately gave Zi Di's proposal his full support.

“Long Fu, come out now!”

“If you could slay Teng Dong Lang, why won't you face me in combat?”

“I'm merely a silver level, what are you afraid of?”

“Come forth and accept my challenge!”

…

Zi Di frowned, hearing the commotion from Zong Ge's end through the alchemy device.

“What's going on?” Zi Di asked immediately.

Zong Ge's tone remained calm: “This is already the third challenger.”

“My location has been leaked. The first two who issued public challenges were taken care of by the Li Jian Clan.”

“But I suspect this situation will only escalate.”

“Though all three challengers are silver level, I don't think it's that simple. This is likely a follow-up offensive by the enemy, aiming to exhaust me and prevent me from recuperating."

Zong Ge possessed a keen battlefield instinct.

He instantly discerned the enemy's intent behind these challenges.
